To address these challenges, we introduce CoverMem, which incorporates three key components: (1) a coverage-constrained memory construction method that selects a compact set of informative full frames satisfying object-quality and representative-scene coverage requirements; (2) a coverage-preserving incremental maintenance strategy that locally reselects only the affected frames to satisfy updated coverage requirements as observations arrive; and (3) a budgeted complementary frame selection method that balances question relevance, additional support for question requirements, and redundancy with selected frames within a fixed budget. Experimental results on three benchmarks covering diverse environments and question types demonstrate that CoverMem improves question-answering performance under limited visual budgets.

</details>


### [69] [Breaking the Binary-Lifting Barrier for DNA Minimizer Density: Exact Optimization of a Native 4-ary Order at (w, k) = (12, 5)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32159)
*Stefan G. Creadore,Peyton Berntsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文绕过二进制提升，直接在原生4元置换空间上优化minimizer scheme的order，在(w,k)=(12,5)达到精确期望DNA密度约0.130052772292（density factor 1.690686），优于OptMini与GreedyMini等现有lookup方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优lookup方案（GreedyMini、OptMini）在2^k二进制序上优化，再通过"单bit-plane为主排序、另一bit-plane字典序破平"提升到Σ^k。该投影把32个DNA 5-mer锁入连续块，将有效UHS前缀限制在1024个5-mer中的448个，构成结构性瓶颈。

Method: 直接在|Σ^k|=1024的原生4元置换上从零优化：(i)基于B(4,4)（256状态）的de Bruijn端点转移矩阵精确DP；(ii)加权set-cover比值初始化SetCover4，得到紧凑U=314的UHS前缀；(iii)保持零代价w-walk-free尾部不变量的bitmask稀疏模拟退火；(iv)两遍O(N)精确best-insertion扫描配合大邻域ruin-and-recreate搜索。

Result: 得到显式4元序，精确期望密度2234289615/2^34≈0.130052772292（density factor 1.690686）；相对OrensteinLab/Minimizers减少11924929个charged窗口（-0.530890%），相对w12_k5.gm减少14689005（-0.653141%）；存储8200字节（位打包1280字节）。热启动松弛停滞于U=379（0.1301728789），原生4元初始化在全部2+2核苷酸划分上打破32/32高位块。

Conclusion: 二进制提升并非必要且会限制性能；直接在原生4元序上优化可获得更低密度，且原生初始化的突破性来自打破二进制投影引入的块结构瓶颈。

Abstract: Minimizer schemes sample at least one $k$-mer from every window of $w$ consecutive $k$-mers via a total order $π$ on $Σ^k$. For DNA ($σ= 4$), state-of-the-art lookup schemes such as GreedyMini and OptMini optimize a binary order ($2^k$ ranks) and lift it to $Σ^k$ by ranking one bit-plane primarily and breaking $2^k$-way ties lexicographically on the other bit-plane. At $(w, k) = (12, 5)$, this projection locks sets of $32$ DNA $5$-mers into contiguous blocks and restricts the active universal hitting set (UHS) prefix to $448$ of $1024$ $5$-mers, giving exact expected DNA density $2246214544 / 2^{34} \approx 0.1307468945$ for OptMini (k5-w12-13.txt) and $2248978620 / 2^{34} \approx 0.1309077849$ for GreedyMini (w12_k5.gm). We bypass binary lifting and optimize directly over native $4$-ary permutations ($|Σ^k| = 1024$) from scratch via: (i) an exact de Bruijn endpoint transfer-matrix dynamic program over $B(4, 4)$ ($256$ states), (ii) a weighted set-cover ratio initializer (SetCover4) producing a compact $U = 314$ UHS prefix, (iii) bitmask-sparse simulated annealing preserving the zero-cost $w$-walk-free tail invariant, and (iv) two-pass $O(N)$ exact best-insertion sweeps with large-neighborhood ruin-and-recreate search. The resulting explicit $4$-ary order achieves exact expected DNA density $2234289615 / 2^{34} \approx 0.130052772292$ (density factor $1.690686$), cutting charged windows by $11924929$ contexts ($-0.530890\%$ relative) below OrensteinLab/Minimizers and $14689005$ ($-0.653141\%$) below w12_k5.gm at $8200$-byte lookup storage ($1280$ bytes bit-packed). Warm-start relaxation stalls at $U = 379$ ($0.1301728789$), whereas native $4$-ary initialization breaks $32/32$ high-bit blocks across all $2+2$ nucleotide partitions.

</details>


### [70] [XMind: Thinking Twice via Cross-Memory Reasoning for LLM-Based Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32209)
*Zuyi Guo,Xianjie Dai,Lei Yang,Xiaocong Zhao,Jingguang Li,Kitae Jang,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架XMind，通过"三思而后行"的跨记忆推理，使LLM驾驶智能体在交通条件变化时仍能复用历史纠错经验，在闭环高速仿真中成功率83.3-93.3%，平均超越强基线6.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强LLM驾驶智能体的记忆主要记录已观测到的结果，缺乏关于替代动作的证据，导致过往学到的纠错在交通条件变化时难以迁移。

Method: XMind为免训练框架：融合近期交通状态的工作记忆与关键风险事件的情景记忆；智能体先基于当前上下文做初始决策，再与检索到的历史经验交叉校验，冲突时依据检索证据做第二次决策；情景记忆通过扰动交通条件下的反事实推演评估替代动作，将验证结果存为结构化"风险胶囊"，并按空间风险模式与场景语义联合匹配检索。

Result: 闭环高速仿真成功率83.3-93.3%，平均超强基线6.6个百分点；在K-Risk上对未见真实高风险交互展现更低决策风险；消融实验证实工作记忆、风险文本检索与反事实验证的互补增益。

Conclusion: 跨记忆推理与反事实验证的记忆构建可有效提升LLM驾驶智能体的经验迁移与决策安全性，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Memory-augmented large language model (LLM) driving agents can reuse past experience, yet previously learned corrections often fail to transfer when traffic conditions change. A key limitation is that existing memories mainly record observed outcomes, providing little evidence about alternative actions. We introduce XMind, a training-free framework that enables LLM-based driving to "Think Twice" through cross-memory reasoning. XMind integrates working memory of recent traffic states with episodic memory of critical risk events. The agent first makes an initial decision from the current context and then cross-checks it against retrieved historical experience. If a conflict arises, it makes a second decision guided by the retrieved evidence. To construct reliable episodic memory, XMind evaluates alternative actions through counterfactual rollouts under perturbed traffic conditions and stores the verified outcomes as structured risk capsules. These capsules are retrieved by jointly matching spatial risk patterns and scenario semantics. Across closed-loop highway simulations, XMind achieves success rates of 83.3-93.3%, exceeding the strong baseline by 6.6 percentage points on average. Evaluation on K-Risk further demonstrates lower decision risk on unseen real-world high-risk interactions. Ablation studies confirm the complementary benefits of working memory, risk-text retrieval, and counterfactual verification. Code is available at https://github.com/xiangxiangliuwo/XMind

</details>


### [71] [PoseVLN: Learning Geometric State Evolution for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32218)
*Pengyu Hou,Mingfei Han,Haihong Hao,Xiaolong Wu,Bo Dong,Xiaojun Chang,Salman Khan,Bin Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PoseVLN提出以相机位姿作为连接过去、现在与未来状态的几何表征，在情节相对坐标系中联合学习导航决策与几何状态演化，仅用单目RGB即在R2R-CE与RxR-CE Val-Unseen上分别超过最强基线2.6与2.9个百分点成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法分别建模长时记忆、状态感知推理与未来预测，缺少跨时间一致的状态演化连接；作者认为相机位姿是桥接过去、现在与未来的天然几何表征。

Method: 将视觉记忆与当前观测统一到情节相对坐标系；联合预测未来K步导航动作及同一时域内的局部ΔPose；将预测ΔPose与当前轨迹级位姿复合得到未来位姿，并在导航过程中递归传播，形成连续几何状态演化。

Result: 在R2R-CE与RxR-CE的Val-Unseen划分上，成功率分别比最强基线高2.6与2.9个百分点，且仅使用单目RGB观测。

Conclusion: 显式建模几何状态演化对VLN有效，位姿可作为统一的时间桥接表征，把记忆、推理与未来预测整合为一致的演化框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to relate past experience, current spatial state, and future motion while following natural-language instructions. Recent approaches improve these capabilities through long-horizon memory, state-aware reasoning, and future prediction, yet these components are often modeled separately, leaving navigation state evolution insufficiently connected across time. We argue that camera pose provides a natural geometric representation for bridging past, present, and future states. Based on this insight, we propose PoseVLN, a pose-aware VLN framework that jointly learns navigation decisions and geometric state evolution. PoseVLN organizes visual memory and the current observation in an episode-relative coordinate frame, and jointly predicts the next K navigation actions together with a local $Δ$Pose over the same horizon. The predicted $Δ$Pose is composed with the current trajectory-level pose to obtain the future pose state, which is recursively propagated throughout navigation, forming a continuous geometric state evolution from past memory to future motion. Experiments on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE Val-Unseen splits demonstrate the effectiveness of explicitly modeling geometric state evolution, with PoseVLN outperforming the strongest compared baselines by 2.6 and 2.9 percentage points in success rate, respectively, using only single RGB observations.

</details>


### [72] [Refining and Aligning Clinical Reasoning with Health Guidelines via Formal Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32247)
*Xiaoyang Fan,Yufan Cai,Zhe Hou,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VerClin可解释框架：将指南解析为可执行知识、把患者对话表示为作用域病例，用确定性四值推理器产生可追溯的指南匹配（保留不确定性并强制排除项），再由多专科智能体筛选病例适用性，最终由会诊模型整合生成建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床推理需在证据不完整或相互冲突时，既遵循健康指南，又能识别低概率但高风险的问题。现有直接生成与大模型RAG方法缺乏可追溯性和对排除条件、优先级的严格约束。

Method: 1) 从指南中抽取条件、排除项、推荐与优先级，构建可执行知识；2) 将患者对话表示为作用域病例（scoped cases）；3) 确定性四值推理器输出可追溯匹配，保留不确定性并强制排除；4) 多专科智能体对匹配到的指南做病例适用性筛查；5) 会诊模型通过专科建议与保句式形式约束通道合成被接受的指南；6) 按优先级与触发强度秩之和升序呈现，以暴露弱触发的高风险指南。

Result: 在HealthBench Hard与HealthBench Professional上优于直接生成与Top-5 RAG；在LLMEval-Med子集上优于直接生成、与Top-5 RAG持平。消融分析考察了语义闭合、示例引导精化、专科筛查与约束条件化交付的作用。

Conclusion: 将指南形式化为可执行知识并结合确定性四值推理与专科筛查，可在不确定与冲突证据下生成可追溯、指南约束、并突出高风险事项的临床建议。

Abstract: Clinical reasoning from incomplete or conflicting patient evidence must account for low-probability but high-risk concerns while remaining grounded in health guidelines. We introduce VerClin, an explainable framework that extracts guideline conditions, exclusions, recommendations, and priorities into executable knowledge and represents patient conversations as scoped cases. A deterministic four-valued reasoner produces traceable guideline matches while preserving uncertainty and enforcing exclusions. Multiple specialty agents then screen matched guidelines for case applicability, and a consultation model synthesizes accepted guidance through specialty recommendations and a sentence-preserving formal-constraint channel. We present accepted guidance in ascending order of the sum of its priority and trigger-strength ranks, which can surface high-risk guidance with weaker triggers. VerClin outperforms direct generation and Top-5 RAG on HealthBench Hard and HealthBench Professional. On the LLMEval-Med subset, it outperforms direct generation and ties Top-5 RAG. Component analyses further examine semantic closure, example-guided refinement, specialty screening, and constraint-conditioned delivery.

</details>


### [73] [VC-OPD: Learning to See What Matters via Visual Cue On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32292)
*Qinxuan Wang,Zheyu Zhang,Yijing Yang,Han Shu,Chuang Wang,Peipei Yang,Shuo Tang,Jie Hu,Shiming Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VC-OPD提出用"问题条件化的视觉线索"替代局部裁剪图像作为教师侧特权信息，通过多阶段质量过滤后仅训练时提供给教师，使学生小模型沿自身轨迹获得token级监督，从而在不增加推理开销的情况下提升细粒度视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉on-policy蒸馏（OPD）常以裁剪/放大的局部图像区域作为教师特权信息，但局部像素级线索不足以支撑需要全局结构、空间关系与高层语义的复杂视觉推理。

Method: 由先进MLLM针对每个问题生成视觉线索（关键视觉细节、结构关系、语义证据）；经多阶段质量过滤筛选可靠线索；仅训练时提供给教师模型，使学生沿自身生成轨迹获得token级监督，学习直接从原图定位并推理关键证据；推理阶段无需任何额外信息。由于先进模型仅用于线索生成，方法兼容开源权重与闭源黑盒模型。

Result: 在STEM与通用视觉推理基准上，跨模型规模均稳定优于强化学习与on-policy蒸馏基线；4B模型经VC-OPD训练后可超过多个8B及以上参数的基线模型。

Conclusion: 问题条件化视觉线索是比局部区域更有效的教师特权信息形式，能够实现高效的能力迁移，并兼顾问答性能、推理零额外开销与黑盒模型可用性。

Abstract: Visual On-Policy Distillation (OPD) commonly uses cropped or zoomed-in image regions as teacher-side privileged information to improve fine-grained perception in small Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, local pixel-level cues are insufficient for complex visual reasoning that requires global structure, spatial relationships, and high-level semantics. We propose Visual Cue On-Policy Self-Distillation (VC-OPD), which extends teacher-side privileged information beyond local image regions to question-conditioned visual cues. Advanced MLLMs generate cues capturing key visual details, structural relationships, and semantic evidence needed to answer each question. A multi-stage quality filtering process selects reliable cues, which are provided only to the teacher during training. This enables informative token-level supervision along student-generated trajectories, helping the student learn to identify and reason over critical evidence directly from the original image. No additional information is required at inference time. Since advanced MLLMs are used only for visual cue generation, VC-OPD is compatible with both open-weight and proprietary black-box models. Experiments on STEM and general visual reasoning benchmarks show consistent improvements across model scales over reinforcement learning and on-policy distillation baselines. Notably, a 4B model trained with VC-OPD outperforms many baselines with 8B or more parameters, demonstrating effective capability transfer through question-conditioned visual cues.

</details>


### [74] [Sparse Poison, Strong Hijack: Efficient Trajectory-Level Backdoors in Vision-Language-Action Models with Only 1\% Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32304)
*Simin Huang,Jiawei Chen,Shaolong Zhu,Qiwei Liang,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出视觉-语言-动作引导的两阶段协同优化框架，在仅1%投毒率下实现VLA模型轨迹级定向后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型依赖示范数据而面临后门威胁；轨迹级定向后门需维持闭环重定向完成攻击者指定的多步任务，其危害大于行为扰乱型攻击。现有方法需高投毒率以获得足够目标轨迹监督，但高投毒率损害干净任务性能，低投毒率则目标监督稀缺、易被主导的干净任务更新干扰，形成难以兼顾的权衡。

Method: 两阶段协同优化框架：阶段一冻结策略，借助可微渲染与多模态监督优化3D触发器的纹理、平移与尺度，从有限投毒示范中强化触发-目标关联；阶段二在优化触发器基础上采用源选择性双LoRA自适应，解耦干净与后门更新以缓解相互干扰，随后经学习的列级校准与解析融合得到统一策略。

Result: 在OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT、π0、π0.5四个模型及四个LIBERO子集上均优于所有对比攻击基线；OpenVLA-OFT在LIBERO-Spatial上仅用1%投毒数据即达86.4%目标成功率与95.2%干净任务成功率；真机实验亦实现轨迹级劫持。

Conclusion: 通过多模态引导的触发器优化与双LoRA解耦，可在低投毒率下有效突破后门攻击的监督稀缺与更新干扰权衡，同时保持干净任务性能，并在多模型、多任务及真机场景中验证了轨迹级定向后门的可行性与安全风险。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models advance robotic manipulation, but their reliance on demonstrations exposes them to backdoor threats. Among these threats, trajectory-level targeted backdoors are particularly challenging and may pose greater safety risks than behavior-disrupting attacks: rather than merely causing task failures or local action deviations, they must sustain closed-loop redirection to complete an attacker-specified multi-step task. To reliably induce such behaviors, existing methods typically rely on high poisoning rates to obtain sufficient target-trajectory supervision. However, high poisoning rates may compromise clean-task performance, whereas low rates leave target-trajectory supervision scarce and backdoor learning vulnerable to interference from dominant clean-task updates. To resolve this trade-off at low poisoning rates, we propose a two-stage co-optimization framework guided by vision, language, and action. Stage I freezes the policy and uses differentiable rendering and multimodal supervision to optimize a 3D trigger's texture, translation, and scale, strengthening trigger-target associations from limited poisoned demonstrations. Building on the optimized trigger, Stage II applies source-selective dual-LoRA adaptation to decouple clean and backdoor updates and mitigate their interference, followed by learned column-wise calibration and analytic fusion into a unified policy. Experiments on OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, $π_0$, and $π_{0.5}$ across four LIBERO suites show consistent gains over all evaluated attack baselines. With only 1% poisoned data, our method achieves 86.4% target success and 95.2% clean-task success on OpenVLA-OFT in LIBERO-Spatial. Real-robot trials also demonstrate trajectory-level hijacking.

</details>


### [75] [Alpha-Beta Pruning via Monadic Continuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32320)
*Martín Escardó,Paulo Oliva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于 continuation monad 与 reader monad 的组合，给出 alpha-beta 剪枝的 monadic 形式化。将 alpha-beta 界记录在 monadic 结果中而非移动类型里，并用普通 continuation monad 同时计算最优结果与最优着法序列，理论证明与穷举 minimax 等价，井字棋实验中着法评估次数减少 39 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作将 alpha-beta 界编码进可用移动的类型中，改变了移动类型；同时为缓解 selection monad 在 alpha-beta 搜索中的低效而提出 continuation monad 的各种推广。作者希望找到一种更标准、更简洁的 monadic 抽象来表达 alpha-beta 剪枝，并支持最优着法（play）的计算与形式化验证。

Method: 构造基于 continuation monad 与 reader monad 组合的 monadic alpha-beta 搜索：将 alpha-beta 界存放在 monadic 结果（outcome）中，保持移动类型不变；通过扩展结果类型使其包含着法序列，用普通 continuation monad 计算最优结果与最优 play；并在井字棋上实现，形式化证明其与穷举 minimax 搜索结果一致。

Result: 井字棋实现中，计算最优 play 时评估的 play 数量相比穷举搜索减少 39 倍；形式化证明 monadic alpha-beta 搜索计算出的最优结果与最优 play 与穷举 minimax 搜索相同。

Conclusion: 展示了 AI 中的基础算法可以用标准 monadic 抽象表达并验证，为对抗搜索的组合式（compositional）方法提供了基础。

Abstract: Alpha-beta pruning is a fundamental technique in game-playing AI, enabling efficient minimax search by avoiding the exploration of branches that cannot affect the optimal decision. We present a monadic formulation of alpha-beta pruning based on the combination of the continuation monad and the reader monad. Unlike previous approaches that encode alpha-beta bounds into the types of available moves, our construction records these bounds within monadic outcomes, leaving the move types unchanged. We further show that the ordinary continuation monad suffices to compute not only optimal outcomes but also optimal plays, by extending the outcome type to include the corresponding sequences of moves. This provides an alternative to generalisations of the continuation monad proposed to address the inefficiencies associated with the selection monad in alpha-beta search. We illustrate our approach with an implementation of Tic-Tac-Toe, observing a 39-fold reduction in the number of plays evaluated, compared with exhaustive search, in computing an optimal play. Finally, we formally establish the correctness of our construction by proving that the monadic alpha-beta search computes the same optimal outcomes and plays as exhaustive minimax search. Our results demonstrate how a foundational algorithm in AI can be expressed and verified using standard monadic abstractions, providing a basis for further development of compositional approaches to adversarial search.

</details>


### [76] [FigmaEval: A Benchmark for Evaluating AI Agents in Professional UI Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32321)
*Xinyue Huang,Yongyong Gao,Zhengyuan Pan,Zheng Wei,Xinya Liu,Duanyi Yan,Zhiyu Hang,Zheng Ma,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 FigmaEval 基准，用于评估 AI agent 在专业 UI 设计（Figma）任务上的能力，包含 84 个任务与 783 个界面，采用"约束规格、开放解法"范式，并设计 22 条专家评分细则与人类评估竞技场，对 11 个模型进行系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent 已能借助 Figma 等专业工具进行自主 UI 设计，但设计任务开放性强、缺乏唯一标准答案，现有基于参考相似度的评估方式难以衡量真实设计质量，缺少系统性评测手段。

Method: 构建 FigmaEval 基准：84 个任务、783 个界面，源自真实 Figma 设计文件；采用 constrained-specification, open-solution 范式，统一任务需求与设计资源，由 agent 自主决定布局、视觉层级、组件构成与视觉呈现；提出覆盖六个维度的 22 条专家评分细则（rubric），并引入专业设计师盲评成对比较的人类评估竞技场；在统一 Figma agent 设置下评测 11 个模型。

Result: 基于 rubric 的自动评估与人类偏好高度一致；不同模型在设计实践与质量—效率权衡上呈现明显差异。

Conclusion: FigmaEval 为系统评估专业 UI 设计能力提供了框架，有助于理解当前 AI agent 的优势与局限。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI agents have enabled autonomous UI design using professional tools such as Figma, yet evaluating their design capabilities remains challenging due to the open-ended nature of design tasks and the absence of unique ground truths. We introduce FigmaEval, a benchmark for evaluating AI agents on professional UI design tasks, comprising 84 tasks and 783 screens derived from real-world Figma design files. FigmaEval adopts a constrained-specification, open-solution paradigm, standardizing task requirements and design resources while allowing agents to independently determine layouts, visual hierarchies, component composition, and visual presentation. To assess design quality beyond reference similarity, we develop 22 expert-grounded evaluation rubrics across six dimensions and introduce a human evaluation arena where professional designers compare agent-generated designs through blinded pairwise judgments. Evaluating eleven models under a unified Figma agent setup, we find substantial agreement between rubric-based assessments and human preferences, alongside distinct differences in design practices and quality--efficiency trade-offs. FigmaEval provides a framework for systematically assessing professional UI design capabilities and understanding the strengths and limitations of current AI agents.

</details>


### [77] [The Control-Flow Tax: Specialized Decision Routing for LLM Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32365)
*Zhikai Cheng,Shuanghu Wang,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估一种异构智能体架构：由专用System 1决策模型Jev负责控制流路由（有限动作选择），由生成模型Sol负责文档生成，以降低"控制流税"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文档工作流中，控制流路由占初始任务基线参考成本的42.5%；仅降低token单价无法解决该问题，因为廉价的通用路由器可能触发对昂贵生成器的额外调用。

Method: 对比异构架构（Jev控制有限动作、Sol生成文档），在四个软件任务上进行实验，共480次有效运行（470次完成、10次请求上限端点）；并单独在100个零售状态上比较Sol与Jev的动作选择。

Result: Jev全路由在所有任务上取得最低平均成本与耗时，40次专家控制运行均以最短轨迹完成。初始任务平均成本下降49.6%（95%配对自助法区间42.9%–56.3%），耗时下降33.2%（19.6%–46.7%）；其余三任务成本降幅40.5%–42.9%，并发耗时降幅12.9%–14.4%。仅"存在性"专用化在初始任务仅节省1.8%。在零售状态上二者均达100/100可接受动作选择，Jev参考成本少97.88%、平均请求时间少72.30%。

Conclusion: 高效智能体控制有两个要求：决策成本低廉，且轨迹能保留这些节省。

Abstract: An agent can spend almost as much deciding what to do as doing it. In our documentation workflow, control-flow routing accounts for 42.5% of baseline reference cost on the initial task. Lower token prices alone do not solve this control-flow tax: inexpensive general-purpose routers can trigger additional calls to the expensive generator. We evaluate a heterogeneous architecture in which Jev, a specialized System 1 decision model, controls finite actions while Sol generates documents. Across four software tasks, 480 valid runs comprise 470 completions and ten request-limit endpoints. Jev full routing achieves the lowest mean cost and elapsed time on every task, completing all 40 specialist-controlled runs along the shortest trajectory. On the initial task, mean cost falls by 49.6% (95% paired-bootstrap interval: 42.9% to 56.3%) and elapsed time by 33.2% (19.6% to 46.7%). Across three further tasks, cost reductions are 40.5% to 42.9% and concurrent elapsed-time reductions are 12.9% to 14.4%. Existence-only specialization saves just 1.8% on the initial task. Separately, Sol and Jev each select accepted actions on 100/100 reviewed retail states, with Jev using 97.88% less reference cost and 72.30% less mean request time. The results identify two requirements for efficient agent control: inexpensive decisions and trajectories that preserve those savings.

</details>


### [78] [Can AI Design a Better nnU-Net? A Hypothesis-Driven Architecture Discovery Loop from Failures to Design Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32384)
*Songtao Jiang,Yuan Wang,Fucheng Niu,Hualiang Wang,Jian Wu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出由LLM智能体驱动的假设驱动架构发现闭环，通过诊断结构化错误、提出可证伪假设、在冻结nnU-Net流水线下做反事实对照，并将验证过的机制蒸馏为可执行的触发-动作规则，最终得到LeanNeXt与LeanNeXt+。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经架构搜索与LLM驱动演化把评估压缩为单一标量适应度，丢弃了可解释设计成败的失败信息，难以产生可迁移、可复用的设计知识。

Method: 构建假设驱动的架构发现闭环：LLM智能体诊断结构化错误模式→形成可证伪假设→在冻结的nnU-Net基线下进行反事实对照实验→将验证有效的机制蒸馏为"触发器→动作"可执行规则；共进行八轮搜索。

Result: 提炼出两条原则：(1)将计算从高分辨率解码器重分配至中层编码器，在降低开销的同时保持细薄解剖结构边界；(2)支持域受限的区域对比，处理嵌套解剖腔室间的误差，并由解剖依赖触发器决定规则适用时机。据此得到LeanNeXt（MACs为nnU-Net的0.78，训练步时0.92，四折KiTS23交叉验证Dice +1.63）与触发路由的LeanNeXt+；后者在全部15个CT/MRI设定上优于nnU-Net，并在12/15基准上超越参数多3.3倍的ResEnc-M，参数仅为其0.30。相较随机采样、一次性LLM生成与分数选择式智能体搜索，该闭环显示出失败分析可产出跨模态可复用设计规则。

Conclusion: 以失败分析为核心的假设驱动闭环优于标量分数驱动的搜索范式，能将失败转化为可解释、可复用、跨模态迁移的设计原则与规则化架构。

Abstract: Neural architecture search and LLM-driven evolution often reduce evaluation to a scalar fitness score, discarding failures that could explain why designs succeed or fail. We present a hypothesis-driven architecture discovery loop in which LLM agents diagnose structured error patterns, formulate falsifiable hypotheses, test counterfactual controls under a frozen nnU-Net pipeline, and distill validated mechanisms into executable trigger-to-action rules. Across eight search waves, the loop identifies two principles for 3D medical image segmentation. First, reallocating computation from high-resolution decoders to the mid-encoder preserves thin-anatomy boundaries while reducing cost. Second, support-confined region contrast addresses errors between nested anatomical compartments, with anatomy-dependent triggers selecting when the rule applies. These principles produce LeanNeXt (0.78 MACs and 0.92 training-step time relative to nnU-Net; +1.63 Dice points on four-fold KiTS23 cross-validation) and trigger-routed LeanNeXt+. The latter improves all 15 evaluated CT/MRI settings over nnU-Net and exceeds the 3.3 larger ResEnc-M on 12 of 15 benchmarks at 0.30 its parameter count. Relative to random sampling, one-shot LLM generation, and score-selected agent search, the loop shows how failure analysis can yield reusable design rules across modalities.

</details>


### [79] [VISE: Verifier-Independent Skill Self-Evolution with Feedback from Tool Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32480)
*Yuxuan Liu,Chongyang Du,Yuhao Zhang,Jiahe Guo,Kawai Chung,Wenbin Hu,Huihao Jing,Hongyu Luo,Qing Zong,Haochen Shi,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VISE方法：从工具源码中提取能力、输入要求、使用条件与输出格式，将其与当前技能指令比对，定位缺失或冲突步骤并给出SKILL.md的具体修改建议，实现无需任务验证器的技能自演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能（可复用的工作流）需要关于缺失或错误指令的反馈来精炼，但针对具体任务的验证器并非总是可用，因此需要一种不依赖验证器的反馈来源。

Method: VISE（Verifier-Independent Skill Evolution）采用"脚本优先"策略，以工具实现作为反馈信号：从源代码中抽取工具能力、输入要求、使用条件与输出格式；结合任务请求与执行轨迹，与现有工作流指令进行比对，识别缺失或冲突的步骤；随后对SKILL.md提出具体编辑建议，并将每项改动链接到相关实现与使用条件。作为附加修订阶段接入四种演化方法。

Result: 在SkillsBench与SkillLearnBench上，相较对应的无验证器条件，整体平均任务成功率提升9.14–13.35个百分点；三次运行均解出的任务比例提升12.36–18.29个百分点。案例研究表明，修订后的指令能将工具输出与后续步骤正确衔接。

Conclusion: 工具实现可作为技能自演化的一种实用反馈来源，在缺乏任务验证器时有效提升技能质量与任务成功率。

Abstract: Agent skills provide reusable workflows that guide LLM agents in using tools. Refining these workflows requires feedback on missing or incorrect instructions, but task-specific verifiers are not always available. We propose VISE (Verifier-Independent Skill Evolution), a scripts-first approach that uses tool implementations to guide skill revision. It extracts tool capabilities, input requirements, usage conditions, and output formats from source code. Using the task request and execution trace, it compares this information with current workflow instructions to identify missing or conflicting steps. It then recommends specific edits to SKILL.md, linking each change to the relevant implementation and usage conditions. We evaluate VISE as an additional revision stage for four evolution methods on SkillsBench and SkillLearnBench. Compared with the corresponding No-verifier conditions, it improves overall average task success by 9.14-13.35 percentage points. The proportion of tasks solved in all three runs increases by 12.36-18.29 points. A case study shows how revised instructions connect tool outputs to later steps. These results support tool implementations as a practical feedback source for skill self-evolution.

</details>


### [80] [RecurSciBench: Benchmarking Recursive Self-Improvement in Data-Driven Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32508)
*Wenxi Xu,Chuan Qin,Zihan Wang,Zixing Chen,Le Zhang,Jingbo Zhou,Hao Wang,Yuanchun Zhou,Hengshu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 RecurSciBench，一个可扩展基准，用于评估基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体在数据驱动科学研究中的递归自我改进（RSI）及其迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RSI 智能体有望通过经验持续改进，但要评估其在科学任务间的迁移，需要可执行、可科学验证的任务，并对自我改进带来的增益进行独立评估，目前缺乏此类基准。

Method: 构建 RecurSciBench，其 RecurSciPipe 框架从原始数据集自动生成科学建模与数据分析任务，并采用基于执行的验证与科学有效性检查；基准覆盖四个科学领域、多种数据模态与多样化科学目标。实验评估四个 RSI 系统，在源任务上进化后，于独立任务上对比其初始状态与进化状态，涵盖同类型、跨类型与跨领域迁移。

Result: 自我改进的收益因智能体系统与科学领域而异；整体正向增益可与个别任务上的退化并存；经验可跨任务类型迁移，并惠及其他领域，即使在源领域无可测增益的情况下。

Conclusion: RSI 在科学研究中的迁移效果具有异质性与非单调性，需以可执行、可验证的独立基准进行评估；RecurSciBench 为此提供了可扩展的评估框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are advancing the automation of data-driven scientific research. Recent advances in recursive self-improvement (RSI) offer the potential for these agents to continuously improve through experience, but evaluating transfer across scientific tasks requires executable, scientifically verifiable tasks and independent assessment of self-improvement gains. We introduce RecurSciBench, a scalable benchmark for evaluating RSI in data-driven scientific research. Its RecurSciPipe framework automatically constructs scientific modeling and data analysis tasks from raw datasets, with execution-based verification and scientific validity checks. RecurSciBench spans four scientific domains, multiple data modalities, and varied scientific objectives. We evaluate four RSI systems by comparing their initial and evolved states on independent tasks after evolution on source tasks, covering within-type, cross-type, and cross-domain transfer. Experiments show that the benefits of self-improvement vary across agent systems and scientific domains. Positive overall gains can coexist with regressions on individual tasks. Experience can transfer across task types and benefit other domains even without measurable gains in the source domain.

</details>


### [81] [GeoSurge: Geometry-Grounded Multimodal Reasoning for Auditable Liver Resection Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32657)
*Wenjie Deng,Zijian Tu,Xueyin Zhou,Huayi Wang,Zhuoyun Du,Mingyu Chen,Haochao Ying,Jian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GeoSurge框架，将解剖理解与分割能力转化为显式受约束、可执行的切除曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术切除规划需要从解剖证据和操作约束生成可执行的切除曲面；现有医学多模态基础模型能理解解剖、分割模型能预测切除区域，但二者都无法输出显式受约束、可执行的切除曲面。

Method: 结合掩膜导出的度量与结构感知点云token；通过“曲面-动作语法”将动作映射为参数化几何，使切除几何可学习、可编辑；用面向解剖与以肿瘤为中心的模板生成候选，经硬约束过滤和语义去重；训练监督来自候选-专家几何一致性与独立重算的可行性指标；推理时由推理器依据患者证据与手术要求选择或修正动作，再由独立几何引擎实体化并验证。

Result: 在DeepTumour Liver与CRLM-QA上取得最佳综合得分；约束满足率在CRLM上由73%提升至100%，在GeoSurge-Bench上由56%提升至99%。

Conclusion: 以语法化的参数几何解耦“决策”与“几何生成/验证”，可高效获得高约束满足率且可编辑的可执行切除曲面。

Abstract: Surgical resection planning centers on generating executable resection surfaces from anatomical evidence and operative constraints. Medical multimodal foundation models enable anatomical understanding, and segmentation models predict resection regions, but neither translates these capabilities into explicitly constrained, executable resection surfaces. We present GeoSurge, an anatomy-grounded framework that combines mask-derived metrics with structure-aware point-cloud tokens to generate executable, editable resection surfaces. To make resection geometry learnable and editable, GeoSurge maps actions to parametric geometry through a surface-action grammar. Using this grammar, anatomy-oriented and tumor-centered templates generate candidates, which are filtered by hard constraints and semantic deduplication. Training supervision is then derived from candidate-expert geometric agreement and independently recomputed feasibility metrics. At inference, the reasoner uses patient evidence and surgical requirements to select or revise an action, which an independent geometry engine subsequently materializes and verifies. GeoSurge achieves the best overall scores on DeepTumour Liver and CRLM-QA, improving the constraint satisfaction rate from 73% to 100% on CRLM and from 56% to 99% on GeoSurge-Bench.

</details>


### [82] [Economic World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32663)
*Qirui Mi,Yujia Ming,Bo Li,Haifeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"经济世界模型"(EWM)概念，即智能体对经济系统运行方式的内部表征，用于反事实模拟与资源约束下的决策，并给出连接"行为模拟—经济转移学习—系统评估"三大任务的通用框架；初步实现以微观数据学习家庭策略、宏观数据校准结构转移模型，实现微观到宏观的评估，结果显示消费分布重合度94.9%、GDP预测贴近真实值，并能复现政府借贷冲击的短期传导、提升LLM智能体的规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是AI能否复现由家庭、企业和政府在市场与制度中互动适应所产生的经济动态。现有工作缺乏对"经济世界模型"的明确界定，也缺少统一的模拟、学习与评估框架，因此作者希望建立可检验的研究范式，并呼吁经济学与AI研究者共同推进。

Method: 将EWM定义为智能体对经济系统运作的内部表征，提出连接三项任务的框架：模拟智能体行为、学习经济转移、系统评估动态。初步实现中，从微观经济学数据学习家庭（消费）策略，用宏观经济数据校准结构性经济转移模型，将个体决策与总体动态相连接；评估采用微观到宏观框架，将模拟的个体行为与群体分布同观测微观数据对比，并通过预测与反事实实验检验宏观动态。

Result: 模拟与观测的家庭消费分布重合度达94.9%；EWM预测能紧密跟踪真实GDP及其他若干总量水平；在政府借贷反事实中，EWM复现了基准生命周期模型关于借贷向消费传导的短期机制；在事前规划任务中，EWM使LLM智能体在疫情冲击下实现比直接LLM决策高10.29%的模拟GDP。

Conclusion: 该EWM仅为起点。作者邀请经济学家贡献更丰富的经济模型与领域知识，AI研究者发展更强的数据驱动方法来模拟智能体行为与学习经济转移；目标是不仅复现真实经济动态，还能发现并检验支持更优决策、造福社会的新机制。

Abstract: Can artificial intelligence reproduce the economic dynamics that emerge as households, firms and governments interact and adapt through markets and institutions? To answer this question, we define an economic world model (EWM) as an agent's internal representation of how an economic system works, enabling counterfactual simulation and decision-making under resource constraints. We then propose a general framework for studying EWMs that links three tasks: simulating agent behaviour, learning economic transitions and systematically evaluating the dynamics. As an initial implementation, we learn household policies from microeconomic data and calibrate a structural economic transition model using macroeconomic data, linking individual decisions to aggregate dynamics. Our micro-to-macro evaluation framework compares simulated individual actions and group distributions with observed microdata and assesses macroeconomic dynamics through forecasting and counterfactual experiments. Results show that simulated and observed household consumption distributions overlap by 94.9%, while EWM forecasts closely track real GDP and several other aggregate levels. In government-borrowing counterfactuals, the EWM reproduces the benchmark life-cycle model's short-run transmission from borrowing to consumption. For ex ante planning, the EWM enables an LLM agent to achieve 10.29% higher simulated GDP under the epidemic shock than direct LLM decisions. This initial EWM is only a starting point. We invite economists and AI researchers to build a stronger EWM together. Economists can contribute richer economic models and knowledge, while AI researchers can develop stronger data-driven methods for simulating agent behaviour and learning economic transitions. Together, we aim not only to reproduce real economic dynamics, but also to discover and test new mechanisms that support better decisions and benefit society.

</details>


### [83] [CAPTA: Identifying LLMs Behind AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32686)
*Kailun Yan,Yue Huang,Sisi Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAPTA方法，通过类CAPTCHA的多选题验证挑战，利用不同LLM的选择偏好差异识别智能体后端模型，25个LLM上10题达99.0%识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程服务方希望通过识别智能体后端LLM来实施模型特定攻击（如提示注入、定向投递操纵内容），但服务无法直接查询后端模型，仅能通过交互中可观测的响应推断，识别难度大。

Method: 受CAPTCHA启发设计CAPTA：向智能体呈现多选题作为验证挑战，依据模型间选项生成多样性筛选问题，并用LLM引导的搜索优化选项集，利用跨模型选择偏好差异完成识别。

Result: 在10个模型家族共25个LLM上，仅用10题即达到99.0%平均识别准确率；在4个常用智能体与7个代表性LLM的28种组合上，提交30个答案达到99.7%平均识别准确率。

Conclusion: CAPTA证明通过精心构造的多选题偏好测试可有效黑盒识别智能体后端LLM，兼具高准确率与低查询开销，凸显LLM智能体面临的模型指纹识别隐私风险。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents access remote services through tools to complete user tasks, often via API calls. In this setting, a remote service may want to identify the backend LLM of an agent to launch model-specific attacks, including prompt injection or selective delivery of manipulated content. However, the service cannot directly query the backend model and must instead collect observable responses through its interactions with the agent, making identification challenging.
  In this paper, we present Completely Automated Preference Test to tell LLMs Apart (CAPTA), a model identification approach inspired by CAPTCHA (Completely Automated Public Turing test to tell Computers and Humans Apart), which distinguishes humans from automated programs. CAPTA presents multiple-choice questions as verification challenges, using differences in models' selection preferences to identify the backend LLM. It selects questions based on cross-model option-generation diversity and uses LLM-guided search to optimize their option sets. Our evaluation shows that CAPTA achieves 99.0% mean identification accuracy with only 10 questions across 25 LLMs from 10 model families. We further evaluate CAPTA with four widely used agents and seven representative LLMs, achieving 99.7% mean identification accuracy across all 28 agent-LLM combinations with 30 submitted answers.

</details>


### [84] [EviCraft: Can Minecraft Agents Find What They Cannot See?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32765)
*Wenyou Yi,Lantian Zhang,Guanlin Chen,Xingqiu Xu,Haiyang Sun,Minrui Huang,Tianle Liang,Hao Chen,Haorui Xu,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"证据闭合"（evidence closure）要求，并构建 EviCraft 运行时与基准，在不改动智能体、工具和世界的前提下，将"世界状态准入规则"变为实验变量，量化 Minecraft 工具栈中信息泄漏对评测分数的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Minecraft 具身智能体基准中，mineBlock 等语义工具直接从模拟器已加载的世界状态中选取目标，缺少可见性检验，导致智能体可一步获取从未见过的埋藏矿物；分数无法反映智能体是否真正进行了搜索，评测结论因此失真。

Method: 将缺失需求形式化为证据闭合：智能体系统（含工具内部）的每个决策只能依赖其持有证据的世界事实。EviCraft 在智能体之下强制闭合，并设置两种对照机制：(1) 感知接地机制 G，仅准入视线内或由智能体自身动作返回的事实；(2) 已加载状态机制 L，复现标准工具栈。二者固定智能体、工具与世界，并做消融：仅移除视线检验、仅在工具内部加载状态。

Result: Survival 任务中机制效应超过最强智能体间的差距：G 下三个中等规模智能体 32%–40% 的回合取得钻石，L 下为 76%–94%，且最强智能体在 G 下低于任一上述智能体在 L 下的成绩。两种模型上，仅移除视线检验即可消除 93%–96% 的分数差距；仅在工具内部加载状态可恢复 70%–98%，说明对观测与查询加约束仍留有大部分泄漏。开放式获取中，L 的增益集中于依赖埋藏资源或生物的物品。

Conclusion: 标准 Minecraft 工具栈存在显著信息泄漏，现有基准分数高估了智能体的搜索与探索能力；证据闭合应作为具身智能体评测的基本约束，EviCraft 提供了可复用运行时与基准。

Abstract: In Minecraft, a common testbed for embodied agents, semantic tools such as mineBlock pick targets from the simulator's loaded world state without a visibility test, so a buried ore the agent has never seen is one call away, and scores cannot show whether the agent searched. We formalize the missing requirement as evidence closure: every decision of an agent system, including those inside its tools, may depend on the world only through facts for which the agent holds evidence. EviCraft is a runtime and benchmark that enforces closure below the agent and makes the admission rule an experimental variable: a perception-grounded regime (G) admits only what is in line of sight or returned by the agent's own actions, and a loaded-state regime (L) reproduces standard tool stacks, with agents, tools, and worlds held fixed. On Survival, the regime effect exceeds the strongest agent's lead: under G, three mid-sized agents reach the diamond in 32%-40% of runs, against 76%-94% under L, and the strongest agent under G scores below each of them under L. In two models, removing only the line-of-sight test closes 93%-96% of the score gap, and loading state only inside tools recovers 70%-98%, so safeguards on observations and queries leave most of the leak open. In open-ended acquisition, the gain from L concentrates in items that depend on buried resources or mobs. Code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/AllenReder/EviCraft

</details>


### [85] [RubikSQL-HB: Agentic Text-to-SQL with Knowledge in the Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32772)
*Haorui Jia,Zihan Qu,Yundi Dong,Ziyang Mei,Chi Zhang,Zezhi Zheng,Dousheng Zhao,Jiahao Zhang,Zui Chen,Han Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RubikSQL-HB：基于 HeavenBase 平台，将数据库的业务语义、存储值与参考查询组织为可查询的知识工作区，使 Text-to-SQL 智能体在推理过程中持续获取知识。在 BIRD Mini-Dev 500 题上，完整流程达 75.2% 执行准确率，优于首候选选择（71.2%）与执行多数投票（70.4%），正确候选覆盖率为 79.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库智能体主要依赖 schema 结构，而业务含义、实际存储值与参考查询等知识在不同推理阶段才变得相关，缺乏统一的按需访问机制；同时多候选答案之间的争议也缺少证据驱动的裁决手段。

Method: 1) 构建数据库特定的可查询知识工作区，将业务语义、存储值、参考查询以知识接口形式暴露；2) 候选智能体在 SQL 构建与执行过程中交替调用知识接口进行探查；3) 下游 Judge 复用同一接口调查候选分歧，实现带工具与修订的证据感知裁决；4) 两阶段示例接口：先发现参考问题，再展开其 SQL；5) 版本化开发研究将反复出现的纠正沉淀为可复用的执行检查。

Result: BIRD Mini-Dev（500 题）上完整流程执行准确率 75.2%，首候选选择 71.2%，执行多数投票 70.4%，正确候选可得率 79.0%；无工作区且重新生成候选的原始知识条件低 3.0 个百分点；相比选择基线，增益主要来自带工具与修订的裁决环节。执行探针记录显示持续调查可化解候选分歧，归档运行显示语义修订能恢复原候选池中缺失的答案。

Conclusion: 可查询知识接口支撑有用替代方案的构造，证据感知裁决将这些替代方案导向最终答案；将知识保持在推理回路中，可使智能体随推理推进持续获取相关观察，从而提升决策质量。

Abstract: Database agents need more than schema structure: business meanings, stored values and reference queries supply knowledge that becomes relevant at different points in reasoning. We introduce RubikSQL-HB, which exposes these materials through queryable, database-specific knowledge workspaces built on the HeavenBase platform. Its knowledge interface remains available as an agent constructs and investigates an answer. Candidate agents interleave knowledge access with SQL construction and execution; a downstream Judge can reuse the same interfaces to investigate disagreements. A two-stage example interface separates discovering a reference question from expanding its SQL. On a 500-question BIRD Mini-Dev evaluation, the full pipeline reaches 75.2% execution accuracy, against 71.2% for first-candidate selection and 70.4% for execution-majority voting, with a correct candidate available on 79.0% of questions. The raw-knowledge condition, which also regenerates candidates without the built workspace, is 3.0 points lower, and adjudication with tools and revision accounts for most of the gain over selection baselines. A recorded execution probe shows how continued investigation resolves a candidate disagreement. Separate archived runs show semantic revision recovering answers absent from their original candidate pools. A versioned development study further illustrates how recurring corrections become reusable execution checks. The system and results connect knowledge-interface design to agent decision quality: queryable knowledge supports the construction of useful alternatives, and evidence-aware adjudication connects those alternatives to a final answer. Keeping knowledge in the loop lets agents acquire relevant observations as their reasoning develops.

</details>


### [86] [Learning World Models through Contrastive Identification of Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32776)
*Hayeong Lee,Youngjun Suh. Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对比式动作辨识（CIA）辅助目标，用"历史条件行为提议"采样替代动作并做对比判别，以奖励平方误差回归无法捕捉的动作相关信息，在五个视觉控制基准上提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型可从离线视觉数据做目标导向规划，但纯预测目标未指明表征应保留哪些与动作相关的区分信息；逆动力学虽提供辅助监督，然而平方误差回归仅通过条件动作均值的变化来奖励额外信息，因此对"不影响最优逆回归误差"的动作信息不敏感。

Method: CIA 将动作识别建模为对比分类：从历史条件的行为提议（behavior proposal）中采样若干在当前历史下合理的候选动作，令模型在候选中辨识实际执行的动作。由于候选动作共享同一上下文，对比目标迫使下一时刻潜表征携带超出历史所提供的信息；并给出在行为精确匹配条件下的理论分析及一个受控学习示例。

Result: 理论上证明在精确行为匹配下，CIA 能奖励那些不改变最优逆回归误差的动作信息；受控实验验证了该区分。在五个视觉控制基准上，CIA 在四种规划器下的平均成功率均高于基础世界模型与回归式逆动力学基线，且未改动规划器。

Conclusion: 对比式动作辨识比平方误差动作回归是更有效的动作表征学习信号，可作为即插即用的辅助目标提升潜世界模型的规划性能，而无需修改规划算法。

Abstract: Latent world models enable goal-directed planning from offline visual data, but prediction alone does not specify which action-related distinctions their representations should preserve. Inverse dynamics provides auxiliary supervision, yet squared-error action regression rewards additional information only through changes in the conditional action mean. We propose Contrastive Identification of Actions (CIA), which identifies the executed action among alternatives sampled from a history-conditioned behavior proposal. Comparing actions plausible in the same context targets information supplied by the next latent beyond history. Under exact behavior matching, we show that CIA can reward action information that leaves optimal inverse-regression error unchanged, and verify this distinction through a controlled learning example. CIA achieves higher planner-averaged mean success than the base world model and a regression inverse-dynamics baseline across five visual control benchmarks, without modifying the planner.

</details>


### [87] [Accuracy of Noise Ceiling Estimators for Brain Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32792)
*Aude Maier,Lucas Gruaz,Martin Schrimpf,Johanni Brea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出脑记录的统一数学框架，推导四种常用noise ceiling估计器的闭式表达，揭示现有方法在不同设定下均低估真实noise ceiling，并给出最佳实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: encoding模型与大脑活动的对齐度（brain score）持续提升，但脑记录含噪声，存在任务模型无法超越的上限即noise ceiling。它决定性能是否还有提升空间，并支撑跨基准的brain score可比性。然而现有多种noise ceiling估计器的统计性质理解不足，无法判断其在何种实验设定下可靠。

Method: 构建脑记录的统一数学框架，据此推导四种常用noise ceiling估计器的闭式表达式，给出各估计器行为的理论预测；用仿真验证理论预测，并与现有fMRI和EEG数据集上的noise ceiling估计进行比较。

Result: 理论预测与仿真结果一致；在fMRI和EEG数据上的比较显示，现有方法在不同设定下均低估真实noise ceiling。

Conclusion: 现有noise ceiling估计器普遍存在低估偏差，需谨慎解读brain score；论文最后针对不同实验情境提出最佳实践建议。

Abstract: The internal activations of modern machine learning models align surprisingly well with the activities recorded in real brains. In the last five years, the alignment between encoding models and brain activities, often called brain scores, has steadily increased. Because brain recordings are noisy, there exists an upper bound to the brain scores that any model can achieve. This bound, called the noise ceiling, is crucial for two reasons: it determines whether further improvements in model performance are possible, and it enables meaningful comparisons of brain scores across benchmarks. The field has developed multiple approaches to estimate noise ceilings, but the statistical properties of existing estimators remain poorly understood, leaving it unclear in which experimental settings they provide reliable estimates. Here, we introduce a unified mathematical framework for brain recordings, use it to derive closed-form expressions for four commonly used noise ceiling estimators, and provide theoretical predictions for the behavior of each estimator. We validate the theoretical predictions with simulations that we compare to noise ceiling estimates in existing fMRI and EEG datasets. We find that the existing methods underestimate the true noise ceiling in different settings. We conclude with recommendations for best practice in different situations.

</details>


### [88] [Interactive Neural Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32811)
*Ross Murphy,Marcel Hedman,Gabriel Brostow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"交互式神经计算机"(Interactive Neural Computer)，将桌面系统本身训练为生成式世界模型，从像素与鼠标键盘动作预测下一帧屏幕；通过惊讶门控槽位记忆、VLM查询桥与残差渲染器三个模块解决无相机位姿记忆、精确算术与文本可读性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机与界面被视为固定不变，人（以及日益增多的AI智能体）只能被动适应操作系统的呈现方式，这在使用者需要自定义工作流、免手操作或存在残障需求时构成限制。作者探索一条被忽视的替代路径：不训练智能体去操作现有计算机，而是直接训练生成模型"成为"计算机。

Method: 将桌面视作交互式世界模型，相比自然视频世界模型面临三大额外难题：缺少相机位姿作为记忆索引、终端算术需精确无误、小字号界面文本需在交互帧率下保持清晰。模型结构仿Neural Turing Machine，端到端从像素与动作训练，含三模块：(1) 惊讶门控槽位记忆，由潜在下一帧预测(MEMORY-JEPA)监督，存储离屏内容；(2) 探索性查询桥，引入预训练VLM的推理能力；(3) 残差渲染器，仅预测帧间潜在变化。

Result: 长闭环推演中，模型在64.2%的试验中成功回忆被遮挡文件（随机水平33.3%），优于循环与滑动窗口基线的最高56.0%；终端文本OCR准确率从1.3%提升至50.1%。

Conclusion: 由于记忆是显式的，可在测试时移植，从而因果性地改变模型所渲染的内容，为可控、可定制的生成式计算机界面提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Computers and their interfaces are usually treated as fixed, so a person, or increasingly an AI agent, must adapt to whatever the operating system presents. This is limiting when someone needs a different workflow, or an interface suited to hands-free use or a disability. We explore an overlooked alternative. Rather than training an agent to operate an existing computer, we train a generative model to be the computer, predicting each next screen from past screens and the user's mouse and keyboard actions. Treating a desktop as an interactive world model raises problems that natural-video world models largely avoid. Without a camera pose to index memory, the model must remember content that has left the screen without any geometric cues. Arithmetic in a terminal has to be exactly right, not merely plausible. Small interface text must also stay legible at interactive frame rates. Our Interactive Neural Computer is structured like a Neural Turing Machine and starts to address these problems with three modules trained end to end from pixels and actions. A surprise-gated slot memory, supervised by latent next-frame prediction (MEMORY-JEPA), stores what is off screen. An exploratory query bridge brings in the reasoning of a pretrained VLM, and a residual renderer predicts only the latent changes between frames. In long closed-loop rollouts, our model recalls occluded files in 64.2% of trials (chance is 33.3%), compared with at most 56.0% for recurrent and sliding-window baselines. It also raises terminal-text OCR accuracy from 1.3% to 50.1%. Finally, because the memory is explicit, it can be transplanted at test time to causally change what the model renders.

</details>


### [89] [Measurement Artifacts in Trustworthiness Evaluations of Quantized Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32848)
*Tanzeel Abbas,Tehseen Zia,David Windridge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 量化评测中观察到的分数变化≠量化真实效应；测量工具本身与量化交互产生的误差被误记为量化效应，作者估计并量化了四类测量选择带来的此类偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化语言模型评测直接把基准分数变化归因于量化，忽略了"仪器误差"（评测工具本身）的贡献。与精度无关的误差只会缩小配对比较，但与量化发生交互的误差既能制造也能反转效应，却被当作量化的效应报告，因此需要估计这类交互误差的大小。

Method: 在8个小于20亿参数的指令微调模型上，对完全相同的数据项，在float16、LLM.int8()和NF4三种精度下，估计四种测量选择对应的交互误差；并与两名人工标注者的标签对照，检验批处理/填充敏感性、Cloze与字母多选题打分的一致性（相关性ρ=0.10，噪声上限约0.77），以及校准损失的来源。

Result: 相对人工标签，4-bit下有害遵从的上升约为修正后关键词检测器所记录"非拒答"上升的1/2至2/3；Cloze与字母多选题对量化效应的估计分歧远超其噪声允许范围（ρ=0.10 vs 上限0.77）；LLM.int8()的答案与生成依赖批内共项，仅padding变化即改变答案，且将激活量化为8位即可复现该依赖性，NF4则具批不变性；报告的最大两项校准损失实为回答偏好偏移。

Conclusion: 量化评测必须区分真实效应与工具交互误差；关键词检测、Cloze/多选题格式与批处理设置均会系统性扭曲结论。作者公开了项目级预测、选项分数与人工标注，并给出压缩小模型评测建议。

Abstract: Evaluations of quantized language models attribute the change in a benchmark score to quantization. We write the measured change as the true change plus a term contributed by the instrument. Instrument error that is the same at every precision can shrink a paired comparison but cannot create or reverse an effect. Error that interacts with quantization can do both, and it is reported as an effect of quantization. We estimate this interaction for four measurement choices in 8 instruction-tuned models below two billion parameters, at float16, LLM.int8() and NF4, on identical items. Measured against labels from two human annotators, the 4-bit rise in harmful compliance is about half to two-thirds of the rise in non-refusal that a corrected key word detector records. Cloze and lettered multiple-choice scoring disagree about the effect of quantization far more than their noise allows: their estimates correlate at \r{ho} = 0.10, against a ceiling of about 0.77. LLM.int8() answers and generations depend on which items share a batch, and padding alone changes its answers. Rounding activations to 8 bits is enough to produce this dependence. NF4 is batch-invariant. The two largest reported calibration losses are response-bias shifts. We release item-level predictions, option scores and human annotations, and give recommendations for evaluating compressed small models.

</details>


### [90] [Lazy Agents Answer Against Their Own Beliefs: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Beliefs Teaches Them to Act on What They Believe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32884)
*Richmond Sin Jing Xuan,Peilin Feng,Wenbin Wang,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RLVB，用每一步可精确计算的后验奖励教智能体"何时提交答案"，避免懒惰早答，在三个基准九个设置上平均提升17.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长任务中AI智能体常在证据不足时提前提交答案（laziness）；仅用结果奖励无法区分"幸运猜中"与"证据充分"，无法教会智能体何时应提交。

Method: 利用训练模拟器每步可计算答案的精确后验：当后验仍含多个候选时奖励"继续探索"，当证据充分时奖励"提交"；奖励无需judge、critic或奖励模型，且不读取智能体书写的信念行；同时构建Pro Worlds视觉谜题基准（小窗口观察大世界）。

Result: 在3个视觉语言模型×3个基准（两个训练未见）共9个设置上全部优于基座，平均+17.1%；纯结果奖励下GRPO/PPO平均低于基座；相比使用LLM judge过程奖励的PPO平均高9.3%且省1.6倍GPU小时；证据充分下作答比例为51%/12%/74%，基座为8%/0.4%/36%；证据不足且信念行仍列多答案时的提交率仅0.1–2.2%，基座为3–68%。

Conclusion: 准确度提升来自"在高证据水平下更频繁作答"；信念行显示智能体依据自身信念行动，RLVB有效抑制了证据不足时的盲目提交。

Abstract: AI agents working on long tasks often commit to an answer before they have enough evidence, a behavior called laziness. In our evaluations, they frequently commit even while their stated belief still names several possible answers. An outcome reward at the end of the episode scores only the final answer, so it cannot teach the agent when to commit, because a lucky guess receives the same reward as an answer backed by evidence. During training, however, the simulator can compute the exact posterior over the answer at every step and tell whether the evidence is already enough. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Beliefs (RLVB), which rewards the agent for taking further steps while this posterior still allows several answers and for committing once the evidence is sufficient. The reward needs no judge, critic or reward model, and it never reads the belief line the agent writes before each action. We also introduce Pro Worlds, a benchmark of visual puzzles where the agent views a large world through a small window. We evaluate three vision-language models on three benchmarks, two of which are never seen in training. RLVB improves the base model in all nine settings, by 17.1% on average. Under the outcome reward, GRPO and PPO fall below the base model on average. RLVB also outperforms PPO with a process reward from an LLM judge by 9.3% on average while using 1.6 times fewer GPU-hours. The trained agent answers with sufficient evidence in 51%, 12% and 74% of the episodes on the three benchmarks, against 8%, 0.4% and 36% for the base model. Its accuracy gain therefore comes from answering more often at high levels of evidence. The belief line shows how the agent reaches this evidence: it acts on its own belief. It commits at only 0.1 to 2.2% of the turns at which the evidence is insufficient and its line still names several answers, against 3 to 68% for the base model.

</details>


### [91] [InstructReplay: Replaying Assistant Turns to Preserve General Behavior During Factual Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32918)
*Xiaofeng Shi,Qian Kou,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 标准微调在学习新事实时会显著削弱指令微调模型的通用能力（平均下降35分），且能力崩塌与回答长度缩短相关；作者提出 InstructReplay，通过约束模型在初始模型对通用指令的自生成答案上保持接近，将五个通用基准的平均下降从28.1分降至2.4分，同时保持新事实学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调指令微调模型注入新事实时会产生灾难性遗忘，通用能力（指令遵循、推理、编程）明显退化；作者进一步发现这种退化与回答长度崩塌高度相关，因此需要一个既能学新知识又能保持通用能力与输出分布的方法。

Method: 先诊断：统计微调后通用基准下降幅度及回答长度变化，建立长度漂移与失败率的关系。再提出 InstructReplay：初始模型对一组通用指令各回答一次，形成自生成答案集；微调新事实时，加入约束使当前模型在这些答案上的输出分布保持接近初始模型（即回放自身答案的正则/蒸馏式约束）。

Result: 在三个模型家族、两个领域共六个设定中，五个通用基准的平均下降由28.1分降至2.4分，回答长度接近初始模型，新事实学习效果与标准微调相当；在全部六个设定中平衡学习与保持的效果优于七种适配方法。控制实验表明，增益来源是回放模型自身对通用指令的答案，而非损失函数的具体形式。

Conclusion: 回答长度崩塌是通用能力退化的关键信号；基于模型自生成通用指令答案的回放式保持约束（InstructReplay）能有效缓解灾难性遗忘，在不牺牲新事实学习的前提下近乎消除通用能力损失，且优于现有适配方法。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an instruction-tuned language model on new facts often erodes its general abilities. We find that standard fine-tuning lowers instruction-following, reasoning and coding benchmarks by 35 points on average, and that these losses track a collapse in answer length: fine-tuned models at least halve their responses, and the further a response drifts from the initial model's length, the more often it fails. We propose InstructReplay: the initial model answers a set of general instructions once, and during fine-tuning the model is kept close to the initial model on these answers. Across three model families and two domains, InstructReplay reduces the average decline on five general benchmarks from 28.1 to 2.4 points and keeps answer length close to the initial model's, while learning the new facts as well as standard fine-tuning. It balances learning and retention better than seven adaptation methods in all six settings. Controlled comparisons attribute the gain to replaying the model's own answers to general instructions rather than to the form of the loss.

</details>


### [92] [Reading Decisions Before Spans: Efficient Generative Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32931)
*Yanbin Gong,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在冻结的2B span验证器中，错误列表是否为空的判断先于所有定位信息，因此只需生成该分支token即可恢复决策；Prefill1将7个固定语法token作为输入、只解码1个分支token，相对完整生成降低84.84%常驻服务开销，F1为83.43（对照83.41）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者核查助手报告时，往往只需一个"该断言缺乏支持"的告警，而生成式验证器却会继续解码以引用并分类错误。作者希望研究：恢复验证器的最终决策究竟需要其输出中的多少内容，从而以最小生成代价提供可用的验证信号。

Method: 在冻结的2B span验证器上观察到"空错误列表 vs 首个错误对象"的抉择先于全部定位步骤。Native8在分支处停止生成；由于前置7个语法token是固定的，Prefill1将其作为输入直接预填，只生成不受约束的分支token，并复用同一native指令。在2,015条代码智能体TEST响应上，与完整生成、Native8对比，并辅以受控证据干预与首位置解码分析。

Result: Prefill1相对完整生成把eager常驻服务开销降低84.84%，发布参考F1为83.43，对照83.41；逻辑token总数与Native8相同时仍额外节省31.98%，源于预填固定token避免了相应解码。默认编译执行下，完整工作负载上额外节省6.74%–26.73%，决策变化很小。

Conclusion: 验证器的核心决策可由极短的读出恢复，固定语法前缀可预填而非生成，从而在几乎不损失F1的前提下显著降低服务成本；受控证据干预与首位置解码进一步界定了这类短读出保留的信息，以及哪些内容仍需继续生成长输出才能获得。

Abstract: A developer checking an assistant's report may need only an alarm that a claim is unsupported. A generative verifier, however, can continue decoding to quote and classify the errors. We study how much of that output is needed to recover its decision. In a frozen 2B span verifier, the choice between an empty error list and its first object precedes all localization. Native8 stops generation at this branch. The preceding seven syntax tokens are fixed, so Prefill1 supplies them as input and generates only the unrestricted branch token, using the same native instruction. On 2,015 code-agent TEST responses, Prefill1 reduces eager resident service by 84.84% relative to full generation, with released-reference F1 of 83.43 versus 83.41. It saves 31.98% over Native8 even though their logical token totals are identical. Under default compiled execution, the extra saving is 6.74-26.73% on the complete workload, with small decision changes. Controlled evidence interventions and first-location decoding establish what these shorter readouts retain and what requires further generation.

</details>


### [93] [ShellBench: What Matters More for AI Agents, Models, or Harness for Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32946)
*Vincent Koc,Aditya Washikar,Rishab Naveen Venkataraman,Onur Solmaz,Zhentong Fan,Xinyang Han,Toby Vincent John,Hao Li,Achyut Mukund,Biresh Chandra Biswas,Eshan Shukla,Louis Pan,Ivan Kudryavtsev,Patthigulla Sai Surendra,Krish Oberoi,Ayaan Zaidi,Hema Veeradhi,Taylor Agarwal,Erik Erlandson,Agustin Rivera,Jacob Tomlinson,Louka Ewington-Pitsos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ShellBench：基于逾千万用户请求构建的100个日常智能体任务基准，含真实杂乱工作区与竞争性数据源，按正确性与安全性双重评分；对12个模型×5种harness×3个推理档共53,100次rollout评测，发现模型选择影响大于harness，但头部三模型仅差1.1个百分点，harness对性能与安全影响很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年公众对AI智能体的使用量激增，但用户难以判断哪种配置（模型、harness、推理档）最适合自身需求，缺乏贴近真实日常使用、同时衡量正确性与安全性的评测基准。

Method: 构建ShellBench：100个日常任务，源自超过1000万条用户请求，每个任务置于含杂乱文件和相互竞争数据源的真实工作区中，并按正确性与安全性双重评分。评测矩阵为12个模型×5种agent harness×3个推理等级，共53,100次rollout，其中20%为公开释放任务。

Result: 模型选择对性能的影响大于harness选择，但三个领先模型之间仅相差1.1个百分点：GPT-6 Astra以62.5%居首；GPT-6.1 Sol在约六分之一单次尝试token成本下达到61.4%。harness对性能与安全性影响均较小，各harness的pass@1介于42.9（Claude Code）至45.2（OpenClaw）之间。

Conclusion: 日常场景下配置选择的关键在于模型而非harness；头部模型性能高度接近，成本效率成为实际选型的重要区分因素，而harness差异在性能与安全性上均不显著。

Abstract: The use of AI agents among the general public has been increasing exponentially throughout 2026, though it is often hard for users to determine which configuration is most effective for their personal use. To help provide clarity here we introduce ShellBench, 100 day-to-day tasks based on more than 10 million user requests, each set in a realistic workspace with messy files and competing data sources, and graded for both correctness and safety. We evaluate 12 models across 5 agent harnesses at 3 reasoning levels for a total of 53,100 rollouts, 20% of which are on tasks designated for public release. We find that model choice impacts performance more than harness choice, but the three leading models differ by only 1.1 percentage points. GPT-6.1 Sol passed 61.4% of attempts at about one-sixth of GPT-6 Astra's estimated token cost per attempt, while GPT-6 Astra led at 62.5%. We also found that harness choice had little effect on either performance or safety with all harnesses scoring between 42.9 (Claude Code) and 45.2 (OpenClaw) for pass@1.

</details>


### [94] [Beyond Expected Value: PPO with Distributional Critics for Systematic Conservation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32948)
*Ana Maria Quintero Ossa,Daniele Silvestro,James Rudd-Jones,Maria Perez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用PPO（Plackett-Luce解非可微动作空间）替换CAPTAIN框架中的进化策略优化器，并用隐式分位数网络（IQN）替换标量critic以建模回报分布，在降低训练成本的同时匹配或超越原方法，并支持风险敏感目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统性保护规划需在生态与气候不确定性下按序分配有限预算；静态工具无法适应环境变化，仅优化期望结果的策略在气候变化下更可能出现的worst-case轨迹上会失效。CAPTAIN虽首次将其建模为序贯强化学习，但其无梯度ES优化器缺乏逐时间步的信用分配，且标量critic无法表达风险。

Method: 以PPO替代ES，通过Plackett-Luce模型处理CAPTAIN中不可微的动作空间；以IQN替代原标量critic，建模完整回报分布；并进行匹配粒度的对照实验以分离优化器与决策粒度的贡献。

Result: 匹配粒度对比显示，生态收益主要来自决策粒度而非优化器；在New Zealand海洋数据集上，PPO+IQN以远低于ES的训练成本达到或超过其结果，并具备向风险敏感目标扩展的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作的实质贡献在于训练效率与可扩展性，而非生态指标提升；为ES黑箱设计无法支持的后续研究（如风险敏感优化）提供了可复用模板。

Abstract: Systematic conservation planning must allocate limited protection budgets sequentially under ecological and climatic uncertainty. Static tools cannot adapt as conditions change, and policies optimised only for expected outcomes can fail on the worst-case trajectories climate change makes increasingly likely. CAPTAIN was the first framework to cast this as sequential reinforcement learning, but its gradient-free Evolution Strategies (ES) optimiser lacks per-timestep credit assignment. We replace ES with Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO) via a Plackett-Luce solution to CAPTAIN's non-differentiable action space, and replace its scalar critic with an Implicit Quantile Network (IQN) modelling the full return distribution. A matched-granularity comparison shows decision granularity, not the optimiser, drives most of the ecological gain, so the real contribution is training efficiency and extensibility to risk-sensitive objectives. On a New Zealand marine dataset, it matches or exceeds ES's outcomes at a fraction of the training cost, offering a template for future work ES's black-box design could not support

</details>


### [95] [LoomKV: Lossless KV Cache Compression from GPU Memory to SSD for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32954)
*Wentao Zhu,Ziao Sheng,Peng Deng,Huiqi Hu,Xuan Zhou,Xing Wei,Zhuang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoomKV 在未修改的 LLM 推理引擎上对两级外部存储与 GPU KV 池同时实现无损 KV 压缩：外部层用 TileStream 存精确长度记录并直接解压到散乱的 GPU 页，GPU 池用 FragPack 打包变长记录并由融合解码注意力核原地读取。在 AgentX 上，压缩层等效于 1.4 倍容量的原始层，并在超出 LMCache 的 LRU 淘汰点后仍保持高吞吐；在受双重约束的 AI IDE 负载上吞吐分别提升 1.24 倍（tier）和 1.59 倍（pool）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引擎将KV缓存卸载至DRAM/SSD以复用前缀，但容量在两个层面受限：外部层级决定淘汰前的复用能力，GPU KV池限制并发长请求；且当前的压缩多为有损，会改变KV值进而影响输出。作者希望在无需修改引擎的前提下，对两个层级都引入无损压缩来突破容量瓶颈，同时不牺牲精度与语义等价性。

Method: 1) 基于BF16高字节（符号+指数高7位）标定的逐头两级3位码本，实现无损压缩；2) tier mode：TileStream 写入精确长度记录，长度读取脱离加载关键路径，保持原有存储、淘汰、预取不变，并将取回记录直接解压至散乱的GPU页；3) pool mode：FragPack 将变长记录打包为更小的共享预算GPU页，由融合解码注意力核原地读取，速度超过BF16下的FlashAttention-3；基于 LMCache 与 vLLM 实现。

Result: 九模型上BF16 KV压缩至原始大小的0.71，FP8 KV为0.85–0.87。AgentX上压缩层等效于1.4倍原始层：跨LRU淘汰点吞吐为原始的2.7–3.6倍，平均TTFT低1.9–3.1倍，在其他替换策略下淘汰点位置一致偏移，SSD→DRAM晋升使重复提示TTFT最高降低1.7倍。tier持有工作集时，pool mode容纳1.31–1.34倍GPU token，8并发时吞吐不变而可接纳12个长请求。双重受限的AI IDE负载下吞吐提升1.24倍（tier）与1.59倍（pool）。原始容量充足时开销至多2.2%。

Conclusion: 无损KV压缩可同时扩展外部层级与GPU池的有效容量，在未修改引擎上实现，且 tier mode 与原始层比特级一致、pool mode 数值精度与FlashAttention-3相当，验证了"容量感知的无损压缩"作为KV管理手段的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving engines offload the key/value (KV) cache from GPU memory to host DRAM and SSD so multi-turn and agent requests can reuse prompt prefixes. Capacity then limits service at two levels: external tiers govern prefix reuse before eviction, while the GPU KV pool bounds concurrent long requests, and lossy compression alters KV values and outputs. LoomKV brings lossless KV compression to both external tiers (tier mode) and the GPU pool (pool mode) on an unmodified engine. Using a calibrated per-head two-level 3-bit code over BF16 high bytes (sign and upper seven exponent bits), LoomKV stores BF16 KV at 0.71 of its raw size across nine models and FP8 KV at 0.85-0.87. In tier mode, TileStream writes exact-length records with length readback off the load path, leaving tier storage, eviction, and prefetch unchanged, and decompresses fetched records straight into scattered GPU pages. In pool mode, FragPack packs variable-length records into smaller shared-budget GPU pages read in place by a fused decode-attention kernel faster than FlashAttention-3 on BF16. Built with LMCache and vLLM, LoomKV makes compressed tiers act as 1.4x larger raw tiers on AgentX: across LMCache's LRU cliff, tier mode delivers 2.7-3.6x raw's goodput and 1.9-3.1x lower mean time-to-first-token (TTFT), shifts the cliff identically under other simulated replacement policies, and cuts repeated-prompt TTFT up to 1.7x via SSD-to-DRAM promotion. When tiers hold the working set, pool mode fits 1.31-1.34x as many GPU tokens, admitting 12 long requests with roughly raw's queue and goodput at 8. On an AI IDE trace where both tiers and the GPU pool are constrained, tier and pool modes raise goodput by 1.24x and 1.59x. Where raw capacity suffices, compression costs at most 2.2% throughput; tier mode is bit-exact with raw tiers, and pool mode matches FlashAttention-3's numerical accuracy.

</details>


### [96] [Can LLM Judges Catch Unsafe Clinical Actions? A Fault-Injection Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33012)
*Neda Ghafouri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在MedAgentBench中注入受控临床错误，系统评估六个开源LLM作为"评审者"识别临床危险动作的可靠性：证据访问显著提升判别力，思维链无益，规则+LLM组合可检测97%注入错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床AI智能体开始操作电子健康记录（EHR），LLM越来越多被用作评判其行为的"评委"。但关键问题是：当智能体表面上成功完成任务时，这些评委能否可靠识别出临床上危险的错误？现有基准的原始评分器可能漏检，需要系统检验评审者的可靠性边界。

Method: 基于MedAgentBench，在已验证的智能体轨迹中注入受控临床错误，构建416条错误轨迹、8类错误，对比6个开源权重LLM评委。通过将每条错误轨迹与其正确对应轨迹配对比较，测量评委是否真正"识别错误"，而非仅倾向多标记不安全动作（控制标记偏置）。实验操纵评委可见信息：仅最终答案、完整轨迹、真实EHR响应、智能体推理，并检验LLM评委与规则检查器组合。

Result: 临床证据访问显著提升评委可靠性：仅见最终答案时，评委无法区分不改变答案的错误，却误标56%的正确轨迹；给予完整轨迹信息后，区分不安全与正确动作的得分达0.75；提供真实EHR响应使判别力再提升0.39。意外的是，纳入智能体推理不改善整体表现，且对"错误报告的临床数值"的检出率下降0.18——该类仍是最难检测的错误。MedGemma表现劣于通用模型，是唯一漏检潜在危险药物过量的评委。此外，基准原始评分器接受了全部38条故意跳过化验检查的轨迹，而LLM评委+依据完整EHR记录的规则检查器可检出97%的注入错误。

Conclusion: LLM评委的可靠性高度依赖其对临床证据的可见性，而非推理文本；单靠最终答案评判不可靠。专用医学模型未必优于通用模型。纯LLM评审存在系统性盲区，LLM评委与基于完整EHR的规则检查相结合是更稳健的临床智能体安全评估范式。

Abstract: As clinical AI agents begin to interact with electronic health records (EHRs), large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to evaluate their actions. But how reliably can these judges recognize clinically dangerous mistakes, especially when an agent appears to have completed its task successfully? We investigate this question using MedAgentBench by introducing controlled clinical errors into verified agent trajectories. The experiments include 416 faulty trajectories, eight error categories, and six open-weight LLM judges. By comparing each faulty trajectory with its correct counterpart, we measure whether judges genuinely recognize errors rather than simply flagging more actions as unsafe. The findings show that access to clinical evidence significantly improves judge reliability. When shown only the final answer, judges cannot distinguish errors that leave it unchanged, while flagging 56% of correct trajectories. With full trajectory information, the ability to distinguish unsafe actions from correct ones reaches a score of 0.75, and providing actual EHR responses improves discrimination by 0.39. Surprisingly, including the agent's reasoning does not improve overall performance and reduces detection of incorrectly reported clinical values by 0.18, which remain the hardest errors to detect. MedGemma performed worse than the general-purpose model and was the only judge to miss potentially dangerous medication overdoses. We also find that the benchmark's original grader accepts all 38 trajectories with an intentionally skipped laboratory check, while combining LLM judges with a rule-based checker informed by the full EHR record detects 97% of injected faults.

</details>


### [97] [RecordRoute: Auditing Agent Failures with Localization-Guided Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33022)
*Yifan Duan,Lihuan Li,Chao Xue,Siyuan Zheng,Du Yin,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RecordRoute，将"比较式定位"与"局部诊断"耦合：先在轨迹内比较排序记录以选出决定性记录，再仅用该记录的文本与元数据预测错误模式，在Who&When Pro、TraceElephant与CatchBench POST上均优于StepFinder、ASCon及三个开源LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计失败的智能体执行需同时给出责任主体、最早的 decisive 步骤与错误模式。现有监督方法要么只做步骤定位，要么用共享轨迹表示同时预测多个归因目标，未利用定位结果去选择用于模式诊断的文本，导致定位与诊断脱节。

Method: RecordRoute 用词级与字符级特征表示每条记录，并通过轨迹内记录两两比较学习排序；排序选出的记录提供 actor 与 step，其文本及元数据单独驱动错误模式预测，形成"定位→局部诊断"的串联结构。

Result: 在受控注入的 Who&When Pro 上，四项官方指标均超过 StepFinder 与 ASCon；固定 actor/step 预测后，局部诊断的 pooled joint accuracy 为 77.47%，全轨迹诊断为 69.28%；在 TraceElephant 与 CatchBench POST 的自然失败上，定位性能优于上述基线及三个开源 LLM。

Conclusion: 显式利用定位结果限定诊断文本范围可显著提升错误模式判定精度，比较式排序是有效的步骤定位范式，二者耦合优于共享表示的联合预测方案。

Abstract: Auditing failed agent executions requires identifying the responsible actor, the earliest decisive step, and the error mode. Representative supervised methods either focus on step localization or predict multiple attribution targets from shared trajectory representations. These methods do not use the localization result to select the text for mode diagnosis. To address this limitation, we propose RecordRoute, which couples comparative localization with local diagnosis. Specifically, it represents each record with word and character features and learns to rank records through comparisons within each trajectory. The selected record supplies the actor and step, while its text and metadata drive mode prediction. On controlled-injection Who&When Pro, RecordRoute outperforms StepFinder and ASCon on all four official metrics. With actor and step predictions fixed, local diagnosis reaches 77.47% pooled joint accuracy versus 69.28% for full-trace diagnosis. On natural failures from TraceElephant and CatchBench POST, it outperforms these baselines and three open LLMs in localization.

</details>


### [98] [Hybrid AI for AI-Native RAN Control: A Functional Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33035)
*Sabrine Aroua,Osama Arouk,Pegah Alizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个技术无关的RAN决策分类法（表征与推理、预测与仿真、自适应控制、决策综合四种能力），并据此梳理AI与模型方法、跨时间尺度的RAN控制方案及评估资源，指出闭环实验资源与任务/指标/评测协议标准化之间的缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI原生RAN控制涉及跨网络实体、协议层与时间尺度的相互依赖决策，缺乏统一基础来比较AI方法与常规方法的功能角色，难以理解二者如何协同支撑这些决策。

Method: 综述性研究：构建以四种互补能力（表征与推理、预测与仿真、自适应控制、决策综合）为核心、按控制流程角色而非实现技术定义的技术无关分类法；据此分析基础模型、世界模型、强化学习与优化等代表性AI及模型方法；将该分类法应用于慢、中、快及跨时间尺度的RAN控制方案，识别其能力构成与混合AI系统中的组合方式；并按对决策流程与可复现评估的支撑度review数据集、基准、仿真器、数字孪生与实验平台。

Result: 形成了跨方法的统一能力视角，明确了各类RAN控制方案所实现与组合的能力；梳理了评估资源现状，并揭示闭环实验资源可得性与任务、指标、评测协议标准化之间的明显缺口。

Conclusion: 指出AI原生RAN中混合AI的研究方向：能力集成、多智能体与跨时间尺度协调、约束处理以及可复现闭环评估，并提供公开的综述文献与评估资源清单。

Abstract: AI-native radio access network (RAN) control involves interdependent decisions across network entities, protocol layers, and timescales. Understanding how AI and conventional methods can jointly support these decisions requires a common basis for comparing their functional roles. This survey introduces a technique-agnostic taxonomy of RAN decision-making organized around four complementary capabilities: Representation and Reasoning, Prediction and Simulation, Adaptive Control, and Decision Synthesis. These capabilities are defined by their roles in the control process, independently of the techniques used to implement them. Through this perspective, we examine representative AI and model-based approaches, including foundation models, world models, reinforcement learning, and optimization. We then apply the taxonomy to representative RAN control solutions across slow, medium, fast, and cross-timescale applications to identify which capabilities they implement and how these capabilities are combined in hybrid AI systems. We also review datasets, benchmarks, simulators, digital twins, and experimental platforms according to their support for the decision-making process and reproducible evaluation. The review highlights a gap between the availability of resources for closed-loop experimentation and the standardization of tasks, metrics, and evaluation protocols needed for meaningful comparisons.Finally, we identify research directions for hybrid AI in AI-native RAN, including capability integration, multi-agent and cross-timescale coordination, constraint handling, and reproducible closed-loop evaluation. A curated list of surveyed works and evaluation resources is available at https://github.com/pegahani/Hybrid-AI-RAN-Survey.

</details>


### [99] [Auditing the Judge: Human-Derived Bias Discovery, Quantification, and Mitigation in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33065)
*Hamin Koo,Chanjoo Jung,Fangzhao Wu,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HUB-J框架，利用人类偏好（人-LLM判断不一致/一致案例）自动发现可解释偏见因子、量化偏见强度，并以无需训练的回帰方法缓解LLM评委的系统性偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自动评估器日益普及，但其判断受系统性偏见影响且与人偏好不一致；已有工作虽识别偏见并提出检测/缓解方法，但较少利用人类偏好指导偏见识别与缓解，且过度依赖预先发现的偏见清单、忽视偏见强度、依赖昂贵干预。

Method: 基于人类偏好的偏见因子的一体化框架HUB-J：1）利用人-LLM判断不一致案例自动发现可解释的偏见因子；2）利用一致案例，通过受控输入修改及模型决策偏移量化偏见强度；3）基于量化偏见，提出轻量、免训练的回归式缓解策略，估计各偏见因子对模型判断的影响并去除其估计效应以纠正决策。

Result: 实验表明HUB-J既能发现已知偏见因子也发现新偏见因子，揭示不同模型易感性的有意义的差异，并持续减少偏见导致的决策翻转，且能跨模型泛化。

Conclusion: HUB-J表明以人类偏好为信号可实现偏见因子的自动发现、强度量化与免训练缓解，为更可靠、可解释且可迁移的LLM评委偏见治理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as automated evaluators in evaluation and training pipelines, their judgements are often affected by systematic biases that conflict with human preferences. Although prior work has identified various biases and proposed methods for detecting and mitigating them, less attention has been paid to using human preferences to inform bias identification and mitigation. Moreover, these approaches rely heavily on pre-discovered bias lists, overlook bias strength, and depend on costly interventions. In this work, we propose HUB-J, an integrated framework based on human-derived bias factors that detects, quantifies, and mitigates biases in LLM-as-a-Judge systems. Our approach leverages human-LLM judgement disagreement cases to automatically discover interpretable bias factors, and utilizes agreement cases to quantify bias strength through controlled input modifications and resulting shifts in model decisions. Finally, building on these quantified biases, we introduce a lightweight, training-free regression-based mitigation strategy that estimates the influence of each bias factor on model judgments and corrects bias-influenced decisions by removing its estimated effect. Empirical results show that HUB-J uncovers both known and novel bias factors, reveals meaningful differences in model susceptibility, and consistently reduces bias-driven decision flips while generalizing across models.

</details>


### [100] [Uncertainty-Aware Candidate Generation for Entity Linking in Cyber Threat Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33086)
*Nasser Alkhulaifi,Direnc Pekaslan,Christian Wagner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知的实体解析(ER)概念验证：在NER阶段用MC dropout捕获跨度边界与类型的不确定性信号，并在EL候选生成阶段自适应调整模糊截断阈值，使Recall@10从0.6186提升至0.7966。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ER流程将歧义过早坍缩为硬决策：上游NER只输出单一跨度与类型，下游EL将其视为真值，丢弃了边界分歧与类型混淆等信息，导致系统在输入不确定时脆弱、失效不可预测。

Method: 基于BERT的NER模型增加两个辅助跨度边界预测头，推理时采用MC dropout得到三个提及级代理信号：MC平均起始边界概率、MC平均结束边界概率、BIO预测分布上的De Luca-Termini模糊熵；用这些信号自适应调整EL候选生成中的模糊截断阈值，并以阻塞步骤的检索召回率进行评估。

Result: 在链接至MITRE ATT&CK知识库的AnnoCTR数据集上，边界感知与双信号模糊链接方法将Recall@10由0.6186提升到0.7966。

Conclusion: 贡献不在于端到端优化的ER流水线，而在于确立"不确定性保留"作为ER流程中可行且有益的组成部分，使下游EL能在不确定条件下做出更有依据的决策。

Abstract: Entity Resolution (ER) pipelines often collapse ambiguity into early hard decisions: upstream Named Entity Recognition (NER) selects a single span and type, and downstream Entity Linking (EL) treats that choice as ground truth. This discards valuable information about the ambiguity of the underlying data (e.g., span boundary disagreement and type confusion), leading to brittle systems that fail unpredictably when faced with uncertainty in inputs. Therefore, this work presents a proof-of-concept for uncertainty-aware ER that treats uncertainty as native data that should be preserved and exploited throughout ER pipelines. We (i) capture uncertainty signals in the NER stage and (ii) leverage these signals into the EL stage to improve candidate retrieval under ambiguity. Using a BERT-based NER model augmented with two auxiliary span-boundary prediction heads, we apply Monte Carlo (MC) dropout at inference time to derive three mention-level proxy signals: the MC-averaged start-boundary probability, the MC-averaged end-boundary probability, and De Luca-Termini fuzzy entropy computed over the Begin-Inside-Outside (BIO) predictive distribution. These signals are used to adapt the fuzzy cutoff in candidate generation (CG) for EL. Because CG is treated as a blocking step, we evaluate retrieval recall. On the AnnoCTR dataset linked to the MITRE ATTCK knowledge base, the boundary-aware and dual-signal fuzzy linking approaches improve Recall@10 from 0.6186 to 0.7966. The contribution lies not in an end-to-end optimised ER pipeline but in establishing uncertainty preservation as a viable and beneficial component of ER pipelines, enabling downstream EL to make better-informed decisions under uncertainty.

</details>


### [101] [Temporal Conditioning for Human Behavioral Cloning in Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33106)
*Victor Dziwniel,Tianxiang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时间感知Transformer（在Chessformer式架构上引入学习型时间条件token编码剩余时钟、时限与近期行棋节奏，并附加思考时间与认输行为头），基于大规模在线对局训练，表明时间状态是人类行为建模中被低估的重要信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有国际象棋行为克隆模型仅从棋盘状态预测人类着法，忽略剩余时钟等时间输入；然而时间压力是决定人类决策质量的关键因素，时间状态构成人类行为建模中缺失的维度。

Method: 扩展Chessformer式架构为时间感知Transformer：加入可学习的时间条件token，编码玩家时钟状态、时限设置与近期节奏；并增设思考时间预测与认输预测的行为头；在大规模在线棋类数据上训练，与时间无关基线及当前最优方法对比评估。

Result: 整体准确率有小幅提升，但在时间压力下增益显著更大；思考时间与认输预测更准确；更贴合人类在关键时间区间内着法质量的退化模式，缓解了时间无关行为克隆模型的系统性“过强”偏差。

Conclusion: 时间状态是国际象棋人类行为克隆中此前未被充分利用但十分重要的信号，引入时间条件能同时改善着法、用时与退出行为的建模保真度。

Abstract: Behavioral cloning models in chess typically predict human moves from board states while ignoring temporal inputs such as the remaining clock. However, time pressure is a major determinant of human decision-making quality, and temporal state is a missing dimension of human behavioral modeling. This paper introduces a time-aware transformer that extends a Chessformer-style architecture with learned temporal conditioning tokens encoding player clock state, time control, and recent tempo, together with behavioral heads for thinking time and resignation. The model is trained on large-scale online chess data and evaluated against time-agnostic baselines and the state of the art. Results show that temporal conditioning yields modest overall accuracy improvements but substantially stronger gains under time pressure. Beyond move choice, it also produces more accurate thinking-time and resignation predictions. More importantly, the model better matches human move-quality degradation in critical time regimes, reducing the systematic over-strength observed in time-agnostic behavioral cloning models. These results suggest that temporal state is a previously underutilized but important signal for human behavioral cloning in chess.

</details>


### [102] [MetaFORGE: A Meta-Learning Framework for Harness Optimization in Automated Code Generation for Parallel Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33119)
*Scott Piersall,Yang Gao,Shenyang Liu,Liqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MetaFORGE：一种基于执行反馈的元学习框架，迭代优化持久化的自然语言技能规范以生成MPI C并行程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成并行程序的正确性与性能高度依赖引导指令，但如何自动获得高质量指令尚不明确。

Method: 将正确性测试、编译器诊断、静态分析、运行时测量与rank级负载不均衡画像聚合成标量奖励，驱动文本梯度式精炼；外部记忆跨运行蒸馏与检索奖励支持的优化知识，形成持久技能规范。

Result: 在6个MPI任务×3种工作负载下，静态分析质量、运行时间和负载均衡均优于零样本生成，总体超过TextGrad迭代基线；模型匹配消融（精炼模型降为gpt-4o）优势仍存；18种配置的检索消融显示运行时间一致受益；受控泄漏的留出与零更新实验表明优化知识保留于技能与外部记忆中。

Conclusion: 执行反馈驱动的元学习可有效积累可迁移的并行代码生成技能，且知识可持久化复用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate parallel programs, but their correctness and performance depend strongly on the instructions that guide generation. We present \textsc{MetaFORGE}, an execution-grounded meta-learning framework that iteratively optimizes persistent natural-language skill specifications for MPI C code generation. \textsc{MetaFORGE} combines correctness testing, compiler diagnostics, static-analysis findings, runtime measurements, and rank-level load-imbalance profiles into a scalar reward that drives textual-gradient refinement, while an external memory distills and retrieves reward-supported optimization knowledge across runs.
