  Across six MPI tasks and three workload sizes, \textsc{MetaFORGE} improves static-analysis quality, runtime, and load balance relative to zero-shot generation and generally exceeds the evaluated \textsc{TextGrad} iterative baseline. A model-matched refinement ablation shows that the MetaFORGE advantage persists when its refinement model is reduced to \texttt{gpt-4o}. An expanded retrieval ablation across all 18 task--workload configurations shows consistent runtime benefit and broad static-analysis and load-balance benefit from retrieved context. Leakage-controlled held-out and zero-update experiments further provide evidence that optimization knowledge persists in both the transferred skill and external memory under the evaluated protocol.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Query Obfuscation with Embedding-Optimized Noise (EON): A Representational Blindspot in Agentic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31344)
*Ankit Gupta,Christoph Adami,Emily Dolson*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 EON（Embedding-Optimized Noise）：仅利用查询自身的自然语言表示、在完全无法访问候选库/标签/排序/反馈/护栏的“盲语料”条件下优化出一段无语义噪声，即可在多种稠密嵌入模型上高度保持原查询的检索结果，并发现该脱节可被用来绕过输入护栏实施安全敏感检索，作者将这一现象命名为 BLINDSPOT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 agentic 与语义检索系统中，稠密嵌入模型决定意图/工具/文档/记忆/图像的召回，但查询表示与候选库之间的可控性未被充分刻画；作者希望检验“一个有意义查询能否被表面无语义的内容替换而保留检索行为”，并关注其安全含义。

Method: 构造 EON：优化目标只基于自然查询的表示，不使用候选嵌入、相关性标签、排序、检索反馈或护栏判定；在意图与工具路由、基准文档检索、情节式 agent 记忆检索、跨模态检索等任务上跨多个稠密嵌入族做评测；并借助嵌入空间几何分析，解释查询级表示匹配为何能对优化时未见候选维持检索结果。

Result: EON 在多个嵌入族与多类检索任务上频繁保持原查询意图与检索结果；对恶意查询，EON 能稳定绕过拦截自然查询的输入护栏，完成预期的安全敏感检索而不被检测；几何分析显示查询级表示匹配可对未见候选可靠延续检索结果。

Conclusion: 双编码器系统中存在系统性的“操作盲区”：系统对无语义伪影不敏感，却仍可靠召回真实且具后果的内容，作者称之为 BLINDSPOT；这揭示了语义检索管线的表示层失效模式与安全风险。

Abstract: In modern agentic and semantic retrieval systems, dense embedding models increasingly determine which intent, tool, document, memory, or image to retrieve for a query. We identify a representational failure mode in this pipeline: a meaningful query can be replaced by visibly nonsemantic content while preserving much of its retrieval outcome, even when the candidate bank is hidden from the optimizer. We introduce Embedding-Optimized Noise (EON), constructed using only the natural query representation, without access to candidate embeddings, relevance labels, rankings, retrieval feedback, or guardrail decisions. Despite this corpus-blind objective, EONs frequently preserve intent and tool routing, benchmark document search, episodic agent-memory retrieval, and cross-modal search across multiple dense embedding families. Crucially, this disconnect carries serious security implications: for malicious queries, an EON can reliably evade input guardrails that block the natural query, executing the intended safety-sensitive retrieval undetected. Through the geometry of the embedding space, we then characterize how query-level representation matching can reliably maintain subsequent retrieval outcomes over candidates unseen during optimization. Together, these findings expose a fundamental phenomenon across dual encoders that we call BLINDSPOT, where systems remain operationally blind to nonsemantic artifacts while reliably retrieving real, consequential content.

</details>


### [104] [MELODI: Similarity-Identity Decoupling for Lightweight Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31940)
*Quoc Phong Nguyen,Vuong Le,Volodymyr Vaskovych,Julien Monteil*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出相似度-身份解耦框架MELODI，用于延迟受限的候选排序：将目标-历史兼容性与仅目标信息分离，理论证明pool-then-similarity形式的合理性，并在公开与工业数据集上取得匹配或更优效果且延迟更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 候选排序存在严格时延约束，现有target-aware（候选条件化）方法计算开销大；需将目标与历史的兼容性建模和纯目标信息拆解，以便采用更廉价的计算形式和复用历史。

Method: 提出相似度-身份解耦框架，分离目标-历史兼容性与仅目标信息。研究pooled similarity两种计算顺序（similarity-then-pool与pool-then-similarity），证明对固定目标而言，相似度算子对历史参数的线性性是其等价性的充分条件，从而支持更廉价的pool-then-similarity形式。实例化为MELODI：多头高效轻量算子，相似度分支为目标条件化的多头历史池化，加单层无残差的交叉层（对池化历史输入线性）；身份分支依问题而定。

Result: 公开基准上MELODI达到或超过target-aware与target-agnostic基线；两个大规模工业数据集上AUPRC、NDCG与Recall均不低于所评target-aware基线；处理器实测在候选数较大时延迟低于候选条件化基线，请求内历史复用可进一步降低延迟。

Conclusion: 相似度-身份解耦在保持或提升排序质量的同时显著降低候选排序延迟，pool-then-similarity的线性性论证为轻量化设计提供理论依据，历史复用是实现工程低延迟的关键。

Abstract: For latency-constrained candidate ranking, we propose a similarity-identity decoupling framework that separates target-history compatibility from target-only information. We study two orders for computing pooled similarity, similarity-then-pool and pool-then-similarity, and identify linearity of the similarity operator in its history argument, for each fixed target, as sufficient for their equivalence, justifying the cheaper pool-then-similarity form. We instantiate the framework as multi-head efficient lightweight operator for decoupling identity and similarity (MELODI), whose similarity branch combines target-conditioned multi-head history pooling with a single residual-free cross layer that is linear in its pooled-history input and whose identity branch is problem-dependent. On public benchmark datasets, MELODI matches or surpasses the evaluated target-aware and target-agnostic baselines. On two large-scale industrial datasets, its area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC), normalised discounted cumulative gain (NDCG), and recall values are equal to or higher than those of the evaluated target-aware baselines. Processor measurements show lower latency than the evaluated candidate-conditioned baselines at large candidate counts, further reduced by request-local history reuse.

</details>


### [105] [User-Adaptive Fusion of Multiple Foundation Models for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31951)
*Sunwoo Kim,Hyunjin Hwang,Geon Lee,Kyungho Kim,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UF2O：按用户自适应地融合多个（跨模态）基础模型的推荐方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同基础模型刻画物品元数据的互补侧面，但简单拼接/组合其表示相对单一模型收益甚微；作者观察到"受益用户因基础模型而异"，即各模型的贡献具有用户特异性。

Method: 为每个基础模型维护独立的推荐视图，将各模型的贡献建模为用户特定量，并在分数层面按用户自适应地加权组合；因融合发生在打分层，无需对齐异构模型（跨模态）的表示空间。

Result: 在四个数据集上联合四个基础模型的实验中，UF2O 在静态与序列推荐设定下均优于现有融合方法，相对最强融合基线最高提升约 7%。

Conclusion: 融合有效性来自用户级的自适应性而非模型数量的简单叠加；分数级融合为异构/跨模态基础模型的组合提供了无需表示对齐的实用路径。

Abstract: Can recommender systems benefit from combining a collection of foundation models within and across modalities? While different foundation models may capture complementary aspects of item metadata, we find that naively combining their representations yields only marginal gains over using a single foundation model. We make a key observation: the users who benefit vary across foundation models. Motivated by this observation, we introduce UF2O, a user-adaptive approach for fusing multiple foundation models for recommendation. In a nutshell, UF2O models the contribution of each foundation model as user-specific by maintaining separate recommendation views and adaptively combining their scores for each user. By performing fusion at the score level, UF2O can also fuse heterogeneous foundation models without directly aligning their representation spaces. Extensive experiments that jointly combine four foundation models on four datasets show that UF2O outperforms existing fusion approaches in both static and sequential recommendation settings, with improvements of up to 7% over the strongest fusion baseline. The code, datasets, and supplementary materials for our work are available at https://github.com/kswoo97/UFFO.

</details>


### [106] [LARF: Learning-aligned Reasoning Framework for Search Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32125)
*Tian Tang,Guo Chen,Reed Li,Hao Wang,Yu Lu,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出三阶段框架LARF（冷启动SFT+多智能体CoT、判别式GRPO+间隔损失、双头蒸馏），在工业级网页搜索相关性基准上以8B模型提升六分类准确率5.78个百分点、NDCG 5.05点，纯分类推理路径延迟降低82.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询-文档相关性估计是AI原生搜索的核心，但直接应用LLM存在三个问题：(1) 常规SFT对领域特定判断理由的监督不足；(2) 轨迹级RL目标未显式约束相邻序数相关性标签之间的间隔分离；(3) CoT推理虽利于训练但在延迟敏感的在线排序中不可行，导致模糊决策边界难以厘清。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 在策略性筛选的样本上以多智能体引导的CoT理由做冷启动SFT；(2) 采用判别式GRPO，结合属性级、列表级与规则一致性奖励，并在末端相关性token上施加margin loss；(3) 将教师蒸馏为双头学生模型，保留分类头用于非自回归推理。

Result: 在约30万训练样本、1.6万测试样本的人工标注网页搜索相关性基准上，LARF-8B六分类准确率提升5.78个百分点，NDCG提升5.05点；仅分类的serving路径推理延迟降低82.9%。

Conclusion: 将结构化推理对齐、末端决策优化与分类头蒸馏相结合，可在工业搜索相关性建模中实现有效的精度—效率折衷。

Abstract: Query-document relevance estimation is a core component of AI-native search systems, where a model must assess whether retrieved documents satisfy users' information needs under complex, multi-dimensional criteria. However, directly applying large language models (LLMs) to this task remains challenging because conventional supervised fine-tuning (SFT) provides limited supervision for domain-specific judgment rationales, while standard trajectory-level reinforcement learning (RL) objectives do not explicitly enforce margin separation among adjacent ordinal relevance labels. Moreover, although such reasoning can be useful during training, chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is impractical for latency-sensitive online ranking. These challenges make it difficult to resolve ambiguous decision boundaries. We propose a three-stage framework LARF (Learning-Aligned Reasoning Framework for Search Relevance): it first performs cold-start SFT on strategically selected samples with multi-agent guided CoT rationales, then applies discriminative GRPO with attribute-wise, list-wise, and rule-consistency rewards together with a margin loss on the terminal relevance token, and finally distills the teacher into a dual-head student whose classification heads are retained for non-autoregressive inference. On a human-annotated web search relevance benchmark with approximately 300K training and 16K test instances, LARF-8B improves six-class classification accuracy by 5.78 percentage points and NDCG by 5.05 points, while the classification-only serving path reduces inference latency by 82.9\%. These results indicate that aligning structured reasoning, terminal decision optimization, and classification-head distillation can provide an effective accuracy--efficiency trade-off for industrial search relevance modeling.

</details>


### [107] [OmniEVIE: Evidence-Vector-Informed Embedding for Multi-modal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32203)
*Zifei Wang,Wei Wen,Qiang Ji,Qian-Wen Zhang,Ruizhi Qiao,He Liu,Xing Sun,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OmniEVIE：一个4.5B多向量嵌入模型，用统一压缩索引覆盖文本、图像、视觉文档、视频和工具检索，并以三阶段检索器实现高效精准的多模态召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态检索方法存在三类缺陷：单向量嵌入丢失局部证据；交叉编码器需对每个召回项在线重读全文；未压缩的late interaction索引过大、难以服务化。

Method: 每条记录最多存储128个64维int8向量（9,472字节，百万条约8.82 GiB），包含均值向量、1-bit符号和int8多向量，分别用于粗、中、精三阶段检索，并将命中定位到页面区域或视频区间。训练与索引协同设计：所有任务共享统一检索格式；三级证据校验剔除假阴性；按基线-教师差距进行动态采样；训练中用ST-HAC压缩候选以匹配索引；按参数块合并分阶段与专家模型；用带噪声底的显著性门细化查询打分。文本、图像、视觉文档、视频、工具均无解析模型直接编码。

Result: 在公开数据集训练后，MMEB-V3（190任务）得60.68（完整d=2048序列）与60.06（K128×d64 int8索引），MMEB-V2（78任务）得77.96，ViDoRe V3（48任务）得62.96。100万/500万记录下单线程查询为54.2 ms/120.2 ms，比4096维单向量精确搜索快11.1×/25.3×，比完整int8 MaxSim快236×/532×。QVHighlights（1,083查询）上峰值落在标注区间内的查询占78.0%，返回片段（视频长度18%）与区间相交率94.3%（随机基线44.9%）。

Conclusion: 统一的压缩多向量索引与三阶段检索可在跨模态多任务上保持接近全精度序列的检索质量，同时显著降低存储与延迟，并支持细粒度定位；视频可直接由帧编码而无需解析模型。权重与代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval must match dense pages, fine-grained entities, ordered video, and agent tools and memory. Single-vector embeddings lose local evidence, cross-encoders reread every recalled item online, and uncompressed late interaction is too large to serve. We present OmniEVIE, a 4.5B multi-vector embedding model covering text, images, visual documents, video, and tools with one compressed index and a three-stage retriever. Each record stores at most 128 int8 vectors of dimension 64 (9,472 bytes, or 8.82 GiB per million records), providing a mean vector, 1-bit signs, and int8 multi-vectors for coarse, middle, and final search while localizing each hit to a page region or video interval. Training, merging, indexing, and retrieval are co-designed: all tasks share one retrieval format, a three-grade evidence check removes false negatives, dynamic sampling follows the baseline-teacher gap, candidates are compressed with ST-HAC during training to match the index, stage-wise and specialist models are merged by parameter block, and a saliency gate with a noise floor refines query scoring. Trained on public datasets, OmniEVIE scores 60.68 (full d=2048 sequence) and 60.06 (K128 x d64 int8 index) on MMEB-V3 (190 tasks), 77.96 on MMEB-V2 (78 tasks), and 62.96 on ViDoRe V3 (48 tasks). On 1M and 5M records, a single-thread query takes 54.2 ms and 120.2 ms (11.1x and 25.3x faster than exact 4096-d single-vector search; 236x and 532x faster than full int8 MaxSim). Video is encoded directly from frames without a parsing model: on QVHighlights (1,083 queries), the peak falls inside the labeled segment for 78.0% of queries and the returned span (18% of video length) intersects it for 94.3% vs. 44.9% random. Weights and code: https://huggingface.co/tencent/OmniEVIE-4B and https://github.com/Tencent/OmniEVIE.

</details>


### [108] [LazUnirec: Unifying Retrieval and Ranking in Multi-scenario Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32590)
*Xiaodong Li,Junfeng Zhang,Shichao Nie,Ziyue Guo,Yiming Liu,Xinxin Xu,Xurong Li,Jinyuan Dang,Zhanming Ou,Jianan Yan,Tao Luo,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 LazUnirec，在共享 Transformer 主干上通过 LoRA 调制注意力、任务自适应稀疏 MoE 与不确定性估计缓解检索-排序梯度冲突，结合排序到检索的知识蒸馏，实现统一的检索排序建模，并已在多场景工业推荐系统落地取得线上收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业推荐系统将检索与排序拆为两个级联阶段，导致优化目标不一致、用户序列重复编码、算力浪费。已有用单一 Transformer 同时建模检索与排序的工作仍存在两点不足：(1) 两任务共享单一主干时，梯度方向不一致引发梯度冲突；(2) 冲突发生时模型无法确定可靠的梯度更新方向。

Method: 在 Transformer 主干上构建任务共享与任务私有参数：注意力部分提出 LoRA 调制注意力，在共享注意力参数的同时保留任务私有参数；FFN 部分提出任务自适应稀疏 MoE，通过共享专家与私有专家缓解梯度冲突；并提出不确定性估计以评估检索与排序梯度的可靠性，在冲突时引导更可靠的更新方向；此外引入排序到检索的知识蒸馏实现跨任务知识迁移。

Result: 大量离线实验与线上 A/B 测试验证了 LazUnirec 的有效性与优越性；系统已在线上多场景工业推荐系统部署，并为平台带来显著收益。

Conclusion: LazUnirec 通过参数共享-私有化设计、可靠性引导的梯度更新与跨任务蒸馏，有效缓解统一检索排序建模中的梯度冲突，具备工业可落地性与实际业务价值。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommendation systems typically divide retrieval and ranking into two separate cascaded stages, leading to inconsistent optimization objectives, redundant encoding of user sequences, and a waste of computational resources. Previous efforts have investigated using Transformers to model both retrieval and ranking simultaneously. However, these studies still face two major limitations: (1) Since retrieval and ranking share a single Transformer backbone, the gradient conflict arises when the gradient directions of the two tasks are inconsistent. (2) When gradient conflict occurs, the model cannot determine a reliable direction for gradient updates. To this end, we propose a Unified Retrieval and Ranking approach for Multi-scenario Industrial Recommendation, termed as LazUnirec. Specifically, we construct task-shared and task-private parameters on the Transformer backbone to mitigate the gradient conflict. Wherein, for the attention components, we propose LoRA-modulated attention, enabling retrieval and ranking to share attention parameters while preserving task-private parameters. For the feed-forward networks (FFN), we present task-adaptive sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) to mitigate gradient conflict by constructing task-shared and task-private experts. Moreover, we propose an uncertainty estimation that evaluates the gradient reliability of the retrieval and ranking, thereby guiding the model toward a more reliable gradient update direction when gradient conflict occurs. In addition, knowledge distillation from ranking to retrieval further enables cross-task knowledge transfer. Extensive offline experiments and online A/B testing demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of LazUnirec. LazUnirec has been deployed in online multi-scenario industrial recommendation system and currently delivers significant gains for the platform.

</details>


### [109] [Speculative Decoding for Semantic-ID-Based Generative Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32855)
*Harshal A. Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SIDECAR通过"近似草稿+树注意力单次打分+分支定界回退"来替代固定L次调用的beam search，能在有界调用内认证精确top-K并对外公布可保证的近似因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级生成式推荐（Semantic ID）检索需每请求用beam search做多候选解码，成本固定为L次串行模型调用；且SID生成器变"钝"时精度下降，且无法给出与精确top-K的偏差界，线上系统无法针对单请求检测检索退化。

Method: 提出SIDECAR：(1) 构造近似草稿候选；(2) 用树注意力机制一次前向对整棵草稿trie打分，并为所有未展开的SID前缀给出概率上界，若草稿包含精确top-K即可认证；(3) 否则用分支定界回退，只扩展仍可能改变输出的trie部分，任意草稿最多L次调用内完成认证；(4) 在每次调用M个token、共Y次调用（Y=L时等同宽度M的beam search资源）的预算下，给出各rank均精确的1+δ因子界。

Result: 视模型锐度而定，SIDECAR用beam search一部分的调用次数或内存即可返回精确top-K；即使预算过小无法恢复精确top-K，也会报告其披露的近似因子δ，而beam search无此能力，因此服务系统可在达到质量目标或延迟上限时提前停止。

Conclusion: SIDECAR将生成式检索从"无保证的固定成本解码"变为"可认证、可按预算提前终止的解码"，在保证精确性或可量化近似度的同时降低调用与内存开销，提升线上检索的可观测性与可控性。

Abstract: Generative recommender systems with Semantic IDs (SIDs) represent each item as a short sequence of $L$ discrete tokens and use a sequence model to predict the next item's tokens from a user's history. Industry-scale deployments typically use these models to retrieve multiple candidates per request with constrained beam search. However, this incurs a fixed cost of $L$ sequential model calls per request, and accuracy degrades as the SID generator becomes less sharp, without a guarantee on how close the output is to the exact top-$K$. Production systems therefore cannot detect degraded retrieval for individual requests.
  We propose SIDECAR, which uses approximate draft candidates and a tree-attention pass to score the entire draft in a single model call, memory permitting. This pass bounds the probability of every unexplored SID prefix and certifies the exact top-$K$ if the draft contains them. Otherwise, a branch-and-bound fallback expands only the parts of the trie that can still change the output, reaching certification within at most $L$ calls for any draft. Moreover, under a budget of $M$ tokens per call and $Y$ calls, which for $Y = L$ matches the resources of a beam search of width $M$, SIDECAR announces a factor $1 + δ$ within which its list will be exact at every rank. Depending on model sharpness, SIDECAR returns the exact top-$K$ using a fraction of beam search's calls or memory. Even when the budget is too small to recover the exact top-$K$, SIDECAR, unlike beam search, reports the announced factor, allowing the serving system to stop early once a quality target or latency limit is reached.

</details>


### [110] [SOARS: System One Assisted Reranking for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32917)
*Amin Bigdeli,Liye Fu,Fattane Zarrinkalam,Joel Stremmel,Elizabeth Lingg*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SOARS：一种免训练框架，用System One模型在深度搜索每步快速完成段落筛选与重排序，替代全程调用重型重排器，在提准的同时降低时延与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索代理通过迭代检索与推理满足复杂信息需求，检索证据的相关性直接决定下游表现；现有重排器虽提升检索效果，但在整个搜索轨迹中反复调用会带来显著时延与开销。

Method: 提出免训练的SOARS框架，基于System One式模型做快速且校准的相关性估计：在每个搜索步中，单次调用即从长文档中选出与查询相关的段落并对候选重排，同时联合考虑原始问题与代理生成的子查询。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus与WebShaper上，跨六个LLM骨干模型一致提升答案准确率与证据召回率，同时减少搜索调用次数、端到端时延与推理成本；BrowseComp-Plus上GPT-5.4 Mini准确率由73.9%升至84.7%，搜索调用由23降至14。

Conclusion: System One式重排可同时提升深度搜索的有效性与效率，为低成本、低时延的检索证据筛选提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deep search agents address complex information needs through iterative retrieval and reasoning, where the relevance of retrieved evidence influences downstream performance. Existing rerankers improve retrieval effectiveness but introduce substantial latency and cost when invoked throughout the search trajectory. We introduce SOARS, a training-free framework leveraging a System One model for fast, calibrated relevance estimation. At each search step, SOARS selects query-relevant passages from long documents and reranks candidates in a single call, jointly considering the original question and agent-generated sub-query. Across six LLM backbones on BrowseComp-Plus and WebShaper, SOARS consistently improves answer accuracy and evidence recall while reducing search calls, end-to-end latency, and inference cost. On BrowseComp-Plus, it improves GPT-5.4 Mini's accuracy from 73.9% to 84.7% while reducing search calls from 23 to 14, demonstrating the potential of System One reranking for more effective and efficient deep search.

</details>


### [111] [Towards Realistic Evaluation of Uncertainty Quantification for LLM-Based Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33036)
*Hao Wu,Sipeng Chen,Weikai Li,Tianyi Chen,Qiancheng Yang,Hang Zhou,Yizhou Sun,Wei Wang,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出固定排序基准，在统一输出契约下比较8种异质不确定性量化信号作为记录级监控器，跨4个数据集、2个LLM骨干评估，并给出三点观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推荐器的排序需要可靠的实例级不确定性监控，但LLM排序器缺乏标准物品分类头，其分数依赖选项标签、提示、生成流程与可用证据；现有UQ方法来源不同、作用阶段不同，导致无法直接比较。

Method: 构建固定排序基准，在共享固定排序协议与统一输出契约下，将八种异质UQ信号作为记录级监控器，在四个推荐数据集和两个LLM骨干上进行评估，考察Error@10检测、流行度尾部目标、成对控制下的NDCG@10差异辨识等任务。

Result: (1) 即使在同一批参考实例上，UQ方法的优劣排序也会随骨干模型变化；(2) 与排序对齐的Top10Mass在多种条件下给出最强的标量Error@10检测，但在流行度尾部目标上优势收窄；(3) 标量UQ信号在判断哪个控制带来更大截断NDCG@10差异上较弱，而扰动排序不稳定性与简单列表级距离等来源特异信号在成对控制一致性上更强。

Conclusion: 应将排序失败检测、跨推荐条件的鲁棒性、以及对受控证据变化的响应视为UQ的三个独立属性分别评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a flexible interface for recommendation systems. However, their issued rankings require reliable instance-level uncertainty monitoring. In this scenario, comparing uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods becomes challenging because LLM rankers lack a standard item-classification head. Their scores may depend on option labels, prompts, generation procedures, and the available user and item evidence. Existing methods derive uncertainty from different sources and apply it at different stages of the recommendation process. Some methods evaluate an issued ranking, whereas others use uncertainty to change the ranking itself, making direct comparison difficult. Here we introduce a fixed-ranking benchmark which evaluates eight heterogeneous UQ signals as record-level monitors under a shared output contract. We evaluate these signals across four recommendation datasets and two LLM backbones under a shared fixed-ranking protocol. From the results, we draw three main observations. First, UQ method ordering changes across backbones even on the same reference instances. Second, ranking-aligned Top10Mass provides the strongest scalar Error@10 detection in several evaluation conditions, reflecting effective cutoff-aligned monitoring, while its advantage narrows for popularity-tail targets. Third, scalar UQ signals are weak at identifying which control has the larger clipped NDCG@10 difference, whereas source-specific signals, including Perturbation Rank Instability and simple listwise distances, provide stronger paired-control agreement, reflecting their sensitivity to the two controlled evidence sources. These findings motivate us to evaluate ranking-failure detection, robustness across recommendation conditions, and responses to controlled evidence changes as distinct properties of UQ.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Rapid Exploratory Maps and their potential role in supporting evidence-to-policy dialogues and processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31693)
*Gareth J. Hollands,Ian Shemilt,James Thomas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"快速探索性图谱"（Rapid Exploratory Maps, REMs）这一新兴方法，借助人工智能技术对现有证据基础进行快速、迭代、响应式的映射，以支撑证据合成早期阶段中证据生产者与使用者之间的对话与协商，并通过两个英国政府政策证据项目的实例加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政策制定者等终端用户委托或引导系统评价时，研究问题与最佳范围往往界定不足，难以直接开展生产；这类参数通常需通过证据生产者与使用者的协同对话来确定，而该过程依赖对现有证据基础的范围界定与图谱绘制。传统上这一环节以人工为主，耗时数周至数月，成为效率瓶颈。近年AI技术发展为加速、迭代地映射相关证据提供了新机遇。

Method: 采用概念性/方法学论述结合案例研究的设计。作者首先界定并刻画REMs这一新兴方法的内涵与特征，即利用AI技术实现快速、迭代、响应式的证据映射；随后选取其所在机构为英格兰政府政策提供证据综述的生产项目中两个第一手案例，进行说明性分析。

Result: REMs在证据映射环节展现出变革潜力，可加快并提升早期"证据—政策"对话中映射工作的响应性；但同时存在重要挑战与潜在风险，其更广泛的适用性与效益目前尚未得到充分评估。

Conclusion: REMs有望提升与终端用户共同开发、设计证据合成的工作效率，但判定其是否真正有用仍需持续的方法开发与审慎评估；未来研究应系统考察其收益、风险与适用边界。

Abstract: Producing useful evidence syntheses requires clearly formulated research questions linked to an understanding of their optimal scope. When policymakers (or other end users) commission or steer the production of systematic reviews or other evidence syntheses to fulfil a demand for evidence, these parameters are rarely specified sufficiently for production to proceed. Instead, these details are typically developed via a collaborative dialogic process involving evidence producers and users to ensure the resulting synthesis will meet users' needs. This process is informed by scoping and (more-or-less formally) mapping the existing evidence base, which can take many weeks or months, slowed by applying mostly manual processes to identify and characterise the evidence. Recent developments in artificial intelligence (AI) technologies have opened up new opportunities for more rapid, iterative, and responsive mapping of relevant evidence to support such early-stage evidence-to-policy dialogues and processes. In this article, we outline and characterise this emerging approach, termed Rapid Exploratory Maps (REMs). We also present two illustrative case studies drawn from first-hand experiences within a programme of evidence reviews production to inform government policy in England. While this approach could be transformative for the mapping process, its broader potential is as yet largely unassessed, and it confers important challenges and likely risks, as well as benefits. Determining whether REMs could usefully support more efficient ways of working with end users when developing and designing evidence syntheses will require continued development and careful evaluation.

</details>


### [113] [Typical-query energy figures mislead institutional AI electricity demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31960)
*Nathan Garland,Ryan Heneghan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出自下而上的SULLEE模型，将token几何、服务硬件与工作流结构耦合，结合蒙特卡洛不确定性传播估计机构级LLM推理年度用电；指出单次查询Wh估算同时忽略右偏任务能耗分布与工作流放大效应，合计低估约3.4倍（2030高集成情景约15倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构大规模采购LLM服务，但能耗估算仍普遍采用单一"每查询Wh"标量，无法反映任务能耗的右偏分布和智能体/工具调用带来的工作流级联放大，导致机构级电力需求被系统性低估。

Method: 构建开源自下而上模型SULLEE：以token几何（输入/输出token规模与结构）、服务硬件（算力与能效参数）、工作流结构三层链接，自下而上累加推断能耗；用蒙特卡洛模拟传播参数不确定性并输出P05–P95区间；以澳大利亚公立大学为案例，设定三种AI集成情景（2026观测使用延续、2026高采纳、2030潜在采纳）外推年度用电。

Result: 单一标量估算与模型结果相比，总推理电量低估约3.4倍（现今日观测使用下），2030高集成情景下约15倍；最高能耗10%的请求贡献超过机构级建模用电的一半。代表性澳大利亚大学年均推理用电由2026观测情景的17.3 MWh（P05–P95：3.1–47.1 MWh）升至2030情景最高287.4 MWh（41.5–830.7 MWh）。

Conclusion: 机构能耗核算不应依赖单次查询标量，需纳入任务能耗分布尾部与工作流放大；SULLEE提供了可审计、可传播不确定性的自下而上核算框架，为机构电力规划与2030情景容量准备提供量化依据。

Abstract: Institutions increasingly buy large language model services, yet we still often estimate electricity use as single Wh-per-query value. We present the Surrogate Large Language Energy Emulator (SULLEE), an open bottom-up model linking token geometry, serving hardware, and workflow structure to annual institutional electricity, with uncertainty propagated by Monte Carlo simulation. When an institution multiplies a single reported "typical" per-query energy figure by its count of visible user prompts our modelling implies that this compounds two separable shortfalls: a right-skewed task-energy distribution not represented by any single scalar, and workflow amplification of each visible prompt into additional agent and tool calls. Together these imply an underestimate of total inference electricity by a factor of approximately 3.4 under present-day observed use, rising to approximately 15 under a 2030 high-integration scenario. Using Australian public universities as a case study, we show how the top 10% of highest-energy requests contributes over half of modelled institution-level energy use. We quantify how institution-level electricity demand may increase across three AI integration scenarios of: 1) a continuation of estimated present-day 2026 usage; 2) a high-uptake present-day 2026 institution, and 3) a potential 2030 uptake scenario - based on present trends of generative AI tools and their uptake. For a representative Australian university, mean annual electricity demand for AI inference purposes is projected to increase from 17.3 MWh (with a 3.1-47.1 MWh uncertainty, within P05-P95 scenario quartile ranges) under the 2026 observed-use scenario to up to 287.4 MWh (41.5-830.7 MWh) by 2030.

</details>


### [114] [Audits Need Access, Data, and Compute. But How Much?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31968)
*Salil Goyal,Sarah H. Cen,Daniel E. Ho*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个沿"模型访问、数据、算力"三维度变化的审计框架，在三项任务上测量三者对审计成功度的联合影响，发现部分模型访问重要但存在边际递减、数据与算力对公平性审计更关键而模型访问对操纵与谎言检测更关键、三者可相互替代（存在交换率），主张审计治理应联合考量审计者权限的多维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI审计的安全性依赖审计者的权限，即其可支配的信息与资源。当前争论集中于"审计需要多少模型访问"，审计方要求更多访问而企业以商业秘密与隐私为由抗拒，争论僵持未决；同时该争论忽略了数据与算力需求这一相关问题，缺乏对三类资源联合作用的系统考察。

Method: 构建一个可分别调节模型访问、数据、算力三个维度的审计框架，系统改变每个维度并测量其对审计成功的联合效应，并将该框架应用于三项审计任务（传统公平性审计、操纵检测、谎言检测）。

Result: （1）一定程度的模型访问很重要，但存在边际收益递减；（2）数据与算力对传统公平性审计更重要，而模型访问对操纵与谎言检测更重要；（3）三种资源具有可替代性，即彼此之间存在"交换率"，模型访问并非唯一关键因素。

Conclusion: 审计治理应结合审计目标，联合考虑审计者权限在模型访问、数据与算力等维度的配置；这对从"少量嵌入型评估者、访问权限高"到"大量第三方审计者、独立性高但访问受限"的审计谱系尤为重要。该框架可作为模板推广至其他审计任务，用以确定审计者权限的合理程度。

Abstract: AI audits have garnered significant attention in recent months, as the ability to test and monitor AI models plays a crucial role in safe development and deployment. However, AI audit success hinges on an auditor's authority; in particular, what information and resources they have at their disposal. Ongoing debates have surrounded the question: How much model access is needed to conduct an AI audit? Auditors seek more model access, while companies resist it for reasons related to confidential business information (trade secrets) and privacy. This tug-of-war remains unresolved. Further, this debate misses a related question: How much data and compute do auditors need? In this work, we systematically investigate both questions. We provide a framework that varies each of these three dimensions-model access, data, and compute-and measures their joint effect on audit success. We apply this framework to three tasks. We find that (1) some model access is important, but there can be diminishing returns; (2) data and compute matter more for traditional fairness audits, but model access matters more for manipulation and lie detection; and (3) these three resources can be fungible (i.e., an exchange rate exists between them), meaning that model access is not the only salient factor. These results suggest that audit governance should jointly consider different dimensions of auditor authority (including model access, data, and compute) in relation to audit objectives. These issues are particularly salient as we consider the spectrum of auditing setups, from a small ecosystem of embedded evaluators with significant access to a large body of third-party auditors with greater independence but limited access. Our framework serves as a template that can be applied to different audit tasks and inform the degree of auditor authority.

</details>


### [115] [Do Clinician-Scored Examples Help LLMs Code ADOS-2 from Hour-Long Transcripts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32050)
*Zeteng Lin,Yikang Liu,Bolin Zeng,Mengyuan Ding,Xihui Wu,Kangyou Li,Chengzhe Fu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过安慰剂对照设计检验专家标注示例能否校准LLM对ADOS-2的自动评分，发现示例标签本身并不提升与临床医生的一致性，一致性仅处于κ=0.22–0.30的有限水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM与临床评分者对齐的常见做法是提供专家评分的少量示例，但"正确标签是否真正改善一致性"缺乏严格检验，且以往报告常缺少污染标签对照与强基线。

Method: 使用48名儿童、时长约一小时的ADOS-2模块3会话中8个言语类条目，采用预注册的安慰剂对照设计，对比5种方案：few-shot评分、锚定比较（模型仅判断儿童优于/相似/劣于已评分锚点，再由固定规则给出分数）、事后校准、基于协议的证据路由、以及全转录本直接评分；在40名留出儿童上以二次加权kappa衡量一致性，并设置"把所有示例标签替换为错误标签"的对照臂，另用第二个更大模型复现。

Result: 一致性整体偏低，κ为0.22–0.30。标签污染改变了约三分之一的few-shot预测且更多偏向被污染标签，但一致性未下降（正确标签0.25，错误标签0.23）；更大模型结果相同。锚定比较未优于few-shot，其跨儿童判断准确性不高于绝对分数所隐含的水平，证据路由亦无帮助。在未校准的注册实验臂中，全转录本直接评分最佳（0.30）。探索性分析显示：模型的等级判断对儿童的排序优于其最终分数；现有批校准将一致性提升至0.46；在受控合成会话中，分片段评分未优于校准后的全转录本评分。

Conclusion: 专家标注示例本身无法校准基于模型的临床评分者；任何关于校准或流程复杂化带来增益的论断，都必须包含错误标签对照与强基线。

Abstract: Large language models can apply a written rubric to a transcript, making automated coding of the Autism Diagnostic Observation Schedule (ADOS-2) look within reach. A common way to align such a rater with clinicians is to show it expert-scored examples, but whether correct labels improve agreement is rarely tested. We test this for eight speech-based items of hour-long ADOS-2 Module 3 sessions from 48 children, with one local model and a pre-registered, placebo-controlled design. We compare few-shot scoring, an anchored comparison in which the model only judges a child better, similar or worse than scored anchors and a fixed rule gives the score, post-hoc calibration, protocol-based evidence routing and direct scoring of the full transcript. On 40 held-out children, agreement with clinicians was modest, with quadratic weighted kappa between 0.22 and 0.30. Replacing every example's label with a wrong one changed about a third of the few-shot predictions, more often towards the corrupted labels than chance, but did not lower agreement, 0.25 with correct and 0.23 with wrong labels. A larger second model did the same. The anchored comparison did not improve on few-shot scoring, its cross-child judgements were no more accurate than those implied by absolute scores, and routing did not help. Among the registered, uncalibrated arms, direct scoring of the full transcript agreed best at 0.30. Exploratory follow-up analyses found that the model's graded judgements ranked children better than its final scores, that existing batch calibration raised agreement to 0.46 on the same children, and that in controlled synthetic sessions scoring episodes separately did not beat calibrated whole-transcript scoring. Expert-scored examples do not by themselves calibrate model-based clinical raters, and claims of calibration or added pipeline complexity need corrupted-label controls and strong baselines.

</details>


### [116] [An expert consensus on identification likelihood in NeuroImaging data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32330)
*Cyril R. Pernet,Mahnaz Arvaneh,Elise Bannier,Michael Breakspear,Vince Calhoun,Jason da Silva Castanheira,Arnaud Delorme,Simon Eickhoff,Anders Eklund,Alex Fornito,Melanie Ganz,Clara Moreau,Tom Nichols,Gustav Nilsonne,Robert Oostenveld,Bryan Paton,Petra Ritter,Ariel Rokem,Michael Schirner,James Mac Shine,Michel Thiebaut de Schotten,Bertrand Thirion,Mattia Veronese,Natalie Voets,B. T. Thomas Yeo,Anita,Jwa*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过名义小组技术与德尔菲法获取专家共识，对34类神经影像数据类型的重识别与"单列识别"可能性进行评级，为数据共享与治理提供分级依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像数据兼具潜在个人相关性与敏感性，其可识别性问题在法律、伦理（脑数据与人格同一性、心理隐私、自主性）及数据共享治理框架中日益突出，但缺乏跨模态、跨处理层级的系统化可识别性评估。

Method: 结合名义小组技术（NGT）与德尔菲过程，组织专家对34种神经影像数据类型（涵盖多种成像模态与处理层级，从策展研究数据到脑图、矩阵及汇总表）的重识别可能性与"单列识别"（singling-out）可能性进行共识评级。

Result: 专家评级表明，约38–67%的被评估数据类型的固有重识别风险较低，可在语境性保障措施下以最小限制共享；并据此形成分级分类。

Conclusion: 该分类可帮助研究者与法务团队协作，按国内与国际法律要求确定适当的保护级别（许可、访问控制、治理），促进国际数据交换；成果通过专门网站（brainimagingprivacy.github.io）向相关方传播。

Abstract: Guidelines on the anonymity of neuroimaging data help manage research data, inform institutional legal and governance frameworks, and support data-sharing workflows. The question of identifiability is particularly salient in human brain imaging because such data are not only potentially personal (i.e., related to an identifiable person) but also sensitive and thus require extra precautionary and protective processes. The identifiability of brain imaging data is also salient for emerging legal and ethical frameworks regarding rights that emphasise the special status of brain data as closely linked to personal identity, mental privacy, and autonomy. Using a combined nominal group technique and Delphi process, we obtained expert consensus ratings of re-identification and singling-out likelihood across 34 neuroimaging data types, spanning multiple imaging modalities and processing levels, from curated research data to maps, matrices, and summary tables. Expert ratings suggest that 38-67% of the assessed neuroimaging data types could be shared with minimal restrictions based on their intrinsic re-identification likelihood, subject to contextual safeguards. The resulting classification enables researchers to engage with relevant legal teams to guide the appropriate and safe release of data, and to determine the appropriate level of protection (licensing, access controls, governance) in line with domestic and international legal requirements, with the ultimate goal of facilitating international data exchange. A website (https://brainimagingprivacy.github.io/) has been created to facilitate the dissemination of these findings and related evidence among relevant parties.

</details>


### [117] [Emerging International Best Practices for AI Model Specs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32768)
*Edward Kembery,Nathan Lambert,Robert McCarthy,Jinge Wang,Peng Xu,Naying Hu,Clement Neo,Xu Jia,Miro Pluckebaum,Seán Ó hÉigeartaigh,Sören Mindermann,Christoph Winter,Lujain Ibrahim,Yawen Duan,Saad Siddiqui*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 针对"模型规格书"（model specs）缺乏独立编写指南的空白，本文系统论述了其价值、类型学、编写与实施指引，并指出八大开放问题，最后向前沿AI公司、政府和研究及公民社会组织提出建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球前沿AI开发者已开始发布描述其模型预期行为的文档（模型规格书），这类文档在AI开发与治理中日益重要，但缺乏独立的编写指南，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用分节论述的综述+指引型结构：①阐述编写模型规格书对AI开发者及社会的益处；②提出可供参考的模型规格类型学（taxonomy）；③给出实践中编写与实施规格书的指引；④梳理与安全及理想行为相关的八大开放问题。

Result: 形成了模型规格书写作的框架性成果：价值论证、分类类型学、实践性编写与实施指南，以及八项开放问题的清单。

Conclusion: 作者总结并向前沿AI公司、政府、研究机构与公民社会组织提出建议，以推动模型规格书的规范化编写与应用。

Abstract: Frontier AI developers from around the world have begun to publish documents that describe how their AI models are intended to behave. Although these 'model specifications'-or 'model specs', for short-are increasingly important in AI development and governance, little independent guidance exists for writing them.
  This paper addresses this gap in five main sections. First, we outline the advantages of writing a model spec for AI developers and society at large. Second, we introduce a taxonomy for model specs that developers can take inspiration from. Third, we propose guidance for writing and implementing a spec in practice. Fourth, we note eight open problems related to writing model specs for safe and desirable behaviour.
  We conclude with recommendations for frontier AI companies, governments, and research and civil society organisations.

</details>


### [118] [AI, Bias, and Inclusion: A Two-Phase Mixed-Methods Study of Gender Bias, Awareness, and Trust in Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32869)
*MD Junayed Talukdar,Khosro Salmani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究采用两阶段混合方法，先以计算内容分析检验ChatGPT在四国生成的360条职业叙述是否存在性别刻板关联，再调查学生对AI偏见的识别与信任，论证AI公平需超越技术层面的整体性路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统已深入医疗、金融、就业与司法等领域，却会延续既有社会不平等；其偏见内嵌于技术与社会结构中而难以察觉，直接挑战公平、多元与包容（EDI）理念。作者据此主张仅靠技术手段无法实现AI公平，需整体性路径。

Method: 两阶段混合方法设计。阶段一：对ChatGPT以性别中立提示在加拿大、德国、印度、孟加拉国四国生成的360条职业叙述进行计算内容分析，考察职业是否偏向关联某一性别及跨地区一致性。阶段二：考察学生对AI潜在偏见的识别程度、对AI用于重大决策的信任，并将其与EDI及AI伦理研究主题相关联。

Result: 通过将输出层面的偏见分析与用户感知数据相结合，研究识别出构建未来EDI-AI协同设计框架所需的实证基础，并勾勒出该框架须涵盖的社会技术维度。

Conclusion: AI系统的公平性不能仅通过技术层面实现，必须采用涵盖输出偏见与用户感知的整体性社会技术方法，为EDI-AI协同设计框架提供依据。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems are widespread across fields such as healthcare, finance, employment, and criminal justice, with a substantial impact on the lives of individuals and society. However, it has been established that AI systems perpetuate existing social inequalities, particularly through biases that are not easily discernible. Such biases are embedded in the technical and social structures of artificial intelligence systems, making them difficult to detect. These biases challenge the concept of Equity, Diversity, and Inclusion (EDI), which recognizes diversity as the acknowledgment of individual differences, equity as fairness and equal access, and inclusion as the value placed on all participants. This study argues that fairness in artificial intelligence systems cannot be achieved by focusing solely on their technical aspects, necessitating a holistic approach. This study employs a two-phase mixed-methods design. Phase 1 applies computational content analysis to 360 occupational narratives generated by ChatGPT across four countries (Canada, Germany, India, and Bangladesh) using gender-neutral prompts to examine whether professions are predominantly associated with one gender and whether these patterns remain consistent across regions. Phase 2 examines the extent to which students recognize potential bias in AI, their trust in AI for consequential decision-making, and how these perceptions relate to themes in EDI and AI ethics scholarship. By combining output-level bias analysis with user perception data, this study identifies the empirical foundations necessary for future EDI-AI co-design frameworks, outlining the sociotechnical dimensions that such frameworks must address to be effective.

</details>


### [119] [Who decides the norms in TCS? Analysing the composition of the STOC 2027 committees and of the Simons Institute "AI+TCS" working group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33082)
*Guillaume Munch-Maccagnoni*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文通过履历/隶属关系统计分析发现：推动在理论计算机科学（TCS）中使用LLM的Simons Institute工作组及其推荐被STOC 2027程序委员会与ACM SIGACT执委会采纳，而这些决策机构在国别与产业关联两个维度上呈现美国机构/企业与AI/大厂背景的严重过度代表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simons Institute工作组关于在TCS中引入LLM（用于交流与评审）的建议被STOC 2027程序委员会与SIGACT执委会几乎全部落实，作者意图检验这些建议的制定与批准主体在国别构成和产业关联上是否具有代表性，以揭示潜在的利益与视角偏差。

Method: 描述性构成分析（composition analysis），沿两个维度：所属国家（affiliation country）与产业关联（affiliated with or funded by，含当前或近期）。对象包括：Simons Institute工作组、STOC 2027与2026程序委员会（按国别，作年度对比）、ACM SIGACT扩展执委会（按国别与产业关联）。

Result: Simons工作组与SIGACT执委会中，美国机构与私营公司研究者占绝对主导，与AI/大型科技公司有（当前或近期）关联者显著过度代表；STOC 2027相对2026，美国机构/公司研究者大幅过度代表，欧盟与亚洲机构研究者大幅不足代表。

Conclusion: 决策与建议主体的构成高度集中于美国与AI/大厂背景，缺乏地域与产业多样性；考虑到这些主体正是批准LLM引入TCS交流与评审的机构，此构成偏差值得关注，并提示需对相关治理结构的代表性进行审视。

Abstract: A working group organised by the Simons Institute released a report last September containing recommendations aiming to increase the usage of LLMs in theoretical computer science (TCS), including introducing them for communication and evaluation. Most of these recommendations were enacted by the STOC 2027 programme committee and the ACM SIGACT executive committee (as the steering committee of STOC). The composition of the Simons Institute working group is analysed along two axes: country of affiliation, and ties with industry (i.e. affiliated with or funded by). The results show an overwhelming representation of researchers from US institutions and private companies, and a large over-representation of researchers with ties to AI/Big Tech companies (in the present or the recent past). The composition of the STOC 2027 and 2026 programme committees is analysed in terms of country of affiliation. The results show a large over-representation in 2027 of researchers from US institutions/companies, and large under-representation of researchers from EU and Asian institutions, as compared to 2026. The composition of the ACM SIGACT extended executive committee is analysed along the same two axes as the Simons Institute working group: country and industrial ties. The results show the same overwhelming representation of researchers from US institutions/companies, and a large representation of researchers with ties to AI/Big Tech companies.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Local subspace encoding for spectral prediction: from TabPFN to operator-adaptive calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31245)
*Gregory Beurier,Robin Reiter,Denis Cornet,Lauriane Rouan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在 nirs4all 生态系统数据集上复分析 RamanPFN 提出的 GCU（全局成分解混）与 LVSE（局部振动子空间编码）两种光谱表示，发现 LVSE 相对原始光谱 TabPFN 显著降低 RMSE（三个 checkpoint 平均 10.22%/12.80%/14.52%），分类加权 F1 提升 2.22/2.56/3.81 个百分点，并可改善 PLS 与 Ridge 等经典校准方法；GCU 在本实现中可靠性较低；在 AOM 基准 35 任务面板上增益仅较小中位数水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN 在近红外光谱上已有良好预测性能，RamanPFN 继而提出 GCU 与 LVSE 两种光谱表示。作者希望检验这些表示在 nirs4all 生态数据集上是否同样有效，以及其价值能否延伸至经典校准方法（PLS、Ridge）。

Method: 对公开光谱数据集目录中已完成的实验进行事后（post-hoc）再分析：在匹配的 61 个数值预测任务子集上（排除 10 个类别编码、元数据或语义歧义目标）比较 LVSE 与原始光谱 TabPFN 的 RMSE；在 76 个分类数据集上比较加权 F1（2.5 checkpoint 对 15 个超出原生类别上限的数据集采用输出编码）；同时检验 LVSE 对 PLS、Ridge 的影响，并将 LVSE 候选加入算子自适应模型，另用源自公开 AOM 基准的 35 任务面板做外部检验。

Result: LVSE 相对原始光谱 TabPFN 在 checkpoint 2.5/3/3.5 上平均 RMSE 降低 10.22%、12.80%、14.52%，中位数降低 4.99%、4.74%、7.82%；76 个分类数据集上加权 F1 提升 2.22、2.56、3.81 个百分点。LVSE 亦改善 PLS 与 Ridge，中位 RMSE 降低 4.75% 与 1.89%。GCU 在本实现中单独使用可靠性较差。将 LVSE 候选纳入算子自适应模型可行，但 AOM 35 任务面板仅显示小幅中位增益。

Conclusion: 结果支持在现有表示选择中一并评估 LVSE，但并未证明在原始输入与 LVSE 输入之间做选择的 selector 能在所有任务上带来提升。

Abstract: TabPFN has already shown useful predictive performance on near-infrared spectra. RamanPFN subsequently proposed two spectral representations for tabular inference: Global Compositional Unmixing (GCU) and Local Vibrational Subspace Encoding (LVSE). We assess whether these representations also help on datasets assembled through the nirs4all ecosystem, and whether their value extends to classical calibration. We reanalyse completed experiments from a public spectral dataset catalogue. On a post-hoc subset of 61 matched numerical prediction tasks, after excluding ten class-code, metadata or semantically ambiguous targets, LVSE reduces RMSE relative to raw-spectrum TabPFN by 10.22%, 12.80% and 14.52% on average for checkpoints 2.5, 3 and 3.5, respectively, with median reductions of 4.99%, 4.74% and 7.82%. Across 76 classification datasets, weighted F1 increases by 2.22, 2.56 and 3.81 percentage points. The 2.5 classification procedure uses output coding on 15 datasets exceeding the checkpoint's native class limit. GCU alone is less reliable in this implementation. LVSE also improves PLS and Ridge in many cases, with median RMSE reductions of 4.75% and 1.89%. Adding LVSE candidates to an operator-adaptive model is feasible, but a separate 35-task panel derived from the public AOM benchmark shows only small median gains. These results motivate evaluating LVSE alongside existing representation choices. They do not establish that a selector choosing between raw and LVSE inputs will improve every task.

</details>


### [121] [Actionwave: A Systematic Benchmark for Waveform-Centric Clinical Prediction Across Care Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31249)
*Jie Peng,Davis Hobley,Song Wang,Zachary PlotkinB,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Actionwave，一个面向连续床旁波形的临床预测基准，覆盖 ICU、手术室与急诊三类场景，通过统一的“决策时刻”预测接口、20 个任务、140 万标注窗口和超 8.7 万次模型拟合，系统评估模型优势如何依赖信号时长、提前预警时间与可用输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有波形预测基准只考察选定的预测条件，模型性能优势是否依赖信号时长、预警提前量、可用输入以及不同诊疗场景，尚缺乏系统认识；且不同研究常因队列不匹配、缺乏单模态对照和时间对齐不清而难以比较。

Method: 构建 Actionwave 基准：采用统一的决策时刻预测界面，按“推断什么、何时推断”组织临床任务；变化与临床使用相关的条件（如干预前所需预警时间、信号时长、输入模态）；在给定模型规模与算力预算下比较多种架构与预训练生物信号编码器；协议要求患者级数据划分、严格信息截断，以及隔离单一建模因素的对照比较。

Result: 在 20 个任务、来自源数据集的 140 万个任务标注窗口上，实验网格总计超过 87,000 次模型拟合；结果识别出任务特定的建模选择，并表明在解释波形模型性能时，匹配队列、单模态对照与显式时间对齐是必要的。

Conclusion: 波形模型的比较结论高度依赖任务、场景与预测设置，须以匹配队列、单模态控制和显式时间约束为实验前提；Actionwave 为跨 ICU、手术室与急诊的波形临床预测提供了可复现、控制严格的统一评测框架。

Abstract: Continuous bedside waveforms record rapid physiological changes that can inform clinical decisions. Existing benchmarks examine selected prediction conditions, but it remains unclear how model advantages depend on signal duration, warning time, and available inputs across care settings. We introduce Actionwave, a benchmark for waveform-centric clinical prediction across intensive-care, operating-room, and emergency-department settings. Actionwave uses a common decision-time prediction interface and groups clinically grounded tasks by what is inferred and when. It varies conditions relevant to clinical use, including the warning required before an intervention. We compare diverse architectures and pretrained biosignal encoders under specified model-size and compute budgets. The protocol requires patient-level splits, strict information cutoffs, and comparisons that isolate individual modeling factors. Across 20 tasks and 1.4M task-labelled windows from source datasets, the experimental grids account for over 87,000 model fits. These comparisons identify task-specific choices and show why matched cohorts, unimodal controls, and explicit timing are necessary when interpreting waveform model performance.

</details>


### [122] [Chain-of-Thought Is Not Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31271)
*Fazl Barez,Tung-Yu Wu,Iván Arcuschin,Michael Lan,Vincent Wang,Noah Siegel,Nicolas Collignon,Clement Neo,Isabelle Lee,Alasdair Paren,John Yan,Adel Bibi,Damiano Fornasiere,Robert Trager,Yanai Elazar,Yoshua Bengio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该立场论文论证：当前思维链（CoT）生成的推理说明常不忠实于模型真实计算过程，既非必要也非充分的可解释性手段，并在高价值领域被过度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT可提升任务表现并给人透明感的错觉，但在医疗、法律、自主系统等高风险场景中被当作承重式可解释性技术使用，一旦其不忠实，相关安全论证与流程将失效。

Method: 立场论文＋文献综合：将忠实性界定为"既人类可理解、又充分反映模型底层推理以支撑负责任使用"；系统梳理证据表明语言化链条常偏离驱动预测的关键隐式计算；并对1000篇近期以CoT为中心的论文进行实证统计。

Result: 约21.7%的论文明确将CoT作为承重式可解释性技术，据此构建的流程或安全论证在CoT不忠实时不成立，其中包含高风险领域研究。

Conclusion: 提出三点建议：(i) 不应在缺乏额外验证时将CoT视为充分的可解释性；(ii) 采用严格方法评估忠实性对下游决策的影响；(iii) 在提出任何基于CoT的可解释性主张时一并报告此类评估，同时继续发挥CoT在沟通与监控方面的价值。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) allows language models to verbalise multi-step rationales before producing their final answer. While this technique often boosts task performance and offers an impression of transparency into the model's reasoning, this position paper argues that rationales generated by current CoT techniques can be misleading and are neither necessary nor sufficient for trustworthy interpretability. We analyse faithfulness as whether CoTs are not only human-interpretable but also reflect the model's underlying reasoning well enough to support responsible use. By synthesising evidence from prior work, we systematically show that verbalised chains are frequently unfaithful, diverging from the critical hidden computations that drive a model's predictions. As CoT is increasingly relied upon in application domains, including high-stakes ones such as medicine, law, and autonomous systems, we further argue that this unfaithfulness is not taken seriously enough in these settings: our analysis of 1,000 recent CoT-centric papers finds that approximately 21.7% explicitly treat CoT as a load-bearing interpretability technique, building pipelines or safety arguments that would not hold if CoT is unfaithful to the model's actual reasoning. They include papers in high-stakes domains that heavily hinge on such interpretability claims. Building on prior work, we make three proposals: (i) avoid treating CoT as being sufficient for interpretability without additional verification, (ii) adopt rigorous methods that assess faithfulness for downstream decision-making, and (iii) report such assessments alongside any interpretability claims based on CoT, while continuing to use CoT for its communicative and monitoring benefits.

</details>


### [123] [Hidden Clues in WM-811K: Uncovering the Spatial Signals Behind Wafer Map Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31292)
*Kabir Tolani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过四种消融变体（Original、Layout-only、Global-shuffle、Radial-shuffle）量化WM-811K晶圆图中各类信息对CNN分类性能的贡献，Macro-F1依次为0.8563、0.3829、0.5803、0.7439，表明性能来自晶圆版图、失效数、径向分布与精确二维位置的多层次信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在WM-811K上分类精度很高，但难以解释究竟是数据集的哪些特征驱动了该性能，因此作者希望厘清预测性能分别来源于晶圆版图、失效芯片数量、其距中心距离以及精确二维位置的贡献。

Method: 先进行数据清洗：分析重复晶圆图、剔除标签冲突的相同图、并确保同一制造批次或完全相同的图不跨训练/验证/测试集，最终得到覆盖8类缺陷的25,513张带标签晶圆图。随后为每张图构造四个变体：Original（原图）、Layout-only（仅保留非背景芯片版图）、Global-shuffle（保持失效数但全局打乱位置）、Radial-shuffle（在8个按中心距划分的径向区间内随机化失效位置）。四种变体的数据划分、预处理与CNN结构完全一致。

Result: Macro-F1分别为：Layout-only 0.3829，Global-shuffle 0.5803，Radial-shuffle 0.7439，Original 0.8563。

Conclusion: WM-811K在多个层次上蕴含有用信息：晶圆版图、失效数量、给定半径内失效分布以及失效芯片的精确二维位置；因此该基准上的高分类性能并非仅源于学习精确的缺陷构型。

Abstract: WM-811K (also known as LSWMD) is a wafer map dataset first made publicly available in a 2015 paper by Wu et al. Convolutional neural networks have been used to achieve high classification accuracy on this dataset, yet it's not easy to decipher which features of the dataset are driving that performance. This study attempts to answer this specifically for WM-811K: how much of the predictive performance results from the wafer layout, the count of failed dies, their distance from the center and their exact 2-D location. After analyzing duplicate wafer maps, removing same wafer maps with conflicting labels and ensuring that the same manufacturing lot or exact same map doesn't appear in training, validation and test data, we were left with 25,513 defect-labeled wafer maps from eight classes. We then constructed four variations of each wafer map. The Original was the actual wafer map present in the dataset. The Layout-only kept only the layout of the non-background dies. The Global-shuffle kept the count of failed dies while shuffling them around. The Radial-shuffle randomized the locations of failed dies only in their radial bin, which was chosen out of 8 radial bins based on distance from the center. The data split, preprocessing and CNN architecture remained the same for all four of them. Macro-F1 progressed like this: 0.3829 (Layout-only), 0.5803 (Global-shuffle), 0.7439 (Radial-shuffle) and 0.8563 (Original). These results show that WM-811K contains useful information at several levels: wafer layout, number of failures, distribution of failed dies in a given radius, and exact 2-D locations of the failed dies. Therefore, high classification performance on this benchmark does not come only from learning the exact defect configuration.

</details>


### [124] [Conformalized Tree-Based Gradient Ensembles for Out-of-Distribution Detection and Plausible Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31319)
*Mariam Zakaria Moussa Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将保形（conformal）OOD门控与可行动性约束的反事实搜索结合，在Adult与GMSC表格基准上校准有限样本异常门（gate_IF、gate_LOF、gate_ALL，δ=0.05），用留出GMM/OCSVM检测器审计反事实，证明门控显著降低OOD标记率但提高成本、收窄可行性，形式化了成本—可行性—合理性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对GBDT的反事实归因（recourse）若仅优化分类器置信度或窄代理分数，会利用决策边界伪影与低密度区域（Goodhart定律），产生名义有利但偏离数据流形的OOD建议，缺乏可执行性与可信度。

Method: 在两个表格基准（Adult、GMSC）上，基于隔离森林、LOF、kNN信号在δ=0.05显著性水平校准有限样本异常门（gate_IF、gate_LOF及合取门gate_ALL），并与可行动性约束的反事实搜索耦合；用留出的GMM与OCSVM检测器审计生成反事实，辅以gen10、gen100、relax消融，并在约束/无约束搜索均成功的配对样本上做统计检验（Holm校正）。

Result: gate_ALL在Adult上显著降低留出OCSVM标记（N=541，Δflag_OCSVM=-0.0296，p_Holm=4.0e-4）；在GMSC上同时降低两类标记（N=540，Δflag_OCSVM=-0.0346，p=1.0751e-4；Δflag_GMM=-0.1663，p=3.5679e-16），代价是匹配反事实成本上升（Δcost=+0.3035，p=1.3365e-5）且整体可行性收窄。

Conclusion: 保形OOD门控可有效审计表格集成模型上反事实归因的可靠性，但以更高成本与更窄可行性换取流形合理性，构成本—可行性—合理性的三方权衡。

Abstract: Counterfactual recourse provides actionable feature adjustments that reverse unfavourable predictions made by automated decision models. However, when recourse search optimizes purely against classifier confidence or narrow proxy scores over gradient-boosted decision trees (GBDT), search procedures can exploit boundary artifacts and low-density regions---an instance of Goodhart's law---yielding out-of-distribution (OOD) recommendations that appear favourable nominally while lacking manifold plausibility. In this paper, we study conformalized OOD gating coupled with actionability-constrained counterfactual search across two tabular benchmarks: Adult and Give Me Some Credit (GMSC). Calibrating finite-sample anomaly gates (gate_IF, gate_LOF, and the conjunctive gate_ALL) at significance level delta = 0.05 over isolation forest, local outlier factor, and k-nearest-neighbor signals, we audit generated counterfactuals against held-out GMM and OCSVM detectors alongside ablation studies (gen10, gen100, and relax). Across matched recourse pairs where both constrained and unconstrained searches succeed, gate_ALL significantly reduces held-out OCSVM flags on Adult (N=541, Delta flag_OCSVM = -0.0296, p_Holm = 4.0e-4) and lowers both held-out flags on GMSC (N=540, Delta flag_OCSVM = -0.0346, p = 1.0751e-4; Delta flag_GMM = -0.1663, p = 3.5679e-16) while increasing matched recourse cost (Delta cost = +0.3035, p = 1.3365e-5) and narrowing overall feasibility. Our findings formalize the cost--feasibility--plausibility trade-off and demonstrate how conformal OOD gating audits recourse reliability on tabular ensembles.

</details>


### [125] [Layer-Readout Interfaces in EEG Foundation Models: Predictive Accessibility and Cross-Dataset Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31320)
*Yanlin Fu,Wei Liu,Liming Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文以EEG基础模型为对象，将中间层优于末层的优势拆解为读出方式、超出末层接口的预测价值、以及深度结构跨数据集可迁移性三个因素，发现改进末层读出可挽回大部分性能但中间层在冻结提取下仍具优势，且源数据集选出的层在目标数据集上依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型作为冻结特征提取器时，中间层常优于末层，但其优势来源不明；作者希望厘清这一现象究竟来自读出方式、真实的深度预测效用，还是可迁移的深度结构。

Method: 在EEG基础模型上做因子化实验：5个模型、3个任务，对比不同读出方式（含改进末层读出），检验中间层在冻结提取下相对末层的增量预测价值；并在5组数据集对、4类任务族上验证源数据选层向目标数据的迁移，以及在睡眠分期任务中检验源端任务方向的迁移价值。

Result: 改进末层读出能一致地恢复可观的预测性能，但中间层在冻结提取框架内仍保持优势；一个数据集上选出的层在另一数据集上仍有用（跨5组数据集对、4类任务族）；源端导出的紧凑任务方向在迁移后仍具预测价值（睡眠分期）。

Conclusion: 深度方向的预测效用并不均匀，其价值取决于读出方式与迁移方式；更关键的是，源数据选出的层可构成跨数据集可复用的特征接口，减少目标端选层搜索，并在目标数据集上优于末层基线。

Abstract: Intermediate layers often outperform final layers when foundation models are used as frozen feature extractors, but the source of this advantage is unclear. We study this question in EEG foundation models by separating three factors: how representations are read out, whether intermediate layers provide predictive utility beyond the final-layer interface, and whether useful depth structure transfers across datasets. Across five models and three tasks, improving the final-layer readout consistently recovers substantial predictive performance, yet intermediate layers remain advantageous within frozen extraction. We also find that layers selected on one dataset remain useful on another across five dataset pairs and four task families, and that a compact set of source-derived task directions retains predictive value after transfer in sleep staging. Together, these results show that predictive utility varies across depth, and its usefulness depends on how representations are read out and transferred. More crucially, source-selected layers provide a reusable feature interface across datasets, reducing the need for target-side layer search while outperforming final-layer baselines on target datasets.

</details>


### [126] [Small Learned Projections for Semantic-Reversal-Aware Retrieval with Spectral Acceleration: A Four-Domain Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31442)
*Sayed Amir Karim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稠密向量检索的对抗性陷阱问题：通过学习小型投影 W 抑制"陷阱文档"进入Top-10，并设计边界值求解加速器支撑十万级规模检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密嵌入检索存在对抗脆弱性：查询本应由文档A回答，却被与A仅差微小语义改变编辑的近复制B（"陷阱"）反转；在E5-Large-v2上以原始余弦相似度检索，5000规模池中82–100%的查询会把陷阱B排进Top-10，亟需抑制手段与可扩展的部署方案。

Method: (1) 训练小型学习投影 W: R^1024→R^d（d∈{11,32}），在声明的案例集上以确定性流程训练，用于压制陷阱；(2) 提出边界条件加速器，将谱图检索(SpectRAG)的稠密特征分解替换为边界值求解；输出为每文档少量浮点数的存储表示，按域/租户构建专用小型索引。

Result: 声明案例上，四个独立领域（法律、医疗、专利、金融）、十二个(领域,池规模)设置、池规模至100,000，均达HN@10=0.0000，正确改写保留率93–99%（精确最近邻搜索）；加速器在n=100,000下各域保持HN@10=0.0000，约300查询套件耗时0.6–1.1 s（约2–4 ms/查询）；专用索引体积约为完整嵌入的1/90，速度按维度比例提升。限定条件：无概率界；改写查询对未变语料正确率降至约0.79（陷阱压制仍成立）；跨域迁移仅部分有效。

Conclusion: 投影+边界求解可在声明案例与实测池上零陷阱命中并支撑十万级检索，许多小型声明式索引可作为单一大型向量库的实用替代；论文明确将结论限定于声明案例，公开配方、透镜、陷阱集与评测框架(AGPL-3.0)，加速器实现为保留商业组件，所有HN指标在精确搜索下可复现。

Abstract: Dense-embedding retrievers can fail adversarially: when a query is answered by a document A and reversed by a near-copy B that differs from A by a small meaning-changing edit (a "trap"), raw cosine over E5-Large-v2 places B in the top-10 for 82-100% of queries at a pool of 5,000. We study two things. First, a small learned projection W: R^1024 -> R^d (d in {11, 32}), trained by a deterministic recipe on a declared set of cases, that suppresses the trap: for the declared cases, HN@10 = 0.0000 (no trap in the top-10) in all twelve (domain, pool-size) settings across four independent domains (legal, medical, patent, financial) and pools up to 100,000, while the correct paraphrase is retained for 93-99% of cases, under exact nearest-neighbour search. Second, a boundary-conditioned accelerator that serves the projection at that scale: an acceleration of spectral graph retrieval (SpectRAG) that replaces a dense eigendecomposition with a boundary-value solve, holding HN@10 = 0.0000 at n = 100,000 in every domain and retrieving the ~300-query suite in 0.6-1.1 s (~2-4 ms per query on average). Because the lens's output is the stored representation (a few floats per document), a hardened, specialised index per domain or tenant is ~90x smaller and dimension-proportionally faster than the full embedding, making many small declared indexes a practical alternative to one large vector store. The claim is scoped to the declared cases on the measured pools, with no probabilistic bound; the correct-answer rate drops for a reworded query against an unchanged corpus (about 0.79, trap suppression still holding). A projection transfers only partly to other domains, which we report in full. The recipe, lenses, trap sets and evaluation harness are released under AGPL-3.0; the accelerator's implementation is a reserved commercial component, and every HN number is reproducible under exact search.

</details>


### [127] [Flagging Batch Shift in Flow Cytometry using Jeffreys Divergence between Gaussian Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31321)
*Jonathan Legrand,Aguirre Mimoun,Audrey Bidet,Pierre-Yves Dumas,Christ{è}le Etchegaray,Baudouin Denis de Senneville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种与模型无关的预测可靠性判据：以高斯混合模型表示每个患者样本，用蒙特卡洛估计其与训练数据参考分布的Jeffreys散度，据此识别不可靠预测并设置拒识阈值。在急性髓系白血病真实漂移队列上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床流式细胞术机器学习模型通常在训练分布上评估，但实际中采集条件与试剂可能变化，导致预测在无预警情况下失效，需要分布漂移下的可靠性判据。

Method: 将每个患者样本表示为高斯混合模型，以训练数据拟合参考GMM，通过蒙特卡洛积分估计二者的Jeffreys散度；基于该散度校准拒识阈值，并在留出样本上评估。实验对象为真实漂移队列的AML患者，分类任务为预测NPM1突变状态。

Result: 图形与统计显示成对散度能刻画采集队列结构；在漂移队列子集上校准阈值后于留出受试者评估，20次运行中被拒样本的误分类率约为保留样本的2.5倍。

Conclusion: 输入散度可帮助识别不可靠预测，是弃权框架的有力候选组件，可与质量控制措施及分类器置信度结合使用。

Abstract: Machine learning models for clinical flow cytometry are usually evaluated on data drawn from their training distribution. In routine practice, however, acquisition conditions and reagents may change, potentially making predictions unreliable without warning. We propose a model agnostic criterion for prediction reliability based on a notion of distance from the training distribution: each patient sample is represented by a Gaussian mixture model, and its Jeffreys divergence to a reference fitted on the training data is estimated using Monte Carlo integration. We illustrate our approach on real-life shifted cohorts of acute myeloid leukaemia patients, using a classifier trained to predict NPM1 mutation status. First, we show graphically and statistically that pairwise divergences capture acquisition cohort structure. We then calibrate a rejection threshold on a subset of shifted cohort samples and evaluate it on held-out subjects. Across 20 runs, the misclassification rate of rejected samples was 2.5 times larger than for retained samples. These results suggest that input divergence can help identify unreliable predictions, providing a strong candidate component for abstention frameworks, along with quality control measures and classifier confidence.

</details>


### [128] [Fusion Gains Depend on the Implementation and the Calibration Target: An 18-Task Study of Prediction Intervals with a Single-Source Fallback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31333)
*Liwei Deng,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较四种融合实现与匹配的单源学习器在18个回归任务上的表现，发现融合的增益大多来自额外学习阶段而非第二数据源，融合实现之间判断分歧严重，并提出以开发集选择器加单源回退、以及在留出数据上报告选择器失败频率的评估建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 融合模型同时引入"额外学习阶段"和"第二数据源"两种因素，既有文献常将二者混为一谈，导致无法判断增益来源；同时不同融合实现的结论是否一致、选择器平均增益背后的失败风险也缺乏系统评估。因此需要匹配单源候选进行受控比较。

Method: 在18个带预测区间的回归任务上，将四种融合实现与匹配的单源学习器对比；采用组内校准与逐点校准两种设置、各做十次重拟合；计算区间评分、相对评分、任务级一致性与相反结论计数；引入带匹配单源回退的开发集选择器（含梯度提升选择器），并在八项留出任务上按预先冻结的准则评估，同时统计选择器在任务级运行中输给单源的比例。

Result: 线性堆叠在组内校准下将区间评分相对无第二阶段的源模型改善6.0%，但相对匹配单源仅改善0.1%；四种实现在18个任务中仅3个一致，12个给出相反的明确结论（含10个自然异源任务中的8个）；逐点校准下一致5个、相反9个。带回退的选择器在两种校准、全部实现下使十次重拟合任务平均评分改善2.2%–6.3%；将融合臂加入单源候选池使平均相对评分提高约5个百分点。梯度提升选择器在组内校准下于八项留出任务中达到冻结准则；后期重拟合中，组内校准下选择器在4.4%–6.1%的任务级运行中劣于匹配单源，符号逐点校准下三种实现为19%–23%。

Conclusion: 融合的边际增益主要来自额外学习阶段，第二数据源贡献很小；不同融合实现结论高度不稳定。融合评估应在留出数据上比较匹配候选，并同时报告选择器的平均增益与劣于单源的频率。

Abstract: A fused model can benefit from an extra learning stage as well as from a second data source. We compare four fusion implementations with matched single-source learners on 18 regression tasks with prediction intervals. Across ten refits under group-wise calibration, linear stacking improved the interval score by 6.0% against a source model without a second stage and by 0.1% against its matched single source. The four implementations agreed on 3 of 18 tasks and gave opposite clear verdicts on 12, including 8 of the 10 tasks with naturally distinct sources. With pointwise calibration, they agreed on 5 tasks and gave opposite clear verdicts on 9. A development-set selector with a matched single-source fallback improved ten-refit task-averaged scores by 2.2--6.3% in every implementation under both calibrations. Adding fused arms to the pool of single-source candidates improved the mean relative score by about 5 percentage points. Under group-wise calibration, the gradient-boosted selector met the criterion frozen before evaluation on eight held-out tasks. In post-opening refits, selectors lost to the matched single source in 4.4--6.1% of task-level runs under group-wise calibration. Under signed pointwise calibration, three implementations lost in 19--23% of runs. Fusion evaluations should compare matched candidates on held-out data and report how often the selector loses alongside its average gain.

</details>


### [129] [See the Answer, Learn to Embed: Learning Universal Multimodal Embeddings from Hindsight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32439)
*Yoonki Cho,Youngju Na,Taegeun Yang,Jinhwan Seo,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEME 让模型在训练时借助"匹配候选"(答案)提供上下文学习更优的查询表示,再通过教师蒸馏获得推理时无需候选条件的独立嵌入器,提升多模态嵌入效果且推理更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理驱动的通用多模态嵌入(UME)方法通过中间推理生成额外上下文,但推理阶段增加额外计算开销。作者探索以匹配候选(配对目标)作为替代的上下文来源,即"训练时看见答案、推理时学以致嵌",但直接条件化会引入查询无法获得的信息或导致表示坍缩。

Method: 提出 Hindsight-Enhanced Multimodal Embeddings(HEME):(1)用 hindsight 注意力掩码、错配候选条件化、查询 token 对齐训练 hindsight 教师,抑制对候选的过度依赖;(2)冻结教师,将其 hindsight 表示与相似度分布蒸馏到独立的学生嵌入器。推理时学生无需候选条件或教师。

Result: 在 MMEB-V2 及 MMEB-V3 的 agent 与文本任务上实验验证了 HEME 的有效性,并表明其推理比显式推理驱动方法更高效。

Conclusion: 匹配候选可作为有效的训练期特权上下文,经 kNowledge 蒸馏后可获得无候选依赖的高效独立嵌入模型,兼顾性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Universal Multimodal Embedding (UME) models map heterogeneous multimodal inputs into a shared embedding space, where pairwise similarity supports diverse downstream tasks. Recent reasoning-driven UME approaches use intermediate reasoning to generate additional context for embedding, but often incur extra computation at inference. In this paper, we explore the matched candidate (i.e., the paired target) as an alternative source of context, asking whether a model can "see the answer" during training and "learn to embed" without it at inference. However, direct conditioning on this privileged context can introduce candidate-specific information unavailable from the query alone or cause the candidate-conditioned query representation to collapse toward the candidate embedding. To address this, we introduce Hindsight-Enhanced Multimodal Embeddings (HEME), which learns query-grounded hindsight representations from such candidate context. HEME first trains a hindsight teacher with a hindsight attention mask, mismatched-candidate conditioning, and query-token alignment to limit excessive reliance on the candidate. It then freezes the teacher and distills its hindsight representation and similarity distribution into a standalone student embedder. At inference, the student independently encodes queries and candidates without candidate conditioning or the hindsight teacher. Experiments on MMEB-V2 and the agent and text tasks of MMEB-V3 demonstrate the effectiveness of HEME and show that it enables more efficient inference than explicit reasoning-driven methods.

</details>


### [130] [GHC:Expanding Residual Interfaces in Hyper-Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31350)
*Tianyu Li,Hongtao Zhang,Dongchen Han,Jingshu Liu,Bojun Wang,Chen Jiang,Yulong Huang,Wei Chen,Tianyu He,Xiangyu Zhang,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Grouped Hyper-Connections（GHC），将每个分支按注意力头或FFN通道切分为并行组，每组读取不同的流混合并以学习权重写回各流，突破现有超连接的分支读写接口瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Hyper-Connections虽在并行流上扩展了残差容量，但每个分支的读/写接口过窄：读取的是单一的流混合，写回时把同一输出向量的缩放副本写入所有流，构成性能瓶颈。

Method: GHC将每个分支沿注意力头维度或FFN通道维度划分为若干并行组；每组获得独立的流混合作为输入，各组独立产生输出，再通过可学习权重聚合写入各条流。

Result: 在稠密模型与MoE模型上，GHC稳定优于Identity Hyper-Connections（IHC），参数与显存访问开销可忽略；训练效率较Pre-Norm提升35%、较IHC提升10%；扩展残差接口还缓解了增加流数目的收益递减，使更宽的残差状态带来进一步提升。

Conclusion: 拓宽残差接口是关键方向：GHC以近乎零额外开销显著提升训练效率，并解除流数目扩展的收益饱和限制，为残差连接设计提供了可扩展的改进路径。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections expand residual capacity across parallel streams, yet restrict every branch to narrow read and write interfaces: each branch still reads a single stream mixture and writes scaled copies of one output vector to all streams. To lift this bottleneck, we propose Grouped Hyper-Connections (GHC). By partitioning each branch into parallel groups along attention heads or FFN channels, GHC supplies each group with a distinct stream mixture and aggregates their separate outputs into each stream with learned weights. Extensive experiments across dense and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models show that GHC consistently improves upon Identity Hyper-Connections (IHC) with negligible parameter and memory-access overhead, increasing training efficiency by 35% over Pre-Norm and 10% over IHC. Moreover, expanding residual interfaces overcomes the diminishing returns of adding streams, allowing wider residual states to yield further gains.

</details>


### [131] [Detecting Harmful Short Videos in Toxic Diet Culture: A Governance-Ready Evidence Framework with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31354)
*Wenli Zhang,Shuang Geng,Jiaheng Xie,Zhonghan Xiang,Sudha Ram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPACE框架，在SAE特征空间中生成"可治理证据"（可追溯、可定位、政策锚定、可审查），用于短视频有害内容审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频内容治理中，算法审核研究多聚焦预测性能或有限解释，缺乏支持审核复核与决策辩护的"决策关联证据"，这一治理缺口未被充分研究。

Method: 以有毒饮食文化为案例，将治理就绪证据操作化为四个维度（计算可追溯性、证据定位、政策锚定、证据可审查性）；在大型多模态模型预训练SAE特征空间上构建SPACE框架：全局相关性门控+可分解线性证据头识别决策关联证据，片段监督与视频内排序实现定位，政策对齐映射至平台准则，帕累托高效选择得到紧凑的任务特定证据词表。

Result: 在3,711条标注TikTok视频上，SPACE在四类基准中取得最高有害类别Macro F1=0.8507；88.0%的有害测试视频中其最高分观测与真实有害片段重叠，87.0%的证据表示可检索到对应政策类别；利益相关者评估认为证据报告对理解、复核、记录与回应审核决策有用。

Conclusion: 研究为在预测型内容审核系统中嵌入治理就绪证据提供了计算设计科学知识。

Abstract: Algorithmic content moderation is increasingly important for governing short-form video content, yet harmful-content detection research focuses primarily on prediction or limited explanations. This leaves a governance challenge underexplored: producing decision-linked evidence that supports the review and justification of moderation decisions. Using toxic diet culture as an empirical case, we conceptualize governance-ready evidence as computational traceability, evidence localization, policy grounding, and evidence inspectability. We develop the Sparse Autoencoder-based Policy-Aligned Contrastive Evidence (SPACE) framework in a pretrained SAE feature space derived from a large multimodal model. A global relevance gate and decomposable linear evidence head identify decision-linked evidence; segment supervision and within-video ranking localize this evidence; policy alignment maps it to platform guidelines; and Pareto-efficient selection yields a compact, task-specific evidence vocabulary. On 3,711 labeled TikTok videos, SPACE achieves the highest harmful-category Macro F1 among benchmarks across four families, at 0.8507. Its highest-scoring observation overlaps a ground-truth harmful span for 88.0% of harmful test videos, while its evidence representation retrieves the corresponding policy category for 87.0%. Stakeholder evaluations indicate that the SPACE evidence report is perceived as useful for understanding, reviewing, documenting, and responding to moderation decisions. The study contributes computational design science knowledge for embedding governance-ready evidence within predictive content-moderation systems.

</details>


### [132] [CaLA: Causal Latent Adjustment for World Models under Adaptive Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31431)
*Robert Ness,Sofiane Ennadir,Oleg Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 世界模型用想象 rollout 回答 "what-if" 问题，但自适应策略采集的 rollout 存在处理—结果混杂；作者在三消游戏难度调整中构建完整 SCM，揭示未校正模型会选出提高流失的难度，并提出 DAG 约束的 Causal Latent Adjustment（CaLA）以改善因果干预估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 想象的 rollout 若来自自适应策略，会引入处理—结果混杂（潜变量玩家技能同时影响难度分配与流失），导致世界模型学到的策略在干预下失效甚至有害，因此需要区分预测拟合与因果校正。

Method: 在三消难度调整场景中构建完全指定的 SCM（含序列机制、自适应分配与配对真值干预）；提出 CaLA：以 DAG 结构约束世界模型，从预处理历史中推断变分玩家表征以校正混杂；并与未校正模型、残差校正等基线对比。

Result: 未校正模型拟合日志轨迹后选出的难度设置反而提高流失；CaLA 在群体层面的干预估计最可靠，个体决策具竞争力；但在个体层面，丰富的人工构造历史特征仍有优势；残差校正虽提升预测拟合，却降低因果决策质量。

Conclusion: 预测精度、混杂校正与下游因果估计器的行为需分开评估；结构约束的潜变量校正对自适应策略下的世界模型至关重要。

Abstract: World models is answering ``imagine" answers to ``what-if'' questions via imagined rollouts. But rollouts collected by adaptive policies induce treatment-outcome confounding in imagined rollouts. We study this problem in Match-3 difficulty adjustment, where latent player skill influences both assigned difficulty and churn. We build a fully specified Match-3 SCM with sequential mechanics, adaptive assignment, and paired ground-truth interventions, on which unadjusted models fit to the logged trajectories select difficulty settings that raise churn. We then introduce Causal Latent Adjustment (CaLA), a DAG-constrained world model that infers a variational player representation from pretreatment history. Across our evaluations, CaLA provides the most reliable population-level intervention estimates and competitive individualized decisions, although rich handcrafted histories can remain advantageous for individual-level adjustment. We further find that residual correction can degrade causal decision quality despite improving predictive fit, highlighting the need to distinguish predictive accuracy, confounding adjustment and the behavior of downstream causal estimators.

</details>


### [133] [Is 4 Bits Enough for EEG Sleep Staging? A Subject-Level Benchmark of MXFP4, NVFP4 and FP8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31436)
*Emese Batizi,Hossein Valavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统评测了从FP16到MXFP4/NVFP4等13种训练后量化(PTQ)配置在单通道Fpz-Cz脑电五期睡眠分期上的表现。结果显示8/16位格式几乎无损，4位下NVFP4优于MXFP4且对时间上下文网络损失较小；将首末层保持FP8可将4位格式误差控制在0.48pp内，无需重训练，且首层是最敏感层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备端做睡眠分期可保护原始脑电隐私并节省传输能耗，但需要小而高效的网络。量化是压缩模型的关键手段，然而缺乏对低比特(尤其是块缩放4位)格式在单通道脑电睡眠分期任务上的系统基准评测，以及层敏感性与无需重训练的补偿方案研究。

Method: 在Sleep-EDF-78(78名受试者)上做受试者-wise交叉验证，采用3个训练种子、值精确仿真和受试者配对统计，基准测试13种PTQ配置(FP16至MXFP4、NVFP4)。比较epoch-wise CNN与时间上下文网络，并进行同编码器消融以分离时间上下文贡献；分析各层量化敏感性，提出首末层保留FP8的混合精度配方，并与QAT比较。

Result: 所有8/16位格式与FP32 Macro-F1差距在0.45pp内；4位权重与激活下，epoch-wise CNN损失1.98–3.04pp，时间上下文网络最多损失1.19pp。同编码器消融表明时间上下文对差距解释有限。相同块大小时NVFP4优于MXFP4。首层(量化原始脑电)在两网络中最敏感。首末层保留FP8使两种4位格式均落在FP32的0.48pp内，权重存储最多增加2.1%，无需重训练，在探索性1pp裕度内等效于QAT。4位损失集中在相同受试者(架构间Spearman 0.69–0.79)，且最降低N3的召回率。嵌套Sleep-EDF-20子集上，NVFP4+FP8首末层仍保持0.25pp内；但MXFP4下架构排序反转，且FP8首末层不能完全恢复三个模型中的两个。

Conclusion: 在所采用的仿真中，NVFP4配合FP8首末层在两个队列上均几乎不损失精度且无需QAT，是可穿戴端单通道脑电睡眠分期的实用低比特量化方案；MXFP4结果对队列和架构更敏感，鲁棒性不足。

Abstract: Staging sleep on a wearable device keeps the raw electroencephalogram (EEG) private and saves the energy needed to transmit it. It also requires small, energy-efficient neural networks. We benchmark 13 post-training quantization (PTQ) configurations, from FP16 to the block-scaled 4-bit formats MXFP4 and NVFP4, for five-stage sleep staging from single-channel Fpz-Cz EEG. We use Sleep-EDF-78 (78 subjects), subject-wise cross-validation, three training seeds, value-exact emulation, and per-subject paired statistics. All 8- and 16-bit formats stay within 0.45 percentage points (pp) of FP32 Macro-F1. With 4-bit weights and activations, an epoch-wise CNN loses 1.98 to 3.04 pp, and a temporal-context network loses at most 1.19 pp. A same-encoder ablation shows that temporal context explains little of this gap. At equal block size, NVFP4 outperforms MXFP4. The first layer, which quantizes the raw EEG, is the most sensitive layer in both networks. Keeping the first and last layers in FP8 brings both tested 4-bit formats within 0.48 pp of FP32. This recipe adds at most 2.1% weight storage, needs no retraining, and is equivalent to quantization-aware training (QAT) within an exploratory 1-pp margin. The 4-bit losses fall on the same subjects across architectures (Spearman correlation coefficient = 0.69 to 0.79), and they reduce N3 recall the most. On the nested Sleep-EDF-20 subset, NVFP4 with FP8 first and last layers again stays within 0.25 pp of FP32. Under MXFP4, however, the ranking of the architectures reverses, and FP8 first and last layers do not fully recover two of the three tested models. In our emulation, NVFP4 with FP8 first and last layers thus loses little accuracy without QAT in both cohorts.

</details>


### [134] [Transformers with Signatures: Towards Universal In-Context Learning in Path Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31439)
*Ziheng Cheng,Xin Guo,Ben Hambly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出基于路径签名特征的路径空间上下文学习(ICL)框架，显式构造两层多头因果softmax transformer以在上下文中模拟Lasso，并给出有限样本预测误差界，突破IID假设，实验验证其高效性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL理论保证有限，尤其当目标响应非线性依赖于观测路径演化时几乎空白；同时现有ICL理论多假设IID数据，无法刻画演示与查询之间的依赖关系。

Method: 利用路径签名的普适线性逼近性质，将路径特征映射到线性空间，显式构造每层两个注意力头的因果softmax transformer，使其在上下文中实现Lasso式稀疏回归；在稀疏签名逼近、矩条件与集中不等式下推导有限样本预测误差界，且允许演示与查询间存在依赖。

Result: 在上下文稀疏恢复、非线性路径回归、执行成本预测与金融收益推断四类任务上，签名增强的transformer对观测网格变化鲁棒，能适应未见市场环境，并在参数量显著更少的情况下匹配或超越原版transformer基线。

Conclusion: 签名特征为路径依赖型ICL提供了可解释的理论框架与高效架构设计，兼顾统计保证与参数效率，拓展了ICL理论至非IID路径数据场景。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables transformers to adapt to new tasks from demonstrations without updating their parameters. However, theoretical guarantees for transformers remain limited in general, and elusive in particular when target responses depend nonlinearly on the evolution of observed paths. We establish an ICL framework for path space, using path signature features. Building on the universal linear approximation property of signatures, we construct explicitly a causal softmax transformer with two attention heads per layer that emulates in-context Lasso. Under sparse signature approximation and suitable moment and concentration conditions, we establish finite-sample prediction error bounds. Our analysis goes beyond the IID data setting in the existing ICL theory, allowing dependence among demonstrations and the query.
  Experiments on in-context sparse recovery, nonlinear path regression, execution-cost prediction, and financial payoff inference demonstrate robustness to changes in observation grids and adaptation to unseen market environments. Across these prediction tasks, transformers with signatures match or outperform vanilla transformer baselines with substantially fewer trainable parameters, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of signature-based ICL.

</details>


### [135] [Transferable, Time-Parallel Graph Dynamics via Space-Time Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31479)
*Wencong Yang,Chaopeng Shen,Abdolmehdi Behroozi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"时空因子化"（space-time factorization）结构：将模型拆为与图无关的局部时间算子与无图相关学习参数的空间混合器，使同一套参数可在未见图上零样本迁移，并非循环地时间并行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间并行的神经算子（FNO及其后继）在规则网格上取得成功，网格型算子（MeshGraphNets、Transolver）将算子学习扩展到不规则几何，但难以在多样拓扑上联合学习并同时保留非循环的时间评估方式。

Method: 将模型因子化为局部时间算子（可学习、与图无关）和空间混合器（跨节点传播信息但不含图相关的可学习参数）；该结构条件使相同参数可运行于未见图，非循环实例支持时间并行求值。零样本迁移的准确性需控制三类信息泄漏（图相关权重、图特定特征、图相关的训练分布），并需合适的任务物理与训练覆盖；协议适用于固定谱与学习注意力两类空间混合器。
