Result: 在三个领域验证：河网洪水波传播中，27K参数的模型在长江上训练、零样本迁移到密西西比河，边界条件运营评估下NSE中位数0.998，训练比稳定化循环基线快5.5倍；跨几何Navier-Stokes上，SepFNO三随机种子在五个未见目标上的平均相对涡量误差随训练几何数从5增至12下降约28%，SepTransolver单次运行趋势一致；跨城市交通预测中，零样本迁移达到目标城市上oracle模型的12.9%以内。跨几何误差随训练几何数量及其与测试的相关性增加而下降，下降速率取决于空间混合器类型。

Conclusion: 该时空因子化原则为不规则拓扑上的图动力学开辟了池化数据、基础模型式（foundation-style）建模的路径。

Abstract: Time-parallel neural operators (FNO and successors) have transformed PDE surrogates on regular grids. Mesh-based operators (MeshGraphNets, Transolver) extend operator learning to irregular geometries, but jointly learning across diverse topologies while retaining non-recurrent temporal evaluation remains challenging. We propose space-time factorization: partition the model into local temporal operators (learned, graph-independent) and a spatial mixer that propagates information across nodes without graph-dependent learned parameters. This structural condition lets the same parameters run on unseen graphs; non-recurrent instances also admit time-parallel evaluation. Accurate zero-shot transfer further depends on controlling three information leaks (graph-dependent weights, graph-specific features, and graph-correlated training distributions) and on suitable task physics and training coverage. The protocol applies to two distinct spatial-mixer families, fixed-spectral and learned-attention. We validate the principle in three domains. For floodwave propagation in river networks, a 27K-parameter model trained on the Yangtze transfers zero-shot to the Mississippi with median NSE 0.998 in boundary-conditioned operational evaluation, training 5.5x faster than a stabilized recurrent baseline. On cross-geometry Navier-Stokes, the three-seed SepFNO mean relative vorticity error on five unseen targets falls by about 28% from 5 to 12 training geometries, with a consistent single-run SepTransolver trend. On cross-city traffic forecasting, zero-shot transfer reaches within 12.9% of an oracle trained on the target city. Across settings, cross-geometry error decreases with both the number of training geometries and their relatedness to the test, at a rate depending on the spatial mixer. This opens the path to pooled-data, foundation-style models for graph dynamics on irregular topologies.

</details>


### [136] [SwiftLoop: Selective Refinement for Adaptive Implicit Reasoning in Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31481)
*Tianyi Zhao,Ke Wan,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SwiftLoop：面向回环Transformer推理期的“逐位置冻结”选择性循环精修框架，实现循环深度与活跃宽度的自适应计算分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有回环（looped）推理器采用固定深度循环，每轮迭代更新全部潜变量位置，在无需进一步精修时产生冗余计算，缺乏按需分配算力的能力。

Method: 提出推理期框架SwiftLoop，通过位置级冻结实现选择性循环精修。用后见之明式（hindsight）监督学习轻量冻结策略，监督信号来自两类互补准则：内部表示层面的K/V可复用性与输出层面的功能影响。推理时依据观测到的隐状态自适应决定冻结。

Result: 大量实验表明，SwiftLoop在准确率—计算量权衡上优于固定循环推理与自适应基线，并实现实测推理加速；此外还验证了其在一般回环模型循环式prompt预填充场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 逐位置选择性冻结可有效消除固定深度循环的冗余更新，将自适应计算扩展到循环深度与活跃宽度两个维度，并可推广至隐式推理之外的回环模型应用。

Abstract: Looped Transformers advance implicit chain-of-thought reasoning through recurrent latent refinement. However, existing looped reasoners typically employ fixed-depth recurrence, updating all latent positions at every iteration and incurring redundancy when further refinement is unnecessary. We introduce \textsc{SwiftLoop}, an inference-time framework for selective recurrent refinement through position-wise freezing, enabling adaptive computation allocation across both recurrent depth and active width. SwiftLoop learns lightweight freezing policies through hindsight-guided supervision derived from two complementary criteria: K/V reusability at the internal representation level and functional impact at the output level. At inference time, these policies guide adaptive freezing based on observed hidden states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SwiftLoop achieves favorable accuracy--computation trade-offs over fixed-loop inference and adaptive baselines, with measured inference acceleration. Beyond implicit reasoning, we further demonstrate its applicability to recurrent prompt prefilling in general looped models.

</details>


### [137] [CHORD: Learning Latent Spaces for Constrained Stellarator Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31486)
*Santiago A. Cadena,Andrea Merlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CHORD——一种条件变分自编码器，通过在训练中惩罚解码潜插值路径上的约束违反，并配合自洽推断对齐条件目标与解码设计的预测指标，实现约束感知的潜空间设计探索；在仿星器几何插值中达到最高81%的模拟器验证可行率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维PDE约束设计（如仿星器）中，在已知可行解间插值可揭示设计权衡，避免重复昂贵优化。但直接插值边界参数即便两端可行也可能违反物理约束；学习式潜表征虽能捕捉非线性关系，潜插值仍可能穿越不可行区域，且以目标指标为条件时可能与解码设计不一致。

Method: CHORD为条件VAE，训练时对解码后潜插值路径上的约束违反量施加惩罚正则；推理阶段采用自洽推断，使条件向量与解码设计所预测的指标对齐。结合线性条件化、数据复用（5次优化活动及模拟器标注的重新缩放）填充Pareto前沿，并用于潜空间优化与自洽采样。通过几何基准与仿星器跨几何族插值验证，并做消融。

Result: 几何基准显示潜插值正则化有效。跨几何族仿星器插值中，CHORD中段可行性达81%（模拟器验证），无正则条件VAE为50%，边界参数直接插值为28%；消融表明两个组件缺一不可。潜空间优化可找到模拟器可行设计，自洽采样产生更多样可行方案。ConStellaration基准上，经低保真筛选后1.2k生成候选的93%通过高保真可行性测试。开源代码及60万+设计。

Conclusion: 结果表明可行性感知的潜空间学习能有效探索受限设计权衡，为高维PDE约束设计提供可复用的数据与生成框架。

Abstract: In high-dimensional, PDE-constrained design, interpolating between known feasible solutions can reveal trade-offs without repeating expensive optimization campaigns. In stellarator design for fusion energy, direct interpolation of plasma boundary parameters can violate physical constraints even when both endpoints are feasible. Learned latent representations can capture nonlinear relationships among design variables, but latent interpolation can still cross infeasible regions, and conditioning on target metrics can become inconsistent with the decoded design. We introduce CHORD, a conditional variational autoencoder that penalizes predicted constraint violations along decoded latent interpolants during training, paired with self-consistent inference that aligns conditioning with the decoded design's predicted metrics. Geometric benchmarks show the benefit of regularizing latent interpolants. In stellarator interpolation between distinct geometry families, CHORD reaches up to 81% simulator-validated mid-segment feasibility, compared with 50% for an unregularized conditional VAE and 28% for direct interpolation of boundary parameters. Ablations show that both ingredients are needed for this gain. Beyond interpolation, latent-space optimization finds simulator-feasible designs, while self-consistent sampling produces more diverse feasible alternatives. On the ConStellaration benchmark, chord-regularized models with linear conditioning reuse data from five optimization campaigns and simulator-labeled rescalings to populate the Pareto front more densely. After low-fidelity screening, 93% of 1.2k generated candidates pass the benchmark's high-fidelity feasibility test. We release code and over 600k designs for stellarator generation, surrogate training, and front-filling evaluation. These results support feasibility-aware latent learning for exploring constrained design trade-offs.

</details>


### [138] [Trust-Region Optimization for Performative Prediction without Response Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31492)
*Siqi Du,Miaolan Xie,Roy Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Performative Trust-Region Optimization (PTRO)，在不建模或拟合种群响应的情况下优化performative风险，同时利用已知损失函数；在矩有界损失与光滑性、估计精度条件下，以O(ε^{-2})迭代和每轮O(d^3 ε^{-4})样本高概率收敛到ε-稳定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有performative prediction方法要么对种群响应施加结构假设（当固定模型类难以刻画响应时失效），要么采用通用黑箱优化而无法利用已知损失函数信息。

Method: PTRO从当前种群采样，在邻近决策处评估已知损失，并仅用额外部署估计种群响应的效应，从而在不指定响应模型的前提下得到performative风险的局部近似；再用信赖域机制决定并自适应调整该局部信息的可信范围。

Result: 在矩有界损失及适当光滑性与估计精度条件下，证明以高概率在O(ε^{-2})次迭代内收敛到performative风险的ε-稳定点，每轮样本复杂度为O(d^3 ε^{-4})（d为决策维度）。实验显示：结构假设准确时基于响应模型的方法样本效率更高，而固定模型类难以刻画响应时PTRO仍有效；消融表明利用已知损失与自适应信赖域更新对精度和效率均重要。

Conclusion: PTRO在"结构化响应建模"与"黑箱优化"之间提供了折中方案：不牺牲对已知损失的利用，也无需承诺响应模型形式，并在理论上保证收敛、在实践中对响应失配具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Performative prediction studies learning problems in which deployed decisions change the distribution of future data. Existing methods often impose structure on the population response, which limits their applicability when a fixed model class poorly captures that response. Generic black-box optimization avoids response modeling but does not exploit the known loss function.
  We introduce Performative Trust-Region Optimization (PTRO), which bridges these two approaches by optimizing performative risk without specifying or fitting a response model while still exploiting the known loss. The key idea is sampling from the current population to evaluate the known loss at nearby decisions, and use additional deployments only to estimate the effect of population response. This gives a local approximation of performative risk without committing to a model of how the population responds. PTRO then uses a trust-region mechanism to determine and adapt how far this local information can be trusted.
  Under moment-bounded losses and suitable smoothness and estimator-accuracy conditions, we establish high-probability convergence to an $ε$-stationary point of the performative risk in $O(ε^{-2})$ iterations, with $O(d^{3}ε^{-4})$ samples per iteration, where $d$ is the decision dimension and $q\geq2$ is the loss-moment order.
  Experiments illustrate methods built around a specified response model can be more sample-efficient when their structural assumptions are accurate, whereas PTRO remains effective as the population response is poorly captured by a fixed model class. Ablations further show that both exploiting the known loss and adaptive trust-region updates are important for controlling the accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [139] [Near-Zero Forgetting Is Not Enough: Out-of-Distribution Robustness in Continual Learning of Pretrained VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31511)
*Adrià Ruiz,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Tinne Tuytelaars,Sergi Masip*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有工作显示预训练VLA模型在经验回放式持续学习中近零遗忘，但本研究揭示其在域偏移下的鲁棒性会严重崩坏：ID场景近零灾难性遗忘，而OOD性能快速崩溃，并伴随集中于多模态与动作特征的表示漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅验证了特定持续学习设定下VLA模型（如Pi0+经验回放）在域内（ID）的近零遗忘，却未考察所学任务对域偏移（domain shift）的鲁棒性，因此需要系统评估OOD条件下的持续学习表现及其表示层面的原因。

Method: 在LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus基准上，对使用经验回放微调的Pi0进行ID与OOD评估；通过表示分析与线性探针（linear probe）考察持续学习过程中的特征漂移与任务信息可读性。

Result: ID设定下接近零灾难性遗忘，但先前任务的OOD性能迅速崩溃；OOD能力在持续学习过程中持续退化，且跨域偏移呈现共享的表示模式，并在两个较简单套件的行为层面亦可见；表示漂移集中于多模态特征与动作特征；线性探针显示OOD特征仍保留任务相关信息，但表现为"不可读"，与表示错位（misalignment）一致。

Conclusion: 即使ID稳定性保持完好，严重OOD遗忘仍会发生，并伴随持续学习中的表示漂移；因此仅以ID遗忘率评估持续学习不足，需关注OOD鲁棒性与特征对齐。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit near-zero forgetting in specific continual learning settings with experience replay. However, robustness to domain shift of learned tasks remains unexplored. In this work, we study the robustness of Pi0 fine-tuned with experience replay under out-of-distribution (OOD) data using the LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. While the in-domain (ID) setup achieves near-zero catastrophic forgetting, OOD performance on previous tasks quickly collapses. Specifically, we show that during the continual learning process, the OOD capabilities of the model degrade, following a shared representational pattern across domain shifts, which is also visible behaviorally in the two simpler suites. Representational analysis shows that drift concentrates in the multimodal and action features. Linear probes indicate that OOD features still retain task-relevant information, but appear unreadable, consistent with representation misalignment. These findings suggest that even when ID stability remains intact, severe OOD forgetting can still occur, accompanied by representational drift during continual learning.

</details>


### [140] [How to build and train your recurrent-depth model: A fixed-point perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31513)
*Thomas Pethick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明循环深度模型中的输入注入等价于Halpern迭代，在步长缓慢消失时可仅用对数多项式（polylog）深度的反向传播窗口保证收敛，并据此提出分组参数约束的优化器Sparrow，可作为Muon/MuonH的即插即用替代且无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环深度模型依赖截断反向传播训练，但其有效性缺乏理论解释；同时恒定/逐渐消失步长的收敛性此前缺乏明确保证，且Lipschitz算子条件在SwiGLU等实际结构上如何转化为参数约束尚不清晰。

Method: 1）揭示输入注入（input injection）与Halpern迭代的精确等价关系；2）证明在步长缓慢消失条件下，收敛所需的回传窗口为深度的polylog级别；3）推导Lipschitz算子约束在SwiGLU上对应行范数与谱范数的双重权重约束，而在门控机制上仅需行范数约束；4）按参数组分别施加相应约束，设计优化器Sparrow。

Result: 给出截断反向传播可行性的理论刻画；Sparrow在无需额外计算开销的前提下加速训练，效果不局限于循环模型，亦可推广至一般模型。

Conclusion: 输入注入即Halpern迭代为截断反向传播提供了收敛性解释；基于结构感知范数约束的分组优化器Sparrow可作为Muon/MuonH的直接替代并带来训练加速。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth models are trained with truncated backpropagation, yet it remains unclear why this works. We show that input injection, commonly used in recurrent-depth models, is exactly Halpern iteration, and that a slowly vanishing stepsize provably converges with a backpropagation window polylogarithmic in the depth. The guarantees require a Lipschitz operator, which for SwiGLU leads to a weight constraint in both row and spectral norm, while only a row norm constraint for gates. Based on these constraints, we develop an optimizer tailored to each parameter group, Sparrow, which provides a drop-in replacement of Muon/MuonH with no overhead, that speeds up training even beyond recurrent models.

</details>


### [141] [SOTS: Signature-based Optimal Transport Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31525)
*Khalid Akhmouch,Eric Jauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOTS: 基于路径签名与Sliced Wasserstein距离的无监督核心集选择框架，用于不规则多变量时间序列分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中专家标注成本高，训练数据选择成为瓶颈；现有时序核心集方法要么忽略时间结构，要么无法处理变长、不规则采样、多通道信号（工业监测与测试中最常见的设定）。

Method: 将每个样本映射为截断路径签名（truncated path signature），刻画多通道的联合几何结构；通过贪心最小化候选子集与全量数据经验签名分布之间的Sliced Wasserstein距离来选取核心集，并对随机核心集给出收敛性保证。

Result: 在真实且高度不平衡的工业I&C测试数据集上，SOTS-Greedy仅用30%训练数据即达到全量数据精度的95%以上，在多数预算下优于标准核心集基线；该优势在公开UEA规则多变量时序基准上同样成立，但仅限于类别判别信息纯粹为几何性质的场景。

Conclusion: 路径签名+Sliced Wasserstein是面向IMTS的有效无监督核心集选择准则，具备理论收敛保证与工业实用性，其适用性取决于判别信息是否以几何形式体现。

Abstract: Training data selection for machine learning models is a critical bottleneck in industrial applications where expert annotation is costly and time-consuming. Existing coreset selection methods for time series either ignore temporal structure entirely or cannot handle variable-length, irregularly sampled, multi-channel signals, the setting most common in industrial monitoring and testing. We introduce SOTS (Signature-based Optimal Transport Selection), an unsupervised coreset selection framework for irregular multivariate time series (IMTS) classification. Each sample is mapped to a truncated path signature, capturing the joint geometric structure of its channels, and the coreset is selected by greedily minimizing the Sliced Wasserstein distance between the empirical signature distributions of the candidate subset and the full dataset, a criterion for which we establish a convergence guarantee for random coresets. We validate SOTS on a real-world, highly imbalanced industrial I&C testing dataset, where SOTS-Greedy exceeds 95% of full-dataset accuracy using only 30% of the training data, outperforming standard coreset baselines at most budgets. This advantage generalizes to public UEA regular multivariate time series benchmarks specifically when class-discriminative information is purely geometric.

</details>


### [142] [Diagnosing Epistemic Uncertainty in Neural Operators: Effects of Initialization, Discretization, and Function Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31526)
*Tian Yu Yen,Yucheng Fu,Pratanu Roy,Guang Lin,Michael Penwarden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了神经算子中认知不确定性的来源（训练函数空间有限采样、输出离散化、模型初始化），训练约46,000个模型并构建集成，以相对L2误差的分布刻画不确定性，揭示其显著的架构依赖性及对基准评测的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子的不确定性量化方法往往只给出预测不确定性总量，而不区分各类认知不确定性来源的贡献。即便底层物理问题确定，训练函数空间有限采样、输出离散化与模型初始化也会导致学习到的算子之间出现显著差异，因此需要系统分离并量化这些来源。

Method: 在多种神经算子架构上，针对训练数据集、模型初始化与输出离散化三个维度构建集成，共训练约46,000个神经算子模型，刻画相对L2误差的分布而非单一训练实现的表现，并进行架构间比较与排序稳定性分析。

Result: 认知不确定性存在显著的架构依赖差异，模型初始化通常占相对L2误差方差的最大比例；随着输出采样数量与空间分布变化，架构的性能排序可能发生反转，稀疏或随机采样下的最优架构与高分辨率或结构化离散化下不同；能可靠刻画整体算子误差的集成，对单个测试函数的误差分位数估计可能不可靠。

Conclusion: 评估神经算子可靠性时必须区分认知不确定性的不同来源，并据此建立稳健的基准评测实践；单一训练模型的性能或聚合层面的集成统计不足以表征算子可靠性。

Abstract: Neural operators provide efficient surrogates for mappings between function spaces, but their reliability cannot be characterized by the performance of a single trained model. Existing neural operator uncertainty quantification approaches often characterize predictive uncertainty without separately identifying the contributions of individual sources of epistemic uncertainty. Finite sampling of the training function space, output discretization, and model initialization can produce substantial variation among learned operators even when the underlying physical problem is deterministic. We systematically investigate these sources of epistemic uncertainty across a diverse set of operator learning architectures. Across our experiments, we train approximately 46,000 neural operator models and construct ensembles over training datasets, model initializations, and output discretizations to characterize distributions of relative $L_2$ error rather than individual trained realizations. We find substantial architecture-dependent differences in epistemic uncertainty, with model initialization often accounting for the largest proportion of variance in relative $L_2$ error. The ranked performance of neural operator architectures can invert as the number and spatial distribution of output samples change, with the best architectures under sparse or randomly sampled observations differing from those at higher-resolution or structured discretizations. Furthermore, ensembles that reliably characterize aggregate operator error can provide unreliable estimates of error quantiles for individual test functions. These results highlight the importance of distinguishing the sources of epistemic uncertainty when evaluating neural operator reliability and establishing robust benchmarking practices.

</details>


### [143] [ECLIPSE: Embedding-Centered Foundation-Model Federated Knowledge Transfer for Data-Free Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31527)
*S. Kawa Atapour,S. Jamal Seyedmohammadi,Maryam Hosseini,S. Mohammad Sheikholeslami,Jamshid Abouei,Konstantinos Plataniotis,Arash Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免数据边缘智能框架 ECLIPSE，以嵌入为中心借助服务器端基础模型实现联邦知识迁移，无需共享原始数据、本地模型或公共参考数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型具备强表征与泛化能力，适合融入智能联邦边缘网络，但在资源受限、隐私敏感的边缘环境中部署困难；尤其在 data-free 设定下缺乏可共享/公共/服务器可访问的参考数据集，难以建立对齐分布式知识的公共参考空间。

Method: ECLIPSE 建立共享的嵌入参考空间，边缘设备将本地任务知识编码为嵌入并仅上传嵌入至服务器进行对齐与聚合；服务器基于聚合嵌入，通过嵌入引导的生成过程构造任务感知的替代数据集（surrogate dataset），用其将服务器端 FM 适配至下游任务，再将适配后的 FM 蒸馏为轻量全局模型分发至边缘设备。

Result: 在多种分类数据集上，ECLIPSE 优于传统 FL 与 data-free FKT 基线，并与基于数据的（database-based）方法性能相当；在不同数据分布与本地架构下对统计异构性和模型异构性保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: ECLIPSE 表明在无公共参考数据条件下，利用嵌入空间对齐与生成式替代数据可实现基础模型与边缘端联邦学习的有效结合，使边缘设备同时受益于分布式任务知识和 FM 泛化能力，并兼顾隐私与资源约束。

Abstract: Foundation Models (FMs) are envisioned to become an integral component of intelligent federated edge networks because of their strong representation and generalization capabilities. However, their integration into resource-constrained and privacy-sensitive edge environments remains challenging. This challenge is particularly pronounced in data-free settings, where no shared, public, or server-accessible reference dataset is available to establish a common reference space for aligning distributed knowledge. To address this challenge, we propose ECLIPSE, which is an embedding-centered FM-based federated knowledge transfer framework developed for data-free edge intelligence. ECLIPSE enables edge intelligence capitalizing on a server-hosted FM without sharing raw data, local models, or a public reference dataset. It establishes a shared embedding reference space through which edge devices encode their local task knowledge. Only the resulting embeddings are transmitted to the server for alignment and aggregation. Using the aggregated embeddings, the server constructs a task-aware surrogate dataset through an embedding-guided generative process. This surrogate dataset is then used to adapt the server-hosted FM to the downstream task. The adapted FM is subsequently distilled into a lightweight global model and distributed to the edge devices. This process allows edge devices to benefit from both distributed task knowledge and the generalization capabilities of the FM. Experimental results across diverse classification datasets show that ECLIPSE outperforms conventional Federated Learning (FL) and data-free Federated Knowledge-Transfer (FKT) baselines. It also achieves competitive performance compared with databased approaches. Furthermore, the framework remains robust under statistical and model heterogeneity across different data distributions and local architectures.

</details>


### [144] [JevBench: Metamorphic Coherence Testing for Typed Probabilistic Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31533)
*Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JevBench：无金标注的蜕变测试基准（50条概率/选择定律、5维度、12000测试、240案例），评估21个开源模型概率决策的一致性（56.5–83.9），发现可加性/互补性普遍失效，且均匀参考模型（89.4）竟超过所有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型化概率决策模型输出"是/否"、选项集合或有序量表上的分布，但其概率是否在改写、否定、组合、更换选项或改变批次时保持概率论/选择论一致性尚不清楚；且无金标注难以评估，准确率本身不足以刻画决策质量。

Method: 构建JevBench：固定输入、仅变换问题或批次的蜕变测试，用50条蜕变关系各检验一条概率或选择定律，覆盖表示、批次独立性、概率测度、逻辑顺序、选择集条件五维度；含JevBench-240（12个决策域、240合成案例、12000测试）与JevBench-mini（1200测试固定子集）。对每个模型施加预先固定的同一请求集，无需金标注即计算一致性得分；另设忽略输入的均匀参考模型作为基线。

Result: JevBench-mini与全量排名高度一致（Spearman 0.983）。21个开源模型一致性得分56.5–83.9；批次独立性多数模型满足；可加性广泛失效，尤其互补（P(A)+P(¬A)=1）违例；均匀参考模型在随机准确率下得89.4，高于所有被测模型的一致性分；解码器准确率82.4%–89.2%，一致性64.2–83.9，二者不重合。

Conclusion: 准确率与一致性度量互补，须并用方能评估概率决策模型；JevBench以开源Python包发布，提供无金标注、可复现的标准化一致性评测。

Abstract: Typed probabilistic decision models return distributions over specified answers, such as yes or no, a set of options or levels on an ordered scale. We ask whether their probabilities remain coherent when questions about the same input are reworded, negated, combined, given different options or placed in different batches. JevBench tests this coherence without gold labels. Its 50 metamorphic relations each test one law of probability or choice across five dimensions: representation, batch independence, probability measure, logical order and choice-set conditioning. Each test changes the question or batch while keeping the input fixed. Every model receives the same requests, fixed in advance. JevBench-240 contains 12,000 tests across 240 synthetic cases in 12 decision domains. A fixed subset of 1,200 tests, JevBench-mini, closely reproduces the full-suite ranking (Spearman 0.983). Across 21 openly released models, coherence scores range from 56.5 to 83.9. Batch independence holds for most models. Additivity fails broadly, particularly for complements, where the probabilities of a proposition and its negation should sum to one. A uniform reference model ignores the input and scores 89.4 at chance accuracy, exceeding every evaluated model's coherence score. Among decoders, accuracy ranges from 82.4% to 89.2%, while coherence ranges from 64.2 to 83.9. These results show why both measures are needed to assess decision models. JevBench is available as an open-source Python package.

</details>


### [145] [Denoising Diffusion with Progressive Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31558)
*Zeyu Luo,Shuai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出渐进后验采样(PPS)与流形投影，自动估计测量噪声方差且无需SVD，在FFHQ/ImageNet四类复原任务上取得有竞争力的重建精度与感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型后验采样方法依赖测量算子的SVD或需已知噪声方差；基于局部梯度的引导只能提供有限的后验几何信息。

Method: 在反向扩散过程中以闭式构造后验分布的高斯近似，将局部先验采样的样本引导至该近似后验均值；随后把引导后的样本投影到近似局部含噪图像流形的球面水平集上，以保持与扩散先验局部几何的一致性；同时自动估计噪声方差，并避免SVD。

Result: 在FFHQ与ImageNet上，跨四种复原任务、三种测量噪声水平，PPS在重建精度与感知质量上均具竞争力。

Conclusion: PPS为含噪线性逆问题提供了一种无需SVD、无需已知噪声方差的扩散后验采样方案，通过闭式高斯后验近似加流形投影提升了后验几何建模能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide effective generative priors for solving noisy linear inverse problems. However, existing posterior sampling methods may require singular value decomposition (SVD) of the measurement operator or prior knowledge of the measurement noise variance, while guidance based on local gradients provides only a limited view of posterior geometry. We propose progressive posterior sampling (PPS) with manifold projection, which automatically estimates the measurement noise variance and avoids SVD. During reverse diffusion, PPS constructs a Gaussian approximation to the posterior distribution in closed form and guides a sample drawn from the local prior toward the approximate posterior mean. We then project the guided sample onto a spherical level set that approximates the local noisy image manifold, promoting consistency with the local geometry of the diffusion prior. Experiments on FFHQ and ImageNet across four restoration tasks and three measurement noise levels demonstrate competitive reconstruction accuracy and perceptual quality by PPS.

</details>


### [146] [Latent Channel Completion for Forecasting in Sensor Networks with Failed Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31575)
*Mehran Shabanpour,Sadaf Khademi,Maryam Hosseini,Haiyan Xu,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis,Arash Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Channel Completion（LCC），在通道轴上做联合嵌入预测，重建缺失传感器的潜表示而非数值，使耦合预测器在传感器掉线（"变暗"）时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通与公用设施监测网络中的传感器会经常性失效，而跨传感器耦合的预测模型也继承了这种脆弱性：缺失值被当作真实读数嵌入，并通过共享表示传播，损害仍健康传感器的预测。此外，现有模型均在"所有传感器都上报"的基准上选型，这种暴露风险在选择指标中完全不可见。

Method: LCC 是一个通道轴上的联合嵌入预测目标：训练时遮盖部分通道，由存活通道预测其表示，并对目标施加 stop-gradient；重建的是潜表示而非原始读数，因为耦合算子只消费嵌入、从不接触原始值。推理时由该算子填入监测系统报告为掉线的传感器，参数与预测器共享。与基于同一存活传感器的跨通道岭回归插补进行对比。

Result: 在三个高速公路检测器网络上，各网络半数传感器掉线后，耦合带来的精度优势变化为 −1.0、−0.5 和 +8.8 个百分点；而未修改的预测器损失达 10.2 至 50.3 个百分点，且在其中一个网络上完全落后于非耦合基线。相比跨通道岭插补，潜空间补全将误差进一步降低 13.9% 与 28.6%；在完整输入下，该方法与骨干模型持平。

Conclusion: 潜空间补全能有效抑制传感器掉线造成的精度退化，同时不损伤完整输入下的表现。其收益大小与网络在完好状态下从耦合中获益的程度相关，因而为运维者提供了一个可在任何故障发生前评估的判据。

Abstract: Monitoring networks for traffic and utility infrastructure lose sensors routinely, and forecasting models that couple sensors together to gain accuracy also inherit an exposure to those losses. When a detector stops reporting, the substituted value is embedded as though it were a reading and propagates through the shared representation that couples the network, degrading forecasts for sensors that are still healthy. Because these models are selected on benchmarks where every sensor reports, the exposure is absent from the numbers used to choose them. We introduce Latent Channel Completion, a channel-axis joint embedding predictive objective that reconstructs missing sensors' latent representations rather than their values, since a coupling operator consumes embeddings and never sees raw readings. Channels are masked during training and their representations predicted from surviving channels against stop-gradient targets; at inference the operator fills in whichever sensors the monitoring system reports as dark, using parameters shared with the forecaster. On three highway detector networks, after half of each network goes dark, the accuracy advantage of coupling changes by -1.0, -0.5 and +8.8 percentage points, against losses of 10.2 to 50.3 points for the unmodified forecaster, which on one network falls behind an uncoupled reference entirely. Against cross-channel ridge imputation drawing on the same surviving sensors, completing the latent lowers error by a further 13.9% and 28.6%, and on complete inputs, the method matches its backbone. The benefit tracks how much a network gains from coupling when intact, giving operators a criterion evaluable before any outage occurs.

</details>


### [147] [LatentLens: Rethinking Latent Interface in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31582)
*Ziqing Yang,Kewei Cheng,Mingjie Li,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LatentLens 框架，分两层解耦潜在接口，发现潜在通信本身并不必然提升多智能体系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作报告潜在（latent）通信带来性能与效率收益，但未将潜在通信与智能体外壳（agent harness）分离，也未剖析其内部组件，因此其真实贡献并不清楚。

Method: 提出 LatentLens 框架，在两个层面解耦潜在接口：外部层面将其与 agent harness 解耦，以度量其对整体性能的贡献（RQ1）；内部层面拆分为重对齐兼容性、信息可访问性与传播动态三部分，以识别各自收益与局限（RQ2）。在 Qwen3 模型上评测七个基准。

Result: 在同一 harness 中引入潜在接口并未带来提升，甚至可能显著降低准确率；内部来看，潜在表示保留了一定领域信息，但不兼容的重对齐会显著损害性能，且重复的潜在步骤会放大表示层面的失效。

Conclusion: 潜在表示虽可能保留信息，但并不必然保证有效通信；有效通信还依赖兼容的重对齐、信息可访问性与稳定的传播机制。

Abstract: Latent interfaces let agents in a multi-agent system communicate through continuous representations rather than decoded text. Prior work reports performance and efficiency gains, but neither separates latent communication from the agent harness nor examines its internal components, leaving its actual contribution unclear. We propose LatentLens, a framework that decouples the latent interface at two levels: externally from the agent harness, to measure its contribution to overall performance (RQ1), and internally into realignment compatibility, information accessibility, and propagation dynamics, to identify the benefits and limitations of each (RQ2). Across seven benchmarks with Qwen3 models, adding the latent interface to the same harness does not improve and can even substantially degrade accuracy. Internally, latent representations retain some domain information, but incompatible realignment can substantially degrade performance, and repeated latent steps can amplify representation-level failures. Thus, while latent representations may preserve information, they do not necessarily ensure effective communication, which also requires compatible realignment, information accessibility, and stable propagation.

</details>


### [148] [One Phase Is Enough: Sim-to-Real Transfer with Direct Visual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31588)
*John Marangola,Antonia Bronars,Younghyo Park,Stephen Andrews,Kushal Kedia,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT：冻结预训练视觉基础编码器，实现高效单阶段视觉强化学习，真实成功率相对提升58.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sim-to-real视觉RL中，两阶段“特权状态教师+视觉学生蒸馏”存在学生显著弱于教师的性能损失；而直接用RL从头训练视觉策略样本效率低，单阶段视觉RL难以落地。

Method: 提出DRIFT配方：使用测试时可得的图像观测，直接进行单阶段视觉RL；关键做法是采用冻结的预训练基础视觉编码器，避免视觉骨干的反向传播开销，同时借助其预训练表征从快速光栅化仿真渲染迁移到真实世界。实验在匹配架构、观测与计算预算下与两阶段蒸馏对比，并进行受控仿真评估（分布内/分布外偏移）。

Result: 在三个操作任务上，DRIFT平均真实世界成功率的相对提升为58.8%；相对于两阶段蒸馏的优势从分布内约9%扩大到分布外偏移下超过61%。

Conclusion: 冻结预训练视觉编码器可使视觉RL聚焦于控制学习，是高效可行的单阶段视觉RL方案；更强的泛化性是真实世界迁移优越的主要来源。

Abstract: Sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) has enabled impressive robotic behaviors, yet deploying policies directly from vision remains challenging. A common approach first trains a teacher policy with RL using privileged state observations, then distills its behavior into a vision-based student via imitation. However, the resulting student can substantially underperform its teacher. Directly training visual policies with RL offers a promising alternative, but learning task-relevant visual representations from scratch can be sample-inefficient, making one-phase visual RL intractable. We propose DRIFT, a simple recipe for efficient one-phase visual RL using image observations available at test time. Our key insight is to use frozen, pretrained foundation vision encoders, allowing RL to focus on learning control from existing visual representations. Freezing the encoder avoids costly backpropagation through the vision backbone, while its pretrained representations enable real-world transfer from fast, rasterized simulation rendering. Under matched architectures, observations, and compute budgets, DRIFT outperforms two-phase distillation and achieves a 58.8% relative improvement in average real-world success rate across three manipulation tasks. Controlled simulation evaluations demonstrate that DRIFT's relative advantage grows from roughly 9% in-distribution to over 61% under out-of-distribution shifts, suggesting that stronger generalization contributes to superior real-world transfer. Project website: https://jmarangola.github.io/one-phase-visual-rl/

</details>


### [149] [Noise-Level Adjacency in Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31605)
*T. Y. Tsui,Jiatao Gu,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散模型训练时在所有噪声级共享一个网络；本文证明这种共享收益来自噪声级嵌入中的"相邻性"，方向并非必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型训练目标在噪声级上是逐点的，若各级独立，同等参数量的分离网络应可匹配共享网络；但实际共享权重效果更好，暗示存在对噪声级顺序的隐式依赖。作者旨在厘清顺序中"相邻性"与"方向"各自的作用。

Method: 从理论上给出共享网络拟合误差的下界：由相邻后验均值之间的距离减去网络在相邻嵌入间可产生的变化量构成。实验上：(1) 在高斯混合与 CIFAR-10 上对比反转嵌入与打乱嵌入，区分相邻性与方向；(2) 用连续区间上的分离网络检验远距离级间共享；(3) 固定参数时在相同位置断开相邻性的代价；(4) 按这些距离的等分位划分噪声级。

Result: 反转嵌入保留全部相邻关系、拟合误差不变，打乱则升高；连续区间上的分离网络在其宽度下可匹配共享网络；固定参数时，在相同位置破坏相邻性的代价不足完全打乱的40%；按距离等分噪声级可降低除最小网络外所有网络的误差。

Conclusion: 噪声级顺序通过"相邻性"进入扩散模型，方向仅为设计选择；该结论为噪声级嵌入与调度的设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Diffusion models train one network across all noise levels, although their training objective is pointwise in the noise level. If training on each pair of adjacent levels were an independent problem, separate networks with the same total parameter count would be expected to match one shared network. But in practice, on images, sharing weights across levels gives better results, which hints at an implicit dependence on their order. An order fixes both which levels are adjacent and the direction in which they run. In this paper, we show that, of the two, the benefit of sharing rests on adjacency in the noise-level embedding. The fitting error of a shared network is bounded below by the distances between neighbouring posterior means, minus the change the network can make between neighbouring embeddings. We first distinguish adjacency from direction: on a Gaussian mixture and CIFAR-10, reversing the embeddings keeps every neighbour and leaves the fitting error unchanged, whereas shuffling them raises it. Second, we rule out sharing between distant levels as the source of the benefit: separate networks on contiguous ranges match the shared network at its width, and with the parameters fixed, breaking adjacency at the same places costs less than 40\% of a full shuffle. Dividing the levels at equal shares of these distances lowers the error of all but the smallest networks. We therefore show that the order of the noise levels enters a diffusion model through adjacency, with its direction being a design choice. Our code is available at https://github.com/TSUITUENYUE/Noise-Level-Adjacency-in-Diffusion-Training.

</details>


### [150] [Eliciting sandbagged LLM capability via structural interventions on attention-head functional networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31615)
*Manzi Fabrice Niyigaba,Ishan Verma Prasad,Sansheng Cao,Zheng Huang,Yujun Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从内部功能组织视角研究AI模型的sandbagging（策略性能力抑制），发现sandbagging伴随检测社区内功能连接减弱的一致结构特征，并利用该信号引导能力激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠评估先进AI系统能力对安全部署至关重要，但sandbagging（模型策略性隐藏自身能力）会破坏此类评估。已有工作多聚焦于受控设定与检测/激发隐藏能力的方法，而对能力被抑制时模型内部神经计算如何变化知之甚少。

Method: 受网络神经科学启发，在固定模型架构与权重的前提下，由注意力头激活构建功能图，比较诚实与sandbagging条件下功能图组织；跨多种模型架构与社区检测方法分析结构差异；并基于最大连接变化对注意力头进行消融以激发能力，同时将结构信号融入现有推理级与权重级激发方法。

Result: 跨架构与社区检测方法一致发现：sandbagging对应检测社区内功能连接降低的结构特征。消融连接变化最大的注意力头可显著恢复被抑制的性能，同时基本保持诚实行为下的性能；引入结构信号还能进一步提升现有推理级与权重级激发方法的效果。

Conclusion: sandbagging伴随内部计算的系统性重组；功能网络结构既可作为理解能力抑制的机制性视角，也可作为恢复隐藏能力的实用信号。

Abstract: Reliably evaluating the capabilities of advanced AI systems is critical for safe development and deployment, yet such evaluations may be undermined by sandbagging, a situation in which a model strategically suppresses capabilities it possesses. Existing work has developed controlled sandbagging settings and methods for detecting or eliciting hidden capabilities, but comparatively little is known about how a model's internal neural computation changes when its capabilities are suppressed. In this work, we study sandbagging through the lens of internal functional organization. Inspired by network neuroscience, we construct functional graphs from attention-head activations and compare their organization under honest and sandbagging conditions while holding the model architecture and weights fixed. Surprisingly, across model architectures and community detection methods, we find a consistent structural signature in which sandbagging is associated with reduced functional connectivity within detected communities. We further demonstrate that this reorganization can guide capability elicitation. Ablating attention heads that exhibit the largest connectivity changes substantially recovers suppressed performance while largely preserving performance under honest behavior. Moreover, incorporating these structural signals further improves existing inference-level and weight-level elicitation methods. Together, our results show that sandbagging is accompanied by systematic reorganization of internal computation and demonstrate that functional network structure can provide both a mechanistic lens for understanding capability suppression and a practical signal for recovering hidden capabilities.

</details>


### [151] [What a Forecasting Leaderboard Can Establish: Resolution Reporting for Zero-Shot Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31619)
*Arya Sikder,Sriram Pillutla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"榜单分辨率"(resolution)概念并开发工具Vernier,可仅凭榜单已公开的各数据集分数计算：多数时序基础模型榜单(resolution 3.6–19倍邻位差距)无法区分前几名，且排名顺序依赖于提交池构成；同时指出榜单未记录输入历史长度，缩短历史使3/4开源模型误差上升(两个达15%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型"一次预训练、预测未见序列"的评估依赖少量公开排行榜，但无人说明这些榜单能检测多小的性能差异；数据稀缺、对比集中，导致微小差异被当作真实排名，而榜单本身的分辨能力未被量化。

Method: 定义榜单"分辨率"(可检测的最小排名差异)，开发Vernier工具，从榜单已发布的per-dataset分数计算分辨率；并报告：(1)哪些条目无法与榜首区分，(2)无关第三方提交时排名是否改变(池相关排序规则的稳定性)，(3)在70组配置上做历史长度消融，测量对误差的影响。

Result: GIFT-Eval分辨率为6.8个名次而其邻位仅差0.91，前十名不足两个可分辨步长；fev-bench、Chronos Benchmark II、TIME分辨率是邻位差距的3.6–19倍；仅BOOM(2,554条序列各自作为独立来源)能分辨其前十。四个榜单上，池相关排序规则在一次更新(七次中一次)中反转了43个前十名次对，且无任何条目被重跑。历史长度消融：3/4开源模型误差升高，其中两个升高15%。

Conclusion: 现有主流时序基础模型榜单的排名精度不足，名次差异多为噪声且受提交池影响；榜单还缺失输入历史长度等关键协议信息。作者开源Vernier，一条命令即可对任何发布per-dataset分数的榜单生成该诊断报告，呼吁提升评估的可分辨性与协议透明度。

Abstract: A foundation model for time series is pretrained once and then asked to forecast series it has never seen, so the evidence for it is its score on held-out datasets. Such datasets are scarce, and a few public leaderboards carry most comparisons, yet none of them states how small a difference its datasets can detect. We call that quantity the resolution of a board and introduce Vernier, a tool that computes it from the files a board already publishes. Vernier also reports which entries a board cannot separate from its leader and whether its order changes when unrelated parties submit. GIFT-Eval resolves 6.8 rank places, and its displayed neighbours sit 0.91 apart. The ten places of its top ten hold fewer than two resolvable steps. fev-bench, Chronos Benchmark II and TIME show the same pattern: their resolution is 3.6 to 19 times the gap between neighbours. With each of its 2,554 series as an independent source, BOOM is the one board that resolves its top ten. Below the resolution the order depends on who else submitted: across four boards, rules that rank against the pool reversed 43 top-ten pairs with neither entry rerun, in one update in seven. Boards also do not record how much history a model receives. On 70 configurations, shortening it raised the error of three of four open models, two of them by 15%. We release Vernier, which runs this report with one command on any leaderboard that publishes per-dataset scores: https://github.com/therealram18/vernier

</details>


### [152] [Reward Basis Decomposition for Interpreting Preference Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31640)
*Mohamed I. Eldagla,Ifesi Onubogu,Prerana Rane,Harsh Sharma,Rachel Freedman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Reward Basis Decomposition (RBD)，将奖励模型的逐用户权重拆分为群体项与个体项，分别用概念对齐和稀疏自编码器分析；在合成对照上能恢复人为植入的个体方向，但在PRISM和Community Alignment上未发现可靠的个体结构，单一群体方向即可解释大部分偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型可解释性方法只针对单一奖励头，无法区分群体共享价值与个体间差异价值，而理解奖励模型所编码的价值对AI对齐至关重要。

Method: RBD将逐用户权重分解为群体项（共享群体方向）与逐用户项（个体用户方向），并分别通过概念对齐与稀疏自编码器（SAE）分解进行分析；先在含人为植入用户方向的合成对照数据上验证方法有效性，再应用于PRISM与Community Alignment两个真实多偏好数据集。

Result: 合成对照中RBD成功恢复植入的逐用户方向；在PRISM和Community Alignment上未发现个体用户结构，且在当前设定下，拟合的逐用户项相对共享群体方向无可靠预测增益，说明奖励嵌入空间中单一方向即可解释大部分偏好。

Conclusion: 奖励模型中大部分偏好可由单一群体方向刻画；RBD为区分群体与个体价值提供了可用工具，但真实数据中个体化奖励结构的证据不足。代码与合成数据集已公开。

Abstract: Canonical reward models specify the optimization target for AI policies, so understanding the values they encode is important for ensuring AI alignment. Existing interpretability techniques interpret reward models with a single reward head, which cannot distinguish between the values that a population shares and those that vary among individuals. We introduce Reward Basis Decomposition (RBD), which splits per-user weights into a population term and a per-user term. This yields a shared population direction and individual user directions that are analyzed separately via concept alignment and sparse autoencoder decomposition. We verify that RBD can recover artificially planted per-user directions on a synthetic control, then apply it to two real-world multi-preference datasets, PRISM and Community Alignment. In both, we find no individual-user structure, suggesting that a single direction in the reward embedding space accounts for most preferences. We verify that RBD can recover artificially planted per-user directions on a synthetic control, then apply it to two real-world multi-preference datasets, PRISM and Community Alignment. In both, we find no reliable predictive benefit from the fitted per-user terms beyond the shared population direction under our setup. Code and the synthetic dataset are available at https://github.com/RachelFreedman/RBD.

</details>


### [153] [Boundary Representation-Driven Graph Transformer Modeling for Machining Operation Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31661)
*Fatemeh Elhambakhsh,Gaurav Ameta,Aditi Roy,Hyunwoong Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于图Transformer的几何到序列学习框架，直接从B-Rep设计几何预测加工工序序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加工工艺规划中零件几何、加工操作与可制造状态相互耦合，传统方法依赖专家知识与规则系统，可扩展性和自动化受限；现有机器学习方法多基于体素或手工特征等简化几何表示，未能充分利用B-Rep中丰富的几何与拓扑信息。

Method: 从B-Rep实体构建拓扑感知图，将面及其邻接关系编码为节点和边特征，同时包含几何与关系信息；用图神经网络编码器学习几何感知嵌入，再以此条件化双解码器Transformer，自回归生成加工工序序列，从而直接刻画工序类型预测与工艺级顺序约束，无需中间特征提取或加工状态表示。

Result: 在2,991个合成加工零件数据集上，主工序预测准确率95%，子工序预测准确率64%，序列长度预测准确率93%；进一步分析表明模型能有效捕捉加工过程中的优先关系与几何相关约束。

Conclusion: 结果体现了B-Rep驱动的图学习在可扩展、自动化加工工艺规划方面的潜力。

Abstract: Machining process planning is inherently complex due to the interdependence between part geometry, machining operations, and evolving manufacturable states. Traditional approaches rely heavily on expert knowledge and rule-based systems, limiting scalability and automation. While recent machine learning methods have shown promise, most operate on simplified geometric representations, such as voxels or hand-crafted features, which fail to fully exploit the rich geometric and topological information encoded in Boundary Representation (B-Rep) models. In this work, we propose a graph transformer-based framework for geometry-to-sequence learning that directly predicts machining operation sequences from B-Rep design geometry. The proposed method constructs topology-aware graphs from B-Rep entities, where faces and their adjacencies are encoded as node and edge features capturing both geometric and relational information. A graph neural network encoder learns geometry-aware embeddings, which are then used to condition a dual-decoder transformer that autoregressively generates machining operation sequences. This formulation enables the model to capture both operation-type prediction and process-level ordering constraints directly from design geometry, without requiring intermediate feature extraction or machining-state representations. Experiments on a dataset of 2,991 synthetic machined parts demonstrate strong performance, achieving 95% accuracy for main operation prediction, 64% accuracy for sub-operation prediction, and 93% accuracy for sequence length prediction. Further analysis shows that the model effectively captures precedence relationships and geometry-dependent constraints inherent in machining processes. These results highlight the potential of B-Rep-driven graph learning for scalable and automated machining process planning.

</details>


### [154] [Neural Interoperability Protocols: Specifying Compatibility Before Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31670)
*Alireza Bagheri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出「神经互操作协议」(NIPs)：以规范先行方式把神经兼容性定义为公开接口义务，而非仅由模型对性能推断的属性，并通过检索/视觉/连续记忆实验验证其可区分性与条件性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络日益作为可复用组件开发，但独立训练模型间的中间表示难以交换。现有方法或对齐表示、或鼓励特征兼容，然而特定模型对之间转移成功本身并不能界定未来实现需满足何种条件才能互操作——缺乏公开、可检验的接口规范。

Method: 规格先行的NIP框架：规定交换边界、交换数据包的语义与可容许条件、接收方必须支持的下游计算，且独立于具体架构与训练过程。实验涵盖受控检索、视觉与连续记忆任务，涉及独立训练模型、不同架构与目标、以及后续训练批次；并设计匹配能力对照、共享语义监督、信息扣留实验、部分契约实验与数据包干预。

Result: 匹配能力对照显示原生任务成功不保证互操作性；共享语义监督在评估设定下实现显著的直接迁移；信息扣留实验表明接收方可继续依赖捐赠方交换时不可得信息的计算；部分契约与数据包干预表明所观察到的兼容性依赖于所评估的语义、数据包群体与接收方后续计算。

Conclusion: 结果支持将公开神经接口规范作为设计前瞻性组件交换的框架，并能区分「已证实的互操作性」与更强的「符合性主张」。

Abstract: Neural networks are increasingly developed as reusable components, yet their intermediate representations remain difficult to exchange across independently trained models. Existing approaches can align representations or encourage compatible features, but successful transfer between particular models does not by itself define what future implementations must satisfy to interoperate. We introduce Neural Interoperability Protocols (NIPs), a specification-first framework that treats neural compatibility as a set of public interface obligations rather than a property inferred solely from model-pair performance. A NIP specifies an exchange boundary, the semantics and admissibility conditions of exchanged packets, and the downstream computations that recipients must support, independently of the architectures and training procedures implementing it. We investigate this distinction through controlled retrieval, vision, and continuous-memory experiments involving independently trained models, differing architectures and objectives, and later training cohorts. Matched-competence controls demonstrate that native task success does not ensure interoperability, while shared semantic supervision enables substantial direct transfer in the evaluated settings. Withheld-information experiments show that recipient models can continue computations requiring information unavailable to donors at exchange time. Partial-contract experiments and packet interventions further establish that observed compatibility is conditional on the semantics, packet populations, and recipient continuations being evaluated. Together, these results support public neural interface specifications as a framework for designing prospective component exchange and distinguishing demonstrated interoperability from stronger conformance claims.

</details>


### [155] [Curvature-Equilibrated Policy Optimization for Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31680)
*Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EQUIP，一种通过平衡正负曲率、利用投影Hessian构建有界局部度量来改善actor优化的二阶方法，嵌入SAC后在6个MuJoCo任务上样本效率优于SAC及两个二阶基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规一阶优化器在深度强化学习的actor优化中未显式利用神经网络actor损失的局部曲率信息，限制了策略搜索的效率。

Method: EQUIP构建轨迹信息驱动的搜索子空间，在子空间内用actor损失的投影Hessian构建有界局部度量以进行曲率均衡；通过一阶损失匹配保持近似的一阶损失下降，并结合梯度混合与基于曲率的梯度选择调整实际用于actor优化的梯度。论文给出曲率均衡的几何性质、梯度对齐与幅度的界，以及直接梯度更新下改善actor损失下降与收敛到稳定点的条件。方法嵌入SAC时不改动actor损失、critic学习与回放机制。

Result: 在6个MuJoCo连续控制基准上，EQUIP-SAC的平均样本效率高于SAC与两个二阶策略优化基线；消融实验显示曲率均衡与轨迹信息几何均对策略训练有增益。

Conclusion: 显式平衡actor损失曲率并结合轨迹子空间几何，可在不改变SAC原有结构的前提下有效提升策略优化的样本效率，并具备理论上的损失下降与收敛性保证。

Abstract: Gradient-based actor optimization is central to deep reinforcement learning. However, conventional first-order optimizers do not explicitly exploit the local curvature of neural actor losses. We propose EQUIP (Curvature-Equilibrated Gradients for Policy Search), a novel approach that seeks to balance local contraction and expansion of the actor parameter space by equilibrating positive and negative curvature. EQUIP constructs a trajectory-informed search subspace and uses the projected Hessian of actor loss to build a bounded local metric for curvature equilibration. First-order loss matching preserves the approximated first-order decrease in actor loss. Meanwhile, gradient mixing and curvature-based gradient selection adjust the gradient applied for actor optimization. We establish the geometric properties of curvature equilibration, derive bounds on gradient alignment and magnitude, and identify conditions for improved actor loss reduction and convergence toward stationarity under direct gradient updates. We integrate EQUIP into Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) without modifying its actor loss, critic learning, or replay mechanism. Experiments on 6 MuJoCo continuous control benchmarks show that the resulting EQUIP-SAC algorithm achieves higher mean sample efficiency than SAC and two second-order policy optimization baselines. Ablation studies further highlight the benefits of curvature equilibration and trajectory-informed geometry for policy training.

</details>


### [156] [Tokenization and Architecture Jointly Allocate Component Roles in Time Series Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31730)
*Mingyu Kim,Doguk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时间序列Transformer的"时间探测"框架，用15个token级诊断任务、以线性可解码增益度量各子层构建时间特征的贡献，发现构建主体由tokenization与架构共同决定：patch tokenization下FFN主导（均值3.2倍于attention），variate tokenization下attention主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列Transformer预测性能强，但究竟是attention还是FFN在构建时间特征尚不清楚，tokenization（如patching）如何改变这一分工亦未知；需要一种可量化的表征层面诊断手段。

Method: 构建时间探测框架：15个token级诊断任务，以线性探针可解码性的逐子层增益度量特征构建（匹配MLP探针在embedding层已饱和）；面向由线性预测头读取表征的模型。应用于8个长期预测基准上训练的5个监督模型；采用激活patch、knockout、去除attention重训等预测侧干预，并对PatchTST做P=1/P=2的受控token粒度操纵。

Result: PatchTST下FFN探测增益在全部8个数据集上超过attention，8数据集均值达3.2倍；移除attention后FFN单独可达近乎相同的总增益，重训模型预测误差仅在完整模型的百分之几以内。iTransformer（variate tokenization）下attention主导；CATS中cross-attention是唯一输入路径。P=1几乎消除FFN的构建作用，P=2即恢复。

Conclusion: 特征构建职责并非固定，而由tokenization与架构交互分配；预测侧干预确立了组件级依赖与充分性。结果为patching增益、无自注意力模型（TSMixer、PatchMLP）的竞争力及CATS中cross-attention的独特作用提供了表征层面的解释。

Abstract: Time series transformers have achieved strong forecasting performance, yet it remains unclear which of their components, attention or the feed-forward network (FFN), constructs temporal features, and how tokenization such as patching changes this. We propose a temporal probing framework that uses 15 token-level diagnostic tasks to measure feature construction as the gain in linear decodability of temporal properties from one sub-layer to the next (a matched MLP probe already saturates at the embedding). It targets models whose probed representations are read by a linear forecasting head. Applied to 5 supervised models trained on 8 long-term forecasting benchmarks, it finds that the constructing component is not fixed but allocated by the interaction of tokenization and architecture. Under patch tokenization (PatchTST), the FFN's gain exceeds attention's in probing on all 8 datasets, and its 8-dataset mean is 3.2x attention's. Without attention, the FFN alone reaches nearly the same total gain, both when attention is removed from a trained model and when PatchTST is trained without it, and the retrained model forecasts within a few percent of the full model. In probing, attention dominates under variate tokenization (iTransformer); in CATS, cross-attention is the only input path. In a controlled manipulation of PatchTST, single-scalar tokens (P=1) nearly eliminate the FFN's construction, while one more point per token (P=2) restores it. Our forecasting-side interventions (activation patching, knockout, and training without attention) establish component-level reliance and sufficiency. These results offer a representational reading of prior findings on the gains from patching, the competitiveness of self-attention-free models (TSMixer, PatchMLP), and the distinct role of cross-attention in CATS.

</details>


### [157] [Geo-SMILE: Statistical Model-Agnostic Interpretability with Local Explanations for Geospatial Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31731)
*Shadieh Mohammadi,Koorosh Aslansefat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的事后解释框架Geo-SMILE，通过特征扰动分支与地理扰动分支，将实质性特征解释与地理位置响应分离，并在三类空间预测任务中验证了加权代理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事后解释方法主要面向非空间输入，对地理上下文缺乏显式处理，难以区分"特征贡献"与"空间位置响应"。

Method: Geo-SMILE含两条互补扰动分支：Feature分支遮蔽实质性预测变量而保留观测坐标；Geo分支在固定实质性变量的前提下迁移地理单元位置。沿用SMILE的统计距离加权代理原则，以输入分布Wasserstein距离确定联盟局部性权重，并用加权Ridge代理模型逼近黑盒期望预测；定义带符号的GeoSensitivity（keep方向）量化模型对地理单元迁移的响应。

Result: 在Hull犯罪预测、Seattle房价预测与Chicago网约车需求预测上，留出加权代理保真度：Feature分支R_w²=0.910–0.989，Geo分支R_w²=0.842–0.906。

Conclusion: 该框架使地理位置成为事后解释的显式对象，但需分别评估其解释性、稳定性与参照依赖性。

Abstract: Conventional post-hoc explanation methods were developed largely for non-spatial inputs and often provide limited explicit treatment of geographic context. We introduce Geo-SMILE, a model-agnostic framework that distinguishes substantive Feature explanation from geographic-position response through complementary perturbation branches. The Feature branch masks substantive predictors while retaining observed coordinates; the Geo branch relocates geographic units while holding substantive predictors fixed. Following the statistical-distance-weighted surrogate principle of SMILE, input-distribution Wasserstein distances determine coalition locality weights and weighted Ridge surrogates approximate expected black-box predictions. GeoSensitivity, signed in the keep orientation, quantifies fitted-model responses to geographic-player relocation under the specified design. Across crime prediction in Hull, housing-price prediction in Seattle and ride-hailing demand in Chicago, held-out weighted surrogate fidelity ranges from $R_w^2=0.910$ to $0.989$ in the Feature branch and from $0.842$ to $0.906$ in the Geo branch. The framework makes geographic position an explicit object of post-hoc explanation while requiring separate assessment of interpretation, stability and reference dependence.

</details>


### [158] [Byte Patches versus BPE at Matched Data: A Matched-Twin Comparison at 350M Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31790)
*Asadullah Maliyawala,Andrej Lisal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在完全相同原始字节、文档顺序与约等每步字节预算下，对比 MEGABYTE 式字节补丁 Transformer（local64 滑窗局部解码器）与 8k BPE 字节级解码器（350M 参数、100 亿训练字节）：matched-span 滑动下 BPE-8k 的 bits-per-byte 领先 5.2%/4.9%/9.3%/6.1%，下游任务互有胜负；结论对上下文外推、bits/token 误作 bits/byte、字节分母不一致等评测陷阱高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多在固定 token 预算下变换子词分词器，或做受 FLOP 约束的字节模型缩放对比，均未回答"在原始字节、字节顺序与优化器配方全部固定时，固定补丁字节模型能否直接替代分词器"这一根本问题。

Method: 构建一对受控孪生模型：MEGABYTE 式字节补丁 Transformer 配 64 字节滑窗局部解码器（local64），对照 8k 词表 BPE 字节级解码器；两者共享原始字节、文档顺序、约等每优化步字节数，规模 350M 参数、训练 1e7 字节→100 亿字节；在 matched-span 文档滑动（相同计分字节区间）下以 bits-per-byte 为主指标，采用配对 bootstrap，并做下游微调（CharBench、fact200、PIQA、ARC-Easy）加 Holm 校正。

Result: matched-span 对齐下 BPE-8k 在 bits-per-byte 上全面领先，相对差距为 -5.2%（总体）、-4.9%（文本）、-9.3%（代码）、-6.1%（数学）。下游分化：CharBench 等量微调 local64 更优（53.7% vs 40.0%），fact200 与 PIQA（原始及长度归一化）、ARC-Easy 长度归一化在 Holm 校正后（α=0.05）偏向 BPE-8k，其余任务打平。评测陷阱显著改变结论：超出 BPE 训练上下文的长度外推、把 bits/token 当作 bits/byte 汇报、字节分母不一致。

Conclusion: 在严格受控条件下，固定补丁字节模型尚不能整体替代 8k BPE：建模效率（bits/byte）明显落后，但下游表现并非一致劣于 BPE，提示效率与下游能力可能解耦。结论受单一规模、单一种子、预固定 8k 词表及孪生模型间嵌入初始化差异等限制，需多规模多种子验证。

Abstract: We compare a MEGABYTE-style byte-patch Transformer with a 64-byte sliding local decoder (local64) to a matched byte-level 8k BPE decoder (BPE-8k) under identical raw bytes, document order, and approximately matched bytes per optimizer step: 350M parameters and 10 billion training bytes. Under matched-span document sliding (identical scored byte ranges), BPE-8k leads with relative gaps of -5.2%, -4.9%, -9.3% and -6.1% in bits per byte (overall, text, code, math; paired bootstrap). Downstream, CharBench equal fine-tuning favors local64 (53.7% vs 40.0%), fact200 favors BPE-8k (16.5% vs 7.0%), and PIQA (raw and length-normalized) and ARC-Easy length-normalized favor BPE-8k after Holm correction at alpha = 0.05; remaining tasks are ties. Evaluation pitfalls that changed conclusions include context-length extrapolation past the BPE training context, bits per token reported as bits per byte, and inconsistent byte denominators. Unlike controlled tokenizer studies that vary the subword tokenizer under a fixed token budget, or FLOP-controlled byte-model scaling studies, we hold the raw bytes, their order and the optimizer recipe fixed and ask whether a fixed-patch byte model can replace the tokenizer at all. Limits: one size, one seed, one 8k vocabulary fixed before the runs, and a known difference in embedding initialization between the twins.

</details>


### [159] [Learning Compact State Abstractions for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31800)
*Yaswanth Chittepu,Raghavendra Addanki,Anup Rao,Branislav Kveton,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以任务性能而非行为匹配定义"充分"的智能体状态抽象，将状态表示为确定性递归程序并用LLM引导的遗传算法搜索，在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上将策略输入缩减71%和57%而保持接近参考智能体的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通常把完整交互历史当作状态代理，不区分过去观察与动作是否仍然相关，造成输入冗余。作者追问：智能体究竟需要携带哪些信息向前。

Method: 将智能体状态定义为交互历史的紧凑抽象，理想状态是能保持固定参考智能体能力的最小抽象；因黑盒策略行为匹配过于严格，改用任务成功率界定充分性。以确定性递归程序表示状态抽象，用LLM引导的遗传算法在其空间上搜索。

Result: 在ALFWorld与ScienceWorld上，学到的抽象保持接近参考的任务成功率，同时将策略平均输入分别减少最多71%与57%。所得程序可直接检视，明确说明交互中保留、更新与丢弃的信息。

Conclusion: 状态抽象可仅凭任务性能准则学习，显著压缩智能体输入且不损失能力；程序化表示带来可解释性，为"状态应包含什么"提供显式、可审计的答案。

Abstract: LLM agents commonly use the full interaction history as a proxy for state, retaining all past observations and actions regardless of whether they remain relevant. We instead ask what information an agent actually needs to carry forward. We define agent state as a compact abstraction of interaction history that preserves the competence of a fixed reference agent, with the minimal such abstraction as the ideal. Because matching the reference agent's behavior is unnecessarily restrictive and difficult for black-box policies, we define sufficiency through task performance rather than behavior matching. We represent state abstractions as deterministic recursive programs and search over them using an LLM-guided genetic algorithm. Across ALFWorld and ScienceWorld, the learned abstractions preserve near-reference task success while reducing mean policy input by up to 71% and 57%, respectively. The resulting programs are directly inspectable, providing an explicit account of what information is retained, updated, and discarded during interaction.

</details>


### [160] [Two-Stage Post-Training for Flow-Based Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31850)
*Jiaming Zhang,Yao Lyu,Haoning Wang,Yongjun Chen,Liping Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向流式具身模型的两阶段后训练框架：先用多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）高效整合专家能力，再用"RL+按采样噪声加权的失败轨迹蒸馏"协同优化，性能超越联合SFT并超过教师聚合成功率，且样本效率高于直接RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练赋予具身模型通用能力，但可靠任务执行仍需后训练。多任务下SFT存在训练-部署分布失配；RL受稀疏奖励与任务间学习进度不均制约；MOPD虽以自身轨迹上的稠密监督整合专家能力，却难以超越教师。

Method: 基于条件流匹配的velocity regression形式构造蒸馏目标，设计两阶段后训练：Stage 1用MOPD整合专家能力；Stage 2将RL与教师蒸馏结合，按采样噪声对速度误差加权，且仅对失败轨迹施加蒸馏，用稠密引导补充稀疏奖励而不直接约束成功行为。

Result: 在多种模型与基准上，框架优于联合SFT，并超过对应教师的聚合成功率；样本效率对比显示，以更少环境交互即获得高于直接RL的成功率。

Conclusion: 验证了"先蒸馏整合、后失败轨迹定向蒸馏+RL"的后训练范式对流式具身模型的有效性，兼顾专家能力继承与超越，并提升样本效率。

Abstract: Large-scale pretraining provides embodied models with general capabilities, but reliable task execution still requires post training. In multitask settings, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) faces distribution mismatch between training and deployment, whereas reinforcement learning (RL) is limited by sparse rewards and uneven learning progress across tasks. Multi-teacher on policy distillation (MOPD) consolidates expert capabilities through dense supervision on the model's own trajectories, but achieving performance beyond the teachers remains challenging. We propose a two-stage post training framework for flow-based embodied models, constructing distillation objectives from the velocity regression formulation of conditional flow matching. Stage 1 efficiently consolidates expert capabilities through MOPD. Stage 2 combines RL with teacher distillation that weights velocity errors according to the sampling noise, where distillation is restricted to failed trajectories to supplement sparse rewards with dense guidance without directly constraining successful behaviors. Experiments across multiple models and benchmarks show that the framework outperforms joint SFT and exceeds the corresponding teachers' aggregate success rates. Sample efficiency comparisons show higher success rates than direct RL with fewer environment interactions.

</details>


### [161] [Start Where the Data Are: Statistical Priors for One-Step Generation on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31856)
*Yuyao Wang,Haoliang Sun,Xiankai Lu,Xinxin Zhang,Yongshun Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于流形统计先验与后验耦合的一步式生成框架，缓解流形上噪声与数据分布不匹配及模式间串扰问题，显著降低分布误差与蛋白质构象离群率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用（如蛋白质设计）需要对流形进行快速一步生成，但现有一步生成器常在数据模态间的低密度间隙中产生伪样本。作者分析指出其根源在于均匀噪声分布与集中分布的数据之间存在严重不匹配，因此需要一个能刻画数据粗粒度结构的统计先验。

Method: 首先通过几何感知的矩匹配拟合切空间高斯混合，估计数据先验；随后引入后验耦合，即对每个数据点采样条件潜在分量，并从该分量中抽取与之配对的噪声，避免独立配对跨模态连接；针对长扭转角链，用共享参数的隐马尔可夫先验建模序列依赖以降低全协方差混合的计算开销。该方法可直接插入现有一步训练流程，保持生成器架构、训练目标与推理时单次网络评估不变。

Result: 在球面、环面与 SO(3) 上的实验表明，该方法在多种生成器上均取得显著提升，分布误差最高降低 125 倍，蛋白质 Ramachandran 离群率从 12.1% 降至 0.55%。

Conclusion: 以流形上的统计先验替代均匀噪声、并用后验耦合保证噪声-数据配对一致性，是提升一步式流形生成质量的有效且通用策略，并可低成本扩展至高维序列化构象数据。

Abstract: Fast generation on manifolds is important for scientific applications such as protein design, yet existing one-step generators often produce spurious samples in low-density gaps between data modes. Our analysis highlights the mismatch between uniform noise and concentrated data, motivating a statistical prior that captures the data's coarse structure. We first estimate this prior by fitting mixtures of tangent-space Gaussians through geometry-aware moment matching. However, independent noise-data pairing can still connect different modes and weaken the benefit of the fitted prior. We therefore introduce posterior coupling, which samples a latent component conditioned on each data point and draws its paired noise from that component. To extend this approach to long torsion-angle chains, where full-covariance mixtures become costly, we use a hidden Markov prior to model sequential dependence with shared parameters. The resulting framework plugs into existing one-step training pipelines while retaining the generator architecture, training objective, and single network evaluation at inference. Experiments on spheres, tori, and SO(3) demonstrate substantial improvements across multiple generators, reducing distributional error by up to $125\times$ and the protein Ramachandran outlier rate from 12.1% to 0.55%.

</details>


### [162] [Subliminal Learning Crosses Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31870)
*Saraswathy Amjith,Kimia Hamidieh,Antonio Torralba,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示文本层面的潜意识学习（subliminal learning）可跨模态迁移：通过仅含数字序列的数据在一个VLM上蒸馏出的偏好（如动物）会改变其下游扩散模型的图像生成与表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明语言模型可通过表观无意义的数据（如数字序列）被潜意识蒸馏出偏好，但此类迁移是否、以及如何跨越模态边界（影响视觉生成）尚不清楚。

Method: 以学生模型作为扩散模型的文本编码器（对比原模型）；分析学生变化中"恒定分量"对图像表征偏移的贡献并做均匀叠加干预；提出weight lens，从单个训练后模型的权重中识别潜意识特质。

Result: 学生驱动的扩散模型更频繁地生成学生的目标动物，而颜色、风格不迁移；学生变化中的恒定分量解释了大量表征偏移，均匀加入可改变生成动物（依赖完整提示）但无法改变单token编码的颜色/风格；weight lens 能识别绝大多数学生的特质，包括未在生成图像中显现的特质。

Conclusion: 潜意识学习在视觉-语言模型中存在跨模态迁移，且具有选择性（语义主题迁移、单token属性不迁移）；其机制可部分归因于权重中的恒定偏移，weight lens 提供了从权重直接检测潜在特质的工具。

Abstract: Subliminal learning refers to a surprising phenomenon in language models wherein a prompt given to one model (e.g., preference for cats) can be subliminally distilled to another model via training on data that is ostensibly meaningless (e.g., number sequences). This paper shows that text-based subliminal learning transfers cross-modally, in particular, a topic/preference (e.g., animals) transferred through number sequences across vision-language models also changes how images are generated and represented. First, we show that a diffusion model using the student as its text encoder generates the student's animal more often than with the original model as its text encoder, but not its color or style. We then investigate the mechanism behind this phenomenon and which concepts transfer across modalities. A constant component of the student's change accounts for much of the shift of image representations toward the trait. Adding this component uniformly can change the generated animal, which depends on the whole prompt, but not concepts such as color or style, which are encoded in a single token. Finally, we introduce weight lens as an approach to identify a subliminal trait from the weights of a single trained model, and show that it identifies the trait of nearly every student, including traits that do not surface in generated images.

</details>


### [163] [EEG-Jev: A Unified Single-Pass Decision Model for EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31875)
*Zhifei Zhu,Hongtao Zhang,Yu Ren,Wei Zhou,Zeping Xie,Yi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EEG-Jev 是一个统一的单次前向 EEG 决策模型，将选择、命题判断和有序评分统一为类型化读出，并在 35 个公开数据源上以单一检查点联合训练，核心任务达到可用精度且校准与延迟表现优于生成式对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 分析涉及选择方案、判断陈述、对有序属性评分等多种任务，需要具有明确语义且可校准的概率输出；现有方法多为任务专用、概率语义不清或需多次推理。

Method: 给定 EEG 窗口、问题与答案规格，一次前向传播输出类型化读出：CHOICE 给出候选分布，NOUL 给出命题概率，SCORE 给出有序等级分布与期望；单一检查点跨 35 个公开来源联合训练，在留出划分上仅评估一次。同时引入先验校正与温度缩放进行概率校准。

Result: 原始读出在 Stieger2021 运动想象达 54.08% 平衡准确率，Sleep-EDF-SC 睡眠分期 70.45%，CHB-MIT 癫痫检测 72.11%（阈值 0.5，AUROC 0.825），ds005292 疼痛评分 0–10 MAE 1.700。先验校正与温度缩放将负对数似然由 0.766 降至 0.606（Sleep-EDF-SC）、0.166 降至 0.019（CHB-MIT）；直接读出将 batch-1 延迟从 122.43 ms 降至 63.11 ms（1.94 倍）。

Conclusion: 统一单次前向的类型化读出框架可在多类 EEG 决策任务上以单一检查点获得具有明确概率语义、可校准且低延迟的输出，优于匹配主干的生成式对照。

Abstract: EEG analysis spans choosing alternatives, judging statements, and rating ordered attributes, requiring calibratable probabilities with explicit meaning. We present EEG-Jev, a unified single-pass decision model for EEG. Given an EEG window, a question and an answer specification, one forward pass returns a typed readout: CHOICE gives a candidate distribution, NOUL a proposition probability, and SCORE an ordered-level distribution and expectation. One checkpoint is trained jointly and evaluated once on held-out splits of 35 public sources. Core raw outputs reach 54.08% balanced accuracy on Stieger2021 motor imagery, 70.45% on Sleep-EDF-SC sleep staging, 72.11% per window at threshold 0.5 on CHB-MIT seizure detection with 0.825 AUROC, and 1.700 MAE on 0-10 ds005292 pain ratings. Prior correction and temperature scaling lower negative log-likelihood from 0.766 to 0.606 on Sleep-EDF-SC and 0.166 to 0.019 on CHB-MIT, while direct readout cuts batch-1 latency from 122.43 to 63.11 ms (1.94-fold) against a matched-backbone generative comparator. Interactive demonstration and aggregate results: https://neuroimglab.github.io/EEG-Jev/.

</details>


### [164] [Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning enhanced Federated Learning for Predicting Remaining Useful Lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31880)
*Rajarshi Chattopadhyay,Chen-Khong Tham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于合作式多智能体强化学习（QMIX）的联邦学习框架，按客户端选择性传输模型层子集并限制重传次数，以应对丢包无线链路，配合GCPATr模型做RUL预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer类RUL预测模型参数多、数据需求大，而工业数据孤岛且隐私敏感，需用联邦学习；但实际客户端常经有损无线链路（偏好UDP低时延）连接，模型交换易受丢包影响，标准FL在此条件下性能退化。

Method: 将FL适配有损信道建模为多智能体决策问题：每个客户端为智能体，选择要传输的层子集并设定重传上限；采用QMIX值函数分解实现可扩展的集中训练；以图卷积位置感知Transformer（GCPATr）作为RUL预测主干。

Result: 在CMAPSS数据集上，RL-FL相较标准FL持续降低MSE，丢包率越高收益越大，如FD003在50%丢包下达约18%改进。

Conclusion: MARL可作为缓解FL-based RUL预测中无线链路退化的有效手段；层选择与重传控制能在通信受限下保持预测精度。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction is central to Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). The growth of industrial IoT has unlocked large multivariate sensor streams and enabled deep models - especially Transformers - for long-horizon sequence learning. However, transformers are parameter-heavy and data-hungry, while operational datasets are often siloed and privacy-sensitive. Federated Learning (FL) addresses this by training across clients without raw data sharing, but in practice many clients connect over lossy wireless links where UDP is preferred for low latency, making model exchange vulnerable to packet drops. We present a cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework that adapts FL to lossy channels by selecting per-client layer subsets to transmit and capping retransmissions. This framework is trained with QMIX value-function factorization for scalability. We instantiate the approach with a Graph Convolution Position-Aware Transformer (GCPATr) for RUL forecasting. On the CMAPSS dataset, our MARL-enhanced FL (RL-FL) consistently improves mean square error over standard FL, with the largest gains at high packet loss (e.g., up to 18% improvement on FD003 at 50% loss). These results show that MARL is an effective countermeasure to wireless degradation in FL-based RUL prediction.

</details>


### [165] [MIFLOW: Dynamic Recent-Behavior Priors for Reference-Calibrated Online Flow-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31902)
*Chenhao Wang,Haonan Ma,Wenjie Zhang,Yifan Wang,Mingbin Gao,Yuan-Ye Zhang,Wenjing Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MIFLOW，用适配近期执行动作的新近自适应流匹配先验来校准在线流策略学习，在演员更新和评论家目标中加入维度归一化的对数密度修正，在多个 MuJoCo 任务上取得有竞争力的回报并降低低密度动作比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的策略虽能表达复杂动作分布，但在线 actor-critic 中离策略价值估计在覆盖不足的动作区域不可靠，高表达力策略会把概率质量移向评论家偏好的动作，导致策略漂移。

Method: 以近期执行动作拟合一个新近自适应流匹配先验，作为移动密度参考；借鉴"渠化"作为路径依赖稳定化的定性类比；将该先验给出的近似近期动作对数密度用于维度归一化修正，同时作用于演员更新与评论家目标，提高低密度动作的代价但不禁用它们。

Result: 在六个 MuJoCo 连续控制任务、五个随机种子上，MIFLOW 的平均回报曲线具竞争力，且在若干任务上后期训练平均回报高于基线；Walker2d-v4 案例中，被学习参考判为低密度的动作比例整体及在高 Q 动作中均更低；先验族与先验缓冲的对比显示回报与先验定义诊断存在任务依赖差异。

Conclusion: 结果支持在所评估设置中使用动态近期行为先验进行参考校准；但诊断仅衡量采样动作与学习参考的一致性，真实动作支撑集与评论家外推误差未测量，结论有局限。

Abstract: Flow-based policies provide expressive action distributions for continuous-control reinforcement learning. In online actor-critic learning, off-policy value estimates can be unreliable in poorly covered action regions, where expressive policies may shift probability mass toward critic-favored actions. We propose MIFLOW, which calibrates online flow-policy learning with a recency-adaptive flow-matching prior fitted to recently executed actions. Inspired by canalization as a qualitative analogy for path-dependent stabilization, we use the prior as a moving density reference for value-guided policy improvement. MIFLOW uses the approximate recent-action log-density in a dimension-normalized correction to both actor updates and critic targets. This correction increases the cost of low-density actions while keeping them available. Across six MuJoCo continuous-control tasks and five random seeds, MIFLOW achieves competitive mean return curves and higher late-training mean returns than the evaluated baselines on several tasks. In a Walker2d-v4 case study, MIFLOW shows a lower proportion of actions assigned low density by the learned reference, both overall and among high-Q actions. Prior-family and prior-buffer comparisons show task-dependent differences in returns and prior-defined diagnostics. These results support dynamic recent-behavior priors for reference calibration in the evaluated settings. The diagnostics measure how sampled actions align with the learned reference. True action support and critic extrapolation error remain unmeasured in these experiments.

</details>


### [166] [OrangeTree: A Linear and Tree-based Time Series Forecasting Model Supporting Multiple Input and Output Lengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31905)
*Yiqi Tang,Zhichen Lai,Yuxuan Yang,Dalin Zhang,Jinpeng Chen,Gang Chen,Huan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OrangeTree，一种线性+树结构的预测架构，将模型参数与序列长度解耦，用 Segment Tree Encoder、Inverse Tree Decoder、Range Weighter 与 Feature Fuser 在统一框架内支持多输入多输出长度（MIOL）预测。六个基准上达到 SOTA，MIOL 场景下精度接近定长模型，且不显著增加推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TSF 模型存在刚性缺陷：单一架构无法处理多输入多输出长度（MIOL）。填充（padding）与自回归等替代方案会带来计算冗余或误差累积。

Method: OrangeTree 采用线性和树状架构，使模型参数与序列长度解耦；由 Segment Tree Encoder 与 Inverse Tree Decoder 将多长度历史输入映射为多长度输出预测；Range Weighter 与 Feature Fuser 作为桥接模块，动态选择并融合相关区间以构建最优预测上下文。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上取得 SOTA 性能；在 MIOL 设置下，精度与定长模型相当，且不像启发式方法那样出现明显精度下降或推理延迟显著上升。

Conclusion: 通过参数与序列长度解耦的树式结构，OrangeTree 在统一框架内高效支持多输入多输出长度预测，兼顾精度与推理效率，为 TSF 的 MIOL 问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting (TSF) models suffer from a critical rigidity: the inability to handle Multiple Input and Output Lengths (MIOL) within a single architecture. Current workarounds, such as padding or autoregression, often incur computational redundancy or suffer from error accumulation. We propose OrangeTree, a linear and tree-based architecture that decouples model parameters from sequence lengths. It employs a Segment Tree Encoder and an Inverse Tree Decoder to translate multi-length input history into multi-length output predictions within a unified framework. Crucially, a Range Weighter and a Feature Fuser bridge these components, dynamically selecting and fusing relevant ranges into optimal contexts for forecasting. Experiments on six benchmarks confirm OrangeTree achieves the SOTA performance. In MIOL settings, it matches the accuracy of length-fixed models without substantial accuracy degradation or a substantial increase in inference latency typical of heuristic methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Tang-Yiqi/OrangeTree.

</details>


### [167] [TQ-HQNet: A Trainability-Aware and Qubit-Efficient Hybrid Quantum Neural Network for Cross-Domain Learning on Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31908)
*Abdur Rasool,Saba Aslam,Hui Chen,Chayut Bunterngchit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TQ-HQNet：面向异质数据的可训练性感知、量子比特高效混合量子神经网络，包含自适应潜空间压缩、紧凑变分电路的可训练性搜索、多浅层量子专家融合三种机制；在四个公开数据集上相对固定混合量子基线分别提升+0.280、+0.170、+0.071、+0.060平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子神经网络在NISQ时代有前景，但受限于可训练性差、电路设计僵化、跨异质真实任务鲁棒性弱，实际效果受限。

Method: 1）自适应潜空间压缩，将领域特异特征映射为量子比特兼容表示；2）在紧凑变分电路候选集上进行可训练性感知搜索；3）多个浅层量子专家混合融合，在不显著增加量子比特开销下提升鲁棒性。在神经筛查、钓鱼检测、金融方向预测、DNA序列分类四个公开数据集上评估，并进行消融与敏感性分析。

Result: 各领域均优于固定混合量子基线，平衡准确率增益分别为+0.280、+0.170、+0.071、+0.060；消融与敏感性研究表明增益源于可训练性感知的紧凑电路选择与专家融合，而非电路规模扩大；部分任务上经典基线仍更优，但固定划分下相对固定混合参考的提升一致。

Conclusion: 紧凑电路选择与专家融合具有实际价值；重复划分实验与硬件感知研究留待未来工作。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum neural networks are a promising direction for noisy intermediate-scale quantum computing, but their practical effectiveness is often limited by poor trainability, rigid circuit design, and weak robustness across heterogeneous real-world tasks. This paper proposes TQ-HQNet, a Trainability-Aware and Qubit-Efficient Hybrid Quantum Neural Network for cross-domain learning on heterogeneous data. The proposed framework combines three mechanisms: 1) adaptive latent compression to map domain-specific features into qubit-compatible representations, 2) trainability-aware search over compact variational circuit candidates, and 3) hybrid fusion of multiple shallow quantum experts to improve robustness without increasing qubit cost excessively. TQ-HQNet is evaluated on four public datasets spanning neurological screening, phishing detection, financial direction prediction, and DNA sequence classification. Across all domains, the proposed model consistently improves over a fixed hybrid quantum baseline, with balanced-accuracy gains of +0.280, +0.170, +0.071, and +0.060, respectively. Ablation and sensitivity studies confirm that gains arise from trainability-aware compact circuit selection and expert fusion, not circuit scaling. Although classical baselines outperform on some tasks, fixed-split results show consistent improvements over the fixed hybrid reference. These analyses support the practical role of compact selection and fusion, with repeated-split and hardware-aware studies left for future work.

</details>


### [168] [ReJEPA: Bounded Predictive Regulation for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31910)
*Fangzhou Lin,Yuping Wang,Shuo Xing,Yang Chen,Yuwen Heng,Xin Li,Wenbin He,Yuhan Yao,Zhengzhong Tu,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ReJEPA：用逐元素 log-cosh 损失替代 L2/L1 做世界模型预测训练，在大误差时梯度有界、小误差时保持二次平滑，无需改动架构即可提升表征的物理结构保真度与规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧序列训练中并非所有帧与空间区域都同等信息量或可预测；强制最小化全部预测误差可能有害。L2 的无界梯度使编码器与预测器过拟合噪声/瞬态帧、牺牲核心物理结构，并加剧与表征正则项的梯度冲突；L1 虽能约束大梯度，但其零点附近恒定梯度破坏规划所需的局部平滑性。

Method: 提出 ReJEPA（bounded predictive regulation）原则：对大误差采用有界梯度、对近零误差保持平滑二次行为，并以逐元素 log-cosh 损失简单实例化；作为即插即用训练替代方案接入 Temporal Straightening、LeWorldModel 与 V-JEPA 2.1，不改变架构与推理流程。

Result: 在三个框架上，log-cosh 一致优于 L2、曲率匹配的二次对照、L1 与 Smooth-L1 基线；增益随环境复杂度增长，从简单导航扩展到接触丰富的操作任务以及 NAVSIM 真实自动驾驶。探针与几何分析表明，放松对极端误差的惩罚所得到的表征更能刻画物理状态，并与真实规划代价空间更对齐。

Conclusion: 预测损失函数形式本身是潜在世界模型的关键设计维度：对极端误差做有界调节可在不牺牲平滑性的前提下抑制噪声过拟合，从而获得更符合物理与规划需求的表征，且具备良好的通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Latent world models learn representations by predicting future states in an abstract embedding space rather than reconstructing raw pixels. When training across multi-frame sequences, however, not all frames and spatial regions are equally informative or predictable. We argue that forcing a world model to minimize every prediction error may be unnecessary and harmful. Under standard $L_2$ loss, unbounded gradients force the encoder and predictor to overfit noisy or transient frames at the expense of core physical structure, while amplifying gradient conflict with representation regularizers. Conversely, $L_1$ loss bounds large gradients, but its constant gradient magnitude near zero disrupts the local smoothness required for planning. We resolve this tension with \textbf{ReJEPA}, a bounded predictive regulation principle that combines bounded gradients for large errors with smooth quadratic behavior near zero, instantiated simply via the element-wise $\log\cosh$ loss.
  As a drop-in training replacement across Temporal Straightening, LeWorldModel, and V-JEPA~2.1, $\log\cosh$ consistently outperforms $L_2$, a curvature-matched quadratic control, $L_1$, and Smooth-$L_1$ baselines without altering architectures or inference procedures. Notably, the gains grow with environmental complexity, scaling from simple navigation to contact-rich manipulation and real-world autonomous driving on NAVSIM. Probing and geometric analyses confirm that relaxing the penalty on extreme errors yields representations that better capture physical states and align with the true planning cost landscape.

</details>


### [169] [Meta-Amortized Cognitive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31914)
*Jerry M. Huang,Lukas Schumacher,Niek Stevenson,Mischa von Krause,Stefan T. Radev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CogFormerV2，一个"元摊销贝叶斯推断"框架，用单一神经网络一次性覆盖整个认知模型空间，实现模型指定、修订与拟合的统一，并开源为交互式应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知建模高度迭代：研究者随理论和数据演进反复修改模型结构、参数化、协变量与实验设计。现有基于神经网络的摊销贝叶斯推断虽能快速估计单个模型，但每次改动都需重新仿真与训练，成本高、不可复用。

Method: 构建元摊销框架CogFormerV2：在模型空间上一次性训练单个Transformer类网络，将摊销推广到整个模型族，而非单一模型；模型空间涵盖二元、多备择与连续决策，涉及不同参数集、协变量结构、试次数与污染水平，共14.6×10^9种以上配置；并借助持续学习与表示迁移扩展到新模型。

Result: 单GPU约一天完成一次训练；在上述超大规模模型配置空间中，后验估计准确且校准良好；可拟合来自不同行为任务的实证数据，并能通过持续学习与表示迁移推广至新模型。

Conclusion: CogFormerV2 提供了可复用的元摊销推断基础设施，使认知建模的模型指定、修订与拟合只需一个网络，有望加速大规模计算建模工作流；已作为开源交互式应用发布。

Abstract: Cognitive modeling is highly iterative. Researchers repeatedly revise model structures, parameterizations, covariates, and experimental designs as theories evolve and data accumulates. Amortized Bayesian inference with neural networks provides rapid estimation for individual models, but every revision requires new simulations and training. Here, we introduce CogFormerV2, a meta-amortized framework that enables researchers to specify, revise, and fit cognitive models with a single network, carrying amortization across an entire space of models all at once.
