Trained once in about a day on a single GPU, it delivers accurate, well-calibrated posteriors across more than 14.6 billion model configurations spanning binary, multi-alternative, and continuous decisions, with varying parameter sets, covariate structures, trial counts, and contamination levels. It can fit empirical data from distinct behavioral tasks and extends to new models through continual learning and representation transfer. Released as an open-source interactive application, CogFormerV2 provides a reusable infrastructure for meta-amortized inference in cognitive modeling, while carrying the potential to accelerate computational modeling workflows at large.

</details>


### [170] [Rethinking Universal Time Series Foundation Models from Unified Representations to Shared Dynamics (UniTFU)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31921)
*Shuxin Li,Yingjie Zhou,Le Zhang,Jingyuan Xia,Shengxi Li,Ce Zhu,Anderson Rocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出从多尺度时序片段中发现可复用的"时间序列基元"(TFUs)，构建显式复用跨域共有局部动态规律的统一基础模型UniTFU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSFM依赖大规模预训练数据的多样性，且以统一范式隐式学习跨异构域的表示，未显式识别域间共享的时间规律，导致跨域泛化能力受限。

Method: 以跨域重复出现的局部动态共性为切入点，从多尺度时序片段中挖掘可复用的时间序列基元TFUs，每个基元刻画一种跨域共享的重复局部动态模式；基于TFUs构建统一模型UniTFU，通过显式复用与组合这些共享时间规律进行预测。

Result: 在七个公开数据集上，UniTFU跨域预测显著优于现有TSFM；仅用少量目标域数据更新可复用组件即可高效适配目标域动态；与SOTA方法相比也具竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式建模并复用跨域局部动态共性，是提升时序基础模型跨域泛化与高效适配的有效途径。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have attracted increasing attention in cross-domain forecasting. Existing works mainly rely on large-scale pre-training and general modeling paradigms. However, the former heavily relies on the diversity and domain coverage of pre-training data. Meanwhile, the latter learns unified representations across heterogeneous domains without explicitly identifying the temporal regularities shared among them, limiting robust cross-domain generalization. This work explores cross-domain generalization by characterizing commonalities in recurring local dynamics across domains, rather than relying solely on implicitly learned temporal representations. To capture such cross-domain commonalities, we propose Time Series Foundation Units (TFUs) as reusable building blocks discovered from multi-scale temporal segments, each characterizing a recurring local dynamic pattern shared across domains. Building on TFUs, we develop a unified time series foundation model (UniTFU) that explicitly reuses and composes shared temporal regularities across domains for forecasting. Experiments on seven public datasets show that UniTFU substantially outperforms existing TSFMs in cross-domain forecasting and can efficiently adapt to target-domain dynamics by updating its reusable components with limited target data. UniTFU also achieves strong forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [171] [Beyond the Last Loop: Reinforcement Learning with a Teacher Extrapolated from the Model Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31939)
*Linghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ELGO方法：在GRPO中加入向"外推循环分布"的稠密逐token蒸馏项，该教师由冻结的锚点模型计算并周期性刷新，从而在可验证奖励下高效后训练循环语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型每token多次复用同一transformer栈，可用共享LM头读出各循环。但其可验证奖励后训练困难：结果奖励每响应仅1比特，摊到各循环会迫使中间循环学习末循环采样的token；蒸馏又需教师要么忽略奖励、要么依赖外部更大模型。

Method: 观察到循环模型自身已含一个强于末循环的教师——从首循环外推到末循环的下一token分布。ELGO在GRPO上增加朝该分布的稠密逐token蒸馏项。教师由锚点副本模型计算，锚点冻结，当学生到教师的KL低于阈值时用当前模型替换锚点，使教师随学生提升而不追逐自身外推；结果奖励在正确/错误响应间分配概率，教师决定响应内哪些token更优。刷新间隔内目标为以教师为锚的KL正则化RL，其最优解为教师经奖励倾斜。

Result: 在Ouro-1.4B-Thinking上，ELGO将五个数学基准的avg@8从GRPO的62.32提升至64.20，且在每个基准上均为对比方法中最优；而将奖励摊到各循环的方法低于基座模型。

Conclusion: 利用循环模型内在的外推分布作为自产教师并与结果奖励结合，可有效解决循环LLM可验证奖励后训练的奖励稀疏与蒸馏依赖问题，显著优于GRPO。

Abstract: Looped language models apply one shared transformer stack several times per token, and every loop can be read out through the shared language-model head. Post-training them with verifiable rewards is hard: the outcome reward carries one bit per response, spreading it over loops trains intermediate loops on tokens sampled from the last loop, and distillation needs either a teacher that ignores the reward or an external, larger model. We observe that a looped model already contains a teacher stronger than its last loop: the next-token distribution extrapolated from the first loop through the last. ELGO (Extrapolated-Loop Group-relative Optimization) adds to GRPO a dense per-token distillation term towards this distribution. The teacher is computed from an anchor copy of the model, which stays frozen and is replaced by the current model once the student's KL to the teacher falls below a threshold, so the teacher improves with the student without chasing its own extrapolation. The outcome reward splits probability between correct and incorrect responses, and the teacher decides which tokens are preferred inside a response. Between refreshes the objective is KL-regularised RL anchored at the teacher, whose optimum tilts the teacher by the reward. On Ouro-1.4B-Thinking, ELGO raises the average avg@8 over five math benchmarks from 62.32 with GRPO to 64.20 and is the best compared method on every benchmark, while spreading the reward over loops falls below the base model.

</details>


### [172] [Sounding the Black Box: Online Training of Embedded Neural Networks in Forward-Only Physical Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31964)
*Ruiqi Shu,Tianrun Gao,Haomin Wen,Xiaomeng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FORGE框架，在仅有前向、不可微的黑盒求解器条件下，实现在线训练嵌入PDE求解器的神经网络闭合项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合物理-神经模拟器将网络嵌入PDE求解器以建模未解析的次网格动力学。由于闭合输出会跨时间步反馈，单步离线回归无法直接最小化多步rollout误差，因此需在线轨迹优化；但BPTT要求求解器可微，而许多实际运行的Fortran/C/C++求解器仅提供前向接口。

Method: FORGE将精确的网络梯度与黑盒求解器动力学解耦，分两阶段：阶段I沿输出正交归一的权重方向做低维高斯-牛顿步预热rollout，并用末层校正抵消多层特征漂移；阶段II扰动空间输出场，拟合增益有界的因果线性响应算子，求解正则化目标输出，再通过网络反向传播更新全部权重。

Result: 在涵盖相分离、反应扩散、流体与MHD湍流、海洋环流的五个2D/3D基准上优于无梯度、强化学习和代理模型基线；粗求解器测试MSE在2D降低50%–96%、3D降低70%；比最强前向基线优16%–69%；恢复Solver BPTT增益的88%–98%。

Conclusion: FORGE为不可微的运筹级模拟器提供了有效的在线闭环训练途径，在无需求解器导数的情况下接近BPTT性能，兼具实用性与性能。

Abstract: Hybrid physics-neural simulators embed neural networks in partial differential equation (PDE) solvers to model unresolved subgrid dynamics while preserving resolved physics and conservation laws. Because closure outputs feed back across time steps, single-step offline regression does not directly minimize multi-step rollout error, motivating online trajectory optimization. Solver backpropagation through time (BPTT) is the standard online solution when solver derivatives are available, yet many operational Fortran and C/C++ simulators expose only non-differentiable, forward-only interfaces. We introduce FORGE (Forward-Only Response and Gauss-Newton Estimation), an online framework that trains embedded networks from forward runs alone by decoupling exact network derivatives from black-box simulator dynamics. Stage I warms up the rollout via a low-dimensional Gauss-Newton step along output-orthonormal weight directions, canceling multi-layer feature drift via a final-layer correction. Stage II perturbs spatial output fields to fit a gain-bounded causal linear response operator across rollout steps, solves for regularized target outputs, and updates all weights via network backpropagation. Across five 2D and 3D benchmarks spanning phase separation, reaction-diffusion, fluid and MHD turbulence, and ocean circulation, FORGE outperforms gradient-free, reinforcement-learning, and emulator-based baselines, cutting coarse-solver test mean squared error (MSE) by 50%-96% in 2D and 70% in 3D, outperforming the strongest forward-only baseline by 16%-69%, and recovering 88%-98% of Solver BPTT's gains. Code is available at https://github.com/ChiyodaMomo01/FORGE.

</details>


### [173] [On the Information Loss of Multi-Token Prediction: Origin and Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31966)
*Tangyu Jiang,Zhanke Zhou,Haodi Wang,Yiu-ming Cheung,Xiaohua Jia,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多token预测（MTP）在块式生成中误差累积的问题，提出Dual-MTP：在完整与随机掩码上下文下共同监督共享预测头，兼顾预测精度与高效多token解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTP通过块式预测加速自回归解码，但顺序生成多个块时存在误差累积、对不完美生成前缀鲁棒性不足的缺陷。作者希望厘清块级与序列级累积信息损失（CIL）的差异，并在此认识下改进生成前缀不完美时的预测能力。

Method: 1）理论分析：区分块级与序列级CIL，指出当模型以自身输出为前缀且无参考答案时，输入条件差距等于参考目标的跨块依赖；2）提出Dual-MTP：在完整前缀与随机掩码上下文两种条件下监督共享的预测头，保留原MTP架构，提供互补训练信号；3）以归一化双损失为界，约束两种上下文下预测分布之间的散度。

Result: 在5个骨干网络、6项任务（数学推理与GLUE理解）上评测：Dual-MTP在5个骨干中的4个取得最高平均任务分数，相较MTP最高提升2.46分；数学推理任务上解码吞吐达到NTP参考的1.4–1.9倍。

Conclusion: 双上下文监督可在保持多token高效解码的同时提升预测精度，说明掩码上下文扰动是缓解MTP误差累积的有效且低开销手段。

Abstract: Multi-token prediction (MTP) accelerates autoregressive decoding through block-wise prediction, but remains vulnerable to error accumulation across sequentially generated blocks. To investigate this limitation, we analyze the distinction between block-wise and sequence-level cumulative information loss (CIL). Specifically, when the model generates using its own previous outputs without access to reference answers, the input-conditional gap equals the cross-block dependence of the reference targets. To improve prediction when these generated prefixes are imperfect, we propose Dual-MTP, which supervises shared prediction heads under full and randomly masked contexts. Together, complete-prefix supervision and context perturbations provide complementary training signals while retaining the MTP architecture. To characterize this training objective, we use the normalized dual loss to bound the divergence between the model's predictive distributions under full and masked contexts. Experiments across five backbones and six tasks spanning mathematical reasoning and GLUE understanding show that Dual-MTP achieves the highest average task score on four of five backbones, with gains of up to 2.46 points over MTP. On mathematical reasoning tasks, Dual-MTP also achieves 1.4 to 1.9 times the decoding throughput of the NTP reference. Together, these results show that dual-context supervision can improve prediction accuracy while retaining efficient multi-token decoding.

</details>


### [174] [Predict Backbone, Graft Dynamics: A Decoupled Paradigm for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31970)
*Lin Zhang,Zilin Li,Jingmei Zhao,Jiayang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出解耦式预测范式"预测主干、嫁接动态"，实例化为GRAFT，在主干预测器基础上用检索式证据恢复局部高价值动态，在7个基准、2个指标、4个预测长度、6个主干上87%的比较均优于对应主干，MSE/MAE平均降低4.6%/3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期预测模型以逐点精度为优化目标，倾向于奖励占主导的低频主干结构，却抑制了突变、瞬时峰值与拐点等高使用价值的局部动态。

Method: 提出解耦范式：基预测器输出可靠轨迹主干，独立的"嫁接路径"通过检索式历史证据、相位感知释放、通道级控制与恢复协同优化来复原反复出现的历史动态；与传统残差后修正不同，其将第二阶段建模为有据可依的动态嫁接，并施加相位、通道与置信度约束。

Result: 在7个基准、2种指标、4种预测长度、6个主干的完整评测网格中，87%的比较优于对应主干，MSE/MAE平均下降4.6%/3.9%；动态窗口分析显示其恢复局部动态的同时保持稳定的主干结构。

Conclusion: "先预测主干、再嫁接动态"可作为高使用价值预测的新准则，在保持结构稳定性的前提下提升对局部关键动态的刻画能力。

Abstract: Modern long-term forecasters are typically optimized for point-wise accuracy, which rewards dominant low-frequency backbones but often suppresses high-utility local dynamics such as abrupt variations, transient surges, and turning points. We propose Predict Backbone, Graft Dynamics, a decoupled forecasting paradigm that separates stable structural prediction from grounded dynamic restoration. We instantiate it as GRAFT, where a base forecaster predicts the reliable trajectory backbone, and a dedicated grafting path restores historically recurrent dynamics through retrieval-based evidence, phase-aware release, channel-wise control, and restoration-cooperative optimization. Unlike generic post-hoc revision methods that correct residual errors, GRAFT formulates the second stage as grounded dynamic grafting, where historical response evidence is calibrated into transferable dynamics and selectively grafted onto the backbone under phase-, channel-, and confidence-aware constraints. Across the full evaluation grid of seven benchmarks, two metrics, four horizons, and six backbones, GRAFT improves over the corresponding backbone in 87% of all comparisons, with average MSE/MAE reductions of 4.6%/3.9%. Dynamic-window analyses show that GRAFT restores local dynamics while preserving the stable backbone structure. These results suggest a new principle for high-utility forecasting: predict the backbone, then graft the dynamics.

</details>


### [175] [Metric-Dependent Scaling Laws for Sketched Multiclass Softmax Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31995)
*Ziyan Chen,Linhe Zheng,Peilin Liu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究sketched多类softmax回归中全批量梯度下降的缩放律，在谱幂律的高斯师生模型下给出交叉熵超额风险与零一超额风险的统一谱尺度刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: sketch会引入模型误配，使分析需同时处理近似误差、非线性有限时间学习与数据复用导致的固定经验扰动，已有缩放律理论难以直接适用。

Method: 采用带幂律特征谱与教师谱的高斯师生模型，学生观测随机特征sketch并在整个优化过程中复用有限带标签样本，分析全批量梯度下降在一般稳定确定性学习率下的行为，并在指数高概率的sketch事件上联合刻画三类误差。

Result: 证明交叉熵超额风险与零一超额风险共享同一谱尺度：分辨率项加上有效学习维数除以样本量；师生决策不一致率等于该尺度的平方根。由此给出最优的sketch规模与累积时间跨度选择，合成实验验证了预测的相区与依赖度量的指数。

Conclusion: sketch导致的误配、有限时间学习与样本复用效应可被统一为单一谱尺度，从而为sketch规模与训练时长的联合调优提供理论依据。

Abstract: We study scaling laws for full-batch gradient descent with general stable deterministic learning rates in sketched multiclass softmax regression. In a Gaussian teacher student model with power law feature and teacher spectra, the student observes a random feature sketch and reuses a finite labeled sample throughout optimization. Sketching generally makes the student misspecified, so the analysis must jointly account for approximation, nonlinear finite time learning, and the fixed empirical perturbation induced by data reuse. On an exponentially likely sketch event, we show that cross entropy excess and excess zero one risk share a common spectral scale, consisting of a resolution term and an effective learned dimension divided by sample size; teacher student decision disagreement follows the square root of that scale. The resulting balance gives rate optimal sketch and cumulative horizon choices, and synthetic experiments support the predicted regimes and metric dependent exponents.

</details>


### [176] [ADE: A Zero-Training Unlearning Method for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31998)
*Sirui He,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Analytical Dynamics Excision (ADE)——一种用于潜在世界模型的确定性、无需训练的闭式遗忘算法，利用预训练缓存的二阶矩通过矩阵降秩/降更新在输入瓶颈、循环隐状态与下游头之间级联闭式编辑，配合正交切除、白化循环阻尼和保均值仿射校准，消除后门触发与陈旧动力学，而无需坍塌方差或破坏有效控制响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的潜在世界模型可能残留后门触发器或来自过时物理动力学区间的陈旧转移。现有遗忘方法在自回归物理环境中的失败原因有三：(1) 梯度更新会扭曲潜在流形并累积 rollout 漂移；(2) 朴素输出归零会在触发激活时压制有效控制响应；(3) 单步删除无法清除循环隐状态内部的污染。

Method: ADE 为确定性、零训练的闭式遗忘算法：仅用预训练缓存的矩统计，通过单遍矩阵 downdating 恢复 retain 协方差（无需原始 retain 数据）；将闭式编辑级联到输入瓶颈、隐状态与下游头；结合正交切除（orthogonal excision）切除 out-of-manifold 后门方向、白化循环阻尼抑制自回归漂移，并以保均值仿射校准对齐 in-manifold 分布偏移，避免方差坍塌。

Result: 在三个 MuJoCo 基准的四种设定下，ADE 在 DreamerV1 上优于七个调优基线；相较微调，将 HalfCheetah 与 Hopper 的速度翻转触发器消除速度提升 4.4×；并可零样本迁移至 DreamerV3。

Conclusion: 闭式、免训练的动力学切除为潜在世界模型提供了一种可靠且可迁移的遗忘范式，可在不破坏有效控制行为的前提下消除后门与陈旧动力学，并具备跨模型版本（V1→V3）的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Latent world models learn compact recurrent dynamics from offline trajectories, yet deployed simulators can retain backdoor triggers or stale transitions from outdated physical regimes. Existing unlearning methods fail in autoregressive physics: gradient updates distort latent manifolds and compound rollout drift, naive output zeroing suppresses valid control responses on trigger onset, and single-step deletion leaves corruption inside recurrent hidden states. We propose Analytical Dynamics Excision (ADE), a deterministic, zero-training closed-form unlearning algorithm for latent world models. Using only cached pretraining moments, ADE recovers retain covariances via single-pass matrix downdating without raw retain data and cascades closed-form edits across the input bottleneck, hidden states, and downstream heads. Pairing orthogonal excision with whitened recurrent damping and mean-preserving affine calibration neutralizes out-of-manifold backdoors and realigns in-manifold shifts without variance collapse. Across four regimes on three MuJoCo benchmarks, ADE outperforms seven tuned baselines on DreamerV1, eliminating HalfCheetah and Hopper velocity-flip triggers $4.4\times$ faster than fine-tuning, and transfers zero-shot to DreamerV3.

</details>


### [177] [To Critic Or Not To Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32013)
*Jason Wei,George Pu,Jingxuan Fan,Yuan Emily Xue,Yuan Christy Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统比较 PPO（带 critic）与 GRPO（无 critic）在偏差-方差权衡上的差异，指出不存在普适更优方法：小 batch 下 PPO 通常占优，大 batch 下 GRPO 可追平甚至反超重度 bootstrap 的 PPO；长时程、复杂任务中 GRPO 需要更大 batch 才能追上；按训练算力计，critic 在小预算下收益明显、大预算下收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 任务趋于更长、更复杂，critic 是否提升 RL 效率成为关键问题。PPO 用 critic 降方差并提供细粒度信用分配，但引入 bootstrap 偏差；GRPO 去掉 critic 以避免该偏差。近期基于 critic 的方法相对 GRPO 报告了强性能，但"何时该用哪种"缺乏清晰判据，本文旨在厘清二者适用条件。

Method: 以梯度方向刻画偏差-方差权衡，度量随 batch size 增大各方法梯度估计与真实策略梯度的对齐程度。在共享 actor 目标的前提下，将 GRPO 与 PPO 在控制任务、短时程数学任务、长时程终端 agent 三类场景中对比，并控制 critic 依赖强度（bootstrap 程度）与 batch size。

Result: 超过 1,200 次训练运行（含 96 次 LLM 实验）显示：小 batch 下 PPO 普遍领先；大 batch 下 GRPO 常反超重度 bootstrap 的 PPO，并与轻度 bootstrap 的 PPO 持平。长时程与更复杂领域中，GRPO 需更大 batch 才能达到 PPO 的性能。计入训练算力后，critic 在小预算下划算，大预算下回报递减。

Conclusion: 方法选择无普适答案，取决于 batch size、critic 依赖程度与任务复杂度的交互作用。实践建议：小算力/小 batch 场景优先考虑带 critic 的 PPO；大算力/大 batch 场景 GRPO 更具优势；长时程复杂任务需为 GRPO 配置更大 batch 才具竞争力。

Abstract: As large language models tackle longer and more complex tasks, understanding when a critic improves the efficiency of reinforcement learning (RL) becomes increasingly important. Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) trains a critic to reduce variance and provide fine-grained credit assignment, while Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) drops the critic and avoids the bias of bootstrapping from the critic's value estimates. Although recent critic-based methods have reported strong performance relative to GRPO, it remains unclear when each approach is preferable. We argue that there is no universal answer; instead, the choice depends on several interacting factors, including batch size, critic reliance, and task complexity. We study the bias-variance tradeoff in terms of gradient direction, measuring how closely each method's gradient estimates align with the true policy gradient as batch size grows. Under a shared actor objective, we compare GRPO with PPO in control tasks, short-horizon math, and long-horizon terminal agents. Across more than 1,200 training runs, including 96 with LLMs, we find that PPO generally leads at small batch sizes, while GRPO often overtakes heavily bootstrapped PPO and reaches parity with lightly bootstrapped PPO at larger batches. At longer horizons and in more complex domains, GRPO needs larger batches to catch up to PPO's performance. Accounting for training compute, critics pay off at small budgets, but their returns diminish at larger budgets.

</details>


### [178] [Structure-Aware Transformers for Hierarchical Authorization in Personal AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32018)
*Dongyu Cui,Jie Ding,Xuan Bi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PATH（Parent-gated Authorization Transformer for Hierarchies），一种面向个人AI代理授权偏好预测的神经网络架构，结合层级感知注意力掩码与父节点门控概率公式，保证层级一致的预测，并提供有限样本风险界与收敛性条件；在模拟数据、AI生成画像及真实用户授权数据上取得强预测性能，层级违规为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI代理执行多步任务时会产生大量权限请求：逐条人工审核耗时，而无差别批准带来隐私与安全风险。预测用户授权偏好可减轻负担并保留用户控制。但授权请求通常具有层级结构，用户决策稀疏且潜在相关，使授权预测颇具挑战。

Method: 提出PATH架构：将层级感知注意力掩码与父节点门控（parent-gated）概率公式相结合，确保预测在层级上自洽；引入层级级嵌入（hierarchy-level embeddings），以从稀疏观测和同一授权层级内共享的模式中学习。理论上建立有限样本风险界，并给出授权概率一致估计与算法收敛的充分条件。

Result: 在模拟数据、AI生成的用户画像以及真实用户授权数据上均表现出较强的预测性能，且层级违规为零。

Conclusion: PATH通过将层级结构先验显式编码进注意力机制与概率建模，有效解决了稀疏、相关、层级化授权决策的预测问题，兼具统计保证与实证效果，为个人AI代理的权限管理提供了兼顾效率与用户控制的可行方案。

Abstract: Personal AI agents can carry out complex, multi-step tasks on users' behalf, but doing so often involves numerous permission requests. Manually reviewing each request is time-consuming, while indiscriminate approval poses privacy and security risks. Predicting users' authorization preferences offers a way to reduce this burden while preserving user control. However, authorization prediction is challenging because permission requests are often hierarchical and observed user decisions are sparse and potentially correlated. To address these challenges, We propose the Parent-gated Authorization Transformer for Hierarchies (PATH) to predict users' authorization preferences for personal AI agents. PATH is a novel neural network architecture that combines hierarchy-aware attention masks with a parent-gated probability formulation to ensure hierarchy-consistent predictions. Hierarchy-level embeddings further support learning from sparse observations and patterns shared within each authorization level. We establish finite-sample risk bounds and provide sufficient conditions for consistent authorization probability estimation and algorithmic convergence. Experiments on simulated data, AI-generated user profiles, and real user authorization data demonstrate strong predictive performance with zero hierarchy violations.

</details>


### [179] [PFedHASNet: A Personalized Federated Hybrid Analog-Spike Network for Multi-Subject Motor Imagery EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32019)
*Peijia Li,Ruidong Ma,Xianghong Lin,Xiangwen Wang,Guoyong Shi,Shuai Huang,Hongqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PFedHASNet——一种个性化联邦混合模拟-脉冲网络，用于运动想象脑电解码，在四个公开数据集上取得最高平均客户端宏F1，并以紧凑客户端模型保持有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MI-EEG数据按被试/机构分散存储，采集与标注成本高；仅用单被试数据训练无法利用跨被试信息，而集中式原始EEG又带来隐私与数据治理问题。联邦学习可在不共享原始数据的前提下实现跨客户端协同建模。

Method: 共享编码器通过可学习融合权重整合三类表征：连续值时序特征、LIF脉冲响应、通道级对数方差统计量；客户端私有的残差适配器进行被试特异特征细化，私有分类器完成个性化预测。

Result: 在四个公开MI-EEG数据集参与被试的留出试次上，18种模型配置的跨模型对比中PFedHASNet取得最高平均客户端宏F1；在相同骨干对比中，以紧凑客户端模型保持有竞争力的解码性能。消融实验分析了混合表征与个性化组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 混合模拟-脉冲表征与客户端个性化适配相结合，可在联邦隐私约束下有效利用多被试数据提升MI-EEG解码，并兼顾模型紧凑性。

Abstract: Motor imagery electroencephalography (MI-EEG) data are often collected and stored separately across subjects or institutions, and EEG acquisition and annotation remain costly. Training models solely on individual subject data limits the use of information from other subjects, whereas centralizing raw EEG recordings raises privacy and data governance concerns. Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients without sharing raw EEG data, providing a practical approach to leveraging information from multiple subjects. Accordingly, we propose PFedHASNet, a personalized federated hybrid analog-spike network for MI-EEG decoding. The shared encoder integrates continuous-valued temporal features, leaky integrate-and-fire (LIF) spike responses, and channel-wise log-variance statistics through learnable fusion weights. Client-private residual adapters and classifiers perform subject-specific feature refinement and personalized prediction, respectively. Experiments on held-out trials from participating subjects across four public MI-EEG datasets show that PFedHASNet achieves the highest mean client macro-F1 among 18 model configurations in cross-model comparisons, while maintaining competitive decoding performance with a compact client-side model in comparisons using the same backbone. Ablation studies further examine the contributions of hybrid representations and personalization components to decoding performance.

</details>


### [180] [Scale-Robust and Global-Aware Representation Learning for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32033)
*Gyuwon Lee,Saejoon Park,Won-Seok Hwang,Bogoan Kim,Yunyong Ko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STAGER方法，联合学习尺度鲁棒时序表示与全局感知关系表示，用于多变量时序异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTSAD在表示学习上存在两个未被充分探索的挑战：C1如何学习能跨不同时间尺度捕获异常的尺度鲁棒时序表示；C2如何通过学习序列级全局特征（而非仅局部窗口）来建模序列间依赖关系，获得全局感知的关系表示。

Method: STAGER包含两个编码器：(1) 尺度鲁棒时序编码器，构建多尺度时序有向无环图（DAG）学习尺度鲁棒时序表示；(2) 全局感知关系编码器，将可学习的序列级全局嵌入融入时序表示，并通过图神经网络建模序列间关系。另设两个互补的辅助目标以增强全局嵌入的区分性。

Result: 在三个真实基准数据集上，STAGER在十种MTSAD方法和三项评价指标下取得最优平均排名；消融与敏感性分析验证了各组件及辅助目标的有效性；效率评估显示其在检测性能与推理时间之间取得良好权衡。

Conclusion: 联合多尺度时序DAG表示与全局关系表示可有效应对MTSAD中的尺度鲁棒性与全局依赖建模问题，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD) is an essential task for identifying abnormal system behaviors and supporting reliable operations across diverse real-world applications. Despite substantial advances in MTSAD, two key challenges in representation learning remain underexplored: (C1) how to learn scale-robust temporal representations that capture anomalies across different temporal scales and (C2) how to learn global-aware relational representations that capture inter-series dependencies by incorporating series-level global characteristics beyond local windows. To address these challenges, we propose STAGER, a novel method that jointly learns two complementary representations: scale-robust temporal and global-aware relational representations. Specifically, STAGER employs (1) a scale-robust temporal encoder that constructs a multi-scale temporal directed acyclic graph (DAG) to learn scale-robust temporal representations and (2) a global-aware relational encoder that incorporates learnable series-level global embeddings into the temporal representations and models inter-series relationships through graph neural networks. Two complementary auxiliary objectives further enhance the distinctiveness of the global embeddings. Extensive experiments on three real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that STAGER achieves the best average rank among ten evaluated MTSAD methods across three evaluation metrics. Ablation and sensitivity analyses validate the effectiveness of the proposed components and auxiliary objectives, while efficiency evaluations demonstrate that STAGER achieves a favorable trade-off between detection performance and inference time compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [181] [Online Environment Repair for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32037)
*Sheng Lu,Rui Mao,Zewei Chen,Chihua Ying,Beiduo Chen,Dingxin Hu,Tinghao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"在线环境修复"（online environment repair），在RL训练中异步修补被攻破的验证环境，累计reward hacking从36.9%降至0.06%，held-out Pass@8从21.7%升至46.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化验证器可为编码智能体提供可扩展的RL奖励，但测试脚手架缺陷与不完整单元测试使策略在未真正解题时即可拿满分。现有训练时防御存在缺陷：惩罚可疑输出并未修复验证器本身，会驱使策略在推理监控下伪装捷径，或因检测误报而清零有效解；过滤任务与rollout则会丢弃/削弱最难的问题，而捷径恰在这些问题上最早出现。预训练阶段的验证器加固虽直接修复环境，却漏掉仅随策略能力提升才暴露的漏洞类别。

Method: 在线环境修复：对触发漏洞的rollout保持奖励不变，以消除"隐藏"激励；训练中异步打补丁。候选修复由执行不变量或差分模糊测试触发，须通过三道自动化验证门（阻断exploit同时保留有效解）后才部署，从而抵消检测误报，并支持跨仓库的结构化传播，无需脚手架标签或参考解。在20组受控GRPO实验的异构软件工程仓库上评估，对比reward惩罚、rollout过滤、静态加固基线。

Result: 累计reward hacking由36.9%降至0.06%；held-out Pass@8由21.7%提升至46.7%；一致优于三类基线，并在未见过的漏洞类别上消除零样本利用。

Conclusion: 从"惩罚策略/过滤数据"转向"直接修复被利用的验证环境"，是抑制reward hacking且不牺牲难题学习信号的有效途径；在线、异步、门控验证的修复机制可随策略演进持续闭合新暴露的漏洞。

Abstract: Programmatic verifiers provide scalable reward signals for training coding agents with reinforcement learning, yet subtle harness defects and incomplete unit tests allow policies to earn full credit without solving the intended task. Existing training-time defenses intervene either by penalizing suspicious outputs or by filtering flagged tasks and rollouts. However, output penalties leave the underlying verifier exploitable, driving policies to disguise shortcuts under reasoning monitors or zeroing out valid solutions under detector false positives, whereas task and rollout filtering discards or mutes the hardest problems where shortcuts emerge earliest. Pre-training verifier hardening repairs the environment directly, but misses vulnerability classes that surface only as the policy improves during reinforcement learning. In this paper, we propose online environment repair, which leaves triggering rollout rewards unchanged to remove hiding incentives and asynchronously patches exploited environments during training. Candidate repairs triggered by execution invariants or differential fuzzing are deployed only after passing three automated validation gates that block the exploit while preserving valid solutions, canceling detector false positives and enabling structural propagation across repositories without scaffold labels or reference solutions. Across 20 controlled GRPO experiments on heterogeneous software-engineering repositories, online environment repair reduces cumulative reward hacking from 36.9% to 0.06% and improves held-out Pass@8 from 21.7% to 46.7%, consistently outperforming reward-penalty, rollout-filtering, and static-hardening baselines while eliminating zero-shot exploitation on unseen loophole classes.

</details>


### [182] [FlashQLA: Efficient Context Parallelism for Gated DeltaNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32039)
*Xi Lin,Chengruidong Zhang,Huiqiang Jiang,Xiao Li,Zekun Wang,Junda Feng,Yunfei Mao,Siqi Zhang,Siyu Wang,Man Yuan,Rui Men,Bo Zheng,Dayiheng Liu,Bohan Zhuang,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashQLA 是一个面向 Gated DeltaNet 的上下文并行执行框架，通过 Dual CP、Auto CP 与内核协同设计降低边界状态预处理开销并提升序列级并行度，单 GPU 前向加速 1.7–3.1×、反向最高 4.4×，8 GPU、1M 上下文下较 FLA KCP 前向最高 4.8×、前反向合计 4.4×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDN 依赖循环状态更新实现线性复杂度，但块（chunk）之间的顺序依赖限制了并行性，在长上下文、小 batch 或单 GPU 头级并行度受限的场景尤为突出。

Method: 提出 FlashQLA：1) 通过 decay-aware preparation 降低边界状态预处理开销；2) Dual CP 将序列并行扩展到多 GPU，并在各并行层级间共享 summary 计算；3) 引入负载自适应的 Auto CP；4) 进行 CP-aware 的算子与内核协同设计。

Result: 单 GPU 上相对 Flash Linear Attention (FLA) 取得 1.7×–3.1× 前向加速、反向最高 4.4× 加速；8 GPU、上下文最长 1M token 时，Dual CP 相对 FLA KCP 前向最高 4.8×、前向+反向合计 4.4× 加速。

Conclusion: 通过上下文并行与内核协同设计，FlashQLA 有效突破了 GDN 块间顺序依赖带来的并行瓶颈，在单卡与多卡长上下文场景均实现显著加速。

Abstract: Gated DeltaNet (GDN) achieves linear computational complexity through recurrent state updates, but sequential dependencies between chunks limit parallelism, particularly for long-context workloads with small batch sizes or limited head-level parallelism per GPU. We present FlashQLA, a context-parallel execution framework for GDN that exposes additional sequence-level parallelism while reducing boundary-state preprocessing overhead. FlashQLA reduces boundary-state preprocessing overhead through decay-aware preparation and extends sequence parallelism across GPUs with Dual CP, which shares summary computation across parallelism levels. Workload-adaptive Auto CP and CP-aware kernel co-design further improve execution efficiency. FlashQLA achieves $1.7\times$-$3.1\times$ forward speedup and up to $4.4\times$ backward speedup over Flash Linear Attention (FLA) on a single GPU. On eight GPUs with context lengths up to 1M tokens, Dual CP achieves up to $4.8\times$ forward speedup and $4.4\times$ speedup for combined forward and backward execution over FLA KCP.

</details>


### [183] [Spec-CoFrGeNet: Reparametrizing Continued Fraction Networks for Stable and Accurate Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32041)
*Soumyadeep Pal,Amit Dhurandhar,Vijil Chenthamarakshan,Dennis Wei,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对连分数（continued fraction）网络中分母趋零导致的极点问题，提出一种受物理格林函数启发、约束三对角哈密顿量谱的部分分母重参数化方法，从而无需增量训练即可稳定训练，并在语言与图像生成任务上以约30%-50%参数节省达到甚至超越基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连分数函数类可构建表达力强且参数高效的神经网络，但其有理形式在分母趋零时引入极点。先前工作通过强制一个小的正（非光滑）下界来规避该问题，而作者发现性能与训练稳定性对该下界的取值高度敏感；同时前作为稳定训练需采用增量训练（incremental training），对标准预训练流程改动较大。

Method: 提出一种新颖的部分分母变换，其动机来自该结构与物理中格林函数的联系，通过对所构造的三对角哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的谱进行约束，从根本上缓解极点问题；该方法消除了对增量训练的依赖，无需对标准预训练流程做繁琐修改。作者将FFN替换为先前提出的连分数架构，在语言与图像生成建模中进行验证。

Result: 在GPT-2 XL、(nano) Llama-MoE、Granite等多样模型上，所提方法在保持约30%-50%参数节省的同时，性能与原FFN架构相当，且在多数情况下优于更复杂的增量训练基线；首次证明连分数网络在采用扩散目标的图像生成建模（如SOTA Diffusion Transformer）上同样有效，以约30%参数节省达到与原FFN模型相当的性能，并显著优于先前的增量训练方法。

Conclusion: 通过受格林函数启发的部分分母重参数化与哈密顿量谱约束，可优雅消除连分数网络的极点敏感性问题并摆脱增量训练；该参数高效方案在语言与图像扩散生成任务中均表现稳健，具备实际替代FFN的潜力。

Abstract: Continued fractions have been shown to be a function class capable of constructing expressive yet parameter-efficient neural networks. These neural networks are not only effective for supervised learning, but also for training LLMs. However, the rational form of this function class introduces poles when the denominator approaches zero, which prior work has avoided by enforcing a small positive (non-smooth) lower bound. We show that performance and training stability can be highly sensitive to the choice of this lower bound. In this work, we introduce a novel transformation of the partial denominators, inspired by its connection to the Green's function in physics, that mitigates the above issue elegantly by constraining the spectra of the constructed tridiagonal Hamiltonian. Our approach further eliminates the need for incremental training used by previous works for stability, thus avoiding involved modifications to standard pre-training procedures. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our reparameterization by replacing FFNs with a previously proposed continued fraction architecture for both language and image generative modeling. Across diverse models, such as GPT-2 XL, (nano) Llama-MoE, and Granite, we show that our proposed method achieves performance comparable to the original (FFN-based) architectures while maintaining ~30%-50% parameter savings as before, and in most cases even outperforms the more complicated incremental training baseline. We further demonstrate, for the first time for continued fraction networks, that their effectiveness also carries over to image generative modeling, that utilize diffusion objectives. On a state-of-the-art Diffusion Transformer, we show that our approach again achieves performance comparable to the original FFN-based models with ~30% parameter savings and significantly outperforms the previous incremental training approach.

</details>


### [184] [Integral DeltaNet: Improved Linear Attention with Adaptive Memory Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32063)
*Junsoo Oh,Jihun Yun,Junhyuck Kim,Hanbaek Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Integral DeltaNet 通过引入双互补循环状态（快速更新的 delta 状态 + 保留其加权历史的积分状态），在读出时自适应融合多时间尺度信息，缓解线性注意力固定状态导致的内存碰撞与信息丢弃问题，并给出分块并行训练方案，在 340M/1.3B 规模上全面优于强循环基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力以固定大小循环状态替代增长的 KV 缓存，实现线性时间计算与恒定内存，但将历史压缩进固定状态会引发内存碰撞，损害长上下文理解。DeltaNet 用 delta 规则更新缓解碰撞，Gated DeltaNet 进一步加入门控，然而这些机制可能丢弃对未来预测有用的信息。

Method: 提出 Integral DeltaNet，维护两个互补循环状态：快速更新的 delta 状态（建模当前键值关联）与积分状态（保存 delta 状态的加权历史）；读出时自适应组合两状态以跨时间尺度访问信息。同时推导分块并行（chunkwise parallel）形式以支持硬件高效训练。

Result: 在 340M（15B FineWeb-Edu tokens）与 1.3B（100B tokens）模型从零训练下，相较强循环基线在语言建模、常识推理、真实与合成检索、长上下文理解及长度外推上整体表现提升；并对比了训练吞吐、激活内存以及解码延迟与内存。

Conclusion: 双状态积分式设计可有效缓解固定状态记忆压缩带来的信息损失，在保持线性注意力效率优势的同时提升长上下文与检索能力，且具备可实用的训练与推理效率。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces softmax attention with a linear formulation, enabling linear-time computation with a fixed-size recurrent state instead of a growing key-value cache. These computational and memory advantages have attracted growing interest in linear attention for long-context language modeling. However, compressing past information into a fixed-size state causes memory collisions that hinder long-context understanding. Although DeltaNet mitigates memory collisions through delta-rule updates and Gated DeltaNet extends this approach with gating, these mechanisms may also discard information relevant to future predictions. To address this limitation, we introduce Integral DeltaNet, which maintains two complementary recurrent states: a fast-updating delta state that captures current key-value associations and an integral state that preserves a weighted history of the delta state. At readout, the model adaptively combines these states to access information across different timescales. We further derive a chunkwise parallel formulation that enables hardware-efficient training. We evaluate Integral DeltaNet using 340M and 1.3B parameter models trained from scratch on 15B and 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, respectively. Comparisons with strong recurrent baselines show improved overall performance in language modeling, commonsense reasoning, real-world and synthetic retrieval, long-context understanding, and length extrapolation. We also compare training throughput and activation memory, as well as decoding latency and memory.

</details>


### [185] [Fix How It Acts, Not How It Thinks: Surgical Fine-Tuning of Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32064)
*Sandeep Singh Sandha,Prann Bansal,Arvind Sundararajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 merge-aware fine-tuning：在训练每个 step 时将 base 权重与 LoRA 更新之和按发布格式（BF16 / NVFP4）取整并保留厂商量化 scale，损失直接在这些"发布后"权重上计算，使训练模型与部署模型数值一致。应用于 Nemotron-3.5 后在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上最高提升 8.4 分，并公开两个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源权重 LLM 在长程 agentic 任务中常因少量重复失败步骤或过早宣告完成而失败，而非推理能力不足，故需"外科式"权重微调纠正动作而不损伤推理。但此类 LoRA 更新幅度极小：BF16 发布版中权重变化中位数仅为单个舍入步长的 1.5%，合并后损失约 2/3 的更新范数；合并进同模型 FP8/NVFP4 混合发布版时 93% 的更新消失。即"训练时看到的权重"与"发布后实际权重"严重不一致。

Method: 提出 merge-aware fine-tuning：标准 LoRA 仅在导出时才将合并权重舍入到存储格式，损失从未见过舍入后权重；本文在每个训练 step 即对 base 权重与 adapter 更新之和按发布格式舍入、保留厂商量化 scale，并在这些权重上前向计算损失。前向与推理引擎数值对齐，梯度直通舍入算子。损失仅优化模型动作，使推理保持接近基座模型。

Result: 在 Nemotron-3.5 上，该方法在 BF16 与 NVFP4 两个发布版本中均提升 agentic coding 能力，Terminal-Bench 2.1 上最高提升 8.4 分，两个模型均已公开释放。

Conclusion: 与其放大更新幅度，不如直接在被量化"幸存"的部分上训练：merge-aware fine-tuning 通过训练-部署数值对齐消除了 LoRA 合并在低精度发布格式下的更新丢失问题，并以实证收益表明"我们训练的模型即我们发布的模型"。

Abstract: Open-weight LLMs are competent software engineers, but they sometimes fail at tasks they are otherwise able to do. Often it is not a failure of reasoning. It is a few small missteps over a long trajectory (repeating a step that has already failed, or declaring the task done while a requirement is still unmet) that make the difference. Correcting this calls for a surgical weight update that steers the model toward the right actions without interfering with its reasoning. Such updates, typically learned with LoRA adapters, are often too small to survive merging into a released checkpoint. In our BF16 release, the median weight change is only 1.5% of a single rounding step, and merging loses roughly two thirds of the update's norm. When the same adapter is merged into the mixed FP8/NVFP4 release of the same model, 93% of the update disappears. Instead of increasing the size of the update, we directly train on the surviving part. We introduce merge-aware fine-tuning. Standard LoRA rounds the merged weights to the storage format only at export, so the loss never sees the rounded weights. We instead round the sum of base weights and adapter update to the release format at every training step, keeping the vendor's quantization scales, and compute the loss on these weights. The forward pass matches the serving engine numerics, and the gradient passes straight through the rounding, so the model we train is the model we ship. The loss optimizes only the model's actions and keeps its reasoning close to the base model. Applied to Nemotron-3.5, the recipe improves agentic coding in both the BF16 and the NVFP4 release, by up to 8.4 points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, and we publicly release both models.

</details>


### [186] [A Fused Gaussian Process Bandit Framework for Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32092)
*Muyun Lu,Jose E Aguilar Escamilla,Huazheng Wang,Ying Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将多保真贝叶斯优化建模为融合高斯过程bandit问题，并给出FGP-UCB算法，兼具准确融合多保真观测与简单遗憾理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真目标评估代价高昂，低保证模型可低成本提供部分信息；但现有MFBO方法要么过度简化保真层间依赖关系，要么使用缺乏性能保证的启发式采集函数。

Method: 将MFBO问题重新表述为融合高斯过程bandit，采用自回归高斯过程模型融合多保真观测以预测高保真函数，并显式量化跨保真层参数估计带来的不确定性，据此构造FGP-UCB采集策略。

Result: 给出简单遗憾（simple regret）的理论保证，弥补现有MFBO算法缺乏可证明性能界的关键缺陷；理论分析与实证研究共同验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FGP-UCB在准确性、代价权衡与理论可证性上优于既有MFBO方法，为多保真优化提供了有保证的bandit式框架。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is widely used to maximize expensive black-box functions when direct evaluation is costly. In many real-world applications, the high-fidelity objective can be approximated by a series of lower-fidelity models that provide partial but informative signals at substantially reduced cost. Multi-fidelity BO (MFBO) algorithms aim to strategically combine these information sources to achieve an optimal trade-off between accuracy and evaluation cost. However, existing MFBO methods often oversimplify the complex dependencies between fidelity levels or rely on heuristic acquisition functions that lack theoretical performance guarantees. To address these limitations, we introduce a new formulation of the MFBO problem as a fused Gaussian Process bandit and develop a corresponding Fused Gaussian Process Upper Confidence Bound (FGP-UCB) algorithm. Our approach accurately predicts the high-fidelity function by fusing multi-fidelity observations through an autoregressive Gaussian Process model and explicitly quantifies the uncertainty arising from parameter estimation across fidelity levels. The proposed method also provides theoretical simple-regret guarantees, overcoming a key limitation of existing MFBO algorithms that lack provable performance bounds. Effectiveness of the proposed method is demonstrated via both theoretical analysis and empirical studies.

</details>


### [187] [PhysRF-WorldJEPA: Transforming Heterogeneous Large Language Models into Manifold-Decoupled Millimeter-Wave Radar World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32113)
*Yicheng Huang,Feng Ke,Linhan Chen,Ziyi Jiang,Xiu Yin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PhysRF-WorldJEPA：流形解耦的 JEPA 架构，将连续电磁状态预测与跨模态语言对齐分离，在保持物理读数精度的同时显著提升雷达-文本检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 LLM 适配到毫米波雷达可实现隐私保护、全天候的物理场景理解，但指令微调把连续雷达流当静态类文本 token，导致全局雷达-文本检索近乎随机、传感器中断时物理漂移严重；而在共享主干中直接加入成对对比损失虽提升检索，却注入 O(B²) 的排斥梯度，破坏连续坐标流形（R²<0）。

Method: 采用两路设计：物理骨干由 1.0x 时钟锚定的并行双尺度 RF 编码器与约 3% LoRA 的轻量 LLM 组成，用两步状态空间 EMA-JEPA（去噪→推演）和样本轴 Batch-VICReg 预测未来 +3.0 s 物理状态；对齐头使用停止梯度，将冻结的提示状态经假阴性掩码映射到文本超球面。

Result: 在 4,541 窗口三频段（77/60.25/60 GHz）数据集上与 10 个基线对比，仅训练解耦头、冻结物理骨干，使 R→T R@10 从 5.95% 提升至 15.01%，MedRank 从 107.0 降至 60.0，且物理读数完全不变：中断 +3.0 s 躯干倾角 3.37°（R²=+0.961）、杂乱 UAV 高度 1.89 m（R²=+0.679；RF 前端 1.19 m/+0.863）、穿墙 NLOS 距离 2.04 m（R²=+0.461）、虚拟 RIS 不变性 0.9976、跌倒召回 100%。在五个 0.5B–1.8B LLM 家族（15 个零开销模型）上，检索与 75% 缺失补全在 10/10 次运行中优于 SFT，中断倾角在 4/5（Span-Euc）、2/5（Scatter）、5/5（各家族最优）中优于 SFT。

Conclusion: 解耦"连续物理状态预测"与"跨模态语言对齐"，可在不牺牲物理读出的前提下突破检索瓶颈，并在多模型家族上具有一致的可迁移性。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar enables privacy-preserving, all-weather physical scene understanding across darkness, smoke, and walls. However, standard instruction tuning treats continuous radar streams as static text-like tokens, causing near-random global radar-to-text retrieval and severe physical drift under sensor blackouts. Conversely, coupling pairwise contrastive losses (e.g., SigLIP) directly into the shared backbone improves retrieval but injects $O(B^2)$ repulsive gradients that fracture continuous coordinate manifolds ($R^2 < 0$). We present PhysRF-WorldJEPA, a manifold-decoupled Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) that separates continuous electromagnetic state prediction from cross-modal language alignment. An unattenuated 1.0x clock anchor and parallel dual-scale RF encoder feed a lean ~3% LoRA LLM trained with two-step state-space EMA-JEPA (Denoise -> Rollout) and sample-axis Batch-VICReg to predict +3.0 s physical states; a stop-gradient alignment head then maps frozen prompt states onto the text hypersphere with false-negative masking. On a 4,541-window tri-domain suite (77/60.25/60 GHz) against 10 baselines, freezing the physical backbone and training only the decoupled head lifts R->T R@10 from 5.95% to 15.01% and improves MedRank from 107.0 to 60.0 while preserving identical physical readout: 3.37 deg ($R^2=+0.961$) blackout +3.0 s torso tilt, 1.89 m ($R^2=+0.679$; 1.19 m/+0.863 at the RF front-end) cluttered UAV altitude, 2.04 m ($R^2=+0.461$) behind-wall NLOS range, 0.9976 virtual-RIS invariance, and 100% fall recall. Across five 0.5B-1.8B LLM families (15 zero-overhead models), World-JEPA improves retrieval and 75%-drop completion in 10/10 runs and blackout tilt in 4/5 (Span-Euc), 2/5 (Scatter), and 5/5 (per-family best) over SFT.

</details>


### [188] [Monte Carlo Tree Search for Classifier Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32118)
*Romain Poggi,Jesse Read*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TSCC，用蒙特卡洛树搜索增强概率分类器链的推理，在标签空间探索与候选标签集预测之间高效平衡，从而更好地估计联合概率以优化精确匹配评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签数据集的标签数量日益庞大，且训练标签可能存在噪声或缺失；在医学等领域，除置信度外，精确多标签评价（预测标签集与真实标签集完全一致）尤为关键，而这需要条件于每个输入实例的标签联合概率（标签依赖模型）。已有的概率分类器链方法要么难以扩展到大量标签，要么通过边缘分布近似联合分布，从而损害了用于精确匹配的不确定性估计。

Method: 提出TSCC：采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（Monte Carlo Tree Search）方法来增强分类器链的推理过程，在标签空间探索与有前景的标签集预测探索之间实现高效平衡，以估计标签联合概率并支持精确匹配预测。

Result: 在预测性能与计算性能两方面均取得有希望的结果。

Conclusion: TSCC通过MCTS改进概率分类器链推理，克服了大规模标签下扩展性与联合分布近似带来的不确定性估计偏差问题，为精确多标签评价提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-label classification continues to be relevant to a variety of modern tasks. Among other challenges, modern data sets are increasingly large with respect to the number of labels, and these labels may be noisy or missing in the training sets. Label-wise scores and ranking-based metrics have been popular, but optimising exact multi-label evaluation (where a predicted set of labels should match the true set exactly) is particularly relevant for many domains, e.g., in medicine; alongside some measure of confidence. This requires an estimation of joint probability across labels (a model of label dependence; conditioned on each input instance). A family of methods known as probabilistic classifier chains provides such inference. However, these existing varieties either do not scale to a large number of labels or do so by approximating joint via marginal distributions in a way that compromises the estimation of uncertainty for exact match accuracy. We overcome this issue by proposing TSCC; leveraging a Monte Carlo Tree Search methodology to enhance chain inference via an efficient balance between the exploration of label space and the exploration of promising label-set predictions. Results are promising both in terms of predictive and computational performance.

</details>


### [189] [Rethinking Reasoning Effort Training in Large Language Models: On-Policy Distillation or Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32131)
*Tianliang Lv,Hao Li,Xinchao Xu,Zhengkun Zhang,Ju Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统比较了推理努力（reasoning effort）训练中强化学习(RL)与on-policy蒸馏(OPD)的差异：序列级奖励擅长压缩推理长度但难以分离努力层级，而OPD的逐状态稠密目标能实现层级分离，并可迁移回RL与自然语言指令控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在复杂任务上能力强，但实际部署中如何控制其推理努力（思考长度/深度）仍是难题。作者希望厘清"压缩推理"与"分离努力层级"分别由什么训练信号驱动，从而为可控推理提供方法论依据。

Method: 对比研究长度惩罚RL与on-policy蒸馏(OPD)；在33个基准上评估多教师OPD的低/中/高三级策略；从训练信号层面分析序列级奖励与逐状态稠密目标的差异；提出在单一RL阶段仅对高难度层级加token级KL锚，并用自然语言努力指令诱导分离；在未见过的agentic任务上检验两种信号的可迁移性。

Result: 长度惩罚RL可在无精度损失下将AIME25思考token减少70%，但联合训练时层级塌缩；多教师OPD实现层级分离：低、中层级比高层级分别少58%和43%的思考token，高层级较参考策略提升3.1分；单token级KL锚可在单阶段RL中实现分离，自然语言努力指令使分离从训练初期即出现，在匹配思考长度下与多教师OPD竞争力相当；稠密目标构建的分离仅泛化到训练中见过的状态，在未见agentic任务上不迁移（该处努力主要作用于交互轮数），而序列级奖励带来的压缩可迁移。

Conclusion: 压缩推理与分离努力层级是正交目标，分别需要序列级奖励与逐状态稠密目标；实践中可用token级KL锚或自然语言指令在RL中合成层级控制，但需注意稠密目标诱导的分离泛化性有限。

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong capabilities on complex tasks, yet how to control their reasoning effort remains a significant challenge for practical deployment. This paper provides a systematic investigation of reasoning-effort training, contrasting reinforcement learning (RL) with on-policy distillation (OPD). We find that compressing reasoning and separating effort tiers are governed by different signals. Length-penalized RL reliably compresses a single policy, reducing thinking on AIME25 by 70\% without accuracy loss, but collapses the tiers when they are trained jointly. Multi-teacher OPD separates them: across 33 benchmarks, low and medium use 58\% and 43\% fewer thinking tokens than high, while high improves on the reference policy by 3.1 points. We trace this difference to the training signal. A sequence-level reward cannot tell tier-specific behavior from shared behavior, whereas OPD supplies a tier-specific target at every visited state, including the decision to stop. Building on this analysis, we show that a single token-level KL anchor on the high tier is sufficient to learn separated tiers within one RL stage, and that natural-language effort instructions make this separation emerge from the start of training, yielding a policy competitive with multi-teacher OPD at matched thinking length. Finally, the separation built by dense targets holds only on the states seen in training: it does not carry over to unseen agentic tasks, where effort acts mainly through the number of interaction turns, whereas the compression induced by sequence-level rewards does.

</details>


### [190] [Calibrated Reading of Latent Reasoning and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32143)
*Zixi Wang,Gaohao Zhou,Yubo Huang,Xin Lai,Yushe Cao,Jingzehua Xu,Shuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Calibrated Latent Reader (CLR)，一个紧凑的 transformer 读取器，通过任务条件化的状态-候选答案交互预测推理结果与消息内容，并借助 source adapter 实现跨模型迁移、借助 conformal calibration 提供覆盖与误报保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间推理与键值通信将中间信息存储在连续表示中，难以直接检视模型行为；现有激活读取器在任务专用探针的低成本与语言模型解码器的灵活性之间存在权衡，缺少兼具高效、可解释且带统计保证的方案。

Method: CLR 采用紧凑 transformer 结构，将隐状态与候选答案进行任务条件化交互以预测推理结果及消息内容；引入 source adapter 支持跨模型迁移；使用 conformal calibration 在可交换性假设下提供覆盖率与误报率保证。

Result: 在两个 CODI 模型上，CLR 相比 MLP 与 attention 探针在最终答案预测和参考值成员判定上提升 4–18 个百分点；在 CODI-GPT-2 上以少 437–648 倍的推理 FLOPs 达到与 1.7B LatentQA verbalizer 相当的准确率；在合成的 Qwen3-4B 通信中，以名义 5% 假阳性率检测出 93.1% 的消息替换。

Conclusion: CLR 在保持低计算成本的同时显著优于传统探针，并具备跨模型可迁移性与统计校准保证，为潜空间通信的可检视性与完整性验证提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Latent reasoning and key-value communication store intermediate information in continuous representations, limiting direct inspection of model behavior. Existing activation readers trade the low cost of task-specific probes against the flexibility of language-model decoders. We propose the Calibrated Latent Reader, CLR, a compact transformer that predicts reasoning outcomes and message content through task-conditioned interactions between states and candidate answers. Source adapters support transfer across models, while conformal calibration provides coverage and false-alarm guarantees under exchangeability. On two CODI models, CLR improves accuracy over MLP and attention probes by 4-18 percentage points for final-answer prediction and reference-value membership. On CODI-GPT-2, it achieves accuracy comparable to a 1.7B LatentQA-based verbalizer using 437-648 times fewer inference FLOPs. On synthetic Qwen3-4B communications, comparing decoded facts with public commitments detects 93.1% of message substitutions at a nominal 5% false-positive rate.

</details>


### [191] [RetraQ: Manifold-Constrained Critic Guidance for Flow Policies in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32148)
*Xuanzhuo Liu,Wu Chen,Ruixiang Wu,Shuai Yuan,Chuankang Li,Tongxin Li,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RetraQ利用预训练流匹配策略的几何结构约束critic梯度的方向与落点，避免critic外推误差被利用，从而提升引导稳定性与真实回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的critic可通过将采样动作引向高价值方向改进流匹配策略，但即便critic对行为数据拟合良好，其动作梯度仍可能具有误导性：指向数据支撑区域之外的梯度分量受数据约束弱，易被引导过程利用critic的外推误差。

Method: RetraQ用预训练流编码的几何同时约束critic引导的方向与目标点。对冻结流做一次速度评估得到去噪映射；用该映射的雅可比矩阵过滤critic梯度，并将映射本身作用于更新后的动作以进行矫正。论文分析了两操作近似切空间过滤与到行为流形的回缩（retraction）的条件。同一更新既用于测试时动作引导，也用于策略蒸馏与在线强化学习的目标构造。

Result: 在受控实验中，RetraQ提升真实回报，同时保持精炼动作靠近数据支撑区域，其蒸馏目标比原始critic上升更稳定。LIBERO与OGBench的离线到在线训练中，RetraQ平均最终成功率高于所评估的critic引导基线。真实插头插入任务中，测试时RetraQ将行为克隆π0.5策略成功率从55.0%提至76.7%；在线训练30分钟达98.3%，一小时内达100%。

Conclusion: 以流几何进行的梯度过滤与回缩矫正是一种通用、有效的critic引导方案，可同时用于推理时引导、蒸馏与在线RL，显著提升稳定性与任务成功率。

Abstract: A learned critic can improve a pretrained flow-matching policy by steering sampled actions toward higher predicted value. However, even a critic that fits the behavior data well can provide misleading action gradients. Gradient components pointing away from the data-supported region are weakly constrained by behavior data and can lead guidance to exploit critic extrapolation errors. We introduce RetraQ (Retraction-based Q-guidance), which uses geometry encoded by the pretrained flow to constrain both the direction and the destination of critic guidance. A single velocity evaluation of the frozen flow defines a denoising map. RetraQ filters the critic gradient with the map's Jacobian and applies the map itself to correct the updated action. Our analysis characterizes when these operations approximate tangent filtering and retraction onto the behavior manifold. The same update guides actions at test time and provides targets for policy distillation and online reinforcement learning. In controlled studies, RetraQ improves true return while keeping refined actions close to the data-supported region, and its distillation targets remain more stable than those of raw critic ascent. In offline-to-online training on LIBERO and OGBench, RetraQ achieves higher mean final success than the evaluated critic-guidance baselines. On a real-world plug-insertion task, test-time RetraQ improves the success rate of a behavior-cloned $π_{0.5}$ policy from 55.0% to 76.7%. Online training further increases success to 98.3% in only 30 minutes and 100% within one hour. Project page: https://2027ICLR.github.io/retraq/

</details>


### [192] [Multi-view Random-set Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32170)
*Mengchi Zuo,Tianyang Wang,Cai Xu,Ziyu Guan,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出多视图随机集学习（MRSL）范式，用随机集保留各视图对候选类别集合的支持，并在跨视图集合交集中完成证据融合，缓解视图退化问题带来的证据扭曲。在8个真实数据集上平均准确率提升4.71%，ECE降低6.33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图数据普遍存在"视图退化"现象：单个视图仅含部分判别信息，无法独立确定实例标签。若强制其输出明确的单类证据，会扭曲证据学习并误导后续融合。现有方法要么只为单类分配证据、未显式表达未决类组，要么在融合前把复合信念投影到单类，无法直接通过跨视图集合交集细化候选类。

Method: 提出MRSL范式：1）量化类间重叠度，为每个视图挑选紧凑的候选类别集合，避免建模全部类别子集的指数开销；2）进行集合级融合，将联合支持分配给集合交集，使互补视图在概率转换前先缩小候选类别范围。

Result: 在合成数据与8个真实数据集上验证了所提重叠度量的有效性；MRSL在全部8个数据集上取得最高准确率，相较最强基线平均准确率提升4.71%，平均期望校准误差（ECE）降低6.33%。

Conclusion: 以随机集替代单类证据、将融合放在集合层级并显式利用集合交集，是应对视图退化的有效途径，可同时提升多视图学习的准确性与置信度校准。数据集与代码将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Trusted multi-view learning aims to integrate heterogeneous views for accurate and reliable predictions. However, multi-view data often exhibit the view degradation phenomenon: individual views contain only partial discriminative information and cannot independently determine the instance-level label. Requiring such views to produce unambiguous class evidence can distort evidence learning and mislead subsequent fusion. Existing methods addressing this problem have at least one of the following drawbacks: (1) assigning evidence to individual classes without explicitly representing unresolved class groups; (2) projecting composite beliefs onto individual classes before fusion, preventing direct refinement of candidate groups through cross-view set intersections.
  To address these limitations, we propose a new Multi-view Random-Set Learning (MRSL) paradigm, which preserves view-specific support for candidate class sets and refines candidate classes through cross-view fusion. Specifically, we quantify class overlap to select a compact collection of sets for each view, avoiding the exponential cost of modeling all class subsets. We then perform set-level fusion by assigning joint support to set intersections, allowing complementary views to narrow the candidate classes before probability conversion. Experiments on synthetic and eight real-world datasets validate the proposed overlap measure and demonstrate that MRSL achieves the highest accuracy on all eight datasets. Compared with the strongest baselines, MRSL improves average accuracy by 4.71% and reduces average expected calibration error by 6.33%. All datasets and code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [193] [A Unified Control Framework for Gradient Inversion Defense in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32174)
*Lianhai Ren,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一控制动力学框架理解现有梯度扰动防御，并据此提出近正交梯度投影（NODE）方法，在保持固定余弦相似度的正交方向注入扰动，显著改善联邦学习中的隐私—效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度扰动是防御联邦学习梯度反演攻击的有效手段，但会损害优化过程，造成隐私与效用之间的持续张力；现有防御公式各异，缺乏统一理解，难以给出通用的收敛性与安全性条件。

Method: 将现有梯度扰动防御统一到受控动力学框架；在此基础上提出NODE，仅在与原梯度正交的方向注入扰动，使防御后梯度与真实梯度保持固定余弦相似度，从而保留一阶下降方向。

Result: 在标准优化假设下建立了收敛性保证，并在温和的梯度分布条件下证明NODE仍能有效防止反演攻击；在CIFAR-10、ImageNet、Chest X-ray等基准数据集及多种梯度反演攻击下的实验表明，该方法在广泛训练设置中取得了优于现有防御的隐私—效用权衡。

Conclusion: 梯度扰动防御可统一为受控动力学，NODE通过正交方向注入扰动、保持固定余弦相似度，在理论与实践上同时兼顾收敛性与隐私保护，实现了更优的隐私—效用权衡。

Abstract: Gradient perturbations are widely recognized as an effective defense against gradient inversion attacks in federated learning, but they are equally known to impair optimization, creating a persistent tension between privacy and utility. In this paper, we show that existing gradient perturbation defenses, despite their different formulations, can be unified under a controlled dynamics framework, providing a cohesive understanding of their optimization behavior and enabling the derivation of universal convergence and safety conditions without relying on case-specific analyses. Leveraging this observation, we propose ``Near Orthogonal Gradient Projection (NODE)'', which injects perturbations only along directions orthogonal to the original gradient so that the defended gradient maintains a fixed cosine similarity with the true gradient. By preserving the first-order descent direction, NODE substantially reduces the optimization cost of strong perturbations while maintaining effective protection against gradient inversion attacks. We theoretically establish convergence guarantees under standard optimization assumptions and further show that, under mild conditions on the gradient distribution, NODE remains effective in preventing inversion attacks. Comprehensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets, including CIFAR-10, ImageNet, and Chest X-ray, together with a variety of gradient inversion attacks, demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an improved privacy--utility trade-off over existing defenses across a broad range of training settings.

</details>


### [194] [Steerable by Design: Conditional Pretraining for Robust Control of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32199)
*Henry Castillo,Yanchen Liu,Claudio Mayrink Verdun,Dylan Hadfield-Menell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分离"指令通道"与"数据通道"的预训练方法与架构，训练出Mancelle-3B/400M，实现内生可控的语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用下一词预测预训练，对齐与控制被推到训练末1%或推理期干预；且指令与数据共用单一输入通道，导致越狱与上下文对齐漂移等失败模式。

Method: 设计编码器提取待生成文本的高层语义特征，通过交叉注意力注入主token流，且该控制通道不可被token流写回；预训练中模型学习依赖这些特征预测下一词，使通道成为有效的控制手柄。

Result: Mancelle-3B在同等开放模型上的引导强度高于基于提示和基于激活的方法，同时保持流畅性，并能抵抗击败基线的黑盒对抗与软提示反向引导；以Mancelle-400M在更受控基线上复现。

Conclusion: 若在早期并有意识地设计，稳健的可控性可内建于语言模型之中。

Abstract: Our goal is to build language models that are steerable by design. Today's language models are pretrained with next-token prediction, which relegates alignment and control to the final 1% of training tokens or inference-time interventions. These models repurpose a single input channel for both instructions and data, leading to common failure modes where the model mistakenly treats data as instructions such as intentional jailbreaks and unintentional in-context alignment drift. To correct this, we propose a pretraining method and architecture that separates the two from the very start, producing Mancelle-3B. An encoder processes high-level semantic features of upcoming text with cross-attention that prevents the token stream from writing back into this control channel. During pretraining, the model learns to rely on these features to predict the next token, turning the channel into an effective handle for steering the model. Mancelle-3B steers more strongly than prompt- and activation-based methods on comparable open models while maintaining fluency, and it withstands adversarial black-box and soft-prompt counter-steering that defeats those baselines. We reproduce this against a more controlled baseline with Mancelle-400M. Our results show that robust control can be built into language models if we do so early and intentionally.

</details>


### [195] [Who gets the information? An $α$-fair label-coverage framework for multi-label feature selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32200)
*Zhenzhi Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示多标签特征选择中信息论滤波器的相关性分配实为隐式社会福利函数，提出FLIC在α-公平福利框架下统一十余种相关性项，并证明贪心选择性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多标签特征选择的信息论滤波器对"相关性应在标签间如何分配"存在分歧：一类放大只服务少数标签的特征，另一类追求各标签信息均衡。作者希望指出这些分歧并非技术细节，而是隐含的社会福利函数选择，从而给出统一的理论框架。

Method: 将标签视为以获得信息为效用的智能体，定义FLIC：贪心地最大化各标签信息覆盖度的α-公平福利。单参数α从功利主义(α=0)、比例公平(α=1)过渡到max-min(α→∞)，已有相关性项多为其特例、一阶近似或极限。使用偏差校正的、标签级条件信息，且无需相关性-冗余权衡参数。理论证明：当各标签信息为单调子模时，目标对任意α≥0单调子模，贪心获得(1-1/e)近似；α<0时失效，max-min极限不可近似。并以Fano与Hellman-Raviv不等式将原始覆盖度联系到Bayes Hamming损失，将归一化覆盖联系到标签级相对误差。

Result: 在22个数据集上，FLIC在9项指标中的7项上优于14个信息论滤波器，117次成对比较中86次显著胜出且无一显著落败。在17种方法中，配两个分类器时在ranking loss、coverage、macro-AUC上排名第一，平均排名最优（与ML-kNN接近）；Hamming loss、one-error、micro-F1上则落后于三个嵌入式方法。

Conclusion: 多标签信息论滤波器的相关性分配可被统一理解为福利函数选择，α-公平框架为设计可证明近似的选择准则提供了原则性基础，FLIC在信息论滤波器中表现领先，但在部分分类指标上仍不及嵌入式方法。

Abstract: Recent information-theoretic filters for multi-label feature selection disagree on how to distribute relevance over labels: some amplify features that inform a few labels, others balance what every label receives. We show these choices are implicit social welfare functions. Treating labels as agents whose utility is the information they receive, we propose FLIC, which greedily maximises the $α$-fair welfare of per-label information coverage. A single parameter $α$ moves from utilitarian ($α=0$) through proportionally fair ($α=1$) to max-min selection ($α\to\infty$), and a dozen published relevance terms lie on this axis, most as special cases, first-order approximations or limits. The objective is monotone submodular for every $α\ge0$ whenever each label's information is, so greedy selection is a $(1-1/e)$-approximation; this fails for $α<0$, and the max-min limit is inapproximable. Fano's and the Hellman-Raviv inequalities tie raw coverage to the Bayes Hamming loss and normalised coverage to per-label relative error. FLIC uses bias-corrected, label-wise conditional information and has no relevance-redundancy trade-off parameter. On 22 data sets, FLIC is the best of 14 information-theoretic filters on seven of nine metrics, winning 86 of 117 pairwise comparisons significantly and losing none significantly. Among 17 methods it ranks first on ranking loss, coverage and macro-AUC with two classifiers and has the best mean rank (narrowly with ML-kNN), while three embedded methods lead on Hamming loss, one-error and micro-F1.

</details>


### [196] [Joint Classification and Generation on Tabular Data: an Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32225)
*Tim d'Hondt,Oleksii Furman,Damian Kwolek,Jan Poręba,Maciej Zieba,Mykola Pechenizkiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在29个分类数据集上，以实测FLOPs与墙钟时间为预算，比较了四类表格联合模型（EBM、概率电路、表格基础模型、表格扩散模型）以及"单独分类器+生成器"配对方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格机器学习应用常同时需要分类器与真实样本生成器，默认训练两个独立模型；单一联合模型可从一套权重同时提供二者，但"哪类联合模型在何种计算预算下更优"缺乏系统证据。

Method: 基于四类SOTA表格建模家族构建联合模型，在29个分类数据集上按实测FLOPs和墙钟时间对比；采用两个聚合评分分别刻画预测与生成能力；并将联合模型与不同预算下所有"分类器+生成器"配对进行对比。

Result: 最小预算下概率电路最优；预算增大后，逐列回归生成的表格基础模型TabICL最优。相对于独立模型配对，联合模型很少占优，但常非常接近最佳配对。

Conclusion: 联合模型在多数情况下并非首选，但以接近最优的性能免去训练与维护两个模型的成本，在特定预算下（低预算的概率电路、较高预算的TabICL）是可辩护的选择。

Abstract: Many applications of machine learning on tabular data need both a classifier and a generator of realistic records. The default is to train two separate models, whereas a single joint model promises both from one set of weights. Several families of tabular deep learning models can be turned into such a joint model, which raises the question of which joint model is preferable at which computational budget. In this empirical study, we introduce and compare joint models based on four state-of-the-art tabular data modeling families (energy-based models, probabilistic circuits, tabular foundation models and tabular diffusion models) against measured FLOPs and wall-clock time, on 29 classification datasets. Methods are evaluated based on two defined aggregate scores that take into account different aspects of prediction and generation. We then also compare these joint models with every pair of a separate classifier and generator at different computational budgets, to see when a joint model may be a defendible choice against the regular practice of keeping a separate classifier and generator. We find that the best joint model is a probabilistic circuit at the smallest budgets and, beyond that, a tabular foundation model that generates column by column using a regressor (TabICL). Compared with pairs of separate models, this study finds a joint model is rarely preferable but often very close to the best pair, without the hassle of training and maintaining two separate models.

</details>


### [197] [Seldon TFM family: Seldon-Mini, Seldon and Seldon+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32257)
*Stanislas Angebault,Nicolas Atienza,Nicola Cancedda,Aziz Fouché,Salomé Gobbi,Ekaterina Iakovleva,Nicolas Makaroff,Arthur Nahmias,Paul Novello,Alexandre Pasquiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Seldon 是一组表格基础模型，其中分类器 Seldon+ 在自建 TabBench（124 个分类数据集）及缺失值变体上排名第一，在 BeyondArena IID 与 TabBench-Bio 上也居首，在 TabArena 分类上第二；开源权重模型 Seldon-Mini(27M) 与 Seldon(213M) 位于 TabArena Elo–推理时间 Pareto 前沿，支持分类与回归，仅用合成表格训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据缺乏通用基础模型范式，现有模型在精度与推理效率之间难以兼顾；作者希望构建精度领先、同时覆盖不同规模/时延预算的表格基础模型族。

Method: 提出 Seldon 模型族：分类器 Seldon+ 与开源权重模型 Seldon-Mini（27M）、Seldon（213M）。架构类似 Kumo-Tabular、TabFM、TabPFN-3.5，仅使用合成表格数据训练，统一支持分类与回归任务。以自建 TabBench（124 个分类数据集，含缺失值变体）及 BeyondArena、TabArena、TabBench-Bio 等公开基准评估，并以 Elo 对推理时间的 Pareto 前沿刻画精度-效率权衡。

Result: Seldon+ 在 TabBench 及其缺失值变体 15 个模型中排名第一；在 BeyondArena IID 分类与 TabBench-Bio 上第一，TabArena 分类第二。TabArena 上 Seldon 在 109 条目中列第四、Seldon-Mini 第七；BeyondArena IID 赛道 42 条目中 Seldon 列第二。Seldon-Mini 与 Seldon 均位于 TabArena Elo-推理时间 Pareto 前沿（无模型同时更快且更准）。

Conclusion: 结果表明仅用合成数据训练的表格基础模型可在多个公开基准达到领先精度，并通过小/大规模变体覆盖精度-时延权衡；自建基准 TabBench 也构成评估贡献之一。

Abstract: We introduce Seldon, a family of tabular foundation models. Our classifier Seldon+ ranks first among 15 models on TabBench, our benchmark of 124 classification datasets, and on its variant with missing values. In our own runs of public benchmarks, it also ranks first on BeyondArena IID classification and TabBench-Bio, and second on TabArena classification. The open-weight models Seldon-Mini (27M parameters) and Seldon (213M parameters) both lie on the TabArena Pareto front of Elo against inference time: no entry is both more accurate and faster. On TabArena, Seldon ranks fourth and Seldon-Mini seventh among 109 leaderboard entries, and on the IID track of BeyondArena, Seldon ranks second among 42 entries. Seldon-Mini and Seldon handle both classification and regression, use an architecture similar to those of Kumo-Tabular, TabFM and TabPFN-3.5, and are trained on synthetic tables only.

</details>


### [198] [Pando: Noise-Coupled Parallel Draft Trees for Efficient Edge Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32269)
*Joren Dumoulin,Hyoukjun Kwon,Subhankar Pal,Steven Li,Vignesh Vivekraja,Marian Verhelst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pando在并行草稿器之上构建动态草稿树，解除草稿链长度受块大小限制的问题；其噪声耦合剪枝在草稿前采样目标模型噪声以排序分支，无损且无需重训；16节点树在边缘设备上实现5.3×加速与4.2×能耗下降，最佳配置仍为访存受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码每步仅生成一个token且每次重载整个模型，边缘端推理受限于内存带宽、算力闲置。投机解码利用闲置算力一次验证多个草稿token。DFlash、DSpark等并行草稿器一次前向生成整块草稿token，但其部署草稿链长度受训练块大小上限约束，导致验证阶段大量计算预算未被利用。

Method: 提出Pando：(1) 在并行草稿器上构建动态草稿树，取消草稿链长度上限；(2) 噪声耦合剪枝——在草稿前抽取目标模型采样噪声，并在该噪声下对候选分支排序，保留目标模型实际会采样的分支，过程无损、无需重训或额外模型前向；(3) 将算子级roofline归一化到AR解码，把各平台统一约简到脊点（ridge point），以便跨硬件优化与评估Pando配置。

Result: 噪声耦合在Qwen3-8B上将接受长度提升最高8.4%。代表性边缘设备上，16节点Pando树相比AR解码实现5.3×加速与4.2×能耗降低；比DSpark已发布草稿链快25%，比EAGLE-3树快27%且能耗效率高72%。所建模的所有边缘设备上最优配置仍为访存受限，增加算力不降低时延。

Conclusion: 动态草稿树可有效突破并行草稿器的块大小上限，充分利用验证算力预算；噪声耦合剪枝以零额外开销显著提升接受长度；归一化roofline提供了跨硬件配置分析框架。由于最优配置始终访存受限，未来收益需来自降低内存流量而非增加算力。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) decoding emits one token per forward pass, reloading the full model each time, so LLM inference on edge devices is memory-bound while the compute units sit idle. Speculative decoding puts that idle compute to work by verifying several drafted tokens in a single pass. State-of-the-art parallel drafters such as DFlash and DSpark propose an entire block of draft tokens in one forward pass, which makes drafting compute-efficient. However, they are deployed with draft chains whose length is capped by the block size they are trained for, leaving most of the computational budget for verification unused.
  To tackle this problem, we propose Pando, which builds dynamic draft trees on top of a parallel drafter and thereby removes this cap. Pando employs noise-coupled pruning, which draws the target model's sampling noise before drafting and ranks candidate branches under that noise, so the tree retains the branches the target will actually sample. Noise coupling is lossless and requires no retraining or additional model pass, yet increases acceptance length by up to $8.4\%$ on Qwen3-8B. To optimize and evaluate Pando configurations across hardware, we normalize an operator-level roofline to AR decoding, reducing every platform to its ridge point. On a representative edge device, a 16-node Pando tree attains $5.3\times$ speedup and $4.2\times$ energy reduction over AR decoding: $25\%$ faster than DSpark's released chain, and $27\%$ faster and $72\%$ more energy-efficient than an EAGLE-3 tree. The best configurations remain memory-bound on every edge device we model, so added compute capacity does not reduce their latency.

</details>


### [199] [CoMNO: Commutator Memory Neural Operators for Long-Horizon PDE Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32274)
*Han Xiao,Xin Zhang,Yuhan Zou,Zhenzhong Wang,Donglin Cao,Dazhen Lin,Min Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Commutator Memory Neural Operator (CoMNO)，通过对比"先降采样再rollout"与"先rollout再降采样"两操作的差异（交换子）提取细尺度动力学细节，并用记忆模块沿轨迹存储与检索这些特征，生成空间自适应修正，提升长时程预测精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子求解长时程非线性PDE时，由于无法捕捉细尺度细节，近似误差沿预测轨迹累积，导致长时程rollout精度下降与不稳定；传统数值方法计算代价高。

Method: 设计交换子记忆神经算子：1）识别"先对高分辨率场降采样再rollout"与"先rollout再降采样"两种操作之间的性能差距（交换子），该差距中编码了被降采样丢弃的细尺度动力学细节；2）通过交换两操作捕获这些细节；3）引入记忆模块沿轨迹存储细尺度特征，并在rollout过程中检索，生成空间自适应修正项。

Result: 在五个系统上的实证结果表明，CoMNO提升了预测精度与长时程rollout的稳定性。

Conclusion: 利用操作交换子揭示并捕获降采样中丢失的细尺度动力学信息，结合记忆式检索与自适应修正，是缓解神经算子长时程误差累积的有效途径。

Abstract: Solving nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs) over long time horizons is computationally expensive using traditional numerical methods. While neural operators provide an efficient alternative, they suffer from the accumulation of approximation errors along the predicted trajectory, typically caused by the failure to capture fine-scale details. In this work, we design a Commutator Memory Neural Operator that identifies fine-scale dynamical details by commuting two operations and leverages these details to enhance the neural operator's capability in capturing fine-scale features over long time horizons. Specifically, we identify a performance gap between two operations: downsampling the high-resolution field before rollout prediction versus performing rollout prediction before downsampling. We observe that fine-scale dynamical details are often encoded in this gap. By commuting these two operations, the fine-scale details removed during downsampling can be captured. Furthermore, we employ a memory module to store these fine-scale features along the trajectory and retrieve them during rollout, generating spatially adaptive corrections that enhance long-horizon fidelity. Empirical results across five systems demonstrate that CoMNO improves prediction accuracy and long-horizon rollout stability.

</details>


### [200] [DAiSy: Online Data Selection by Adaptive Utility Estimation for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32294)
*Simin Fan,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAiSy：用线性辅助模型从冻结文档嵌入预测数据效用分数，替代逐候选文档的前向-反向传播打分，实现高效在线数据选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线选择器基于梯度效用分数对候选文档排序，需对每个候选文档做完整前向-反向传播，打分成本等同于训练，但仅极小比例文档用于实际更新，计算与基础设施开销大。

Method: 提出DAiSy：轻量线性辅助模型，在冻结文档嵌入上回归预测数据效用分数；该线性估计器与主语言模型同步更新，以常规训练步反向传播得到的精确梯度效用分数为监督目标；打分时仅需一次线性变换。

Result: 在英文与多语言LLM预训练中（多种引导目标设计）验证了有效性与效率；DAiSy带来的提升可延续至指令微调阶段。

Conclusion: 以低成本线性代理打分替代梯度对齐计算，可在大幅降低开销的同时保持并传递在线数据选择收益。

Abstract: Model-aware online data selection prioritizes the training documents that contribute the most given the model's current state. Existing online selectors rank candidates upon gradient-based data utility scores, which requires the full forward-backward pass of the main model on every candidate document. Scoring each document therefore pays as much as training on it, yet only a small fraction is used for the real model update. We introduce DAiSy, a lightweight linear auxiliary model that learns to predict data utility scores from frozen document embeddings, which eliminates the computation overhead and infrastructure burdens. The linear estimator is updated alongside the main language model, fitting to the exact gradient-based utility scores obtained from the backward pass of regular training steps. Consequently, scoring a candidate requires only a linear transformation, bypassing the costly gradient-alignment computation. We demonstrate its effectiveness and efficiency for LLM pretraining in English and multilingual scenarios, with various steering target designs. The improvements from DAiSy can consistently survive the instruction-tuning phase.

</details>


### [201] [SPRUNN: Structural Proximity for Pruning in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32317)
*Babak Rokha,Ali Azarpeyvanda,Alireza Khanteymoori,Tara Ghasempouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPRUNN：基于结构邻近性(SP)准则的卷积层滤波器剪枝，并联合全连接层细粒度剪枝，配合重训练阶段学习率精调策略，实现高精度压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN参数与计算量巨大，推理耗时；剪枝的核心难点在于如何在尽量不损失精度的情况下选出应剪除的参数。

Method: 1) 以Structural Proximity计算滤波器间相似度：将每个滤波器与其余所有滤波器逐一比较，识别卷基层中冗余/不重要的滤波器并剪除；2) 将该方法与全连接层的细粒度剪枝结合，可压缩同时含卷积层与FC层的网络；3) 在重训练阶段引入学习率精调策略以提升剪枝模型精度。

Result: 在多种网络架构与数据集上，SPRUNN在保持高精度的同时实现显著压缩。

Conclusion: 基于结构邻近性的两阶段（滤波器级+细粒度）剪枝结合学习率精调，是一种兼顾压缩率与精度的有效DNN压缩方案。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) have demonstrated significant accuracy across various machine learning tasks. However, these networks suffer from a huge number of parameters and computations, which leads to time-consuming processes. Consequently, parameter pruning approaches have been proposed to compress DNNs and efficiently implement them. The main challenge in pruning is selecting the appropriate parameters for pruning without significantly damaging the accuracy. In this paper, we propose SPRUNN, an algorithmic pruning approach based on the structural proximity criterion for filter pruning in convolutional layers of deep neural networks. Structural Proximity (SP) calculates filter similarity by considering all filters and comparing each one with the others, effectively identifying less important filters for pruning in the convolutional layers. Furthermore, we integrate this approach with fine-grained pruning in Fully Connected(FC)layerstocompresstheDNNsthatinclude both convolutional and FC layers.Toenhancetheaccuracyoftheprunedmodel,weproposealearning rate refinement strategy during the retraining phase. Experimental results demonstrate that SPRUNN achieves significant compression with high accuracy across diverse architectures and datasets.

</details>


### [202] [When Exact Token Importance Is Not Enough: Limits of Top-K Selection in LLM Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32324)
*Irmaan Mirzanejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文质疑长上下文模型中"逐位置独立打分取Top-K"的稀疏注意力支持集选择策略，论证独立排序并非最优，并提出联合支持集质量分析与SwapCert审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM在注意力查询时仅保留有限历史token位置，常用做法是独立打分并保留最高分K个。但注意力权重会在所选集合上重新归一化，两个各自重要的位置未必构成最优组合；作者要检验即便每个重要性分数精确度量"单独删除该位置导致的下游变化"，独立排序是否依然充分。

Method: 构造性反例分析：一个四位置构造与参数族证明排序对与最优对之间的KL散度差距可无界放大；给出精确交换恒等式与对任意K≥2的偏好反转解释交互效应。实验方面对SmolLM2、Gemma、Qwen进行冻结模型穷举审计（24位置候选池、Top-4、完整枚举），并提出同预算单交换审计方法SwapCert检测可改进支持集；另以KL-margin证书关联支持集保真度与下一token稳定性，并在K=128/256进行更大预算、多层缓存解码实验。

Result: 四位置构造中排序对产生最优对133.2倍的KL散度且差距无界；穷举审计中精确单例Top-4在55/60、49/60、60/60案例里可获至少10%相对改进；SwapCert在164个此类案例中检出157个并返回可直接检验的改进支持集。

Conclusion: 联合支持集质量是稀疏注意力分析的核心对象，独立逐位置排序在理论上可严重次优；结果动机指向推理约束下下游感知的支持集选择方法。

Abstract: Long-context language models often retain only a limited set of past token positions for an attention query. A common strategy scores positions individually and keeps the highest-ranked K. Yet attention weights are renormalized over the chosen set: two individually important positions need not form the best pair. We ask whether independent ranking suffices even when each importance score measures the exact downstream change in next-token predictions caused by deleting that position alone. It does not. A four-position construction makes the ranked pair incur 133.2 times the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence of the optimal pair, and a parametric family proves that this multiplicative gap is unbounded. An exact exchange identity and preference reversals for every K >= 2 explain the interaction. In exhaustive frozen-model audits of SmolLM2, Gemma, and Qwen, exact-singleton Top-4 leaves at least 10% relative improvement within a fully enumerated 24-position candidate pool in 55/60, 49/60, and 60/60 cases, respectively. SwapCert, a same-budget one-swap audit, detects 157/164 such cases and returns directly checkable improving supports. A KL-margin certificate relates support fidelity to next-token stability; larger-budget, multi-layer cached-decoding experiments measure predictive fidelity at K = 128 and 256. These results establish joint support quality as an essential object of analysis for sparse attention and motivate downstream-aware support selection under inference constraints.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Hard Tokens: Predictive Memory for Multimodal Protein Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32347)
*Xiaoliang Shi,Zichen Wang,Zhijian Wei,Zhanpeng Shi,Zhenghong Zhou,Yunpeng Wang,Shuangjia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Predictive Memory (PredMem) 自条件循环机制，在迭代掩码解码的每一步将未提交位置的软概率信念（Top-k 期望嵌入）跨步传递，显著提升多模态蛋白质语言模型的无条件序列-结构协同生成成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态蛋白质语言模型（DPLM-2、ESM3 等）采用迭代掩码解码来统一序列与离散结构 token，但标准解码器每一步都会重置掩码：仅保留少量新提交的硬 token，其余未解析位点一律回退为同一 [MASK] 嵌入。在无固定外部锚点的开放式协同生成中，这种无状态转移限制了单步细化深度，也使两条模态轨道缺少稳定的跨步软锚点，导致收敛缓慢或退化为重复性崩塌。作者观察到未提交的预测随步数演化具有明显时间一致性而非随机噪声，因此希望把这类软信念作为跨步记忆加以利用。

Method: 提出 Predictive Memory（PredMem）：一种无需训练的自条件递归。每一步将原始 logits 汇总为每个残基上的概率加权 Top-k 期望嵌入，并以仿射方式插值到下一步符合条件的掩码输入中，不增加去噪器计算成本。该机制在 DPLM-2、DPLM-2.1 与 ESM3 上以插件形式接入，并进行对照诊断实验（算力、熵、轨迹稳定性）以定位增益来源。

Result: 在 DPLM-2、DPLM-2.1、ESM3 上，无条件序列-结构协同生成成功率分别提升 3.6、17.7、48.7 个百分点；同时改善质量-多样性权衡并抑制重复性模式崩塌。对照诊断表明增益主要来自无锚点跨模态共演化中的早期轨迹稳定，而非额外算力或单纯的熵降低。

Conclusion: PredMem 以零额外推理开销将迭代掩码解码由无状态转移改造为带软记忆的状态化过程，是一种高效的多模态离散生成推理机制；其有效性根源在于早期轨迹稳定化，对无外部锚点条件下的开放式蛋白质协同设计具有通用性与可移植性。

Abstract: Multimodal protein language models unify sequence and discrete structural tokens via iterative masked decoding, offering a promising paradigm for joint protein design. However, standard decoders rely on a memoryless mask reset at each iteration: only a sparse subset of newly committed hard tokens is carried forward, while all remaining unresolved sites are reset to an identical [MASK] embedding. In open-ended co-generation where neither modality provides a fixed external anchor, this stateless transition limits single-step refinement depth and deprives both tracks of stable cross-step soft anchors, leading to slow convergence or degenerative repetitive collapse. Observing that uncommitted predictions evolve with pronounced temporal coherence rather than erratic noise, we introduce Predictive Memory (PredMem), a training-free self-conditioning recurrence that carries these soft beliefs across steps.
