At each step, PredMem summarizes raw logits into a probability-weighted Top-$k$ expectation embedding at every residue and affinely interpolates it into eligible masked inputs on the next step at zero extra denoiser cost. Across DPLM-2, DPLM-2.1, and ESM3, PredMem raises unconditional sequence-structure co-generation success by 3.6, 17.7, and 48.7 percentage points, respectively, while improving the quality-diversity trade-off and suppressing repetitive mode collapse. Controlled diagnostics show that these gains stem primarily from early trajectory stabilization in unanchored cross-modal co-evolution rather than extra compute or simple entropy reduction, establishing PredMem as an efficient inference mechanism for multimodal discrete generation.

</details>


### [204] [CriticJEPA: World Models Are Better Critics Than Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32357)
*Xvyuan Liu,Jianjie Fang,Honglin Zhang,Zhaolu Wang,Xin Zhang,Wei Wu,Fengli Xu,Chen Gao,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CriticJEPA：在冻结的层次化世界模型上，用目标条件流式提议 + 冻结 rollout 前向选择替代长时间梯度优化，在视觉导航与操作任务上以更低算力取得更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的规划器通过持续优化动作降低预测目标代价，但执行收益停滞后的继续优化会利用预测误差（模型被"刷分"）；同时，延长梯度搜索得到的候选方案其相对评分不具信息量，难以比较与更新。

Method: 1）从无奖励轨迹中学习目标条件流式（flow）提议分布；2）在每个层级用冻结世界模型的 rollout 做前向选择，比较候选结果的预测差异；3）前向选择构成完整规划器，选择前辅以带锚定的短程更新精修候选；4）全程保持世界模型权重冻结，仅训练提议/评论模块。

Result: 精确状态执行实验显示，冻结的层次化世界模型能对目标条件提议给出有区分度的排序，而长时间梯度搜索输出间的排序无信息量。在视觉导航与操作任务中，CriticJEPA 以一小部分规划算力提升成功率，且同一超参设置可跨独立训练的世界模型、不同层次深度及未见任务迁移。

Conclusion: 关键不在于更强的梯度优化，而在于"提议 + 可区分的前向选择"这一组合；在冻结世界模型下即可实现高效、可迁移的目标导向规划。

Abstract: Visual world models enable goal-directed planning by predicting action outcomes. Gradient-based planners optimize actions to lower predicted goal cost, but continued optimization can exploit prediction errors after execution stops improving. Comparing plans requires informative relative scores; improving them also requires reliable update directions. Exact-state execution reveals that a frozen hierarchical world model ranks goal-conditioned proposals even when its rankings among outputs of prolonged gradient search are uninformative. Effective planning pairs goal-directed candidates with predictions that distinguish their outcomes. We introduce CriticJEPA, which learns goal-conditioned flow proposals from reward-free trajectories and uses frozen rollouts to select among them at every hierarchy level. Forward selection forms a complete planner; short anchored updates refine candidates before selection. Across visual navigation and manipulation, CriticJEPA increases success at a fraction of the planning compute, transferring shared settings across independently trained models, hierarchy depths, and unseen tasks while keeping every world-model weight fixed.

</details>


### [205] [From the ELBO to Maximum Likelihood: Convergence of Variational Autoencoders for Nonlinear Mixed-Effects Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32372)
*Jan Rohleff,Johannes Schropp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文给出VAE-NLME算法的收敛性分析：ELBO版本可几乎必然收敛到ELBO的驻点集（无需编码器精确训练）；完整算法用基于ELBO梯度训练的编码器作重要性加权充分统计量的提议分布，可几乎必然收敛到边际对数似然的驻点集，且对编码器训练不作任何假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLME模型是药物计量学的核心统计模型，用于剂量选择、个体化给药与监管决策，其统计本质是边际似然问题。VAE通过神经网络编码器摊销难解后验的近似，可将AI组件引入NLME建模，但该VAE-NLME算法缺乏收敛性保证。

Method: 对VAE-NLME算法进行收敛性分析，分两层：(1) 针对证据下界(ELBO)，在不假设编码器被精确训练的情况下，证明其几乎必然收敛到ELBO驻点集；(2) 对最终算法，编码器以梯度法在ELBO上训练，并作为重要性加权充分统计量的提议分布，证明其几乎必然收敛到边际对数似然的驻点集，且对编码器训练不作任何假设。

Result: 两类收敛结论均以几乎必然收敛的形式建立：ELBO版本收敛至ELBO驻点集；完整VAE-NLME算法收敛至边际对数似然驻点集，且前者不要求编码器精确训练，后者不要求对编码器训练作任何假设。

Conclusion: 该算法将VAE基于梯度的快速训练与最大似然估计的极限目标相结合，为VAE-NLME在药物计量学中的统计可靠性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Nonlinear mixed-effects (NLME) models are the central statistical models of pharmacometrics, where they support dose selection, individualized dosing and regulatory decisions in drug development. Statistically, NLME modeling is a marginalized likelihood problem. Variational autoencoders (VAEs) solve such problems with a neural network encoder that amortizes the approximation of the intractable posterior distribution, and allow the integration of AI components into NLME modeling. This paper provides the convergence analysis of the VAE-NLME algorithm. First, its version for the evidence lower bound (ELBO) is proven to converge almost surely to the set of stationary points of the ELBO, without assuming that the encoder is trained exactly. Second, the final VAE-NLME algorithm trains the encoder on the ELBO by gradient-based optimization and uses it as proposal for importance-weighted sufficient statistics. It converges almost surely to the set of stationary points of the marginal log-likelihood, without any assumption on the training of the encoder. Thus, it combines the fast gradient-based training of VAEs with the limit of maximum likelihood estimation.

</details>


### [206] [Metamorphic Testing of Learned Spatial Surrogates for Transit Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32373)
*Till Zacher,Markus Lienkamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用神经代理模型替代昂贵的可达性计算以嵌入公交网络设计优化循环，并强调除预测精度外还须单独报告受控行为测试结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公交网络设计需评估大量候选方案，但基于路径规划的详细可达性计算过慢，无法在优化循环中反复调用，因此需要快速近似模型。

Method: 训练神经代理模型：输入候选公交网络及固定的轨道服务与周边机会信息，输出可达性空间变化量；在地理留出区域上与路径推导的目标值比较，并在受控输入变化下检验设定的定性关系，还用构造网络上的参考计算评估具备路径层面对应物的关系。

Result: 具有正预测技能（预测有效性）的模型仍可能通不过基本连通性测试；一项针对性干预显著提高了两条连通性关系的通过率，同时预测技能也有所提升。

Conclusion: 当学习模型被用作优化目标时，平均预测误差不足以保证可用性；预测一致性与受控行为测试应分别报告。

Abstract: Transit-network design requires evaluating many candidates, but detailed accessibility calculations are too slow to repeat inside an optimization loop. We therefore train a neural surrogate to approximate them. Given a candidate bus network and fixed information about rail service and nearby opportunities, it predicts the calculated spatial change in accessibility. We compare its predictions with routing-derived targets in geographically held-out regions and test specified qualitative relations under controlled input changes. Reference calculations on constructed networks assess the applicability of relations that have a routing-level counterpart. Models with positive predictive skill can still fail basic connectivity tests. One targeted intervention substantially increases pass rates for two connectivity relations while predictive skill also improves. Average prediction error is therefore insufficient when a learned model becomes an optimization objective. Predictive agreement and controlled behavioral tests should be reported separately.

</details>


### [207] [The Activity Geometry of Discrete Generative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32380)
*Xinlong Guo,Jie Zhang,Lingyou Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"活动几何"(Activity Geometry)框架刻画离散扩散/流模型的有限步采样耦合，分解期望转移成本为边际传输下界与耦合超额项，并提出最小活动有限时间耦合(MAFTC)实现无重训的最小活动采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散与流模型通过允许修改已生成token实现迭代精修，采样器设计中的"耦合"自由度作用日益重要，但尚缺乏充分刻画；且轨迹活动与多样性收益之间的关系不明。

Method: 以期望转移成本（称为activity）为对象研究有限步耦合；证明activity可分解为诱导边际传输所要求的最小值加耦合引起的超额项；以此最小值为参照提出MAFTC，在无需重训练、不增加模型评估的条件下实现给定边际的最小活动耦合；分析连续时间极限下的单位时间最小活动（收敛于与给定路径相容的最小期望跳率）；对度量诱导的Gibbs路径给出动力学最优通量的活动几何分解。

Result: 仅看总活动会掩盖耦合超额：采样器可接近边际传输所需最小活动，却仍保留显著耦合超额。语言生成中，随模型评估次数增加，精修型采样器累积额外轨迹活动但多样性增益有限；MAFTC在保持可比生成质量与更高词汇多样性的同时降低轨迹活动。

Conclusion: Activity Geometry为离散采样耦合提供了可量化的理论刻画，并可指导设计低活动、高质量的采样器；MAFTC是无需额外成本的实用实现。

Abstract: Iterative refinement is a key capability of diffusion-based generation and has motivated growing efforts to bring similar behavior to discrete diffusion and flow models. A growing line of work enables such refinement by allowing previously generated tokens to be revised during sampling, thereby shaping the coupling between successive sampling states. Despite its growing role in sampler design, this coupling freedom remains insufficiently characterized. We introduce Activity Geometry to study these finite-step couplings through expected transition cost, which we call activity. We show that activity decomposes into the minimum required by the induced marginal transport and an excess term arising from the coupling. Using this minimum as a reference, we propose Minimal-Activity Finite-Time Coupling (MAFTC) to realize prescribed marginals with minimum activity without retraining or additional model evaluations. In the continuous-time limit, the minimum activity per unit time converges to the minimum expected jump rate compatible with the prescribed path. For metric-induced Gibbs paths, we further show that the activity of the kinetic-optimal flux admits a geometric decomposition. We find that total activity alone can obscure coupling excess: a sampler may approach the minimum activity required by the prescribed marginal transport while still retaining substantial coupling excess. In language generation, as the number of model evaluations increases, refinement-based samplers accumulate additional trajectory activity with limited diversity gains, whereas MAFTC reduces trajectory activity while maintaining comparable generation quality and higher lexical diversity.

</details>


### [208] [Evolve to Learn: Gradient-aligned experience evolution for model self-improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32390)
*Haoyuan Li,Gangyi Zhang,Dong Wang,Yang Liu,Daoxuan Liu,Jiancan Wu,Shiu-hong Kao,Wu Liu,Yicong Li,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析多种"经验"的蒸馏价值，发现梯度对齐度比即时测试时提升更能预测下游蒸馏收益，据此提出 E2L 自进化框架，按估计的训练效用迭代地生成、评估并蒸馏经验，实现经验生成与参数学习的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化语言模型可通过测试时交互、反思与提示优化积累经验，但把经验内化为模型参数仍困难。On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) 提供了将测试时信息内化的机制，却默认"生成的经历都值得学习"，而作者指出该假设不总成立：能提升测试时表现的经验，其训练信号可能有限甚至有害。因此需要刻画"哪些经验应被内化"。

Method: 系统分析多任务、多反思模型下各类变异经验，比较"即时测试时提升"与"梯度对齐"两种指标对下游蒸馏增益的预测能力；据此提出 E2L 框架，按估计的训练效用对经验进行迭代的生成—评估—蒸馏，闭环衔接经验生成与参数学习。

Result: 在多样化变异经验上的实证表明，梯度对齐是比即时测试时提升更可靠的下游蒸馏收益预测指标；基于该判据的 E2L 能自主筛选并内化高价值经验，支撑自我改进。

Conclusion: 测试时收益与训练效用并不等价，应以梯度对齐作为经验筛选依据；E2L 将经验评估纳入自蒸馏循环，使智能体能够自主产出适合自我进化的经验。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models can continuously acquire useful experience through test-time interaction, reflection, and prompt optimization, yet turning such experience into model itself to ensure self improvement remains challenging. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides a natural mechanism for internalizing test-time information into model parameters, yet it largely assumes that the gener- ated experience is worth learning. We show that this assumption does not always hold: experiences that improve test-time performance may provide limited or even harmful training signals. To better characterize which experience should be inter- nalized, we systematically analyze diverse mutated experiences across multiple tasks and reflection models, and find that gradient-based alignment is a more reli- able indicator of downstream distillation gains than immediate test-time improve- ment. Based on this finding, we propose E2L, a self-evolution framework that iteratively generates, evaluates, and distills experiences according to their esti- mated training utility. E2L closes the loop between experience generation and parameter learning, enabling agents to autonomously produce experience suitable for self-improvement.

</details>


### [209] [Fractal Neural Synthesis: Constant-Storage Procedural Weight Derivation via Complex Quadratic Boundary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32393)
*Volkan Dagli,Zerrin Dagli,Daghan Dagli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分形神经合成（FNS）：用恒定24字节坐标种子隐式生成网络权重，在C^1光滑性、秩保持和边缘Flash体积上均优于傅里叶/噪声/PRNG基，多个基准上以INT8读出超越线性INT8模型并实现10.7x–2773x的Flash压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘微控制器资源受限，传统网络以显式张量存储权重，持久化Flash占用随参数量线性增长（O(W)），难以扩展。作者希望用程序化权重推导替代显式存储，将隐层参数压缩为常量级。

Method: 用常数24字节坐标种子 Theta=(c_x,c_y,ell)∈F_64^3，锚定在f_c(z)=z^2+c的复二次分支边界dM附近；引入大逃逸半径R=256的平滑逃逸核；设计四种零开销几何采样孔径（Cartesian Grid、Z/9Z互素3x3三分法、phi-Vogel螺旋、多尺度Harmonic Tripod）；证明条件Kolmogorov复杂度上界（K_Kolm≤192+c_0比特）及Shannon熵界；在10^0–10^8放大倍数下对比C^1 Fourier、格点噪声与SHA-256 PRNG基线；采用无泄漏10折交叉验证与零标准化字节的非线性基准评估。

Result: Fourier与格点噪声基经标准化后秩坍缩至~2.25，SHA-256 PRNG出现雪崩不连续（rho~0.019，微步保持率50.6%）；dM同时维持秩~10.0与局部连续性（rho∈[0.81,0.97]，保持率89.2%）。FNS+INT8读出相较0隐藏层Linear-INT8提升+3.6至+10.8个百分点，相对收敛Float32 MLP实现10.7x–129x总Flash压缩（隐层725x–2773x）：Ionosphere 90.30%/285 B，Optical Digits 97.22%/2598 B，PenDigits 97.90%/2598 B，Satimage 87.00%/1570 B。

Conclusion: 分支边界dM是唯一能同时提供高有效秩与局部光滑性的坐标域，为程序化权重合成提供了可行的几何先验；FNS在极低Flash预算下仍保持竞争精度，证明"常数种子+过程化推导"可作为O(W)显式权重存储的替代方案，适用于边缘微控制器部署。

Abstract: Conventional neural networks store synaptic parameters as explicit tensors whose persistent Flash footprint scales linearly (O(W)) with parameter count, constraining resource-limited edge microcontrollers. We formulate and systematically evaluate Fractal Neural Synthesis (FNS), a procedural weight derivation architecture that replaces hidden-layer matrices with a constant 24-byte coordinate seed Theta = (c_x, c_y, ell) in F_64^3 anchored near the complex quadratic bifurcation boundary dM of f_c(z) = z^2 + c. We introduce a large-bailout (R = 256) smooth escape kernel, four zero-overhead geometric sampling apertures--Cartesian Grid, coprime 3x3 Rule-of-Thirds (Z/9Z), phi-Vogel spiral, and multi-scale Harmonic Tripod--and prove conditional Kolmogorov (K_Kolm <= 192 + c_0 bits) and Shannon entropy bounds. Across magnifications zeta in [10^0, 10^8], C^1 Fourier and lattice-noise bases collapse to post-standardization rank ~2.25 and SHA-256 PRNG exhibits avalanche discontinuity (rho ~ 0.019, 50.6% micro-step retention), whereas dM uniquely sustains both rank (~10.0) and local continuity (rho in [0.81, 0.97], 89.2% retention). Under leak-free 10-fold CV across four zero-scaler-byte non-linear benchmarks, FNS with an INT8 readout surpasses 0-hidden Linear-INT8 by +3.6 to +10.8 pp while delivering 10.7x-129x total Flash reduction (725x-2,773x hidden reduction) relative to converged Float32 MLPs (90.30% Ionosphere in 285 B; 97.22% Optical Digits in 2,598 B; 97.90% PenDigits in 2,598 B; 87.00% Satimage in 1,570 B).

</details>


### [210] [Not All Pairs Align: Caption-Guided Audio-Visual Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32399)
*Omar Emara,Nan Lu,Dima Damen,Hazel Doughty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的Caption Guided Alignment Aware Loss (CGAAL)，利用模态专属描述估计音视频语义一致性并加权正样本对，结合预热与渐进阈值策略，在三个对比基线上一致提升音视频检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对比学习通常假设配对观测是同一底层信息的共享视图，但实际中共现的音视频内容可能因画外音等因素而显著不一致；标准对比目标却将所有配对样本视为同等对齐，忽略了这种语义错配。

Method: CGAAL利用各模态自身的caption估计音视频之间的语义一致性（agreement score），并用该分数对每个正样本对的贡献进行加权；同时采用warm-up阶段与渐进阈值化策略，逐步引入这种对齐感知的训练信号。该损失为模型无关的通用目标，可套用于现有对比基线。

Result: 将CGAAL应用于三个对比学习基线，在音视频检索任务上均取得一致的性能提升，验证了其通用性与有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模配对样本的语义对齐程度比等同对待所有正样本更合理，CGAAL作为一种可插拔的加权目标能稳健改进音视频表示学习。

Abstract: We address the problem of learning audio-visual representations from co-occurring modalities. Multimodal contrastive learning typically assumes that paired observations provide shared views of the same underlying information. While this may be true for datasets collected in constrained environments, it does not always hold. For instance, co-occurring audio and visual signals can differ substantially in their content due to off-screen sounds. Nevertheless, standard contrastive objectives treat all paired samples as equally aligned. In this work, we propose Caption Guided Alignment Aware Loss (CGAAL), a model-agnostic objective that leverages modality-specific captions to estimate semantic agreement between audio and video. CGAAL uses these agreement scores to weight the contribution of each positive pair. A warm-up period and gradual thresholding strategy progressively introduce this alignment-aware training signal. We demonstrate the generality of CGAAL by applying it to three contrastive baselines, consistently improving their performance on audio-visual retrieval.

</details>


### [211] [Optimal Transport Meets t-SNE: Integrated Visualization without Batch Effects in OT-tSNE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32402)
*Azhar Akhmetova,Edward Eichhorn,Boris Landa,Dmitry Kobak,Sebastian Damrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OT-tSNE，用熵正则最优传输得到的全局耦合亲和替代最近邻匹配，实现更稳健的批次整合可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批次效应会掩盖数据集间的共享结构、干扰联合可视化；现有方法依赖跨批次最近邻关系，且这种局部对应在困难的跨批次几何下不可靠。

Method: 将t-SNE中的最近邻匹配替换为由熵正则最优传输（entropic optimal transport）导出的全局耦合亲和度，构建OT-tSNE。

Result: 受控合成实验显示：最近邻方法在困难几何下性能退化，而最优传输亲和度及OT-tSNE嵌入保持稳健；在七个真实单细胞数据集上，OT-tSNE在批次混合与生物学保守性之间取得Pareto有效权衡。

Conclusion: 全局最优传输亲和度优于局部最近邻对应，OT-tSNE是面向批次效应下联合可视化的稳健方法，批次整合效果尤佳。

Abstract: Visual exploration is an integral step in understanding the structure of datasets. However, systematic variation between datasets collected under different conditions, known as batch effects, can obscure shared structure and complicate their joint visualization. Existing methods often rely on cross-batch nearest-neighbor relations to integrate the batches. Yet, these local correspondences can be unreliable for challenging cross-batch geometries. We propose optimal-transport t-SNE (OT-tSNE), which replaces nearest-neighbor-based matching with globally coupled affinities derived from entropic optimal transport. Controlled synthetic experiments demonstrated that nearest-neighbor-based methods can deteriorate, while optimal-transport affinities and OT-tSNE embeddings remain robust. Across seven real single-cell datasets, we benchmarked OT-tSNE against a broad range of integration and visualization methods and found that it achieves a Pareto-efficient trade-off between batch mixing and biological conservation, and integrates batches particularly well.

</details>


### [212] [A Framework for Graph Node Symmetric Quantification under Dataset Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32403)
*Alessio Micheli,Alejandro Moreo,Marco Podda,Fabrizio Sebastiani,Domenico Tortorella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GSQ框架，实现无需节点级监督与节点分类器的图节点对称量化，直接估计无标签节点样本的类别流行度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图节点量化方法遵循聚合范式，需先用有标签节点训练中间节点分类器，再聚合预测并做偏移校正，属于"非对称"量化（训练项与预测项类型不同）。作者希望摆脱节点级监督与节点级预测，实现对称量化。

Method: 构建由节点编码器、嵌入池化器、流行度预测器和可选的事后缩放步骤组成的流水线；训练阶段仅需以类别分布标注的节点样本（而非单节点标签），直接估计流行度值；各组件可选用不同的可训练或储层（reservoir）编码器、池化算子及预测目标。

Result: 在二分类与多分类数据集上，涵盖不同同质性水平，并在先验概率偏移与结构协变量偏移两种设定下对GSQ框架进行评估。

Conclusion: GSQ通过对称量化范式省去节点级分类环节，为图节点量化提供了一种灵活的模块化框架，并在多种偏移条件下验证了其适用性。

Abstract: Graph node quantification is the supervised learning task of estimating the prevalence of different classes within an arbitrary sample of unlabelled nodes from a graph, subject to some form of dataset shift. Existing approaches to graph node quantification typically follow the standard aggregative paradigm, i.e., they use labelled nodes from the graph to train an intermediate node classifier, and obtain an estimated class distribution for the sample by aggregating the classifier's predictions for the individual nodes and then correcting the result via some adjustment strategy that accounts for dataset shift. In this work we propose a framework for graph node quantification that does away with both node-level supervision, since its training phase only requires node samples labelled by their class distribution, and node-level predictions, since it estimates class prevalence values directly, without the need of any intermediate node classifier. The framework thus allows symmetric quantification, since (unlike in standard ``asymmetric'' quantification methods) the labelled items used for training and the unlabelled items on which predictions must be issued are of the same type (i.e., node samples). This framework (that we dub GSQ, for Graph Node Symmetric Quantification) allows building pipelines consisting of a node encoder, an embedding pooler, a prevalence predictor, and an optional post-hoc scaling step. These components can be instantiated by choosing from different trainable and reservoir-based encoders, different pooling operators, and different prediction objectives. We evaluate the GSQ framework on binary and multi-class datasets with different levels of homophily, under prior probability shift and under structural covariate shift.

</details>


### [213] [The Teacher Is Already Inside: Self-Distilling Looped Language Models Beyond Their Last Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32452)
*Linghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELPSD 将循环模型"首循环到末循环"外推得到的下一 token 分布作为自蒸馏目标，用冻结副本计算，无需奖励、外部教师或特权上下文，在五个数学基准上刷新最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型（共享 transformer 栈多次迭代、每次经同一 LM head 可解码）的后训练困难：纯奖励 RL 需验证器且信号稀疏；把奖励摊到各循环会塌缩每层熵；跨循环自蒸馏（如 LoopOPD）只能教中间循环，末循环无法从自身冻结副本学习（初始化时损失为零）。

Method: 观察到模型内已存在比末循环解码更优的策略——由首循环经末循环外推出的下一 token 分布。ELPSD 用模型冻结副本计算该外推分布作为教师，在自采样 rollout 的每个访问前缀上以精确 reverse KL 蒸馏自身末循环；教师与学生同源，无需奖励/外部教师/特权上下文，不改变 rollout 与解码。理论分析表明目标以 1+w 权重拉向基模型末循环、以 w 权重推离基模型首循环，且教师必须冻结（理想化地由当前模型重算教师会无界外推）。

Result: 在 Ouro-1.4B-Thinking 上于五个数学基准评测：无任何奖励下，ELPSD 将 avg@8 从 61.15 提升至 63.69，五个基准全部最优，优于使用验证器的 GRPO（62.32）和需要参考解的 OPSD（61.77）。

Conclusion: 外推循环策略是一种可利用的"隐式更优策略"，可用作自蒸馏目标；ELPSD 以零外部监督代价超越奖励型与自蒸馏基线，但依赖冻结教师以保证有界、稳定。

Abstract: Looped language models apply one shared transformer stack several times per token, and every loop is readable through the shared language-model head. Post-training them is hard. Reward-only reinforcement learning needs a verifier and learns from one sparse signal per response; spreading the reward over loops collapses the entropy of every loop; and cross-loop self-distillation, as in LoopOPD, can only teach intermediate loops, because the last loop cannot learn from its own frozen copy: the loss is zero at initialisation. We observe that a looped model already contains a policy that decodes better than its last loop: the next-token distribution extrapolated from the first loop through the last. ELPSD (Extrapolated-Loop Policy Self-Distillation) turns this distribution into a self-distillation target: a frozen copy of the model computes it, and the model's own last loop is distilled towards it on its own rollouts, using the exact reverse KL at every visited prefix. Teacher and student are the same model, so ELPSD needs no reward, no external teacher and no privileged context, and it leaves rollouts and decoding unchanged. We show that the objective pulls the last loop towards the base model's last loop with weight 1+w and away from the base model's first loop with weight w, and that the teacher must stay frozen, since an idealised teacher recomputed from the current model extrapolates without bound. We evaluate ELPSD on Ouro-1.4B-Thinking across five math benchmarks against the base model, GRPO, RLTT and OPSD. Without any reward, ELPSD raises the average avg@8 from 61.15 to 63.69 and is the best method on all five benchmarks, ahead of GRPO (62.32), which uses a verifier, and the self-distillation method OPSD (61.77), which needs reference solutions.

</details>


### [214] [When Knowledge-Tracing Rankings Depend on Available History: A Source-Correction and Matched-Information Audit on ASSISTments 2009 and 2015](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32489)
*Devis Wawan Saputra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文考察知识追踪基准中数据来源、历史长度与表示方式如何影响模型比较：发现ASSISTments 2009镜像与官方发布存在序列差异，并在统一事件窗口下发现序感知逻辑回归优于GRU，提示既往比较结论依赖基准设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪基准常比较对学习者历史响应访问权限不同的模型，导致比较不公平；同时数据来源（精选镜像 vs 官方更正版）与历史窗口长度可能系统影响结论，需要厘清这些因素对模型对比的影响。

Method: (1) 对比固定版ASSISTments 2009序列镜像与更正后的官方发布，统计学习者级有序技能-响应序列差异；(2) 保留以往在2009与2015数据上的学习者不相交评估，区分探索性结果与2015更正性复现；(3) 在4,000名不重叠的2015学习者样本上，用计数逻辑回归、序感知逻辑回归与GRU三种模型，约束于相同1事件或20事件前序响应窗口，采用学习者聚类bootstrap区间比较Brier分数。

Result: 两个版本共有的4,148名学习者中817名有序序列不同，镜像标注事件更少。在582名学习者、20,592条测试响应上，20事件窗口Brier分数为：计数逻辑回归0.17667、序感知逻辑回归0.17389、GRU 0.17514；GRU减序感知差值0.00125（95%区间[0.00008, 0.00243]），单事件窗口差值为0.00212[0.00120, 0.00301]，均不利于GRU。以往比较则偏向GRU。

Conclusion: 在同等允许事件流与统一窗口条件下，序感知逻辑回归优于GRU，说明早期GRU优势可能源于历史摘要表示与数据处理的差异；结果仅针对特定历史队列与模型设置，未隔离架构效应，也不能外推到当代课堂。

Abstract: Knowledge-tracing benchmarks often compare models that have different access to a learner's previous responses. We examine how data provenance, history length, and representation affect those comparisons. First, we compare a pinned ASSISTments 2009 sequence mirror against the corrected official release. Of 4,148 learners represented in both, 817 have different ordered skill-response sequences; the mirror also contains fewer labeled events. Second, we retain earlier learner-disjoint model evaluations on the 2009 and 2015 datasets, distinguishing exploratory results from a corrective 2015 replication. Finally, we evaluate a new, nonoverlapping sample of 4,000 ASSISTments 2015 learners using three models constrained to the same one-event or 20-event prior-response window. On 20,592 test responses from 582 learners, the 20-event Brier scores were 0.17667 for count-based logistic regression, 0.17389 for an order-aware logistic model, and 0.17514 for a GRU. The GRU-minus-order-aware difference was 0.00125 (95% learner-cluster bootstrap interval [0.00008, 0.00243]); at one prior event, the corresponding difference was 0.00212 [0.00120, 0.00301]. Earlier comparisons had favored the GRU against simpler history summaries. The newer analysis instead favors a logistic comparator that represents order using the same permitted event stream. These results concern predictive performance on particular historical cohorts and model settings. They neither isolate an architectural effect nor establish generalization to contemporary classrooms.

</details>


### [215] [Assessing Robustness to Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Deep Reinforcement Learning for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32496)
*Kim N. Nolle,Ivana Dusparic,Rhodri Cusack,Vinny Cahill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在非平稳自主泊车环境中比较PPO、SAC与Drama三类RL算法的抗遗忘鲁棒性，发现off-policy算法因经验回放更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在信息物理系统中日益普及，智能体需适应环境变化以维持安全与可靠性；核心问题是不同类别的RL算法在前序行为遗忘（持续学习的关键问题）方面的鲁棒性是否存在差异。

Method: 在非平稳自主泊车环境中评估三类代表性算法：无模型on-policy的PPO、无模型off-policy的SAC、基于模型的Drama。智能体需依次在四个车位角度不同的场景中学习成功泊车。

Result: off-policy算法因使用经验回放（replay）而表现得比on-policy算法更鲁棒；基于模型的算法相比无模型算法未显示出直接优势。实验还揭示了寻找环境鲁棒抽象以及超参数敏感性的额外挑战。

Conclusion: 经验回放是提升持续学习抗遗忘能力的关键因素；基于模型的方法本身并不自动带来鲁棒性收益；鲁棒抽象设计与超参数敏感性仍是待解决的难点。

Abstract: As the use of reinforcement learning (RL) becomes more widespread in cyber-physical systems, it becomes increasingly important to design RL agents that are able to adapt to changes in their environments in order to maintain safety and reliability. This paper addresses the question of whether different classes of RL algorithm are more robust against forgetting previously learned behaviours, a critical issue in continual learning, in non-stationary environments. We evaluate the robustness of representative examples of three important classes of RL algorithm in a non-stationary autonomous parking environment. These are Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), which is a model-free on-policy algorithm, Soft-Actor-Critic (SAC), which is a model-free off-policy algorithm, and Drama, which is a model-based algorithm. The agents must learn to successfully park in four different scenarios, which are presented successively, with parking spaces oriented at varying angles. We find off-policy algorithms appear to be more robust compared to on-policy algorithms due to their use of replay, while model-based algorithms do not appear to offer any direct advantage over model-free algorithms. These experiments also highlight the additional challenges of finding robust abstractions of the environment, and oversensitivity to hyperparameters.

</details>


### [216] [InducedTFMs: A Family of Sub-Quadratic Tabular Foundation Models Using Induced Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32504)
*Salome Gobbi,Nicolas Atienza,Nicola Cancedda,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InducedTFM用Set Transformer的induced attention替换双向表格基础模型的两个注意力阶段，在保持cell级表示的同时将样本与特征维度的计算代价均降为线性，匹配TabICLv2精度并在窄表上实现最多30倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）通过in-context learning在单次前向中预测新表，但注意力对样本数和特征数均为二次复杂度，导致预训练只能限于小型合成表，大表推理需依赖分块或下采样。行压缩模型虽降低了特征维成本，却丢弃了cell级表示且对样本仍为二次。论文希望在不牺牲cell级表示的前提下同时实现两个维度的线性复杂度。

Method: 提出InducedTFM，将bidimensional TFM中的两个注意力阶段（样本方向与特征方向）均替换为Set Transformer的induced attention，用可学习的诱导点（inducing points）做低秩近似，从而在保留cell级表示的同时使计算代价在样本数与特征数上均为线性。实验在与其训练预算匹配的full-attention TabICLv2重实现下对比，并设置同渐近代价的kernel linear-attention基线。

Result: 在与full-attention TabICLv2匹配的训练预算下，InducedTFM在TabArena及更大数据集基准上的精度与TabICLv2相当，并明显优于同渐近代价的kernel linear-attention基线；在窄表推理上超过TabICLv2，640K行时达到30倍加速；而在宽表上行压缩方法仍更优。

Conclusion: 训练预算匹配时，低秩induced attention无需精度损失即可获得样本与特征双线性的复杂度，说明TFM的二次瓶颈可通过架构层面的诱导注意力有效缓解。窄表是大规模推理加速的主要受益场景，宽表则宜采用行压缩，二者可根据表宽选择。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a serious competitor to gradient-boosted trees, predicting on a new table in a single forward pass by in-context learning. However, their attention is quadratic in the number of samples and features, which caps pretraining at small synthetic tables and forces inference on large ones to rely on chunking or subsampling. Row-compression models reduce the feature-wise cost, but only by discarding the cell-level representation, and remain quadratic in the number of samples. We introduce InducedTFM, which replaces both attention stages of a bidimensional TFM with the induced attention of the Set Transformer, preserving cell-level representations while making its cost linear in both samples and features. Under a training budget matched to a full-attention TabICLv2 reimplementation, InducedTFM matches its accuracy on TabArena and on a benchmark of larger datasets, and clearly outperforms a kernel linear-attention baseline of the same asymptotic cost. It overtakes TabICLv2 in inference time on narrow tables, reaching a 30x speed-up at 640K rows, although row compression remains preferable on wide tables.

</details>


### [217] [Branch and Consolidate: A Unified Recipe for Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32515)
*Zihan Liang,Zikun Qu,Ben Chen,Fan Yang,Wenwu Ou,Min Zhang,Zhongxiang Dai,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对专家模型与共享检查点差异较大时合并失效的问题，提出统一的多模态多教师在线蒸馏（MOPD）配方，将差异化专家整合回单一学生模型并补充精炼阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合并方法（权重合并到MOPD）主要针对与原检查点接近的专家，当专家显著偏离共享检查点时效果不佳，工业与开放场景下难以有效整合互补能力。

Method: 统一的多模态MOPD配方，明确初始化、数据选择、教师间均衡监督、既有能力保持，并加入针对落后能力的定向精炼阶段；辅以机制消融与训练动态分析。

Result: 工业六专家场景：学生在本域接近各专家并超越感知专家，恢复专家平均增益97.0%（IM-MOPD为79.8%）；Qwen3-4B五域全开放场景：整合后恢复95.7%，精炼后97.5%（IM-MOPD为94.1%）。

Conclusion: 该配方在专家显著偏离时仍能有效整合互补能力，精炼阶段进一步提升，并提供诊断不完整整合的实践指导。

Abstract: Specialists post-trained from a shared checkpoint acquire complementary capabilities, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) can consolidate them into a single model. However, we find that existing consolidation methods, from weight merging to MOPD, fall short when specialists diverge substantially from the shared checkpoint, whereas prior studies mostly examine specialists that remain close to it. We present a unified recipe for multimodal MOPD that consolidates such specialists back into the single student they branched from. The recipe specifies initialization, data selection, balanced supervision across teachers, and preservation of existing capabilities for consolidation, together with a targeted refinement stage for capabilities that still lag behind their specialists. In an industrial setting with six specialists, the student closely approaches each specialist in its own domain and surpasses the perception specialist. It recovers 97.0% of their average gain, compared with 79.8% for IM-MOPD. In the fully open five-domain setting with Qwen3-4B, our method recovers 95.7% of the specialists' average gain after consolidation and 97.5% after refinement, compared with 94.1% for IM-MOPD. We further analyze the full pipeline through mechanism ablations and training dynamics, providing practical guidance for diagnosing incomplete consolidation. Code will be released.

</details>


### [218] [Beyond Robust Algorithmic Recourse: Sharing the Burden of Validity through Model Multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32543)
*Prakhar Ganesh,Afaf Taïk,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示算法追索（recourse）的"鲁棒性"与公平性之间存在系统性张力：鲁棒追索将可行建议约束到高密度（多数群体）区域从而放大群体差异，而该权衡源于"仅由个体承担有效性负担"的隐含假设；作者提出利用模型多重性（multiplicity），由系统所有者通过策略性模型选择与个体共担有效性负担，从而在放宽个体鲁棒性要求的同时维持建议有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有追索研究强调鲁棒追索（模型更新后仍有效的建议），将其视为保护个体的手段；但作者指出鲁棒性本身会带来系统性伤害，而"有效性负担应完全由个体承担"这一隐含假设才是权衡的根源，需重新审视负担分配问题。

Method: 概念性/理论分析：形式化刻画鲁棒追索对可行建议集的约束，论证其如何偏向数据高密度区域并放大群体间差异；进而提出以模型多重性（存在多个性能相当但相互竞争的模型）为杠杆，通过策略性模型选择实现负担共担。

Result: 非鲁棒追索面临建议失效风险，鲁棒追索则以牺牲个体灵活性与群体公平为代价；借助多重性进行模型选择，可同时保持建议有效性并放宽对个体的鲁棒性要求，从而缓解有效性与不公平之间的张力。

Conclusion: 追索设计不应默认由个体独自承担有效性负担；模型所有者的策略性模型选择可将该负担部分内化，为构建既有效又公平的追索机制提供新路径。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse provides actionable recommendations that enable individuals to achieve favourable outcomes. Implementing recourse for individuals requires significant time and effort, thus emphasizing robust recourse: recommendations that remain valid even under future model updates. While robustness preserves validity, we show it introduces systematic harms by constraining feasible recommendations to regions that disproportionately reflect majority behavior, reducing individual flexibility and amplifying disparities. Thus, non-robust recourse risks invalidation, whereas robust recourse creates a preference for high-density regions in the data that induces unfairness. However, this trade-off is driven by an implicit assumption that individuals alone should bear the burden of validity. We challenge this narrative and show that strategic model selection can help maintain validity while relaxing robustness requirements for individuals. By leveraging multiplicity, the existence of equally good yet competing models, system owners can share this burden with individuals, thus resolving the tension between validity and unfairness.

</details>


### [219] [Contact Geometry for Generative Models: An Unbalanced Optimal Transport Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32547)
*Andrea Testa,Søren Hauberg,Andras Gabor Kupcsik,Tamim Asfour,Leonel Rozo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Real-world generative problems in biology, medical imaging, or robotics, rarely come with perfectly paired source and target distributions. While unbalanced Optimal Transport (uOT) provides a robust framework to bridge unpaired datasets via minimum-length paths in probability space, existing formulations based on the Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao (WFR) geometry introduce a structural bias toward high density regions, often triggering mode collapse. We introduce Contact unbalanced Optimal Transport (CuOT), a novel uOT formulation grounded in contact geometry that decouples density transport direction from mass growth to eliminate structural bias. This allows CuOT to adapt sampling steps to the local data structure, naturally reducing step sizes in high-density regions to prevent overshooting and ensuring stable convergence. The result is a stable, scalable path-length minimization solver that consistently outperforms WFR-based methods on biological dynamics reconstruction, unpaired image-to-image translation, and video generation.

</details>


### [220] [Representation and Model Contributions on Small Molecular Benchmarks: Controlled Comparisons and External Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32568)
*Nora A. M. A. Talkhan,Ibrahim Abdelbaky,Fatma A. Omara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文用2×2因子设计分离分子性质预测中的"表示效应"与"模型效应"，发现对溶解度和亲脂性，表示效应大于模型效应；建议基线比较应使用多种表示并分别报告两种效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测的性能提升通常归因于模型本身，但新模型常与"分子指纹+随机森林"基线比较，这一比较同时改变了模型与表示，混杂了两者的贡献，无法判断增益究竟来自模型还是表示。

Method: 采用2×2因子设计：模型为RF与CatBoost（或表格基础模型TabPFN、TabFM），表示为分子指纹与RDKit描述符，交叉组合；用配对scaffold bootstrap检验各主效应。在7个小型MoleculeNet数据集（3回归、4分类）上评估，涵盖DGT的原始划分、10个额外scaffold划分及外部数据集。

Result: 在溶解度与亲脂性任务上，所有主因子设计、三类评估中表示效应均超过模型效应；水合自由能结果依赖初始指纹（ECFP4下表示效应更大，MACCS下两者均不显著）；分类数据集仅一个主设计两效应差异显著。此外，使用RDKit描述符时，RF在全部7个数据集上被TabFM超越的结论成立，且RDKit描述符下的RF基线在溶解度上超过DGT公布分数。

Conclusion: 模型效应在溶解度/亲脂性上较小，性能增益主要来自表示。建议模型比较中对经典基线评估多种表示，并分别报告表示效应与模型效应，以避免将表示贡献误归为模型贡献。

Abstract: Improvements in molecular property prediction are usually attributed to the model, although part of the gain may come from the molecular representation. New models are commonly benchmarked against a random forest (RF) trained on a molecular fingerprint, a comparison that changes the model and the representation at the same time. We separate the two contributions with a two-by-two factorial design in which the RF and a second model (CatBoost or the tabular foundation models TabPFN and TabFM) are each trained on a fingerprint and on RDKit descriptors, and each effect is tested with a paired scaffold bootstrap. Seven small MoleculeNet datasets (3 regression, 4 classification) are evaluated on the published split of a recent graph transformer (DGT), on 10 additional scaffold splits and on external datasets. For solubility and lipophilicity, the representation effect exceeds the model effect in every main factorial design, in all three evaluations. For hydration free energy, the result depends on the starting fingerprint: from ECFP4 the representation effect is larger, and from MACCS neither effect is significantly larger. On the classification datasets, only one main factorial design has a significant difference between the two effects. Although the model effect is smaller on solubility and lipophilicity, TabFM outperforms the RF on all 7 datasets when both use RDKit descriptors. With RDKit descriptors in place of a fingerprint, the RF baseline reported for DGT surpasses the transformer's published solubility score. We recommend that model comparisons evaluate classical baselines on more than one representation and report the representation and model effects separately.

</details>


### [221] [Boiling Down Uncertainty: Deep Generative Scenario Search with Decision-Guided Evolution Strategies for Two-Stage Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32573)
*Atefeh Hemmati Golsefidi,David Pisinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为两阶段随机规划中的场景缩减方法"深度生成场景搜索"补齐了解析基础：从recourse对偶与正则化约简问题的伴随求解推导出闭式梯度，并将该决策导向梯度（对偶不可用时以启发式方向兜底）用作引导进化策略的采样子空间；(期望更新对平滑目标构成下降方向)；在六个问题族上均优于基线，决策导向版本将平均gap降低四分之三，且在整数变量最多的两个问题族上多数运行以约五分之一时间超越精确求解器的两小时可行解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景数量在不确定性表达能力与计算成本之间存在权衡。已有方法 Deep Generative Scenario Search 通过生成合成场景并从其诱导的决策中学习来改进场景集，但决策随场景呈分段常数，原方法只能使用黑盒梯度估计，缺乏解析基础。

Method: 基于 recourse 对偶性，并对正则化约简问题做伴随求解，闭式推导梯度；将决策导向方向（对偶不可用时用启发式方向兜底）用于定义引导进化策略的采样子空间，而非直接更新；理论上证明对任意引导子空间，期望更新均为平滑目标的下降方向。

Result: 六类问题族上，两种引导估计器均优于基线；决策导向版本将平均gap降低约四分之三；在整数变量最多的两个问题族上，缩减场景集在多数运行中超越精确求解器两小时得到的可行解，耗时约为其五分之一。理论分析表明不精确引导仅增加方差，期望步仍下降。

Conclusion: 为场景搜索提供了闭式梯度与下降性保证的解析框架，引导进化策略对引导质量鲁棒，显著提升解质量与求解效率，尤其适用于含大量整数变量的两阶段随机规划。

Abstract: One of the most common models for handling uncertainty is two-stage stochastic programming. Choosing the right number of scenarios is a trade-off between the uncertainty that can be represented and computational cost. A recent method, Deep Generative Scenario Search, generates synthetic scenarios and improves them by learning from the decisions they induce. The original approach used a black-box gradient estimate, since decisions are piecewise constant across scenarios. This paper provides the missing analytical foundation by deriving the gradient in closed form from recourse duality and an adjoint solve of the regularized reduced problem. The decision-focused direction, with a heuristic direction as a backup when duals are unavailable, defines the sampling subspace of a guided evolution strategy rather than being used as a direct update. For any guiding subspace, the expected update is a descent direction of the smoothed objective, so an inaccurate guide adds variance while the expected step still descends. For six problem families, both guided estimators outperform the baseline. The decision-focused version reduces the mean gap by three-quarters, and on the two families with the most integer variables the reduced sets beat the exact solver's two-hour incumbent in most runs, using a fifth of the time.

</details>


### [222] [When Can a Rewiring Control Test LLM Weight Readers? C2 and an Eligibility Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29120)
*Vaishak Girish Kumar,Sanika Vilas Najan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反事实干预 C2（置换末层读出矩阵的列），并给出"资格协议"三条件，用以区分大模型是在"计算"还是在"识别权重空间指纹"；实验表明现实设定几乎都不合格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作显示大语言模型可以从原始权重预测小神经网络的输出，但这一"成功"可能只是识别了权重空间中的规律（指纹），而非真正执行计算；现有的保函数控制手段无法区分二者。

Method: 构造 C2 反事实：置换网络最终线性读出矩阵的列，使其余参数以及读出的奇异值、范数、元素多重集均不变，同时函数变化可被精确重算并逐例验证。进一步提出资格协议三条件：(1) 仅用 C2 不变摘要的策略已能预测目标；(2) C2 必须改变与重接线特异性相关的性质；(3) 读者身份须可被证实。

Result: 在微型 MLP 上，前沿读者路线在全部 24 个网络均跟随重接线函数，但不变基线接近随机且后端无法证实；两个公开 CNN 库中条件相互掣肘（权重统计可预测分布内 CIFAR-10 精度，但 C2 效应非特异；逐图目标特异却无法由不变摘要预测）；仅在人为构造的"植入指纹规则"群体中三条件全部成立。自托管 Qwen3-27B 跟随重接线而非捷径，禁用思维时无使用权重证据；新网络上的注册复现确认（跟踪减卡住 0.875，95% 区间 [0.750, 1.000]），DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B 同样跟随（0.729 [0.552, 0.885]）。

Conclusion: 现有自然设定不足以证明大模型真正"计算"权重；C2 与资格协议提供了可证伪的判别框架，但在公开模型库中尚不满足，结论有待更严格可证实的读者与设定。

Abstract: Large language models can predict the outputs of small neural networks from raw weights, but success could reflect recognition of weight-space regularities (fingerprints) rather than computation, and function-preserving controls cannot tell these apart. We propose C2, a counterfactual that permutes the columns of a network's final linear readout: every other parameter, and the readout's singular values, norms and entry multiset, are unchanged, and the function can change in an exactly recomputable way; we verify that each evaluated permutation does. C2 separates fingerprinting from computation only under three conditions, which we state as an eligibility protocol: a strategy restricted to C2-invariant summaries must already predict the target; C2 must change a property specific to the rewiring; and the reader's identity must be attested. No natural setting we tested is eligible. On tiny MLPs a frontier reader route followed the rewired function on all 24 networks, but invariant baselines were near chance and its backend could not be attested. In two public CNN zoos the conditions traded off: weight statistics predicted CIFAR-10 accuracy in-distribution, but C2's effect on accuracy was not specific to the swap, while per-image targets were specific but not predictable from invariant summaries. On a constructed population where a planted fingerprint rule works, all conditions hold and C2 separates a rule-following reader from an executor. A self-hosted Qwen3.8-27B followed the rewired function rather than the shortcut, and showed no evidence of using the weights with thinking disabled. A registered replication on fresh networks confirmed both findings (tracking minus stuck 0.875, 95% interval [0.750, 1.000]), and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B also followed the rewiring (tracking minus stuck 0.729 [0.552, 0.885]). The transforms, audits and raw records are available from the authors.

</details>


### [223] [Understanding the Importance of Massive Activations through Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32585)
*Tigran Fahradyan,Riccardo Ali,Sergio Calvo-Ordoñez,José Miguel Hernández-Lobato,Pietro Liò,Álvaro Arroyo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示"大规模激活"（massive activations）与深度学习中的"区域迁移"(region migration)及延迟鲁棒性现象相关联：大规模激活将表示推离网络非线性边界，降低输入-输出映射在数据点附近的复杂度，且该现象普遍存在于多种架构中，并与对抗鲁棒性共现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模激活在LLM中普遍出现，已被联系到attention sinks、表示压缩与信号传播，但其出现的原因与后果尚不清楚。作者希望从理论上解释其作用机制。

Method: 在样条理论（spline theoretic）视角下解释深度网络，分析大规模激活对非线性边界与表示位置的影响；并在多种架构（Residual MLP、ResNet18、ViT、GPT）与数据集（MNIST、CIFAR10、Imagenette、Shakespeare Text）上开展实验，包括LLM大规模预训练中监测对抗准确率随大规模激活上升的变化。

Result: 一致观察到大规模激活与区域迁移同对抗鲁棒性共现；首次发现该现象并非Transformer独有，而出现在多种执行简单监督学习任务的常见架构中；在LLM预训练中，大规模激活的上升与token embedding扰动下的对抗准确率变化密切相关。

Conclusion: 大规模激活通过将表示推离非线性边界、降低映射复杂度，构成延迟鲁棒性中区域迁移的机制性解释，且是一种跨架构的普遍现象，为理解LLM行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: A common occurrence in Large Language Models (LLMs) is the emergence of so-called massive activations. This phenomenon is well known in the literature and has been connected to attention sinks, representational compression, as well as signal propagation. However, the reasons and consequences for their appearance are not yet well understood. In this paper, we explain the role of massive activations in deep learning by showing that they are connected to the region migration phenomenon in delayed robustness. Under the spline theoretic interpretation of deep networks, we show that massive activations push the representations away from the nonlinearity boundaries of the network, decreasing the complexity of its input-output map around data points. Furthermore, we uncover for the first time that this phenomenon is not unique to transformers and actually appears in a number of common architectures performing simple supervised learning tasks. We validate this connection through extensive experiments on a diverse set of architectures including residual MLP, ResNet18, Vision Transformers and GPT and datasets including MNIST, CIFAR10, Imagenette and Shakespeare Text. We consistently observe that massive activations and region migration co-occur with adversarial robustness. Furthermore, we illustrate that this connection holds in the large-scale pretraining of LLMs by showing that the rise of massive activations in LLMs closely tracks the adversarial accuracy of the model under perturbations of the token embeddings.

</details>


### [224] [On the Effectiveness of Resampling for Supervised Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32598)
*Cyril Perosino,Rino Di Chiara,Hamida Seba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在GADBench低标签设定下系统评估类加权与六种过采样策略，发现过采样仅在人工注入(属性交换)异常上带来增益，且该增益依赖注入方式并可被调参与无标签基线解释，故应优先调参、重复划分、分开报告天然与注入异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图异常检测中类不平衡与异常标签稀缺促使人们采用过采样，但这些策略在不同检测器与基准上的实际收益缺乏系统、可复现的验证，作者因此对其有效性提出质疑并做对照研究。

Method: 在GADBench低标签设定(20个异常、80个正常标签节点)下，于17个数据集、10种检测器配置(GNN、基于邻域聚合特征的树集成、表格基础模型三大类)中比较类加权与最多六种过采样策略；对有/无注入异常的数据集分开分析，并考察注入配方(单随机供体、保范数交换)、超参调优(树模型)、以及同源节点的普通副本与采样图视图的差异，配合无标签的度与特征范数基线。

Result: 8个天然异常数据集上，任何策略在任何配置下均无统计显著的平均增益，标注哪些节点与策略选择同样重要；9个注入异常数据集上，高斯噪声过采样(ROSE)在6种树/表格基础模型配置中的4种最多提升约5.2 AUPRC点，仅在属性交换异常时六种配置全部有效，而4种GNN上其增益与零不可区分。该增益源于注入配方赋予被交换节点异常的特征范数，改用单随机供体或保范数交换后增益不再显著(但多数检测器也随之失效)。跨17个数据集，树模型调参约带来3个点提升，与ROSE相当，并使ROSE增益减半以上；同源节点与类权下，采样图视图与普通副本不可区分。

Conclusion: 过采样的收益多为人造异常注入伪影而非真实不平衡问题的解决。建议：优先调优基线、重复标注划分以报告方差、分开报告天然与注入异常结果，并加入无需标签的度与特征范数基线作为参照。

Abstract: Class imbalance and scarce anomaly labels motivate oversampling in supervised graph anomaly detection (GAD), but its benefits across detectors and benchmarks remain unclear. In the low-label setting of GADBench, with 20 labelled anomalies and 80 labelled normal nodes, we compare class weighting and up to six oversampling strategies in ten detector configurations from three families: graph neural networks (GNNs), tree ensembles on neighbourhood-aggregated features, and tabular foundation models, on 17 datasets. On the eight datasets with organic anomalies, no strategy has a statistically resolved average gain in any configuration, and which nodes are labelled matters as much as the strategy. On the nine datasets with injected anomalies, Gaussian-noise oversampling (ROSE) gains up to 5.2 AUPRC points in four of the six tree and tabular foundation model configurations, and in all six only on attribute-swap anomalies; in four GNNs its gain is not distinguishable from zero. This gain depends on the injection recipe, which gives swapped nodes an atypical feature norm: it is no longer distinguishable from zero when the swap uses a single random donor or preserves the norm, although most detectors then also stop detecting the swaps. Over the 17 datasets, tuning the tree detectors adds about three points, as much as ROSE, and cuts ROSE's gain by about half or more. With the same source nodes and class weight, sampled graph views are not distinguishable from plain copies of those nodes. We recommend tuning baselines, repeating labelled splits, reporting organic and injected anomalies separately, and checking label-free degree and feature-norm baselines.

</details>


### [225] [Gated Concept Adapters: Layer-Wise Concept Bottlenecks for Auditable Text Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32600)
*Niloofar Ranjbar,Saeedeh Momtazi,Mohammad Mehdi Homayounpoor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出门控概念适配器（GCA），在预训练Transformer的选定层输出上接入低维、门控的概念读出适配器，把token表示映射为概念激活并池化为层级摘要，作为概念中介接口暴露给预测头，从而提升人类面向文本理解系统的可审计性与可干预性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感推断、有害内容审核与社会敏感决策等人类面向的文本系统依赖Transformer，但其预测源于高维隐状态，缺乏紧凑、可检视的接口，难以供人类分析或干预，可审计性差。

Method: GCA为逐层概念瓶颈框架：在预训练Transformer若干层输出上附加带门控的低维概念读出适配器；token表示映射为概念激活，经学习门控过滤，池化为层级汇总，再以概念中介形式输入预测头。检视手段包括使用统计、签名n-gram、事后概念命名与定向概念干预。实验涵盖细粒度情感分类（GoEmotions）、毒性检测、主题分类（AG News）及受控动词完形压力测试。

Result: 在情感与安全相关分类任务中，GCA学到冗余较低、对干预敏感的概念表示，并保持有用的预测性能。毒性检测上接近无约束ModernBERT基线，同时支持概念级检视、定向干预与轻量事后控制。GoEmotions上端到端概念模型相对无约束RoBERTa有macro-F1损失，任务初始化并冻结的变体显著缩小该差距。AG News上与近期概念基线具竞争力；Qwen动词完形实验揭示概念中介全词表预测的局限。

Conclusion: GCA为可审计文本理解提供了神经概念接口，以透明性、诊断与干预为优先，而非无约束的精度最大化，并明确了概念中介方法的适用边界。

Abstract: Human-facing text understanding systems are used to infer affect, moderate harmful content, and support socially sensitive decisions. However, Transformer predictions are difficult to audit because they are produced from high-dimensional hidden states that do not provide a compact, inspectable interface for human analysis or intervention. We introduce Gated Concept Adapters (GCA), a layer-wise concept bottleneck framework that attaches gated, low-dimensional concept-producing readout adapters to selected outputs of pretrained Transformers. Token representations are mapped into concept activations, filtered by learned gates, pooled into layer-level summaries, and exposed to the prediction head as a concept-mediated interface. The dimensions can be inspected through usage statistics, signature n-grams, post-hoc concept names, and targeted concept interventions. We evaluate GCA on fine-grained emotion classification, toxicity detection, topic classification, and a controlled verb-cloze stress test. In the affective and safety-relevant classification settings, GCA learns comparatively low-redundancy and intervention-sensitive concept representations while retaining useful predictive performance. On toxicity detection, it remains close to an unconstrained ModernBERT baseline while enabling concept-level inspection, targeted intervention, and lightweight post-hoc control. On GoEmotions, the end-to-end concept model incurs a macro-F1 cost relative to unconstrained RoBERTa, while a task-initialized frozen variant substantially reduces this gap. On AG News, GCA remains competitive with recent concept-based baselines, and the Qwen verb-cloze experiment clarifies the limits of concept-mediated full-vocabulary prediction. Overall, GCA provides a neural concept interface for auditable text understanding, prioritizing transparency, diagnosis, and intervention over unconstrained accuracy maximization.

</details>


### [226] [ST-GNN-AAAS: Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks for Anytime Automatic Algorithm Selection in the Traveling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32602)
*Zeyu Zhang,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-GNN-AAAS，用时空图神经网络建模求解器执行过程中的时序与空间信息，实现TSP任意时间预算下的自动算法选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Anytime Automatic Algorithm Selection（AAAS）方法主要依赖静态实例特征，对求解器执行过程中揭示的时序信息利用有限。

Method: 构建时空图神经网络（ST-GNN）框架：用随时间变化的节点特征表示演进的当前最优回路（incumbent tour），包括各节点到当前回路中邻居的距离；并将这些特征与短历史窗口内的求解器性能观测相结合，由短历史预测更长未来时间跨度上的求解器选择。

Result: 在来自NATIONAL、TNM、TSPLIB、VLSI的多样化TSP基准子集上评估并公开求解器执行数据以支持可复现性；选择准确率达76.59%，填补单最优求解器（SBS）与虚拟最优求解器（VBS）平均排名差距的46.05%。

Conclusion: 利用求解过程中的时空动态信息可有效提升AAAS的求解器选择质量，显著缩小与VBS的差距。

Abstract: Anytime Automatic Algorithm Selection (AAAS) aims to make solver choices under varying time budgets. The existing AAAS approaches largely rely on static instance characteristics and make limited use of temporal information revealed during solver execution. We propose ST-GNN-AAAS, a Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Network framework for AAAS in the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP). The framework represents evolving incumbent tours using time-varying node features, including distances from each node to its neighbors in the current tour, and combines these features with solver performance observations over a short history window. Given this short history, ST-GNN-AAAS predicts solver selections over a longer future horizon. We evaluate the method on a subset of diverse TSP benchmarks from NATIONAL, TNM, TSPLIB, and VLSI, releasing the corresponding solver execution data to support reproducibility. Our model achieves a selection accuracy of 76.59% and closes 46.05% of the average rank gap between the single best solver (SBS) and the virtual best solver (VBS).

</details>


### [227] [HiCal: Offline Calibration for Hierarchical Scale Encoding in Accurate HiF4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32624)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Yanzhao Li,Hui Dong,Sifan Cheng,Mingen Zheng,Hui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HiCal，一种离线校准方法，通过优化HiFloat4三级缩放编码决策（Level-1 E6M2舍入偏置与Level-2/3扩展阈值）提升低比特LLM量化精度，推理时保持原确定性编码且无逐块多候选搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HiFloat4（HiF4）采用全局E6M2缩放加两级二进制缩放扩展的分层编码，但其常规舍入规则与固定扩展阈值难以充分匹配实际张量的统计特性，导致缩放表示次优、量化误差偏大。

Method: 离线校准：为Level-1 E6M2缩放引入校准舍入偏置；基于局部组幅值的经验分布优化Level-2与Level-3的缩放扩展阈值。校准参数离线确定、推理时固定；分层编码仍通过局部值自适应各块，避免逐块多候选缩放搜索，保留原HiF4确定性编码流程。

Result: 在5种合成与真实激活分布上，量化MSE相对原始HiF4降低9.91%–23.50%，其中真实Qwen3-8B激活降低13.00%；6个基准端到端评测中，Qwen3-8B与Qwen3-30B-A3B平均分分别提升0.91与1.25个百分点，推理期无需逐块多候选搜索。

Conclusion: 基于张量统计特性的离线缩放校准可在不增加推理开销、不改变确定性编码流程的前提下显著提升HiF4量化精度与下游任务表现，是一种实用且低成本的BFP量化改进方案。

Abstract: Block floating-point (BFP) quantization is widely adopted for efficient low-bit large language model (LLM) inference, where the choice of block scales plays a critical role in quantization accuracy. HiFloat4 (HiF4) employs a hierarchical scale encoding scheme consisting of a global E6M2 scale and two levels of binary scale extensions. However, its conventional rounding rule and fixed scale extension thresholds may not fully capture the statistical characteristics of the quantized tensors in practice, leading to suboptimal scale representations. In this work, we propose HiCal, an offline calibration approach that improves HiF4 quantization accuracy by refining the encoding decisions across all three scale levels. Specifically, we introduce a calibrated rounding bias for the Level-1 E6M2 scale and optimize the scale-extension thresholds at Levels 2 and 3 based on the empirical distributions of local group magnitudes. The resulting calibration parameters are determined offline and remain fixed during inference, while the hierarchical encoding continues to adapt to individual blocks through their local values. This design eliminates the need for per-block multi-candidate scale search while preserving the original deterministic HiF4 encoding procedure. Experiments across five synthetic and real activation distributions show that the proposed method reduces quantization MSE by 9.91%-23.50% relative to the original HiF4 scheme, including a 13.00% reduction on real Qwen3-8B activations. End-to-end evaluations across six benchmarks further show average-score improvements of 0.91 and 1.25 percentage points on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-30B-A3B, respectively, without per-block multi-candidate search during inference.

</details>


### [228] [TeamFlowRL: Online Policy Optimization of Decentralized Flow Policies for Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32645)
*Guowei Zou,Beiwen Zhang,Haitao Wang,Hong Hong,Zhiquan Chen,Hejun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeamFlowRL：离线预训练+在线微调的两阶段多智能体流策略框架，绕开动作似然不可解问题，在51组配对配置中实现47.3%平均归一化得分提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂协作任务需要多智能体协调行为。离线生成式策略虽能从轨迹中学到表达性强的协作行为，但受限于固定数据集；在线强化学习可通过环境交互改进策略，然而流策略因动作似然难以计算而难以微调。因此需要一个既能利用离线数据又能在线上持续改进的多智能体流策略微调方案。

Method: 提出TeamFlowRL两阶段框架：先离线预训练多智能体流策略，再在线微调。核心是在去中心化流策略的完整去噪轨迹上建立团队策略优化目标，回避不可解的动作似然计算；该目标用共享的团队优势对每个智能体的轨迹比率加权，并分别对各比率做裁剪，从而把局部策略更新对齐到共同团队回报。

Result: 在7个仿真组的51组配对配置中，TeamFlowRL对每种离线初始化均有提升，归一化得分平均提升47.3%；在6个双臂任务上，带探索噪声条件下平均成功率比高斯策略微调高33.1%。

Conclusion: 在完整去噪轨迹上做团队级策略优化可有效微调流策略，兼顾离线表达性与在线改进能力，并可稳定增益于多种初始化与任务。

Abstract: Complex cooperative tasks require coordinated multi-agent behavior. Offline generative policies learn expressive cooperative behaviors from trajectories but remain limited by fixed datasets. Online reinforcement learning can improve them through environment interaction. However, fine-tuning flow policies is difficult because action likelihoods are hard to compute. We propose TeamFlowRL, a two-stage framework that pretrains multi-agent flow policies offline and fine-tunes them online. We formulate team policy optimization over the complete denoising trajectories of decentralized flow policies, avoiding intractable action likelihoods. The resulting objective weights each agent's trajectory ratio with a shared team advantage and clips each ratio separately, aligning local policy updates with the common team return. Across 51 paired configurations in seven simulation groups, TeamFlowRL improves every offline initialization, with a 47.3% average gain in normalized score. It also achieves 33.1% higher average success under exploration noise than Gaussian-policy fine-tuning across 6 bimanual tasks.

</details>


### [229] [LATEC: Learned Axial TEnsor Compression for High-Dimensional Scientific Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32651)
*Hippolyte Verninas,Hadrien Hendrikx,Thomas Moreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LATEC——一种基于GNN的维度无关学习型预测器，实现逐点误差有界的科学数据压缩编解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学模拟规模增长使负担从生成数据转向分析存储，压缩是扩展存储容量关键，但需满足用户指定的逐点误差界以保证下游工作流有效性。现有图像/视频学习型编解码器优化聚合失真、难以满足误差界，且多针对固定维度，或需高昂自适应/重训练成本。

Method: LATEC采用基于GNN架构的维度无关学习型预测器，在合成的2-D与4-D场上预训练，并在3-D至5-D的13个科学场上评估，分为有/无领域微调两种设定。

Result: LATEC与SZ3持续具竞争力，平均提升18.1%，且在各领域间一致性优于其他压缩器。

Conclusion: 共享学习型预测器可作为跨张量维度的单一误差有界编解码器，与专用压缩器竞争，同时保留逐点误差保证。

Abstract: With the growing scale of scientific simulation and infrastructure, part of the burden has shifted from producing scientific data to analyzing and storing it. Compression is a key step to expanding storage capacity, but requires dedicated techniques to enforce user-specified pointwise error bounds that ensure the validity of downstream scientific workflows. To further push the limits of compression, novel learned codecs have been developed, in particular for images and videos, but these methods often optimize for aggregated distortion and fail to meet error bound standards. Moreover, these methods often target fixed field dimensionality, failing to account for many scientific data use cases, or at the expense of large adaptation or retraining costs. In this work, we introduce the Learned Axial TEnsor Compression (LATEC) method, an error-bounded codec with a dimension-agnostic learned predictor based on a GNN architecture. LATEC is pretrained on synthetic 2-D and 4-D fields, and evaluated with and without domain fine-tuning on 13 scientific fields in 3-D to 5-D. Results show that LATEC is consistently competitive with SZ3, achieving an 18.1% average improvement and greater consistency across all domains than other compressors. These results demonstrate that a shared learned predictor can serve as a single error-bounded codec across tensor dimensionalities, competitive with specialized compressors, while retaining pointwise error guarantees.

</details>


### [230] [Load Balancing Neurons: Firing-Rate Control Sustains Plasticity in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32691)
*Jan Robine,Marc Höftmann,Stefan Harmeling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出以 ReLU 单元激活率（正预激活比例）作为诊断持续学习中神经元失活/饱和的指标，并据此设计轻量的逐神经元阈值负载均衡机制，将激活率维持在目标区间以缓解可塑性丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中的神经网络常出现可塑性下降：部分神经元失活，另一些几乎恒激活，二者都限制对新分布数据的适应并浪费容量。已有方法通过周期性重置低效用单元来缓解，但重置会破坏已学特征，且属于被动事后处理，无法主动防止低效用。

Method: 以 ReLU 单元激活率（正预激活占比）为诊断量：低激活率对应死单元，极高激活率对应线性化的恒激活单元。基于该视角，引入轻量负载均衡机制，按神经元调整阈值，使激活率落在目标区间内。

Result: 在持续 ImageNet 与类别增量 CIFAR-100 上，激活率分布可解释包括 L2 正则与非仿射归一化等知名方法在内的多种方法的可塑性差异；负载均衡在两个基准上达到或超过基于重置的方法；同一机制迁移至强化学习，在 Atari 上使 Rainbow DQN 优于 LayerNorm 基线。

Conclusion: 激活率是一个简洁有效的可塑性诊断量，据此设计的阈值负载均衡可作为重置类方法的主动替代方案，并具有跨任务（持续学习与强化学习）的通用性。

Abstract: Neural networks in continual learning often lose plasticity: some neurons become inactive, while others fire almost constantly. This limits adaptation to shifting data and wastes capacity. Prior work mitigates this by periodically reinitializing low-utility units, but such resets can destroy previously learned features and do not proactively prevent low utility. We study a simple diagnostic measure: the firing rate of ReLU units, defined as the fraction of positive pre-activations. Low rates identify dead units, while very high rates indicate linearized, always-on units. Based on this view, we introduce a lightweight load-balancing mechanism that adjusts per-neuron thresholds to keep firing rates within a target range. Across continual ImageNet and class-incremental CIFAR-100, firing-rate distributions explain differences in plasticity across a range of approaches, including well-known techniques such as L2 regularization and non-affine normalization, and load balancing matches or exceeds reset-based methods on both benchmarks. The same mechanism carries over to reinforcement learning, improving Rainbow DQN on Atari over a LayerNorm baseline.

</details>


### [231] [Contrastive Proxy Forests: Completeness-Preserving Proxy Learning from Multimodal Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32708)
*Kyoungeui Hong,Zijun Gao,Ruishan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Contrastive Proxy Forests (CPF)：在近端因果推断框架下，通过在独立无标签样本上对比治疗侧与结局侧模态（等价于岭典型相关分析，有精确闭式解），学习满足排除限制且继承完整性的两个代理变量，再交给近端因果森林估计因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端因果推断需用混杂的两个代理（结局侧 W、治疗侧 Z），二者须满足排除限制与完整性。实际中代理多从结构化表格手工挑选，而临床文本、生理波形含更丰富的混杂信息；这些高维记录超出近端估计器处理能力，而 PCA 等通用压缩可能保留模态特有的风格变异而非混杂，从而破坏完整性。

Method: 核心观察：在近端条件下，混杂是治疗侧与结局侧模态在给定协变量后唯一的联系，学习二者共享成分即可捕获混杂并滤除模态特有风格。CPF 在独立无标签样本上，给定协变量对比两个模态来学习两个代理，再传入近端因果森林。该对比目标有精确闭式解，即岭典型相关分析。理论上证明 CPF 精确保持排除限制，并在所刻画条件下其代理继承原始记录的完整性。

Result: 在风格较强的情形下，相较 PCA 代理，CPF 将条件效应估计误差降低 10–22%；在 MIMIC-IV 上，CPF 是唯一使华法林对卒中效应的点估计落在随机试验结果范围内的方法。

Conclusion: 以跨模态对比学习替代手工或通用压缩构造代理，可在保持近端识别条件的同时利用高维临床记录的信息，提升近端因果效应估计的准确性与可信度。

Abstract: Proximal causal inference removes unmeasured confounding using two proxies of the confounder, an outcome-side W and a treatment-side Z. The proxies must satisfy exclusion restrictions and be complete, meaning that they reflect every aspect of the confounder. In practice, they are a few variables hand-picked from a structured table, even though clinical notes and physiological waveforms carry rich information about the confounder. Such records are too high-dimensional for a proximal estimator, and generic compression such as PCA may keep modality-specific variation (style) instead of the confounder, destroying completeness. Our key observation is that, under the proximal condition, the confounder is the only link between a treatment-side and an outcome-side modality beyond the covariates. Learning what they share therefore captures the confounder and filters out modality-specific style. Contrastive Proxy Forests (CPF) learns both proxies by contrasting the two modalities, given the covariates, on a separate unlabeled sample, and passes them to a proximal causal forest. This contrastive objective has an exact closed-form solution, ridge canonical correlation analysis. We prove that CPF preserves the exclusion restrictions exactly and that its proxies inherit the completeness of the raw records under conditions we characterize. Where style is strong, CPF lowers the error of conditional effects by 10-22% relative to PCA proxies, and on MIMIC-IV it is the only method whose point estimate of warfarin's effect on stroke falls inside the range of randomized trials.

</details>


### [232] [FourStat: A Dual AutoEncoder Leveraging Frequency and Stationarity for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32714)
*Saman Hadian,Ramtin Moslemi,Mohammad Mohammadi,Sarina Heshmati,Siavash Ahmadi,Babak Hossein Khalaj,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FourStat：结合傅里叶自编码器（FAE）与平稳自编码器（SAE）的双模型单变量时间序列异常检测框架，并提出无阈值主观性的新评测指标 Perfect Point。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多变量时间序列异常检测方法在单变量场景下表现欠佳或难以直接适用；同时传统 F1 评估依赖阈值选择，带有主观性。

Method: 双模型互补框架：FAE 通过频域分析捕获季节性等全局模式；SAE 通过施加统计平稳性约束聚焦局部结构。另提出 Perfect Point 指标，判断能否直接从异常分数获得完美 F1。

Result: FourStat 优于现有基线，尤其在困难异常类型上提升明显；新指标更清晰地揭示模型性能差距与优势。

Conclusion: 频域与平稳性双视角互补可有效提升单变量异常检测性能，Perfect Point 为免阈值评估提供了更客观的性能比较方式。

Abstract: While numerous studies have focused on multivariate time series anomaly detection, many existing methods perform poorly or are not directly applicable to univariate settings. We introduce FourStat, a dual-model framework for univariate time series anomaly detection that combines two complementary approaches. The Fourier Autoencoder (FAE) captures global patterns like seasonality through frequency-domain analysis, while the Stationary Autoencoder (SAE) focuses on local structures by enforcing statistical stationarity. We also propose a new evaluation metric called Perfect Point that assesses whether perfect F1-scores can be achieved directly from anomaly scores, eliminating thresholding subjectivity. Results demonstrate that FourStat outperforms existing baselines, particularly on challenging anomaly types, while the new metric provides clearer insights into model performance gaps and advantages.

</details>


### [233] [SGD with Large Learning Rates: General Analysis & Improved Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32762)
*Konstantinos Emmanouilidis,Ziqing Xu,Salma Tarmoun,Lachlan MacDonald,Rene Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明在大步长条件下随机梯度下降（SGD）用于交叉熵损失（线性预测器与两层神经网络）时仍保持加速收敛，达到 O(T^{-2}) 速率，从而正面回答了「大步长加速现象是否在随机噪声下成立」这一公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SGD分析将步长限制在依赖曲率的稳定性阈值以下；近期确定性优化发现适当的大步长既能保持GD收敛又能加速。但随机梯度噪声是否会破坏这一加速机制一直未知（Wu et al., 2024 提出的开放问题）。

Method: 针对交叉熵损失下的线性预测器与两层神经网络，对随机大步长动力学进行细粒度刻画，给出更精确的轨迹控制；该分析同时在稳定区域内给出紧致的收敛保证。

Result: 建立了 O(T^{-2}) 的加速收敛率；获得的收敛界在稳定区域内更紧，甚至改进了确定性情形下的已有界；数值实验验证了大学习率的实际收益。

Conclusion: 大步长诱导的加速机制能够抵抗随机噪声，并可推广到线性模型之外的模型（两层神经网络）。

Abstract: Stochastic Gradient Descent lies at the core of modern machine learning. However its classical analysis typically restricts the stepsize to be below a curvature-dependent stability threshold. Recent developments in deterministic optimization have challenged this paradigm, showing that suitably chosen large stepsizes can not only preserve convergence, but also accelerate Gradient Descent (GD). Whether this phenomenon persists in the presence of stochastic gradient noise has remained an open question (Wu et al., 2024). In this work, we answer this question affirmatively for the cross-entropy loss with linear predictors and two-layer neural networks, establishing an accelerated $\mathcal{O}(T^{-2})$ convergence rate. At the core of our analysis is a fine-grained characterization of the stochastic large-stepsize dynamics. This sharper control of the trajectory further yields tight convergence guarantees within the stable regime, improving existing bounds even in the deterministic setting. Taken together, our results show that the acceleration mechanism induced by large stepsizes survives stochasticity and extends beyond linear models. Numerical experiments demonstrate the benefits of large learning rates in practice.

</details>


### [234] [Measuring Layer-wise Intrinsic Dimensionality of FFNs in LLMs via PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32782)
*Daehee Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过PCA测量58个开源LLM各层FFN的内在维度，发现"中间层重"的稳定剖面，并据此指导宽度剪枝，在匹配参数预算下优于均匀宽度约定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM沿用原始Transformer约定，使所有层的FFN中间宽度相同；但机制研究表明不同深度层功能不同，其容量需求是否也不均匀、固定参数预算能否更优分配，尚不清楚。

Method: 对每个FFN下投影的输入做PCA，定义在固定余弦相似度重构阈值下所需最少主成分数为该层内在维度；扫描14个家族、70M–72B共58个开源模型；并将该信号用于知识蒸馏学生的宽度剪枝分配。

Result: 普遍出现中间层内在维度高于边缘层的"中间层重"剖面，对校准语料（含中英文）稳健，基本经后训练保留。PCA引导分配在匹配预算下较均匀宽度提升+2.3（Qwen3-8B）和+2.5（Qwen2.5-3B）；离散度较低的教师提升较小（Gemma-3-4B +0.9，LLaMA-3-8B +0.5）；预算匹配的反向对照在四位教师上均最差。

Conclusion: FFN容量需求沿深度非均匀，内在维度剖面可作为结构性有效信号指导参数分配；提升幅度与教师内在维度的层间不均衡程度（σ99）相关，且优势在下游SFT后仍保持。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) keep the feed-forward network (FFN) intermediate width identical across every layer, a convention inherited from the original Transformer. Yet mechanistic work suggests layers at different depths do qualitatively different work, raising a natural question: are their capacity demands also non-uniform, and if so, can a fixed parameter budget be allocated more wisely than uniformly? To probe this, we apply principal component analysis (PCA) to the input of each FFN down-projection and measure, for every layer, its intrinsic dimensionality as the smallest number of principal components needed to reconstruct the activation up to a fixed cosine similarity. Scanning 58 open-weight LLMs spanning 14 families and 70M to 72B parameters, we find a recurring middle-heavy profile in which middle layers carry higher intrinsic dimensionality than edge layers; the profile is robust to the calibration corpus, even across English and Chinese, and is largely preserved through post-training fine-tuning. As a direct test that this signal is structurally meaningful, we use it to guide width pruning in a knowledge-distilled student under a matched parameter budget: the PCA-guided allocation outperforms the uniform-width convention by +2.3 points on Qwen3-8B and +2.5 on Qwen2.5-3B, while lower-dispersion teachers show smaller gaps (+0.9 on Gemma-3-4B, +0.5 on LLaMA-3-8B), and a budget-matched inverted control does worst on all four teachers. Across these four teachers, the size of the gap tracks how unevenly the teacher's intrinsic dimensionality is distributed across layers (a single-number summary we call $σ_{99}$), and the advantage persists through downstream supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/naver-ai/lidf.

</details>


### [235] [Scaling Laws of Ensemble Uncertainty in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32801)
*Siamak Ravanbakhsh,David Nitchi,Khang Ngo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证贝叶斯深度学习方法因受限于固定模型族而无法刻画LLM规模下的完整认知不确定性，并用三条幂律分别描述容量误差、集成内部不一致的收缩以及集成多样性损失，指出多样化集成在规模化与不确定性量化上更具前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯深度学习方法通常限定于固定模型族，模型族容量有限所导致的误差无法通过先验支持下的模型间分歧反映出来，因此在实用LLM规模下会低估认知不确定性。作者希望厘清这一被忽视的误差来源，并评估集成相对于单纯增大单模型这一缩放轴的价值。

Method: 理论分析框架：(1) 将模型族容量导致的误差与LLM损失随模型规模变化的幂律缩放关系相联系；(2) 用第二条幂律刻画集成内部模型间分歧随规模增大而收缩的规律，并与容量幂律比较衰减速率；(3) 用第三条幂律刻画集成成员在分叉前共享更大比例训练过程时多样性下降的规律，用以说明LoRA集成及基于优化/二阶方法在单一训练解附近近似不确定性的额外局限。

Result: 集成内部不一致的幂律衰减速率慢于容量幂律，说明随规模增大集成能捕获更大比例的认知不确定性；同时集成多样性随共享训练比例上升而按幂律下降，构成超出容量误差的另一重限制。

Conclusion: 多样化集成是一条值得重视的缩放轴，同时适用于大规模下的不确定性量化；单纯增大单模型或依赖单一训练解附近的近似方法难以覆盖全部认知不确定性。

Abstract: Many use cases for large language models (LLMs)---from AI safety to exploration and human interaction---can benefit from epistemic uncertainty estimates. We argue that existing Bayesian Deep Learning solutions, which are often restricted to a fixed model family, fail to capture the full epistemic uncertainty at practical LLM scales. This is because the error due to the bounded capacity of the model family remains invisible to disagreement among models supported by the prior. We relate this error to the well-known power-law scaling of LLM loss with model size. We then show that a second power law quantifies the shrinking disagreement within ensembles as model size increases. Its slower decay relative to the capacity law above suggests that ensembles capture a growing share of epistemic uncertainty and become more competitive as an axis for scaling, compared to a single larger model. Finally, a third power law quantifies the loss of ensemble diversity as members share a larger fraction of training before branching, and therefore identifies a further limitation, beyond the capacity error, for LoRA ensembles and optimization-based or second-order methods that approximate uncertainty around a single training solution. At a high level, these findings suggest diverse ensembles can be a promising axis, both for scaling and uncertainty quantification at larger scales.

</details>


### [236] [Sample-Efficient In-Context Adaptation via Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32802)
*Sangyun Lee,Zhang Enyan,Michael Xu,Hao Tang,Zinan Lin,Weijia Xu,Chris Quirk,Jianfeng Gao,Giulia Fanti,Xiaodong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"睡眠"机制（对上下文进行N次循环前向传播以精炼表示）能否提升Transformer语言模型的上下文内适应能力；实验表明更大的N使模型在少样本任务上学得更快，并在OOD评估中取得更低损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限经验中学习和泛化（样本效率）是智能的核心，作者希望构建能仅凭少量用户交互、陌生代码片段或上下文实验结果表明实现泛化的语言模型。已有研究发现睡眠机制可提升长上下文推理，但仅限于SSM-注意力混合模型，且多在合成任务上验证，因此作者探究该方法对普通Transformer模型在一般非合成任务上的上下文内适应是否有帮助。

Method: 在最多100B token上预训练参数量至多1.2B的Transformer语言模型，并引入睡眠机制，即对一段上下文执行N次循环前向传播以精炼表示；通过改变N考察其对上下文内适应的影响，并在少样本算法任务、问答任务及分布外（OOD）评估和留出代码数据上进行评测。

Result: 更长的睡眠（更大的N）使模型在多个少样本算法与问答任务上上下文学习更快，同时在OOD评估中获得更低的下一词预测损失。在留出代码数据上，N=2与N=4的模型分别以2.10倍和2.39倍更少的上下文token达到与无睡眠基线相同的损失。

Conclusion: 睡眠机制可提升Transformer语言模型在一般非合成任务上的上下文内适应与泛化效率，本研究是朝着构建在上下文适应与解码延迟之间具有更优权衡的模型迈出的一步。

Abstract: The ability to learn and generalize from limited experience, or sample efficiency, is a cornerstone of intelligence. How can we build language models that turn a few user interactions, a few snippets of unfamiliar code, or a handful of experimental results provided in context into knowledge that generalizes? Prior work has found that sleep, a process where the model performs $N$ recurrent forward passes to refine its representations of a chunk of context, can improve long-context reasoning (specifically, within SSM-attention hybrid models). We study whether this method also improves in-context adaptation on general, non-synthetic tasks for transformer language models. To this end, we pre-train language models up to 1.2B-parameters on up to 100B tokens with sleep mechanisms. Notably, longer sleep (i.e., using a larger number of recurrent forward passes $N$) enables the model to learn faster in-context on multiple few-shot algorithmic, question-answering tasks, while also achieving lower next-token prediction loss on out-of-distribution evaluations. On held-out code, models with $N=2$ and $N=4$ match the no-sleep baseline's loss using $2.10\times$ and $2.39\times$ fewer tokens in-context, respectively. More broadly, we view our work as a step toward building models that have better tradeoffs between in-context adaptation and decoding latency.

</details>


### [237] [EPRO: Robust Policy Optimization Through an Imperfect Execution-Policy Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32810)
*Yibo Wang,Changyi Yang,Yuanxin Liu,Zhaoran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EPRO：把RL中训练引擎与rollout引擎不一致导致的"不可观测执行策略"优化问题形式化，用可微预测器估计执行策略响应（TSC），并以Policy Shift代理风险、SSC抑制高风险更新，在7个数学推理基准上取得最优平均留出准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练LLM时，rollout生成与策略训练分离：两个数值引擎虽共享参数，却可能诱导出不同的策略。已有工作多关注"修正已知的训练-推理不匹配"，但真正的难点在于learner更新过程中，当前参数对应的执行策略无法被直接观测。作者将其视为"通过一个不完美的演化预测器来优化不可观测的执行策略"，由此引出两个基本问题：如何估计执行策略对learner更新的响应，以及在优化真实执行策略时应在多大程度上信任该估计。

Method: 提出Execution-Policy Robust Optimization（EPRO）。以观测到的rollout时刻执行策略为起点，利用训练策略的变化构造当前执行策略的可微预测器，其score导出Transported Score Centering（TSC）。通过robust-improvement分析刻画预测误差如何限制可靠更新的幅度。实践中引入Policy Shift作为预测风险的可观测代理，并用Shift-Aware Soft Clipping（SSC）衰减高风险更新。

Result: 在四种Qwen3模型设置下，EPRO在七个数学推理基准上取得了所比较方法中最高的平均留出准确率。

Conclusion: 将训练-推理分离导致的执行策略不可观测性建模为"带不完美预测器的鲁棒优化"是可行且有效的；预测误差是限制更新幅度的关键量，需以可观测风险代理（Policy Shift）配合软裁剪来约束更新，从而在多个模型规模与基准上稳定获得更优性能。

Abstract: RL for LLMs often separates rollout generation from policy training. Although the two numerical engines share model parameters, they can induce different policies. This creates a challenge beyond correcting a known training-inference mismatch: during learner updates, the execution policy corresponding to the current parameters cannot be directly observed. We view this as optimizing an unobserved execution policy through an imperfect predictor of its evolution. This perspective raises two fundamental questions: how can we estimate the execution policy's response to learner updates, and how much should we trust that estimate when optimizing the actual execution policy? We introduce Execution-Policy Robust Optimization (EPRO) to address these challenges. Starting from the observed rollout-time execution policy, we use training-policy changes to construct a differentiable predictor of the current execution policy, whose score yields Transported Score Centering (TSC). Our robust-improvement analysis reveals how prediction error limits the magnitude of reliably justified updates. For practical implementation, we introduce Policy Shift as an observable proxy for prediction risk and use Shift-Aware Soft Clipping (SSC) to attenuate risky updates. Across four Qwen3 model settings, EPRO achieves the highest average held-out accuracy among the compared methods on seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [238] [Breaking the Joint Action Space Dependence in Distributionally Robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32812)
*Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限时域、一般和、分布鲁棒马尔可夫博弈中鲁棒粗相关均衡（CCE）的样本复杂度，提出仅依赖最大单玩家动作数 A_* 而非联合动作数 ∏A_i 的算法，突破多智能体诅咒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对 (s,a)-矩形 TV 不确定集的生成模型保证，样本复杂度随联合动作数 ∏_i A_i 增长，玩家数增多时指数爆炸，产生"多智能体诅咒"。作者希望在不修改不确定集的前提下消除这种对联合动作数的依赖。

Method: 设计新算法，包含两个关键要素：(1) 鲁棒备份的插件式估计，其平均误差不随状态数增长；(2) 每轮仅用单批样本同时作为所有玩家的 bandit 反馈，从而只需 O(SA_*H^5D^4/ε^4) 个转移样本。

Result: 以至少 1-δ 的概率返回一个马尔可夫策略，使其从每个初始状态出发都是 ε-鲁棒 CCE；样本复杂度仅通过最大单玩家动作数 A_*=max_i A_i 依赖动作集，其中 D:=max_i min{H, 1/ρ_i}，ρ_i 为玩家 i 的 TV 半径。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在保持 (s,a)-矩形不确定集不变的情况下，可以避免对联合动作数 ∏_i A_i 的依赖，从而缓解多智能体诅咒。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of learning robust coarse correlated equilibria (CCE) in finite-horizon, general-sum, distributionally robust Markov games with known rewards and a generative model of the nominal transitions. Each player evaluates a policy by its worst-case return over total-variation (TV) balls centered at the nominal transition distribution of every stage, state, and joint action; this is the standard $(s,a)$-rectangular uncertainty set. Existing generative-model guarantees for this model scale with the number $\prod_iA_i$ of joint actions, where $A_i$ is the number of actions of player~$i$. Since this number grows exponentially with the number of players, these guarantees suffer from the curse of multiagency. We design an algorithm whose sample complexity depends on the action sets only through the largest individual action-set size $A_*:=\max_iA_i$. The algorithm uses $\widetilde O(SA_*H^5D^4/\varepsilon^4)$ transition samples and, with probability at least $1-δ$, returns a Markov policy that is an $\varepsilon$-robust CCE from every initial state. Here $S$ is the number of states, $H$ is the horizon, and $D:=\max_i\min\{H,1/ρ_i\}$, where $ρ_i$ is the TV radius of player~$i$. Two ingredients make this possible: a plug-in estimate of the robust backup whose mean error does not depend on the number of states, and a single batch of samples per round that serves as bandit feedback for all players at once. The result shows that the dependence on $\prod_iA_i$ can be avoided without modifying the $(s,a)$-rectangular uncertainty set.

</details>


### [239] [E(n)-Equivariant Spherical Decision Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32815)
*Pavlo Melnyk,Michael Felsberg,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.LG
