TL;DR: 提出一种构造性方法，将已有O(n)-等变超球神经元扩展至包含平移，得到恰好E(n)-等变的球面决策曲面，并给出显式闭式矩阵表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有O(n)-等变超球神经元缺少平移分量，无法实现完整的E(n)等变性；且常见做法依赖输入中心化或显式成对差分，不够优雅。

Method: 对O(n)-等变神经元特征进行分解，给出平移与E(n)变换的显式表示以满足等变约束，构造性推导出恰好E(n)-等变的球面决策曲面。

Result: 所得决策曲面无需输入中心化或显式成对差分即具备精确E(n)等变性，并具有显式闭式矩阵表示；通过数值实验验证推导正确性，并对所得几何基元进行下游任务检验。

Conclusion: 为E(n)-等变几何深度学习提供了一种紧凑、可解释的球面基元构造方案，兼具理论精确性与实用性。

Abstract: We present a constructive derivation of exactly E(n)-equivariant spherical decision surfaces by extending prior O(n)-equivariant hypersphere neurons to include translations. To achieve this, we present a decomposition of the features of the O(n)-equivariant neurons and provide explicit representations for translation and E(n)-transformations to fulfil the respective equivariance constraints. The resulting decision surfaces are exactly E(n)-equivariant without input centring or explicit pairwise differences, and admit explicit closed-form matrix representations. In addition, we numerically verify the correctness of the derivations and perform a downstream check of the resulting geometric primitives.

</details>


### [240] [PtychoFM: A Foundation Model for Ptychographic Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32830)
*Aileen Luo,Emon Dey,Ming Du,Tao Zhou,Peco Myint,Himanshu Goel,Shashank Subramanian,Xiangyu Yin,Tekin Bicer,Siniša Veseli,Cheng-Chu Chung,Jeffrey A. Klug,Aldo Peña Pérez,Llorenç Fanals-i-Batllori,Christopher Kenney,Henry Shi,Lorenzo Rota,Angelo Dragone,Zirui Gao,Douglas A. Bennett,John A. B. Mates,Leila R. Vale,Michael R. Vissers,Yi Jiang,Junjing Deng,Tejas Guruswamy,Varuni K. Sastry,Bowen Zheng,Takenori Shimamura,Oliver Hoidn,Aashwin Mishra,Hannah Parraga,Brandon Stone,Jonathan Baldwin,Aaron Michelson,Michael Wojcik,Xiaoyin Zheng,Robert Underwood,Yijiang Li,Jinxing Jiang,Nicholas Schwarz,Apurva Mehta,Johanna Nelson Weker,Hassina Bilheux,Joel N. Ullom,Alexander Hexemer,Antonino Miceli,Kibaek Kim,Hanfei Yan,Mathew J. Cherukara,Alec R. Sandy,Stefan Vogt,Laurent C. Chapon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PtychoFM，一个物理感知的基础模型，结合大规模预训练与实验特定微调，直接从单张衍射图重建叠层成像的复物体图像，实现最高 200 帧/秒的实时重建与可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠层成像（ptychography）虽可在 X 射线、电子与光学显微中实现高分辨率无透镜成像，但重建通常依赖计算密集的迭代相位检索，且需针对具体实验做专门分析，难以在采集速率提升时提供实时反馈。

Method: 构建物理感知基础模型 PtychoFM：利用可微分前向模型进行大规模预训练（无需物体空间标签），再结合实验特定微调，从单张衍射图直接输出复物体图像；并实现探测器到单 GPU 的流式推理管线用于同步辐射线站实时可视化。

Result: 模型可从某样品的步进扫描训练数据迁移到其他样品（不同特征尺寸、形貌与折射率）的飞行扫描测量；在同步辐射线站上实现最高 200 帧/秒的推理并实时回传可视化。

Conclusion: PtychoFM 为快速、可迁移的叠层重建提供了实用路径，可在非理想实验条件下实现实时实验反馈。

Abstract: Ptychography provides high-resolution lensless imaging across X-ray, electron, and optical microscopy, but reconstructing images from diffraction measurements generally requires computationally intensity iterative phase retrieval. These methods often require experiment-specific analysis, limiting real-time feedback as data acquisition rates increase. Here, we demonstrate PtychoFM, a physics-aware foundation model for ptychographic imaging that combines large-scale pre-training with experiment-specific fine-tuning to reconstruct complex object images directly from individual diffraction patterns. Trained using a differentiable forward model without object-space labels, PtychoFM transfers from step-scan training data on one sample to fly-scan measurements of other samples with different feature sizes, morphologies, and refraction indices. At a synchrotron beamline, diffraction frames were streamed from the detector to a single GPU for inference at up to 200 frames per second and returned for real-time visualization. PtychoFM provides a practical route to rapid, transferable ptychographic reconstruction and live experimental feedback under nonideal conditions.

</details>


### [241] [Tabra Blends: Improving the Accuracy--Latency Trade-off with Complementary Tabular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32859)
*Jing Jia,Guanyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧凑集成能否以更低延迟获得更高精度：在816个TabArena划分上，将4个Kumo估计器与1个LimiX流水线混合，取得2063.8 Elo，比同一次拟合中公开最优条目高45.4点，归一化拟合+预测时间比Kumo-16低15%、比完整LimiX低98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在预测精度与推理延迟之间存在不同的权衡，作者希望验证"更小、更紧凑的集成"是否可能同时改善二者，即用更少时间换取更高精度。

Method: 构建紧凑混合集成：4个Kumo估计器 + 1个LimiX流水线，在TabArena的816个数据划分上进行统一拟合与评估；以Elo作为精度指标，以归一化拟合+预测时间作为效率指标，并与Kumo-16、完整LimiX做对比；同时分析两模型间的预测多样性对误差下降的解释作用。

Result: 集成达到2063.8 Elo，高于同一拟合中已发表最优条目45.4点；归一化拟合+预测时间分别比Kumo-16低15%、比完整LimiX低98%；发现两模型之间更大的预测多样性有助于解释误差的降低。

Conclusion: 紧凑的异构集成可在更低延迟下超越更大规模的单一表格基础模型，精度—效率并非必然对立；跨模型的预测多样性是误差下降的重要来源，为表格基础模型的实用化部署提供了低成本高效能路径。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models make different trade-offs between prediction accuracy and latency. We study whether a compact ensemble can deliver higher accuracy in less time. Across 816 TabArena splits, our blend of four-estimator Kumo and one LimiX pipeline reaches 2063.8 Elo, 45.4 points above the top published entry in the same fit. Its normalized fit + predict time is 15% lower than Kumo-16's and 98% lower than full LimiX's. We find that greater prediction diversity between the two models helps explain the reduction in error.

</details>


### [242] [Weight Scale Rebalancing: Transferable Training Recipes for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32866)
*Yikun Hou,Naomi Graham,Alp Yurtsever*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UDV-Rebalance（UDVR）重参数化：在成对权重矩阵(U,V)间插入辅助对角矩阵D并周期性重平衡，使单组超参可跨模型规模、架构与数据分布通用，实现免调优的尺度自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer训练对超参数调优敏感，且大模型上调优成本高昂；现有方法（Weight Normalization、σReparam等）缺乏跨规模、跨架构、跨数据分布的可迁移调优规则。

Method: 在Transformer中天然成对的权重矩阵(U,V)之间插入辅助对角矩阵D，形成U-D-V结构；周期性重平衡使U列、V行保持单位范数，尺度由D吸收；由此得到仅依赖权重矩阵维度的免调优超参规则，并只需在ViT/CIFAR上拟合一次。

Result: 该规则在激活函数或优化器改变后仍有效；可迁移至DomainNet新图像域、ImageNet上的ViT/Swin/MaxViT，以及最高1.3B参数的语言模型预训练；UDVR稳定优于Weight Normalization与σReparam，并与逐模型扫参的vanilla基线持平。

Conclusion: 通过重平衡实现尺度解耦，UDVR将超参调节转化为与维度相关的确定性规则，具备强跨规模与跨模态可迁移性，为大模型提供实用的免调优训练方案。

Abstract: Training Transformers requires careful hyperparameter tuning, which becomes increasingly costly for large models. We introduce UDV-Rebalance (UDVR), a reparameterization that inserts an auxiliary diagonal matrix $D$ between naturally paired weight matrices $(U,V)$ appearing throughout Transformer architectures. UDVR periodically rebalances the factors to maintain unit-norm columns in $U$ and unit-norm rows in $V$, with $D$ absorbing the corresponding scales. With rebalancing, a single hyperparameter setting of UDVR remains effective across model scales, architectures, and data distributions. This yields a tuning-free rule that adapts to each weight matrix using only its dimensions. We fit this rule once on ViT models trained on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, and it remains valid when the activation function or the optimizer is changed. The same rule transfers to new image domains in DomainNet, to ViT, Swin, and MaxViT on ImageNet, and to language-model pretraining with up to 1.3B parameters. UDVR consistently outperforms Weight Normalization and $σ$Reparam and matches a vanilla baseline whose hyperparameters are swept on each target model.

</details>


### [243] [MXFP4 for LLM Inference: Complementary Post-Training Quantization Techniques and Their Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32873)
*Natalia Frumkin,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Eric Dellinger,Stuart Biles*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究六种LLM在MXFP6/MXFP4/NVFP4三种微缩放格式下PTQ配置的可组合性，发现有效量化配方依赖于具体数值格式，并提出免校准的Midmax尺度选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微缩放格式（每16/32个元素共享一个缩放因子）可支持激进低精度量化，但其精度取决于数值格式与旋转、PTQ、尺度选择的交互方式；现有工作缺乏对三者组合效果的系统性分析，且不同格式间技术迁移性不明。

Method: 在6个LLM与3种格式（MXFP6、MXFP4、NVFP4）上系统变化Hadamard旋转粒度、PTQ算法与尺度选择策略，考察PTQ配置的可组合性；提出Midmax——一种免校准尺度选择策略，用于缓解2的幂块尺度带来的截断误差；并额外研究per-tensor macroscale对MXFP4的作用。

Result: 量化配方具有格式依赖性：MXFP4显著受益于旋转与纠错型PTQ，NVFP4因尺度更细粒度而受益较小；Midmax在多数MXFP4配置上提升精度；MXFP6激活+MXFP4权重可恢复4-bit大部分精度损失，在Llama-3.1-8B上达到或超过内部BF16基线；为MXFP4添加per-tensor macroscale未提升平均精度。

Conclusion: 有效的低精度LLM推理需按格式定制组合数值格式、旋转、PTQ与尺度选择；全局重缩放无法弥补E8M0块尺度在MXFP4上的局限，而Midmax与混合精度（MXFP6激活/MXFP4权重）提供了实用改进路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in low-precision representation have introduced several microscaling numerical formats for LLM quantization. By sharing a scale factor across groups of 16 or 32 elements, these formats enable aggressive low-precision quantization with minimal accuracy degradation, particularly when combined with modern PTQ algorithms. Their accuracy depends on how the numerical format interacts with rotation, PTQ, and scale selection. We study the composability of PTQ configurations across six LLMs and three microscaling formats: MXFP6, MXFP4, and NVFP4, systematically varying Hadamard rotation granularity, PTQ algorithm, and scale-selection strategy. Our central finding is that effective quantization recipes are format dependent: both MXFP4 and NVFP4 support accurate 4-bit inference, but techniques that benefit one do not necessarily transfer to the other. MXFP4 benefits substantially from rotation and error-correcting PTQ, whereas NVFP4 gains less from rotation, consistent with its finer-grained scales capturing more within-block variation. Building on these findings, we introduce Midmax, a calibration-free scale-selection strategy that mitigates clipping error in power-of-two block scales and improves accuracy across a majority of the evaluated MXFP4 configurations. We show that pairing MXFP6 activations with MXFP4 weights recovers most of the accuracy lost at 4-bit and, on Llama-3.1-8B, matches or exceeds our in-house BF16 baseline. An additional study finds that augmenting MXFP4 with a per-tensor macroscale, a mechanism intrinsic to NVFP4, does not improve average accuracy, suggesting that global rescaling alone does not address the limitations of E8M0 block scales for MXFP4 quantization. Together, these results provide practical guidance for combining numerical formats, rotation, PTQ, and scale selection for accurate low-precision LLM inference.

</details>


### [244] [Shared information governs multi-view planted matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32892)
*Ruiqi Wang,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究同一组 $n$ 个对象在 $K$ 个乱序视图（shuffled views）之间的行匹配问题：对每个固定 $K$，当单个对象 $K$ 次观测的共享信息 $J>(1+\epsilon)\log n$ 时可实现至多 $o(n)$ 误差的匹配，而 $J<(1-\epsilon)\log n$ 时不可行；整体精确匹配由视图的“最弱二分割”决定，弱相关下阈值为 $2\log n$；对独立噪声下的弱公共信号，阈值为 $4/K$ 与 $4/(K-1)$，回答了 Wee–Yang–Fan–Mao 的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图乱序数据的对齐（multi-view matching / seriation 类问题）是统计与机器学习中的基础问题，但此前缺乏对“共享信息量为多少时才能把全部 $K$ 个视图同时匹配”的精确刻画，尤其缺少关于各视图 pairwise 均不可匹配、但联合可匹配这一现象的理论解释，以及与信息论阈值相关的定量答案。

Method: 设定高斯特征模型：每个对象在每视图被观测一次，协方差已知。以共享信息 $J$（单个对象 $K$ 次观测的互信息）为核心量，给出匹配误差 $o(n)$ 与不可匹配的上下阈值；刻画整体匹配由视图的最弱二分割（weakest bipartition）控制，弱相关时以跨该二分割的互信息 $2\log n$ 为阈值；对弱公共信号情形把阈值换算为 $\sum_\ell\rho_\ell^2/\log n$ 的单位；并指出在非弱相关时二分割条件不充分，稀有子总体要求对每个至少三块的分割满足 Rényi 条件；提出两条件联合刻画高斯分布下精确恢复的猜想。算法上用 equal-correlation 下的 layered screening（$O(K n^2 d)$ 复杂度，$K=3,4$ 达到 $4/K$）与一般分布下的递归解码器（达到 $J$）。

Result: （1）对每个固定 $K$，$J>(1+\epsilon)\log n$ 时全部视图可匹配至 $o(n)$ 误差，$J<(1-\epsilon)\log n$ 时不可，且此情形可在任意两视图单独不可匹配时成立；（2）阈值以下两视图仍可匹配当且仅当存在包含二者的视图集合其共享信息超过 $\log n$，否则任何估计量（即使读取全部视图）在该对视图上重叠趋于零；（3）弱相关下整体匹配阈值为 $2\log n$；弱公共信号+独立噪声时两阈值分别为 $4/K$ 与 $4/(K-1)$（以 $\sum_\ell\rho_\ell^2/\log n$ 为单位），回答了 Wee–Yang–Fan–Mao 的问题；（4）非弱相关时二分割条件不充分，需对每个 $\ge3$ 块的分割附加 Rényi 条件；该二条件组合的猜想在 $K=3$ 等相关情形及若干其他情形下被证明，部分借助计算机辅助；（5）等相关时 layered screening 对 $K=3,4$ 达到 $4/K$，复杂度 $O(Kn^2d)$；一般分布下递归解码器达到 $J$；（6）近似精确阈值可推广到已知 product laws 与对象仅在视图随机子集中被观测的情形。

Conclusion: 本文为多视图乱序匹配给出了近乎完整的信息论阈值刻画：固定 $K$ 时 $J$ 的 $\log n$ 阈值决定全局可匹配性，阈值以下的可匹配性由“包含两视图的某集合共享信息是否超过 $\log n$”精细决定，整体匹配受最弱二分割支配；非弱相关情形下稀有子总体导致需要额外的 Rényi 条件，作者据此提出联合刻画高斯分布精确恢复的猜想并在若干情形（含三视图等相关及计算机辅助验证）下证明。所提 layered screening 与递归解码器在计算上可行并达到相应阈值，结果还可推广到其他已知 product laws 与随机子集观测场景。主要遗留问题是一般高斯分布下猜想的完整证明。

Abstract: We study when the rows of $K$ shuffled views of the same $n$ objects can be matched, where each object is observed once in every view through a Gaussian feature vector with known covariance. For every fixed $K$, all views can be matched up to $o(n)$ errors if the shared information $J$ of one object's $K$ observations exceeds $(1+ε)\log n$, and not if it is below $(1-ε)\log n$; this can hold although no two views can be matched on their own. Below the threshold, two views can still be matched if some set of views containing both has shared information above $\log n$; otherwise every estimator, even one that reads all views, has vanishing overlap between them. Matching every row is governed by the weakest bipartition of the views: with weak correlations the threshold is $2\log n$ for the mutual information across it. For a weak common signal observed through independent noise, the two thresholds are $4/K$ and $4/(K-1)$ in units of $\sum_\ellρ_\ell^2/\log n$, which answers a question of Wee, Yang, Fan and Mao. Beyond weak correlations the bipartition condition is not sufficient: rare subpopulations impose a Rényi condition for every partition into at least three blocks. We conjecture that the two conditions together characterize exact recovery for Gaussian laws, and prove this for three views with equal correlations and in several further cases, in part with computer assistance. With equal correlations, layered screening attains $4/K$ for $K=3,4$ in $O(Kn^2d)$ operations, and for general laws a recursive decoder attains $J$. The almost-exact thresholds extend to known product laws and to objects observed in random subsets of the views.

</details>


### [245] [Polynomial-Time Learning of Random Automata from One Label per Word](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32959)
*Yilin Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究随机二元确定性有限自动机的可学习性，在典型转移表上给出多项式时间、样本复杂度为多项式的预测算法，并反驳了Fish–Reyzin关于会合时间的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习者在只能观测到词的整体接受/拒绝单个比特（而非内部状态）的条件下学习DFA，且需在绝大多数随机转移表上可学习；同时澄清会合时间的理论问题。

Method: 对2n条转移独立均匀随机选取的二元DFA，给定固定β>0，排除概率o(n^{-β})的转移表后，用多项式时间算法进行预测；关键技术比较两个连续历史长度的标签矩，从而估计同一隐藏状态下两条延续的有符号输出乘积的期望；并对Fish–Reyzin会合时间猜想给出反例，部分证明步骤在Lean中形式化。

Result: 对固定起点与接受集，在新均匀长度m词上的误差≤ε，且以至少1-γ概率（对训练样本和学习器随机性）成立；样本量与运行时间关于n、m、1/ε、log(2/γ)为多项式，次数仅依赖β。会合方面：两个预先固定的不同状态，长度⌊cn⌋词中使其到达不同状态的比例依概率收敛到e^{-c}；典型表上将该比例降至1/n渐近需n log n个字母。

Conclusion: 随机DFA在典型转移表下可从单比特标签高效学习，且会合时间遵循e^{-c}衰减、需n log n步才能几乎完全混合，从而否定Fish–Reyzin猜想4；形式化验证增强了证明可信度。

Abstract: We study a binary deterministic finite automaton whose $2n$ transitions are chosen independently and uniformly from its $n$ states. The learner observes independent uniform words of length $m$, each labeled with a single bit indicating acceptance. For every fixed $β>0$ we give a polynomial-time algorithm that works outside a set of transition tables of probability $o(n^{-β})$. On each remaining table, for any start state and accepting set, the predictor has error at most $ε$ on fresh uniform words of length $m$ with probability at least $1-γ$ over the training sample and the learner's random choices. Its sample size and running time are polynomial in $n$, $m$, $1/ε$ and $\log(2/γ)$, with degree depending only on $β$. Although each word reveals only one output, comparing label moments at two consecutive history lengths lets us estimate the expected product of the signed outputs of two continuations from the same hidden state.
  We also refute Conjecture 4 of Fish and Reyzin on meeting times. For two distinct states fixed before the table is drawn and every fixed $c>0$, the fraction of words of length $\lfloor cn\rfloor$ that send them to different states converges in probability to $e^{-c}$. On a typical table, driving this fraction down to $1/n$ takes asymptotically $n\log n$ letters. Selected proof steps are formalized in Lean.

</details>


### [246] [Eliciting the Recall Capacity of Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32969)
*Johan Lindqvist,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究线性注意力在联想召回(AR)上相对softmax注意力的差距，发现该差距主要源于训练激励不足而非容量限制，通过句内重复与上下文敏感损失重加权等训练干预可显著缩小长距离AR差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型在语言建模的联想召回任务上相对softmax注意力存在不成比例的劣势，作者希望厘清这一差距究竟来自架构容量(状态大小)限制，还是来自训练过程中的归纳偏置与优化激励不足。

Method: 结合自然语言评测、合成召回任务与针对性训练干预：(1) 在合成设定下量化状态大小对可召回项目数的影响，并在多种线性注意力架构上验证类似的实证缩放规律；(2) 检验单层大规模模型能否学习跨长距离检索数千条关联；(3) 在训练中引入句内上下文重复(in-context sentence repetition)与上下文敏感损失重加权(context-sensitive loss reweighting)以强化召回监督，保持架构、token预算与优化步数不变。

Result: 合成实验显示可召回项数随状态大小呈现跨架构一致的实证缩放；即使单层大模型也能学习跨长距离检索数千条关联。标准语言模型训练无法可靠地让线性模型学会从上下文复制，而可比的softmax模型可以。两种训练干预均提升了召回密集型基准表现，并将长距离AR差距缩小至softmax基线水平，仅以轻微的一般语言建模性能损失为代价。

Conclusion: 线性注意力架构本身具备可观的召回容量，但受归纳偏置与语言模型训练需求的制约，在缺乏专门激励时难以发展出该能力；针对性的训练改动可激发其未被充分利用的检索能力，说明AR差距在很大程度上是可训练性/激励问题而非纯架构容量问题。

Abstract: Linear attention models exhibit a disproportionate deficit relative to softmax attention in associative recall (AR) in language modeling. We investigate this gap through natural language evaluations, synthetic recall tasks, and targeted training interventions. Using a synthetic setting, we quantify the role of state-size in how many items can be recalled from memory and find a similar empirical scaling across the tested linear-attention architectures. Testing this relationship reveals that even a single layer at scale can learn to retrieve thousands of associations across long distances. Yet, standard language model training does not reliably teach a linear model to copy from context, whereas a comparable softmax model learns to do so. Considering this, we strengthen recall supervision during training through in-context sentence repetition and context-sensitive loss reweighting. Both methods improve recall-intensive benchmark performance and narrow the long-distance AR gap to the softmax baseline without changing the architecture, token budget, or number of optimization steps, at a slight cost to general language modeling. These targeted changes to training can thus elicit substantial but underutilized retrieval capacity in linear-attention models. These findings suggest that, although linear-attention architectures have substantial capacity for recall, their inductive biases and the demands of language model training make it difficult to develop this capability without dedicated incentives.

</details>


### [247] [File Size as a Potential Dataset Bias in Audio-Visual Deepfake Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32979)
*Marcella Astrid,Niki Maria Foteinopoulou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文揭示两个常用音视频深伪数据集中存在文件大小偏置：仅凭文件大小导出的分数在1M-Deepfakes验证集上达70.13% AUC，结合视频时长后在该集超90%、在FakeAVCeleb超85% AUC，而DFDC未出现同样模式，说明该关联依赖数据集而非深伪视频固有属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深伪检测基准旨在评估模型区分真实与篡改音视频内容的能力，但数据构建流程可能无意间在类别标签与简单文件级属性之间引入伪关联，导致评测高估模型的真实内容判别能力，因此需要系统检验此类捷径特征。

Method: 在两个广泛使用的音视频深伪数据集（1M-Deepfakes Detection Challenge、FakeAVCeleb）及对照集DFDC上，仅使用文件级元信息构造简单分类器：单独使用由文件大小导出的分数，以及将文件大小与视频时长联合使用，并以AUC衡量其区分真伪的能力；同时定性讨论编码、压缩、源数据、篡改流程与后处理等可能成因。

Result: 1M-Deepfakes验证集上仅文件大小即达70.13% AUC；文件大小结合时长后，1M上超过90% AUC、FakeAVCeleb上超过85% AUC；DFDC中未观察到相同模式，表明该关联为数据集依赖性现象而非深伪视频的固有特性；分析未能确定具体成因。

Conclusion: 构建与评估深伪数据集和基准时必须检查简单文件级属性，以排除捷径学习带来的评测偏差；与文件大小相关的因素是否会在解码后的视频或音频中留下痕迹、进而影响基于内容的检测，是重要的后续研究方向。

Abstract: Deepfake detection benchmarks are commonly designed to evaluate whether models can distinguish authentic from manipulated audiovisual content. However, benchmark construction pipelines may introduce unintended associations between class labels and simple file-level properties. In this work, we identify a potential file-size bias in two widely used audio-visual deepfake datasets. In the 1M-Deepfakes Detection Challenge dataset, a score derived from file size alone achieves 70.13% AUC on the validation set. When file size is considered together with video duration, simple file-level methods exceed 90% AUC on 1M and 85% AUC on FakeAVCeleb. Although the same pattern is not observed in DFDC, the results suggest that the observed association is dataset-dependent rather than an inherent property of deepfake videos. We discuss several possible explanations, including differences in encoding, compression, source data, manipulation procedures, and post-processing, although the present analysis does not establish the underlying cause. We further discuss whether the factors associated with file size may also leave traces in decoded video or audio, and consequently whether they could influence content-based deepfake detection, as an important direction for future work. Our findings highlight the importance of examining simple file-level properties when constructing and evaluating deepfake datasets and benchmarks.

</details>


### [248] [AVQ-Attention-2: Adaptive Vector-Quantized Attention with Exact Local Blocks and Linear-Cost Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32989)
*Winfried van den Dool,Patrick Forré,Amir Habibian,Yuki M. Asano,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AVQ-Attention-2，将向量量化注意力与精确局部注意力融合，并对每个簇内键的均值（而非码字）进行注意力计算，实现O(NM)训练与推理成本、更优质量及可无微调插入预训练Transformer的近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力成本为O(N²)；向量量化注意力通过聚类键、对M个码字注意力将成本降至O(NM)，自适应形式AVQ-attention可在查询注意力集中处提升码本分辨率，但仍有质量与训练成本上的改进空间。

Method: 将AVQ-attention与精确局部注意力相结合，并把注意力目标由码字改为每个簇内键的均值；从而构造近似精度更高的层设计。

Result: 1) 可比成本下质量一致更优；2) 训练开销从O(N²)降为O(NM)；3) 近似精度高到可将该层直接替换进预训练Transformer而无需微调；在多种序列长度下不仅优于其他近似注意力，也与精确稠密注意力相当，且比FlashAttention更快而质量几无损失。

Conclusion: AVQ-Attention-2在成本、质量与可迁移性三方面同时改进，作为近似注意力层在速度上超越FlashAttention且质量近乎无损，具备替代精确稠密注意力的潜力。

Abstract: Attention costs $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ in the number of tokens. Vector-quantized attention reduces this to $\mathcal{O}(NM)$ by clustering the $N$ keys, attending to the $M$ codewords instead, and its adaptive form, AVQ-attention, concentrates codebook resolution where a query's attention mass actually falls. We present AVQ-Attention-2, a better design that merges it with exact local attention and attends to the mean of each cluster's keys rather than to a codeword. We show three improvements: consistently better quality at comparable cost; training that now also costs $\mathcal{O}(NM)$ rather than $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$; and an approximation accurate enough that the layer can be dropped into a pretrained transformer without fine-tuning. Across a variety of sequence lengths the result is competitive not merely with other approximate attention but with exact dense attention, running faster than FlashAttention at little to no loss in quality.

</details>


### [249] [On Potentiality and Actuality Of Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.32991)
*Fabian Gröger,Shuo Wen,Maria Brbić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"潜能原理":基础模型已蕴含任务所需能力,适配(如LoRA/全量微调)的增益主要来自与输入无关的恒定偏移分量,用逐层学习的常量向量(shift adapter)即可复现其行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一个核心问题:适配究竟带来了真正的新能力,还是只是把基础模型已具备的能力"激发"出来。作者希望厘清适配更新中"新增能力"与"已有潜能"的界限。

Method: 将不同适配方式(如LoRA、全量微调)引起的参数更新分解为两部分:一个在所有输入上共享的分量,以及一个随输入变化的残差分量;进而设计只学习逐层常量向量的shift adapter,并与LoRA/全量微调的行为进行对比验证。

Result: 共享分量贡献了适配增益的大部分,且可直接学习得到;逐层恒定的shift adapter能够在行为上复现LoRA与全量微调的效果,说明适配主要起到"方向推动"而非"能力注入"的作用。

Conclusion: 总结为"潜能原理":基础模型已包含任务专业知识所需容量,适配只需通过输入无关的恒定偏移将其"现实化";该原理对参数高效适配、引导(steering)、收敛性具有启示,并可解释"neural thickets"(神经荆棘丛)现象。

Abstract: Foundation models are built through large-scale pretraining, often followed by post-training, and are then adapted to specific tasks and behaviors. But, does the adaptation add truly new capabilities, or the base model already holds it? We decompose the update induced by different adaptations into a component shared across all inputs and a residual that varies with the input. We find that the shared component suffices for most of the adaptation gain and can be learned directly: a shift adapter that learns a constant vector per layer, identical for every input, reproduces the behavior of LoRA and full fine-tuning. We call this the potentiality principle of foundation models: the base model already contains the capacity needed for task-specific expertise. To adapt to a new task, the model just needs to be actualized, i.e., pushed in a certain direction via a constant, input-independent shift. We show the implications of this principle on parameter-efficient adaptation, steering, and convergence across models, and how it explains the neural thickets phenomenon.

</details>


### [250] [LEGOSpec: A Composable and Efficient Framework for Speculative Draft Design and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33004)
*Hanzhen Wang,Jiaming Xu,Guohao Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEGOSpec是一个可组合的投机解码草稿模型设计与训练框架，将草稿模型分解为Context、Backbone、Proposal、Objective四个组件并在统一训练基础设施下系统消融；基于发现提出DFlux，接受长度较最强基线最高提升13.7%（常规）与34.7%（长上下文），训练吞吐提升最高79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法通常以"完整配方"为整体，在不同训练配置下评估，导致各组件自身效应、组件间交互以及组合收益不清晰，难以指导有针对性的设计。

Method: 提出LEGOSpec可组合框架：将草稿模型解耦为Context、Backbone、Proposal、Objective四组件，共享统一训练基础设施；在匹配训练条件下系统变化组件并评估组合以隔离效应与交互；并集成元数据感知调度与异步执行以加速训练。基于结论构造DFlux（卷积增强的SWA–GQA混合骨干 + Residual Corrector + 分层监督）。

Result: 三条发现：(1) 不同工作负载偏好不同骨干，取决于上下文长度与显存预算，长上下文下块内依赖建模尤为关键；(2) Proposal设计需在接受率与延迟间权衡；(3) 训练目标应局部匹配token分布并在块级别优化接受长度。DFlux在Qwen与Gemma上接受长度最高提升13.7%（常规基准）、34.7%（长上下文）；训练吞吐最高提升79%，离线监督准备较SpecForge加速最高3.11倍。

Conclusion: 组件化、条件匹配的实证分析能揭示投机解码草稿设计的独立与交互效应，并有效指导工作负载感知的配置；据此得到的通用草稿DFlux在多种模型与场景下显著优于强基线，同时具备更高的训练效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) generation using a lightweight draft. Existing methods evaluate complete recipes under different training configurations, leaving component effects, interactions, and composition benefits unclear. Therefore, we present LEGOSpec, a composable framework for speculative draft design and training. LEGOSpec decomposes draft models into Context, Backbone, Proposal, and Objective components that share a unified training infrastructure. Using LEGOSpec, we systematically vary components and evaluate selected combinations under matched training conditions to isolate their effects and interactions. These experiments reveal three findings: (1) Different workloads favor different backbones, depending on context length and memory budget, with within-block dependency modeling particularly important for long contexts. (2) Proposal design must balance acceptance and latency. (3) The training objective should match token distributions locally and optimize acceptance length across the block. These findings guide workload-aware configurations and DFlux, a balanced general-purpose draft combining a convolution-equipped SWA--GQA hybrid backbone, Residual Corrector, and hierarchical supervision. Across Qwen and Gemma models, DFlux improves acceptance length over the strongest baseline by up to 13.7% on regular benchmarks and 34.7% on long-context workloads. To support efficient training, LEGOSpec integrates metadata-aware scheduling and asynchronous execution, increasing training throughput by up to 79% and accelerating offline supervision preparation by up to $3.11\times$ over SpecForge.

</details>


### [251] [PAC-style Bounds on the Conditional Validity of Conformal Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33023)
*Nicolo Colombo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在无需数据分布假设的条件下，为任意保形预测器的输入条件覆盖率推导了PAC型概率下界（基于Markov与Cantelli不等式），并提出以条件覆盖率方差作为可扩展的条件有效性经验度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测集仅保证在属性空间上边际有效，而局部（输入条件）覆盖率可能偏离用户设定的目标1-α；在有限校准数据下，对一般数据生成分布构造条件有效预测集是不可能的。因此需要在不假设分布的前提下，对条件覆盖率给出可验证的概率性刻画。

Method: 基于Markov不等式和Cantelli不等式推导任意保形预测器输入条件覆盖率的PAC型概率下界，且同时适配校准-边际与校准-条件两种设置；进一步给出一个依赖条件覆盖率方差的、更紧的模型相关Cantelli界，并借助完全非渐近的Hoeffding论证从数据中估计该量。

Result: 得到了无需分布假设的条件覆盖率PAC下界族；其中模型相关的Cantelli界更紧，且其所需的条件覆盖率方差可由数据非渐近估计。

Conclusion: 条件覆盖率方差可作为理论上合理且可扩展的条件有效性经验度量，为实践中评估与比较保形预测器的局部可靠性提供了可行工具。

Abstract: Conformal prediction sets are guaranteed to be marginally valid over the attribute space, but local coverage can diverge from the user-defined target $1-α$. Constructing conditionally valid prediction sets with finite calibration data is impossible for a general data-generating distribution. We derive PAC-type probabilistic lower bounds on the input-conditional coverage of any conformal predictor, without assumptions on the data distribution. They are based on the Markov and Cantelli inequalities and adapt to both calibration-marginal and calibration-conditional setups. A tighter, model-dependent Cantelli bound depends on the variance of the conditional coverage and is estimable from data using a fully non-asymptotic Hoeffding argument. The results suggest conditional-coverage variance as a scalable and theoretically motivated empirical measure of conditional validity.

</details>


### [252] [Best Arm Identification with Unknown Post-Action Context Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33024)
*Amir Mohammad Abouei,Mohammad Shahverdikondori,Emad Emamjomeh,Negar Kiyavash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究动作后上下文未知情形下的固定置信度最佳臂识别（BAI），给出实例相关下界并提出渐近最优的Track-and-Stop算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用动作后上下文可显著降低识别最佳臂的样本复杂度，而已有工作（Shahverdikondori et al., 2026a）仅处理上下文条件分布已知的情形，未知分布更贴近实际但更具挑战性。

Method: 推导实例相关的样本复杂度下界；提出借助优化 oracle 的 Track-and-Stop 算法，其停止规则采用同时控制上下文分布不确定性与奖励均值不确定性的新集中不等式；针对非凸优化难题，给出带实例相关近似保证的凸松弛。

Result: 算法在渐近意义上达到下界；新集中不等式消除了分别使用两个集中界所带来的一倍因子损失；凸松弛在困难高斯实例上渐近紧；实验显示即使上下文分布未知，利用动作后上下文仍比忽略它的基线降低样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 在上下文分布未知的设定下，利用动作后上下文可获得渐近最优的BAI性能，所提集中不等式与凸松弛为可计算实现提供了理论与经验支持。

Abstract: We study best arm identification (BAI) with post-action context in the fixed-confidence setting. After choosing an action, the learner observes a post-action context together with a reward whose distribution depends only on this context. Incorporating such intermediate information can substantially reduce the sample complexity of identifying the best arm. While Shahverdikondori et al. (2026a) analyzed the case where the distribution of contexts given actions is known, we address the more challenging and practically relevant case where this distribution is unknown. We derive an instance-dependent sample complexity lower bound and propose a Track-and-Stop algorithm that matches it asymptotically with access to an optimization oracle. Our stopping rule uses a new concentration inequality that jointly controls uncertainty in the context distributions and reward means, removing the factor of two incurred by separate concentration bounds. Implementing the algorithm generally requires solving nonconvex optimization problems. We propose a convex relaxation with instance-dependent approximation guarantees, including asymptotic tightness on hard Gaussian instances. Experiments demonstrate that exploiting post-action context reduces the sample complexity relative to baselines that ignore it, even when the context distributions are unknown.

</details>


### [253] [Score-Based Diffusion for Hypergraph Signals in the t-Spectral Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33026)
*Zhiyuan Dong,Carlos Mundo-Levano,Daniel L. Lau,Gonzalo R. Arce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个基于 t-HGSP 谱域的超图信号分数生成模型，通过逐模式频移修复扩散算子不适定性，实现稳定采样与缺失节点恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图信号缺乏分数匹配生成模型；直接在 t-HGSP 谱域上使用张量热半群会遇到不适定性：回文对称化后拉普拉斯张量的谱切片不再半正定，导致前向扩散过程定义失效。

Method: 在 t-HGSP 谱域构建分数生成模型。采用逐模式频率平移（per-mode frequency shift）修复不适定热半群，得到按 (本征模式, 切片) 解耦的前向 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 扩散，具有闭式边缘分布、精确分数目标和各向同性平稳律。用切片条件化的轻量去噪器在实谱网格上训练；采样时将数据活跃模式上的分数与其余模式的闭式分数结合；并以闭式余弦形式观测算子将缺失节点恢复转化为线性高斯引导的后验采样。

Result: 在最多 500 节点的超图上，协方差误差相对 clique-expansion 基线由 2× 提升至 27×；对数据图谱统计的匹配优于图基模型本身；在偏离 t-谱轴旋转的数据上达到有限样本估计下限。后验采样将缺失节点 NMSE 降至 0.28–0.64（经典基线 0.87–1.1），并较该框架自身去噪基线最多降低 3.6×。

Conclusion: 逐模式频移是使 t-HGSP 谱域扩散良定义的关键；闭式分数与学习分数在活跃/非活跃模式上的混合对规模化稳定采样不可或缺，闭式观测算子使恢复问题能以稳定后验采样求解。

Abstract: We introduce the first score-based generative model for signals on a fixed hypergraph, formulated in the spectral domain of t-product hypergraph signal processing (t-HGSP). The naive tensor heat semigroup is ill-posed, since after palindromic symmetrization the spectral slices of the Laplacian tensor are no longer positive semidefinite. A per-mode frequency shift repairs it, yielding a forward Ornstein-Uhlenbeck diffusion that decouples per (eigenmode, slice) pair with closed-form marginals, exact score targets, and an exactly isotropic stationary law. A lightweight slice-conditioned denoiser is trained on the resulting real spectral grid. Sampling combines its score on data-active modes with the closed-form score elsewhere, which proves essential for stability at scale. A closed cosine-form observation operator turns missing-node recovery into linear-Gaussian guidance with stabilized posterior sampling. On hypergraphs of up to 500 nodes, our model beats a clique-expansion baseline by margins growing from $2\times$ to $27\times$ in covariance error, matches the graph-spectral statistics of the data better than the graph-basis model itself, and reaches the finite-sample estimation floor on data rotated off the t-spectral axes. Posterior sampling reduces missing-node NMSE to 0.28-0.64 (classical baselines 0.87-1.1) at conservative coverage, and cuts the framework's own denoising baseline by up to $3.6\times$.

</details>


### [254] [Can a Frontier LLM Repair a Quantized Network by Tool-Free Weight-Space Programming?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33031)
*Steffen Urban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将20k参数CIFAR-10 CNN的全部权重以文本形式输入Claude系列LLM，在无工具、无数据、无法运行推理的条件下要求其生成4-bit网络；分析表明前沿LLM可通过推理整数权重、平衡3×3卷积核内舍入误差，将4-bit精度从RTN的60.4%恢复至77.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM是否具备"权重空间编程"能力：即在不调用任何工具、不访问校准数据、无法执行前向推理的情况下，仅凭阅读权重文本直接对神经网络做量化优化，从而检验LLM对数值与优化问题的内隐推理能力，并与标准RTN及需数据的AdaRound对比。

Method: 将20k参数CIFAR-10 CNN的完整权重（float与4-bit RTN版本）作为文本输入四个Claude模型。设计四种评估协议：前两种同时提供float网络与4-bit RTN网络并告知精度显著下降；后两种仅提供float网络并要求量化，用以区分LLM是自主施加优化还是退化为普通RTN。每层仅采一个答案，分析LLM输出的舍入方案及其误差平衡策略。

Result: RTN 4-bit量化使测试精度由81.4%降至60.4%。在协议一下，Opus 5.5通过在每个3×3卷积核内平衡舍入误差，将精度恢复至77.95%，距float网络差3.5 pp，距利用校准数据学习的AdaRound差2.8 pp。仅给float网络时，LLM虽能指出可抵消误差的修正方向却不予执行，退化为RTN，最多附带轻微的scale裁剪，仍可将Fable 5.2的量化网络提升至70.0%。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM具备一定程度的免数据、免工具的权重空间量化优化能力，其有效性主要来自核内舍入误差的相互抵消；但模型能否发挥该能力高度依赖提示中对问题（精度下降）的显式揭示，缺少该线索时会退化为RTN。这揭示了LLM作为"无数据PTQ优化器"的潜力与局限。

Abstract: We give four large language models (LLMs) of the Claude family the complete weights of a 20k-parameter CIFAR-10 CNN as text and ask them to produce a 4-bit network without tools, data or the ability to run inference. Standard round-to-nearest (RTN) quantization to 4 bit lowers the test accuracy from 81.4% to 60.4%. We design four evaluation protocols that differ in the input and task description given to the LLMs and analyze their responses. In the first two protocols the LLMs are given the float and the 4-bit RTN network and are told that the accuracy has dropped significantly. In the last two they receive only the float network and the task to quantize it, which tests whether they apply an optimization on their own or fall back to plain RTN. Our analysis shows that a frontier LLM can recover most of the lost accuracy. Given one answer per layer, Opus 5.5 raises the accuracy of the 4-bit network to 77.95% by balancing the rounding errors within each 3x3 kernel, reasoning over the integer weights without tools. This is 3.5 pp below the float network and 2.8 pp below AdaRound, which learns the rounding from calibration data. Given only the float network, the LLMs name error-cancelling corrections but do not apply them and fall back to plain RTN, at most with mildly clipped scales, which still lifts Fable 5.2's quantized network to 70.0%. We analyze in detail what the LLMs did and why it works. Code and prompts can be found at https://github.com/urbste/weight-space-programming-ptq

</details>


### [255] [When Is Product Decoding of ECOC MAP-Consistent? Pair Structure and the n-vs-Rest Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33044)
*Gendo Kumoi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文刻画了ECOC乘积解码器的MAP一致性条件：当且仅当对每个类对，两类不同的列与交换两类（或其补）所得到的列相匹配，等价于码本为n-vs-Rest码的并；由此排除Hadamard等高效等距码，并在6个真实数据上给出和/积解码器的校准相关比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECOC通过求和（概率相加）或乘积（对数似然相加）解码器组合二分类器。无噪声情形下已知和译码器与MAP一致的充要条件是等距码（James & Hastie, 1998），但乘积解码器的一致性条件尚未回答；而等距码家族中的Hadamard码因高效而极具吸引力，需要判断其是否可用。

Method: 将类对比较表述为：仅通过两类取值不同的列的投票，和译码器用样本均值决策，乘积译码器用影响函数为artanh的M-估计量决策；每个投票等于MAP间隔加上仅由其他类决定的偏差。据此证明乘积解码器MAP一致性的充要条件（类对列匹配/交换结构），并证明其等价于码本为n-vs-Rest码之并。对Hadamard码分析列对缺失与Sylvester型的反转间隔下界；全部定理在Lean 4中机器验证；并在6个真实数据（估计概率）上比较两种解码器与校准误差的关系。

Result: 主定理：乘积解码器MAP一致 ⇔ 对每个类对，两类差异列与交换两类（或其补）所得的列匹配 ⇔ 码本为n-vs-Rest码的并。因此高效等距码被排除：Hadamard码（M≥8，满足Plotkin界、M−1列）不存在此类列对；Sylvester型的反转间隔下界为常数0.0182，与M无关。实证：各列校准良好时乘积解码器略优；各列校准误差不一致时和译码器更优；两种情形下解码器仅在接近平局时分歧。

Conclusion: 乘积解码器的MAP一致性完全由码本的组合对称结构决定，n-vs-Rest并是其唯一实现，故不能兼得Hadamard类等距码的效率；解码器的实际差异局限在近似平局区域，选择应依据各列的校准质量而非仅码本结构。所有结论均经Lean 4形式化验证。

Abstract: Error-Correcting Output Codes (ECOC) combine binary classifiers by the sum decoder, which totals probabilities, or the product decoder, which totals log-likelihoods. In the noiseless setting, where the binary classifiers output the true posteriors, we ask on which code matrices each decoder always agrees with the Bayes-optimal (MAP) class. For the sum decoder the answer is known: the code must be equidistant (James and Hastie, 1998). We answer the question for the product decoder. Two classes are compared through the votes of the columns on which they differ: the sum decoder decides by their sample mean, the product decoder by an M-estimator with influence function artanh, and each vote is the MAP margin plus a bias determined by the other classes alone. Our main theorem states that the product decoder is MAP-consistent if and only if, for every class pair, the columns on which the two classes differ are matched with the columns obtained by swapping the two classes (or their complements), and equivalently if and only if the code is a union of n-vs-Rest codes. Efficient equidistant codes are therefore excluded: Hadamard codes ($M\ge 8$), which meet the Plotkin bound with $M-1$ columns, have no such pairs, and for the Sylvester type the reversal margin can be at least a constant 0.0182 independent of $M$. All theorems are machine-checked in Lean 4. On six real datasets with estimated probabilities, the product decoder is slightly more accurate when each column is calibrated, and the sum decoder is better when the calibration error differs across columns; in either case the decoders disagree only near ties.

</details>


### [256] [Latent Merger: Latent-Space Composition of Hypernetwork-Generated LoRAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33048)
*Yanlai Yang,Minki Kang,Divyam Madaan,Sung Ju Hwang,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何组合超网络生成的 LoRA。作者发现这类 LoRA 的几何性质与直接微调的 LoRA 显著不同（谱更集中、跨文档对齐更强），据此提出"潜空间 LoRA 合并"：先合并超网络中间潜表示，再经冻结的 LoRA 生成头解码，效果稳定优于权重空间与因子空间基线，且保持单一固定秩适配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超网络可单次前向将文档转为低秩参数更新（LoRA），实现向冻结 LLM 快速注入新知识；但在流式场景下为每篇文档单独存储 LoRA 适配器不可持续，需要有效的多 LoRA 组合机制。

Method: 以 Doc-to-LoRA 与 SHINE 两个代表性系统为对象，先分析超网络生成 LoRA 与直接微调 LoRA 的几何差异（谱集中度、跨文档对齐/正交性）；据此提出潜空间合并：在超网络的中间潜表示上做组合，再通过冻结的 LoRA 生成头解码出单个固定秩适配器；与权重空间、因子空间合并基线对比。

Result: 超网络生成的 LoRA 相比 NTP/QA 微调适配器具有更强的谱集中与跨文档对齐；后者则分散且近正交。潜空间合并方法在固定输出秩预算下一致优于权重空间与因子空间基线。

Conclusion: 潜空间组合是在固定秩预算下合并超网络生成 LoRA 的有前景机制。

Abstract: Recent work proposes hypernetworks that convert a document into a low-rank parameter update (LoRA) in a single forward pass, enabling fast injection of new knowledge into a frozen LLM. However, storing separate LoRA adapters for each document can be unsustainable in streaming settings. In this paper, we study the problem of composing hypernetwork-generated LoRAs using two representative systems, Doc-to-LoRA and SHINE. We show that hypernetwork-generated LoRAs have a geometry sharply different from directly fine-tuned LoRAs. They exhibit greater spectral concentration and stronger cross-document alignment than next-token prediction and QA fine-tuned adapters, which are diffuse and nearly orthogonal across documents. Guided by this observation, we propose latent-space LoRA merging. We compose the intermediate hypernetwork latents and then decode the merged latent through the frozen LoRA-generation head. We show that latent-space merging techniques consistently improve over weight-space and factor-space baselines while preserving a single fixed-rank adapter. These results support latent-space composition as a promising mechanism for combining hypernetwork-generated LoRAs under a fixed output-rank budget.

</details>


### [257] [Local Indices Are Not Enough: Synchrony and Global Information in Bandit Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33056)
*Aakash Gore,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究局部索引 bandit 算法的信息需求，证明异步局部索引算法无法达到最优的 O(log T) 遗憾，其可达遗憾位于次多项式、超多对数区间；并提出可达该区间的异步局部索引策略，同时证明同步局部随机化（每臂独立随机预算）即可保持 O(log T) 最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL-UCB 与 Thompson Sampling 等标准索引算法可达到最优 O(log T) 遗憾，但前者依赖全局时间步构造索引，后者需每步对每臂信念分布采样。全局信息与同步索引更新的必要性此前未被系统检验，作者希望厘清"异步 vs 同步""局部 vs 全局"信息对最优遗憾的根本影响。

Method: 定义并研究异步局部索引算法：仅在拉动某臂时更新该臂索引，且仅使用该臂自身的历史奖励与局部随机性预算。通过对异步更新所导出的函数方程进行分析，给出这类算法的遗憾下界；并据此构造一个异步局部索引策略以匹配该界。对同步局部算法（每时刻对所有臂采样索引，类似 Thompson Sampling），分析每臂局部随机预算是否足以维持最优性。

Result: 证明不存在任何异步局部索引算法能在所有 bandit 实例上达到 O(log T) 遗憾；分析相应函数方程后，得出其可达遗憾处于次多项式、超多对数（sub-polynomial, super-polylogarithmic）区间。所提出的异步局部索引策略的遗憾上界同样落在该区间。对同步局部算法，证明每臂仅需局部确定的随机性预算即可保持 O(log T) 遗憾。

Conclusion: 全局信息与同步性是索引算法达到 O(log T) 最优遗憾的必要条件（在局部随机性框架下），同步性即便配合有限局部随机化也足以维持 Thompson Sampling 的最优性；而纯异步局部索引存在不可逾越的遗憾下界。

Abstract: Standard index-based stochastic bandit algorithms such as KL-UCB and Thompson Sampling achieve optimal $O(\log T)$ regret over a horizon $T$. The former uses the global timestep to construct each arm's index, while the latter samples each arm's belief distribution at every timestep. The necessity of global information and synchronous index updates for achieving optimal regret has not been examined yet. In this paper, we investigate the fundamental limits of asynchronous local index algorithms---those that recalculate an arm's index only upon pulling it, using solely that arm's own reward history and a local randomness budget. We prove that no such algorithm can achieve $O(\log T)$ regret across all bandit instances. We then analyse the functional equations arising from asynchronous updates, and show that the achievable regret lies in a sub-polynomial, super-polylogarithmic regime. We propose an asynchronous local index policy whose regret upper bound also lies in this regime. Turning to synchronous local algorithms, which sample every arm's index at all timesteps as in Thompson Sampling, we show that a locally determined randomness budget per arm suffices to retain $O(\log T)$ regret, demonstrating that synchrony, albeit with some randomness, is essential to the optimality of Thompson Sampling.

</details>


### [258] [Understanding Clipping In Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33062)
*Saket Gollapudi,Elisa Bertino,Sewoong Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了零阶优化（ZO）中逐样本裁剪（per-sample clipping）提升 LLM 微调性能的现象，揭示学习率 η 与裁剪阈值 c 成反比（ηc=常数）、裁剪使 ZO 收敛到不同且测试精度更高的解，且该现象仅出现在逐样本裁剪的 ZO 中，其机理是隐式降低难拟合样本权重从而提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化因损失景观低维性而成为 SGD 的资源高效替代方案，仅依赖函数值两点估计梯度、规避反向传播。此前有偶然发现：在差分隐私版 ZO（DPZero）中使用极大隐私参数 ε（即几乎无隐私）时，逐样本裁剪仍能提升模型性能，但该现象缺乏系统解释。

Method: 对逐样本裁剪的零阶优化进行系统性实证分析：探究学习率 η 与裁剪阈值 c 的关系；比较裁剪 ZO 与其他同样提供稳健梯度估计的 ZO 变体；并注入人工标签噪声进行机理分析。

Result: (i) 最优学习率 η 与裁剪阈值 c 成反比，满足 ηc=常数；(ii) 裁剪驱动 ZO 收敛到不同的解，且具有更高测试精度；(iii) 该现象仅在逐样本裁剪 ZO 中出现，其他稳健梯度估计的 ZO 变体未见此效应；标签噪声实验表明裁剪带来更鲁棒的预测，源于对难拟合训练样本的隐式降权。

Conclusion: 逐样本裁剪并非仅为隐私保护手段，其隐式重加权机制本身可提升零阶优化微调的泛化与鲁棒性；ηc=常数的缩放规律为该方法的超参设置提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Zeroth-order optimization has emerged as a resource-efficient alternative to Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) when fine-tuning LLMs, thanks to the inherent low dimensionality of the loss landscape. It relies on a two-point estimate of the gradient that only accesses the function value and not the gradient, bypassing the resource-heavy backpropagation. Recently, it was accidentally discovered that per-sample clipping can improve the performance of the trained model, while running a differentially private version of zeroth-order optimization \cite{zhang2024dpzero} with a very large privacy parameter $ε$ (corresponding to very little privacy). In this paper, we systematically investigate this phenomenon and demonstrate that ($i$) the optimal choice of learning rate $η$ is inversely proportional to the clipping threshold $c$, satisfying $ηc =$ constant; ($ii$) clipping drives the zeroth-order optimization to a different solution with higher test accuracy; and ($iii$) this phenomenon is only observed with per-sample clipped zeroth-order optimization and not other zeroth-order alternatives that also provide robust estimates of the gradient. Further analyses with artificially injected label noise reveals that clipping leads to more robust predictions, which stems from the implicit down weighting of hard-to-fit training examples. Our code is available at https://github.com/SewoongLab/ClipZero

</details>


### [259] [U-Statistics-Powered LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33064)
*Shijin Gong,Kai Ye,Victor Chernozhukov,Xinyu Zhang,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将GRPO组大小选择重构为方差比估计问题，提出考虑分母估计不确定性的悲观比值估计器，实现自适应组大小选择，降低策略梯度均方误差并改善优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO及其变体被广泛用于训练大推理模型与智能体，但其中关键超参数"组大小"长期依靠试错法选取，缺乏统计理论指导。

Method: 利用GRPO策略梯度的U统计量结构，把组大小选择重述为方差比估计问题；针对分母较小时朴素plug-in估计器不稳定的问题，提出计入分母估计不确定性的悲观比值估计器（基于jackknife的悲观规则），并刻画其在三种样本量情形下的统计性质。

Result: 在Reasoning Gym合成任务及覆盖数学推理与代码生成的真实基准上，自适应组大小选择降低了策略梯度的均方误差，相对固定组大小基线改善了策略优化效果，且额外计算开销有限。

Conclusion: 组大小不应凭经验试错，而可基于方差比估计给出有统计依据的自适应选择方案，兼具理论刻画与实证收益。

Abstract: Since its introduction by DeepSeek, group relative policy optimization (GRPO) has been widely adopted for training large reasoning models and agents, spurring a proliferation of its variants. Yet the group size, a crucial hyperparameter in these GRPO-type algorithms, remains largely chosen by trial and error. Exploiting the U-statistic structure of GRPO's policy gradient, we recast the selection of the group size as a classical statistical problem of variance ratio estimation. This problem is particularly challenging when the denominator of the ratio is small, rendering naive plug-in estimators unstable. To address this challenge, we propose a pessimistic ratio estimator that accounts for the denominator's estimation uncertainty. We characterise the statistical properties of this estimator across three sample-size regimes. Empirically, using %a jackknife-based pessimistic rule on synthetic tasks from Reasoning Gym and real-world benchmarks covering both mathematical reasoning and code generation, we show that our adaptive group size selection reduces the mean squared error of the resulting policy gradient and improves policy optimisation over fixed-group-size baselines, with modest additional computational cost.

</details>


### [260] [Shifter Quantization: Compact Representations for Native Integer Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33080)
*Yufeng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Shifter Quantization (SQ) 及变体 Shifter Integer (SHI)，通过共享比特的拼接与移位重构原生位宽整数操作数，使分组量化权重可用标准整数 MMA/INT4 内核高效推理，在质量与速度上均优于标准分组量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分组量化（groupwise quantization）能在低位宽下提升 LLM 精度，但分组会破坏标准整数矩阵乘加（MMA）对完整点积的覆盖，导致 GPU 推理效率下降，即精度与硬件效率之间存在矛盾。

Method: 提出 SQ：对共享比特进行追加与移位，重构出原生位宽的整数操作数，从而让标准整数 MMA 覆盖完整点积；并进一步提出变体 SHI，使其可直接复用 INT4 内核以提升效率。

Result: 以投影误差和 KL 散度衡量质量、以解码与预填充阶段测速衡量效率，SQ/SHI 均优于标准分组量化。2-bit：SQ2/SHI2 预填充吞吐（4096 token）达 llama.cpp Q2_K 的 2.71–3.77 倍，WikiText-2 KL 降低 6.6–7.8%；4-bit：SQ4 预填充吞吐为 NInfer 分组整数基线的 1.43–2.47 倍，KL 低 24–40%；8-bit：SQ8 单批预填充吞吐比 vLLM 在线 FP8 高 27.4%，KL 低 72.7%。

Conclusion: SQ/SHI 在 2/4/8-bit 各精度档位同时改善量化质量与推理速度，缓解了分组量化中精度与硬件效率的取舍，是一种可直接落地于现有整数内核的量化格式。

Abstract: Groupwise quantization is used in LLM quantization to improve accuracy at low bit widths, but grouping weights keeps standard integer MMA from spanning the full dot product for efficient GPU inference. We develop a new quantization method, Shifter Quantization (SQ), that reconstructs native-width integer operands by appending and shifting shared bits. To further extend the efficiency, we develop a special variant of SQ, Shifter Integer (SHI), to use INT4 kernels. We show that SQ and SHI formats perform better than standard groupwise quantization in quality, as measured by projection error and KL divergence, and in inference speed, as measured during decoding and prefill. For low precision like 2-bit quantization, SQ2 and SHI2 achieve 2.71-3.77x the 4,096-token prefill throughput of llama.cpp on a Q2_K checkpoint and reduce WikiText-2 KL by 6.6-7.8%. For mid precision like 4-bit quantization, SQ4 achieves 1.43-2.47x the prefill throughput of NInfer's groupwise-integer baselines across the tested models and prompt lengths, with 24-40% lower KL than the NInfer baselines. For high precision like 8-bit quantization, SQ8 achieves 27.4% higher batch-one prefill throughput and 72.7% lower KL than vLLM's online FP8.

</details>


### [261] [GREMLIN: Generating Random Environments for Meta-Learning with Interacting Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33101)
*Octave Gaspard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验随机参数化神经网络环境能否为强化学习规则发现提供有用经验：用16个神经环境替代人工设计游戏来训练DiscoRL的规则发现框架，发现生成的交互经验能够改进学习规则，但能否得到有竞争力的规则仍不确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiscoRL等规则发现框架依赖人工设计的训练游戏，作者希望探究能否用随机参数化的神经环境自动生成训练经验，从而减少对人工游戏设计的依赖，并检验这类合成经验对学习规则发现的实用价值。

Method: 在DiscoRL规则发现框架中，用16个选定的神经环境替换人工设计游戏；每个游戏由有界向量动力学、基于随机策略的奖励和独立的结果奖励构成。经过2.248亿次智能体-环境交互步后冻结教师（teacher），再用全新智能体重新训练，比较DQN与GREMLIN的表现。

Result: 在多数游戏（包括生成游戏与人工设计游戏）上DQN的得分高于GREMLIN；但GREMLIN在全部26个游戏上均优于未训练的教师，表明生成的交互经验确实能改进学习规则。

Conclusion: 随机神经环境产生的经验可以提升学习规则，验证了该途径的可行性；但能否通过更长的元训练获得具有竞争力的规则，目前尚不明确，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Can randomly parameterized neural environments supply useful experience for learning a reinforcement learning algorithm? We investigate this question using DiscoRL's rule-discovery framework, replacing human-designed training games with sixteen selected neural environments. Each game combines bounded vector dynamics with a random strategy-based reward and an independent outcome reward. After 224.8 million agent-environment steps, we froze the teacher and trained fresh agents. DQN outscored GREMLIN on most games, both generated and human-designed. GREMLIN nevertheless outperformed the untrained teacher on all 26 games, showing that generated experience can improve a learning rule. It remains unclear whether this approach can yield a competitive rule with longer meta-training.

</details>


### [262] [Reinforcement Learning Can Be Recovered From Its Own Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33102)
*Jiayi Sheng,Jingwen Gu,Guanning Zeng,Zhaoyi Zhou,Stefano Viel,Qianqian Wang,Fahim Tajwar,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究RL后训练产生的rollout本身保留了多少知识，发现对归档rollout按生成顺序做最大似然（不论对错）即可复现在线RL的学习曲线与最终性能，表明RL的大量算力实际用于发现更好的采样分布而非从样本中学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL后训练中rollout用完即弃，知识仅存于权重。作者想探究rollout本身保留了多少知识，以及能否用这些归档数据替代在线RL计算。

Method: 直接在归档rollout上重放原RL算法会导致灾难性崩溃，作者给出简单解释并提出补救措施：对归档rollout做最大似然训练。从同一基座模型出发，按rollout生成顺序回放，不区分正确与错误响应，每个rollout批次做一次最大似然更新。

Result: 按顺序最大似然回放足以恢复在线RL的性能，单次更新/批次的似然训练紧密跟踪在线RL的完整学习曲线；仅对最后若干RL步的rollout做最大似然也常能恢复最终模型性能。

Conclusion: RL投入的算力很大程度用于发现改进的采样分布，而非从样本中学习该分布；rollout本身携带了在线RL习得的大部分知识，可用于高效离线复现。

Abstract: Broadly speaking, reinforcement learning (RL) post-training converts compute into model capabilities by training on model-generated data. Typically, the rollouts are discarded after each update, and the knowledge acquired during RL resides in the model weights. In this work, we investigate how much of this knowledge is retained in the rollouts themselves. A natural first attempt is to replay the original RL algorithm on archived rather than freshly generated rollouts, but this leads to catastrophic collapse. We provide a simple explanation for this phenomenon, which in turn suggests a remedy: maximum likelihood on the archived rollouts. Surprisingly, starting from the same base model and replaying the rollouts in the order in which they were generated, without any distinction between correct and incorrect responses, is sufficient to recover the performance of online RL. In fact, a single maximum likelihood update per rollout batch closely tracks the entire learning curve of online RL. Furthermore, maximum likelihood on the rollouts of the last few RL steps often suffices to recover the final model's performance. This suggests that much of the compute invested in RL goes into discovering an improved sampling distribution rather than learning it from samples.

</details>


### [263] [Draft Calibration for Speculative Decoding: Accelerating LLM Serving by Matching Draft and Target Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33111)
*Yuxing Liu,Wei Zuo,Walid Krichene,Tong Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究推测解码中的草稿分布校准问题：开源草稿模型对目标模型普遍“过度自信不足”（underconfident），通过事后温度缩放校准可提升接受长度约10%且几乎无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码的加速比取决于草稿token在目标模型验证时的接受概率，而该概率直接关联目标分布p与草稿分布q之间的L1距离‖p−q‖₁。作者观察到社区开源草稿模型虽能正确学到top候选token，但相对目标模型普遍欠自信，导致持续存在的分布失配，限制了加速效果。

Method: 提出对草稿分布进行事后校准（draft calibration），以缓解分布差距。尝试多种事后校准方法：标准温度缩放（temperature scaling）与逐token校准（token-wise calibration），并分析更一般化的校准方法的潜力。

Result: 在不同任务上，对草稿分布施加标准温度缩放可将接受长度提升最多约10%，且几乎不带来额外计算开销；吞吐量分析进一步验证了服务效率的提升。对更通用校准方法的分析揭示了仍有改进空间。

Conclusion: 草稿模型的欠自信是限制推测解码接受率的重要失配来源，简单的事后温度缩放是一种低成本、有效的校准手段；更一般的校准方法具备进一步提升推测解码加速比的潜力。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by employing a lightweight draft model to propose draft tokens and allowing the target model to verify the new tokens in parallel to improve inference-time compute utilization. The speedup relies on the acceptance probability of draft tokens in target verification, which is directly related to the gap $\|p-q\|_1$ between the target distribution $p$ and the draft distribution $q$. In this paper, we study draft calibration of speculative decoding to improve the speedup of speculative decoding, where we employ post-hoc methods to calibrate the draft distribution to mitigate the distribution gap. We observe that though current community open-source draft models may learn the top candidate token correctly, they are generally underconfident compared to the target model, suggesting a consistent distribution mismatch. To mitigate the effect of this mismatch on the acceptance rate of the draft models, we try various post-hoc calibration methods, including standard temperature scaling and token-wise calibration. Our results suggest that a standard temperature scaling applied to the draft distribution improves the acceptance length by up to approximately 10\% with almost no additional cost across different tasks. Throughput analysis further verifies that this improvement in serving efficiency. Analysis of more general calibration methods reveals further potential room for improvement.

</details>


### [264] [A Bias in Disguise: Tiny Gradient Edits Stop a Small Language Model's Thinking Loops and Complement a Frontier Model's Weight Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33133)
*Hussamuddin Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为"无梯度权重编辑"（Astra 演示）提供缺失的梯度基线：仅微调梯度最大的少量权重即可在4k token预算下将思考模式MATH-500准确率从15.4%提升到39.0–45.5%，且少量训练样本即可；梯度编辑本质上等价于在前三层残差流上添加三个常数向量；两种编辑互补，组合后32k预算下达到62.2%，比单独Astra编辑高10个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Qwen3.5-0.8B在思考模式下几乎无法在预算内闭合think块（4k预算下500题仅1–2题完成），准确率仅15.4%；而近期一个无梯度的Astra权重编辑演示声称MATH-500从7.9%升至48.1%，但缺少可比的梯度基线，作者意图补齐该基线并检验两种编辑的关系。

Method: (1) 梯度基线：仅对梯度最大的140至139,750个权重在模型自身答对的MATH训练题上做微调，单GPU训练2–9分钟，50个样本即可；(2) 消融：1,398个随机位置仅得16.3%，验证梯度选择的关键性；(3) 机理分析：梯度集中于前三层各自的"最接近常数"通道，因无偏置项，该通道编辑等价于向残差流加常数向量，用三个固定向量复现同等精度；(4) 在Astra协议（32k token、presence penalty 1.5）下复现Astra编辑并与本文全强度/半强度编辑对比及组合。

Result: 梯度编辑在4k预算下达39.0–45.5%（随机位置仅16.3%）；Astra协议下复现Astra编辑52.1%，本文全强度编辑45.6–45.7%，但在≤16k预算下本文编辑更优（4k时39.5% vs 6.7%）；组合Astra编辑+半强度139,750权重编辑在32k下达62.2%，比单独Astra编辑高10点（p=3e-9）且token用量减半，半强度单独编辑为46.7%。

Conclusion: 梯度微调是一种高效、低成本的基线，其效果可由前三层残差流的常数向量偏移解释；梯度编辑与无梯度Astra编辑机制互补，组合可显著提升准确率并降低推理token开销。

Abstract: Qwen3.5-0.8B rarely finishes reasoning in thinking mode: at a 4,096-token budget only 1 or 2 of 500 MATH-500 answers close their think block (two seeds), and accuracy is 15.4%. A recent demonstration had GPT-6 Astra edit 131,072 of the model's weights without gradients and reported MATH-500 rising from 7.9% to 48.1% at a 32,768-token budget. We provide the missing gradient baseline, then combine the two edits. Fine-tuning only the 140 to 139,750 weights with the largest gradient, on the model's own correct answers to MATH training problems, raises thinking-mode MATH-500 at 4k tokens to 39.0-45.5% after 2-9 minutes of training on one GPU; 50 training examples suffice, and 1,398 random positions give only 16.3%. The gradient concentrates the edit on the most nearly constant channel of each of the first three layers. Because the model has no bias terms, an edit confined to those channels acts as a constant vector added to the residual stream: adding three fixed vectors to the unedited model matches the accuracy of such an edit. Under the Astra protocol (32k tokens, presence penalty 1.5), the Astra edit scores 52.1% (finished and correct) in our reproduction and our full-strength edits reach 45.6-45.7%, but ours win at every budget up to 16k tokens (39.5% against 6.7% at 4k). The two edits are complementary. The Astra edit plus our 139,750-weight edit at half strength scores 62.2% at 32k, 10 points above the Astra edit alone (p = 3e-9), with half as many tokens; our half-strength edit alone scores 46.7%.

</details>
